Chapter 351: Venomous Dentures

The necromancer took in a cold breath, calmed down, and cautiously pondered.

He had no choice but to admit that although Zi Di’s plan was sloppy, it was still feasible. Or in other words, it was quite doable.

But it also had shortcomings.

Cang Xu muttered, “War Merchant’s tower spirit is damaged, and we only have one yellow crab alchemy puppet left, do we really possess the ability to modify a gold level treasure?”

“Even if we can modify it, don’t we at least need a shipyard?’

“If we succeed, we will have an alchemy airship. But from the schematics, we can see that this type of airship is mainly used for transportation, it needs armed escort airships or flying cavalry like griffins guarding it.”

“If we have only one airship, it will undoubtably be frail.”

Zi Di nodded and didn’t deny it, “That’s right, these are challenges we need to overcome.”

“Then we’ll give it a try.” Replied Cang Xu.

He didn’t hesitate anymore, because he felt Z Di’s determination.

It was something seldom felt.

Zi Di was taking a big risk, and her desire was very strong.

But perhaps it was because of that risk that Cang Xu felt a stimulation and passion he hadn’t felt in a long time.

Cang Xu, the dragonman youngster, and Zi Di were already a tight knit team.

If Zi Di’s plan was put into practice, then this small team’s future would be even brighter, and even more hopeful.

In room 21, the two reached a consensus.

The other bidders had laid down the flag already, and Zi Di smoothly won the Balloon Knapsack.

Everyone in the auction hall slowly subsided their shock.

3,800 gold was quite the premium price.

Room 21 raising it to this price made clear they had substantial finances.

No one would have thought that these gold coins in Zi Di’s plan were merely an insignificant investment in the interim. The expenditure needed to remodel it into an alchemy airship would be far more astronomical.

After the Balloon Knapsack was a pair of bracelets.

Magic Conduction Bracelets.

As a silver level magic item, it was very practical. When a magician wore them, they would improve their meditation efficiency and accelerate their natural magic power restoration rate.

The wearer could also engrave different magic formation runes on each bracelet. These runes could speed up a magician’s mental construction of magic models and expedite casting.

This thing seemed to only be appealing towards magicians.

But as a matter of fact, all three of the large mercenary corps, including the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, and pirate groups were interested, with no exceptions.

Large organizations all liked stocking up on magician equipment.

Because magicians were far rarer than fighters, and more difficult to recruit. Such magician equipment were one way organizations, large and small, used to attract magicians.

A single bracelet had a market price of 180 gold, and a pair had a price of 360 gold.

When the price reached 340 gold, there were only two competitors. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in room 14 and the mysterious people in room 21.

Both sides placed bids in turn.

The dragonman youngster shouted, “380 gold!”

Zi Di from room 21 transmitted, “400 gold.”

The dragonman youngster was silent for a moment, then bid, “420 gold.”

Zi Di followed up soon after with oppressive power, “450 gold.”

The dragonman youngster went silent.

Everyone sighed to themselves; these Magic Conduction Bracelets would be delivered to room 21.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has two fire path magicians, one bronze level, one iron level. It seems that the dragonman commander wanted to purchase this equipment for them.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were also defeated. Room 21 truly is rampant. Is there really no one who can reign them in?”

The auction had already reached its midpoint, and gradually, Zi Di and Cang Xu in room 21 turned into a great devil king in everyone’s hearts.

Even the Ten Touch Pirate’s Qi You suffered defeat at their hands.

In this unusual arena, everyone felt the formidable pressure from room 21.

Many people hoped that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would defeat them; they were all disappointed.

Some people sighed: “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps are rich too; they earned so much during the beast tide.”

“But I can understand that they don’t find it necessary to spend money on pointless expenses. 450 gold is a serious premium.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps too has contributed towards depleting the other party’s financial resources. Many drops make an ocean; I don’t believe they have infinite gold coins on hand!”

During these veiled interactions, Zi Di and Cang Xu gradually became the public enemy these buyers unconsciously began to target.

Zi Di’s sense in this aspect was very keen.

During the next auction, she immediately sensed the undercurrent aimed at her.

It did nothing.

The survivors’ plan was to buy, buy, buy.

The implementation of this plan would cause many buyers to despair. If they couldn’t pay the price, how could they accomplish this goal?

This kind of situation would happen sooner or later, it was unavoidable.

Luckily, they had planned a response in advance and divided into two groups. Furthermore, in the previous auction, they had competed against each other so none would suspect that these two groups were actually one.

A witch broom was moved onto the auction stage.

The auction master cleared his throat, then presented it, “This is a witch broom, a silver level alchemy product. It comes from the Broom Star Workshop, an alchemy workshop in the Sheng Ming Empire one of the most renowned producers of flying brooms. Not only are they well regarded in the imperial market, but they also sell them in other faraway continents, and their alchemy works were well received by the elves.”

After saying this, the auction began.

Zi Di’s first bid expressed to outside powers her determination and impatience.

Many buyers kept pace with her.

The broom’s price continuously rose, then after it surpassed the market price, Zi Di suddenly pulled back.

“Why did they stop?” A silver level alchemist master was somewhat stupefied.

He was Hun Tong’s subordinate and a resident of Two Eyes Island.

Zi Di had changed her style, and her withdrawing at the last moment caught many people off guard.

The alchemy master was forced to carry out the deal with a pained face and accept a witch broom designed for women.

The next item was quite unusual, it was called Venomous Dentures.

This was alchemy technology that dealt with the human body.

The silver level dentures were in a drinking glass that was filled with a water-like health solvent.

The dentures only had two teeth, they looked like a mantis’ front claws, bent in the middle, folded, and their tips as sharp as needles.

The auction master introduced it, “In past auctions, we have sold false legs, hook hands, glass eyes, and other wonderful alchemy things. These dentures are one of those.”

“To use these venomous fangs, one needs to have space to put them in. Therefore, we have to pull out one’s teeth and expose their empty gums.”

“Usually, these dentures are folded up so we can eat and drink normally.”

“When it comes time to make use of them, these dentures will straighten, their tips will pop up, and one can effortlessly puncture an iron level’s defense with them. If facing a silver level, one will need to use a compatible battle qi to get a better combat result.”

“It is worth mentioning that these dentures can not only tear, but they can also spray venom up to a distance of 10 meters.”

“In my opinion, the following is the denture’s primary function.”

“It can absorb poison!”

“When we have the misfortune of being poisoned, these dentures can absorb the toxins in our bodies and concentrate them into its teeth. Then when it comes to attack, these toxins can be used by us.”

“Alright then, these silver level venomous dentures start at a price of 120 gold. Let the next auction begin!”

These venomous dentures were more well received than the witch broom.

Naval battles had ship boarding battles.

Because there was a finite amount of space on ships, this caused both sides to fight at close quarters, hand to hand. During that, the sudden use of these venomous dentures could catch the enemy unprepared.

Many treacherous pirates often coated their daggers with poison.

The venomous dentures could not only attack, but they could also respond to the user being poisoned, they were very practical.

The bidding was already very fierce, and when Zi Di joined in, it became fiercer.

The price clambered up to its peak when Zi Di suddenly raised the price substantially.

Everyone fell silent, except for Dian Wan who was impatiently keeping pace and pushed down Zi Di’s bid.

Zi Di laughed and chose to withdraw.

The venomous dentures were won by a dumbfounded Dian Wan.

“Captain, you can’t do that.” His first mate lamented, “We still need to buy medicine; however, you have already wasted almost half our money.”

Dian Wan gnashed his teeth, “Those people in room 21 are too crafty!”

After seeing two people in a row eat a loss, the other buyers gradually returned to their senses.

Room 21 was not so easy to target.

They wanted to raise the price, deplete room 21’s finances, yet instead, they were beaten at their own game.

“A bit of a miscalculation.”

“If we want to deal with them, we have to be more careful!”

“Have you still not investigated who the people in room 21 are?” Hun Tong asked in room 1. He was growing even more interested in Zi Di and Cang Xu.

As an observer, he saw things clearly.

It wasn’t simple to achieve the extent of success that Zi Di had.

Not only did one need to understand and perceive the mood of their competitors, but they also had to have a clear awareness of the specific market price of every item.

“The first time, room 21 decisively pulled back when it exceeded market price. The second time with Dian Wan, they saw through Dian Wan’s temperament, substantially increased the price to provoke Dian Wan, and then pulled back suddenly at the last moment to deplete the Lightning Pirates’ finances.” Hun Tong concluded to himself.

“This subordinate is incompetent.” The subordinate at Hun Tong’s side bowed his head in shame, “This one still doesn’t know who they are.”

Many others were investigating them too.

Pirates being unable to do so was actually pardonable, but Hun Tong was the feudal lord here, yet his investigation had failed, thus it was hard to forgive this subordinate.

However, Hun Tong faintly smiled and didn’t criticize his subordinate too much.

This was actually a secret indulgence of his.

At the time, to build a relaxed ambience, he allowed pirates to come to Two Eyes Island to dispose of their stolen goods and had deliberately slackened his control.

It wasn’t strange that his subordinates were cultivated to think like that too. To some degree, it was Hun Tong’s fault.

“Anyways, Two Eyes Island is confronted with a transition. As a naval transportation hub, I will have to ruthlessly administrate, and such a subordinate is unqualified for that.” Hun Tong admonished himself inwardly.

He muttered a command to the subordinate, “Your inability to investigate them is a dereliction of duty, you need a proper review. But for now, I will give you a way to complete this.”

“Go and knock on room 2’s door, tell the people inside that I, Hun Tong, need to know all pertinent information on those in room 21.”

Chapter 352: Twisted Thorn Cluster Shells

The subordinate blankly stared, while Song Shou too revealed his astonishment.

All of Two Eyes Island was Hun Tong’s domain, yet it seemed there was someone who had a deeper control over it than he?

The subordinate was even more curious: from the beginning, no one had entered room 2. How could there be anyone in there?

But since Hun Tong had commanded it, the subordinate immediately complied.

When room 2’s door opened up, the subordinate was more surprised, “This room had always been closed, how could the attendants inside get in and out?”

“Don’t tell me there is a secret door?” The subordinate recalled some rumors.

It was hard to see into room 2, for it was filled with smoke.

After coming in, the subordinate felt unwell and coughed many times, for the smoke was dense and burned his eyes.

He was barely able to discern that there were two people sitting inside the room.

Their life auras were very distinct.

One was bronze level, the other was an awe inspiring gold.

The subordinate instantly saluted respectfully towards the mysterious gold level.

The mysterious gold fighter’s mouth had a thick cigar in it; he was resolutely breathing it out.

There was a small flame burning at the cigar’s tip and emitting rolling thick smoke, it was the reason why the room was curling with smoke.

After the gold fighter heard his purpose in coming, he smiled and pointed towards the bronze level fighter next to him, saying nothing at all.

The bronze level fighter seemed to be his subordinate and replied, “There are two people in room 21, they are the Wisteria Shop’s level seven elders.”

“Please pass this on to the Feudal Lord, don’t touch those two, they are my quarry.”

The subordinate was greatly surprised, firmly remembered their answer, and immediately withdrew.

As he left, he cautiously closed the door to room 2, still inwardly guessing who that gold level fighter might be.

They not only knew who the buyers in room 21 were, but they also treated them as their quarry.

Who were those two?

What was their relationship with Feudal Lord Hun Tong?

The bronze level was haughty, a fox exploiting a tiger’s might, if he told Hun Tong about it, would he get in trouble for it?

No matter what, he still had to pass on their message.

The subordinate anxiously returned to room 1 and told Hun Tong their answer and warning.

When Hun Tong heard that the two were the Wisteria Shop’s level seven elders, he showed no hint of surprise.

He had actually anticipated that somewhat.

By the time the auction reached its midpoint, each purchase would leak information about them.

They had such financial resources, did not fear Qi You, and had profound auction skills. In an overall survey of Two Eyes Island, it seemed that these two elders had only been heard about, none had ever met these Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders.

“Since they are his quarry, then I’ll have him deal with them. You may leave.” Hun Tong waved his hand. Room 2’s warning words showed they didn’t respect him as the feudal lord, but he wasn’t offended or angry.

“It seems the Feudal Lord knows who the gold fighter in room 2 is and seems to have a deep relationship with them.” The subordinate harbored questions as he withdrew from the room and continued to guard its door.

The auction was still going.

The auction master spoke brightly, “Respected guests, next we shall have consecutive auctions of 12 artillery shell boxes. These artillery shells are all iron level, there are 22 shells in each box, and are of the twisted thorn cluster type.

“When these shells hit a ship, they will cause countless vines to frantically grow, and can, within a few seconds, cover a small ship’s entire deck with them.”

“The vines are filled with thorns that can pierce nearby sailors. The vines also have a formidable entanglement ability that can twist around and snap masts. Together with the thorns, they can cut open sails, penetrate hulls, and trap ships in their entanglement. A fairly outstanding combat result!”

This artillery shell couldn’t be demonstrated here.

But the auction stage had prepared a magic image.

In the magic image, a cannon shot a twisted thorn cluster shell into an empty wooden ship wreckage.

Within a few seconds green vines seemed to slither like countless pythons that strangled the wooden wreckage. Sharp thorns even impaled the surrounding vines, with exceptional malevolence.

In the second magic image, they were facing a pod of narwhals.

The twisted thorn cluster shell was shot into the sea, causing a large amount of vines to burst forth and wrap around two narwhals.

The narwhals mournfully cried out.

After a struggle, the two bronze level narwhals wrenched themselves free with heavy injuries. But not long after swimming away, they died and their blood dyed the surrounding seawater red.

This kind of actual combat result enticed the interest of many.

“I didn’t expect to see artillery shells being auctioned, aren’t they munitions?”

“In past auctions some were sold, but only in limited quantities. This time there were a lot, perhaps some pirates captured them, keeping part of it for themselves, and coming here to dispose of the rest of them.”

“It’s no wonder they weren’t on the auction list!”

The auction audience discussed among themselves while beginning to bid.

This time, the first to act were the pirates.

The Lightning Pirates, the Stench Pirates, the Applause Pirates, the Ten Touch Pirates, and others competed to be the winner.

The pirates had a strong desire for firepower.

The twisted thorn cluster shells especially suited the pirates!

Because when pirates were looting, shooting too many cannons could easily blow up a ship’s merchandise and even sink merchant ships.

The twisted thorn cluster shells solved that necessary sore point.

Although it still harmed ships, they wouldn’t go so far as to sink them, rather the snapped masts or punctured hulls leaking water would greatly hinder a merchant ship’s movement.

The pirates would first use these shells to alter a merchant ship’s motive force, then calmly sail to them, launch a sword fight, settle things, and complete their looting.

The pirates competed with each other, and even One Eye, Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet leader, joined in too.

He wanted to find out how strong these artillery shells really were.

Relying on the two magic images would only give a superficial understanding of them.

Merchant alliance representatives too piled in to bid.

To them, this type of artillery shell could be used to deal with pirate ships and give their transportation fleet time to escape.

The price of iron level artillery shells was about 3 gold coins. 22 iron level shells was then 66 gold coins.

This was the normal price, but twisted thorn cluster shells were rare.

Finally, at 80 gold coins, the Ten Touch Pirates became the ultimate winner.

But that was only the first box.

The others needn’t be anxious.

For the next twisted thorn shells, all parties acted in succession.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was one of them and managed to get one box.

Zi Di also made a move, suffering obstruction and price increases, until finally, she won the survivors another box at 88 gold coins.

The twisted thorn shells were very popular and after the auction finished, there was still people reluctant to move on.

All parties that obtained these artillery shells were delighted, especially when they thought they pulled a little something over room 21.

After the twisted thorn cluster shells was a set of axes.

They were extraordinary weapons made mainly out of star iron – star iron hand axes.

There were 30 altogether.

The hand axes were different from lumber axes; these were clearly made for battle. They were smaller than normal axes and appeared sharper and savage.

The pirates’ eyes shone.

They battled often and could see these axes suited naval battles at a glance. Especially during ship hold fights where these axes could be easily used in those narrow spaces!

There was only one box of hand axes, and as expected, they drew many panicked buyers.

Zi Di robbed them of what they cherished, displaying her substantial wealth as she won the box of hand axes.

After the iron level hand axes was a batch of bronze level swords.

The auction master presented it, “There are a total of 50 bronze level ring swords. When they are thrown, they can whirl around in a loop, and after beheading the enemy, they can automatically fly back to their user’s hand.”

A magic image appeared to display how the ring sword functioned.

In room 14, Zong Ge’s eyes shined at this, and he said to the dragonman youngster, “If I had those ring swords and paired them up with a new battle skill, it would add a hidden trump card to the newly built Quick Knife Battle Squad.”

Thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps joined the bidding war and persevered until the end.

When it was only the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the Bandage Mercenary Corps, and room 21, the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ doorbell rang.

Tripleblade acted as an envoy at the dragonman youngster’s behest and had come to discuss things with the Bandage Mercenary Corps, “Room 21 has robust finances, we should ally against them.”

“What does your corps mean by that?” Commander Beng Dai was somewhat suspicious.

Tripleblade explained in detail, “Our two corps can join hands to deal with room 21. We must win these ring swords and cannot let them revel in their might again!”

“No matter which party is victorious, we will divide these ring swords evenly and give the winner half the price they paid.”

After hesitating for a moment, Commander Beng Dai replied, “Alright!”

He agreed.

The ring swords’ auction price slowly rose.

Because they needed to communicate with each other, the Dragon Lion and Bandage Mercenary Corps both bid at a slow rate, often bidding at the third cry of the auction master.

Meanwhile in room 21, Zi Di was bidding the opposite manner – instant bids.

She raised the price several times in a row, in an attempt to scare off her competitors.

But every time, either the Dragon Lion or Bandage Mercenary Corps followed up slowly afterwards.

After a few confrontations, everyone in the auction hall gradually noticed that something was fishy.

The ring swords’ price had already greatly surpassed the market value, yet none of the three had given up the contest.

“The situation is a bit queer.”

“Don’t tell me those ring swords are worth that much?”

“I actually know that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is raising a suspected battle squad of cultivators, they all use curved blades.”

“The Bandage Mercenary Corps also mostly use cutlasses; their auxiliary commander is a decommissioned officer that teaches the mercenary corps’ younger generation and newcomers.”

“Even if that is so, they would be unlikely to pay such a premium for that.”

“It’s still rising…”

The auction continued.

Commander Beng Dai’s forehead was already covered in sweat.

For the first time, Zi Di’s bid revealed some hesitation.

Finally, after the auction master called out three times, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps became the final winner.

After the dragonman youngster completed the auction delivery, he immediately got up and brought half the bronze level blades to the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ room.

Chapter 353: How Could Others Take It?

The dragonman youngster received a warm welcome when he came into the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ room.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps had naturally come to the auction with ample cash, and both sides exchanged money for goods.

“These blades are adequate.”

“Fortunately, we joined hands and were able to win these.”

“This was a good plan, and if there are more chances, I’d want to collaborate more!” Commander Beng Dai was very happy and felt that getting to know the dragonman youngster was the correct decision.

Zong Ge too was satisfied.

He had 25 blades, more than enough for the Quick Knife Battle Squad.

News that the Dragon Lion and Bandage Mercenary Corps had worked together swiftly spread from those who were smart enough to realize to the rest of the audience.

They expressed their admiration.

“Both the Dragon Lion and Bandage Mercenary Corps did well.”

“That’s right, they suppressed that arrogant room 21!”

“That room really has been too arrogant for too long.”

“That is an interesting collaboration, two corps wanted those things for themselves, and even though it was at a large premium, by sharing the cost the price isn’t too high.”

The guests’ eyes shone, and they seemed to discover a new method.

In room 21, Zi Di and Cang Xu smiled.

They worked together again.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps not only obtained the ring swords, but they also obtained the good will of the other buyers and opened a cooperation with the Bandage Mercenary Corps. Although it was at a high premium, the Bandage Mercenary Corps also paid a portion of the bill.

The survivors had two groups, one positive, one negative. One in the light, one in the dark. They managed to purchase the things they wanted, being bombastic in a low key manner and were able to expand the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ contacts.

It could be predicted that after this auction, the layered identity of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would become even more useful!

When Hun Tong heard about this, he expressed his admiration for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, “There are capable people in that corps.”

He was pleased to see this happen.

Room 21 was too oppressive, and he looked forward to these two mercenary corps working together more.

As far as he was concerned, the higher the auction price, the better!

Hun Tong then began muttering to himself.

The next item was a piece of gold level equipment.

It was a defensive tool, one that could trigger another upsurge of bidding.

“But now…it would be better to make some adjustments to encourage the trend of cooperation against room 21.”

Thus, the item up next was swapped for a pile of belts.

The auction master presented them, “Honored guests, these are famous seaweed survival belts. Although they are just bronze level, they are very practical.”

“As long as they are imbued with energy, they can inflate with air in seawater and form a life buoy, allowing those tossed overboard to float in the ocean.”

“We can also use this belt if we are stranded on a barren island without food; the inflated kelp buoy can be eaten.”

“As of this time, it has already become standard imperial naval equipment.”

According to naval battle data, those allocated with this equipment would usually be able to save many more wounded that were cast into the sea after the battle. If a fleet lacks supplies, a large amount of these seaweed survival belts can provide crucial emergency provisions!”

Everyone listened carefully, and many eyes began to brighten.

Regardless of whether it was pirate groups, the patrol fleet, mercenary corps, or merchant alliance fleets, all of them were very interested in these belts.

Voyages were full of hazards, no one knew what tomorrow would bring.

Having these belts would undoubtably improve their safety floor.

They seemed unremarkable, but every old sailor understood how precious they were.

Even lone figures like Hong Zhu and Shi Fa also thought that wearing this belt would be the dependable choice.

The seaweed survival belt was not expensive.

All parties acted one after the other.

“Honored guests, if an accident happens in the future, and you need food urgently, will you regret it if you don’t have a belt?”

“I stake my reputation on this my friends, this is the only auction of seaweed survival belts here.”

The auction master continued to liven the mood.

In the wake of an intensifying bidding war, the lone figures had no choice but to withdraw from it.

They only wanted one belt, while a batch of them were undoubtedly catered more towards mercenary corps and private groups.

When the bids for the belts rose above market price, the auction had come to an end.

There were still five parties bidding for it.

Among them was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and room 21: Zi Di and Cang Xu.

The dragonman youngster was about to repeat the same trick and contact other bidders when there was a knock at his room’s door.

The door was opened, and the dragonman youngster received One Eye’s envoy.

One Eye was Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet leader and was responsible for buying equipment for his fleet at the auction.

Especially after Hun Tong explicitly told him this post beast tide defense battle: Soon, Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet will be expanded and the waters it protected would also increase several folds.

The envoy gave One Eye’s request for cooperation.

One Eye’s intent was very clear cut, he wanted to copy how the Dragon Lion and Bandage Mercenary Corps worked together.

“They learn quickly!” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself as he agreed to this request.

One Eye was not the only one who contacted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, there was also a merchant alliance representative.

The three parties worked together to compete against the other two.

The fourth party sensed something was amiss and immediately withdrew from the bidding.

After bidding three times, the fifth party, Zi Di, was “forced” to retreat in defeat.

“Room 21 was beaten again.”

“If they didn’t work together, how would they be able to win those belts?”

The delivery to the three parties was swiftly completed.

This news spread, encouraging morale.

Everyone at the auction had finally found a way to deal with room 21.

In room 21, after Zi Di learned of the aftermath, she faintly narrowed her eyes and surmised, “One Eye must have obtained Hun Tong’s directions, and taken the initiative to contact the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“If I am not mistaken, this will happen in the following auctions too.”

“One Eye represents Hun Tong, bringing us closer to all organizations here. Even pirates are no exception.”

He is the tyrant here, and his relationship with merchant alliances and pirate groups is very deep. He also knows far more about them too, advantages we don’t have.”

Sure enough, just as Zi Di had guessed, the next few auctions were all batches of alchemy items that were convenient for parties working together to divide up afterwards.

Zi Di willingly conformed and in each bidding war, was “defeated”.

In every auction, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gained something, sometimes small, sometimes big.

Hun Tong intentionally showed consideration for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, roping in the dragonman youngster every time. His intent was very clear, he wanted to build a closer relationship between them so that in the future, these arms smugglers would choose to sell their munitions on Two Eyes Island.

After several auctions of this type, room 21 had been subdued by a lot.

“They have received sufficient guidance.”

“There is still strength in numbers!”

“But is that really good? Isn’t it unfair for buyers to team up with each other, doesn’t that run counter to the principle of fairness?”

Some were delighted while others questioned the practice.

Some buyers or organizations with bad interpersonal relationships were disgusted at how people were bidding together.

Hun Tong understood the problems with this better than most.

He had been prepared for this.

The next item was a set of Golden Silk Chainmail.

When the buyers saw this chain armor, their minds shook.

“Gold level armor!”

Many low level cultivators began to drool when they saw it, understanding it would never be theirs.

Faces changed in every room on floor two, including Song Shou, Hun Tong, Qi You, as well as the smoker in room 2, anticipation stirred.

It had to be said that Hun Tong was a business genius. He had planned the auction order, and the appearance of this gold level armor not only pleasantly surprised the audience, but it also constrained the general mood of cooperation in the auction and would undoubtedly trigger a surge of bidding.

For a moment, the price bids rose and fell in succession, never ceasing.

Armor was usually more expensive than weapons.

Life was treasured above all else, and no one would shun one’s own protection.

Assuming enough money and battle qi, fighters would usually wear as much armor and protective gear as possible to secure their safety.

This practice was customarily known as “armor layering”.

In this unusual path, there were many famous figures.

For example, one of the navy’s three admirals, Yi Gui, liked to collect all kinds of extraordinary apparel. Every time he went out into the field, he would cover himself in them to improve his defenses.

The fierce competition soon reached its end.

“Room 21, 3,800 gold going once.”

“Room 21, 3,800 gold going twice.”

“Room 21, 3,800 gold going three times.”

Bang.

The auction master’s small hammer fell, “Congratulations to room 21 on winning this gold level armor!”

The auction welcomed its victor.

The buyers were forced to.

Gold level armor could not be partitioned up.

It would be very difficult to join hands in this instance.

Room 21 had revitalized its dynamism in the auction when they succeeded in winning the Golden Silk Chainmail.

“After this armor is disguised, we’ll give it to the commander.” Zi Di happily planned.

“That’s right.” Cang Xu nodded his clear endorsement.

Obviously giving it to Zong Ge would raise their group’s strength more, but to these two, the dragonman youngster was the most important person.

How could the half beastman compare?

After the Golden Silk Chainmail, there was 12 sets of external glass armor.

This type of armor was very peculiar. Normally they would shrink into the form of a bracelet and be worn around a person’s wrist.

When a battle broke out, the spirit would activate it, instantly adorning them with a set of weak glass armor.

This thin layer of weak armor couldn’t stop sword attacks.

But it was good at withstanding energy type attacks and conventional types of magic and divine spells to a remarkable effect.

According to some gossip, this external armor was the Ten Touch Pirates’ spoils of war, looted from an imperial supply ship.

Because the external armor was too singular and its niche too extreme, it was only given to imperial special forces.

Therefore, the final price of this external armor was not that high.

It was still won by Zi Di though.

After that, the next item was a set of mushroom tights, silver level undergarments that combined both healing and defense and was suited for both genders. This garnered the interest of the lone figures and was wrested away by Zi Di at a high price.

After those tights there was three sets of sacred plate armor, all iron level and auctioned in succession.

This sacred plate armor was classified as heavy armor and quite special, for it had been excavated from some seafloor ruins.

According to research, they should have been the equipment of some dead god’s templar knights.

The plate armor had remnants of divine spells and divine power on them, giving the sacred plate armor some unusual abilities.

Under the irate gaze of everyone, the three sacred plate armor sets were taken by room 21.

The armor auctions are hereunto concluded.

The auction had entered its late stage.

A small item appeared on stage, causing many buyers to toss the whole armor affair to the back of their mind.

The auction master lifted a small glass bottle with both hands.

The potion inside the bottle was a light yellow, like urine.

Countless scorching eyes locked into the potion.

The auction master’s presentation was unprecedentedly simple, “This is a gold level healing potion, the price starts at 380 gold.”

Everyone took a long breath, waiting for the auction master to proclaim the beginning of the auction.

“1000 gold!” Zi Di transmitted from room 21.

“?!”

“I…”

Innumerable derisions cried out from every buyer’s heart.

They suddenly noticed that the price was just at the cusp of spilling over!

How could they take it?

Chapter 354: Cooperation

“1,000 gold.” Zi Di shouted out the price.

In his room, Dian Wan’s eyes widened.

He felt his life flash before him.

The Lightning Pirates’ main goal this auction was that gold level healing potion.

For this reason, the pirates had prepared for two months and had accumulated the most gold coins Dian Wan ever had in his entire life.

He had been confident and ready to make a big showing here.

But before the bidding began, Zi Di’s high price of 100 gold seemed to instantly crush him like a mountain.

Dian Wan clenched his fists, he turned red, and his heart welled with resentment and anger.

That price, how could he compete?

It was more than all of the cash he had on hand!

His preparations for this gold level healing potion were useless and even seemed somewhat ridiculous.

Dian Wan felt quite sullen.

Seeing his captain’s distressed expression, the first mate felt things were going bad and promptly held Dian Wan, “Captain, calm down, just calm down a little!”

“I…that damned…room 21…I will…” Dian Wan fumed with rage, gnashing out curses intermittently through his teeth.

The first mate’s heart jumped, and he loudly warned him, “Calm down captain, this is not the sea. This is Two Eyes Island, and there are many gold level fighters here.”

“Ahh!” Dian Wan was hopping mad, he pushed away the first mate with shaking arms and clutched his head as hard as he could.

Despite his best efforts, his hedgehog-like hair still remained tenacious and resumed their straightness soon after.

“1,100 gold” Bid Qi You.

Dian Wan was forced to give up, but Qi You wasn’t.

As a gold level pirate leader, he had started his career far earlier than Dian Wan and had far more money than the latter.

Zi Di immediately followed up with a fierce offensive, “1,200 gold.”

Qi You coldly snorted and wouldn’t be outdone, “1,300 gold.”

Zi Di continued to raise the price without hesitation, “1,400 gold.”

Qi You’s eyelids throbbed, and he hesitated, Zi Di always raised the price by 100 gold, and her voice was very steady, it gave people a feeling that it was neither too fast nor slow, causing Qi You to feel like he was confronting an imperial army. They were calm, steady, and had the style of a regent.

But the hesitation was temporary and Qi You still planned to continue bidding.

The price still hadn’t reached his bottom line yet.

One of his main goals this auction too was that gold level healing potion.

It was something crucial to ensuring his survival as well as recovering the initiative in a deadlocked battle that could tip the scales towards victory, it was invaluable!

But as Qi You wanted to speak, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ room 14 suddenly bid, “1,450 gold.”

Qi You blankly stared.

In the great hall, many other guests were the same too.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is joining this auction too?”

“Do they not have a gold level? Why are they interested in this level of potion?”

“During the beast tide, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps earned too much. They have so much money!”

In room 1, Song Shou asked, “I heard the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is the most popular mercenary corps here? But aren’t they actually arms smugglers?”

Hun Tong smiled, and he answered, “This mercenary corps is quite good, they have many talents, and as for them being arms smugglers, those are merely rumors and there is no evidence proving it.”

Overall, Hun Tong had a good opinion of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The reason was not only because of their information on the necromancer, but also during the beast tide, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had the most pressure on their defensive line yet had done outstanding despite so.

Hun Tong continued, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ second in command has almost reached the gold level but is injured. That injury is very troublesome and needs a gold level healing potion to cure it.”

“For their second in command, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is bound to vie for this potion with all of their strength.”

Hun Tong’s words were skillful.

He leaked some secrets of his own accord to bring him and Song Shou closer.

Song Shou couldn’t help but be curious, “You’re saying their second in command is stronger than their leader. What does their leader think about that? They seem to be very tolerant. Aren’t they worried their second in command will usurp them?”

This was the world of the strong.

The strong were always respected across the world.

A leader had to be the strongest in their organization, otherwise they wouldn’t be able to control the situation, and morale was liable to be unstable.

Hun Tong shook his head, “I’m not sure what they are thinking.”

He didn’t want to continue speaking about this, thus he put his attention to the potion auction proceeding before him.

Hun Tong harbored anticipation for this potion auction, “Today’s gold level healing potion will surely sell higher than before.”

“Because on the Wilderness Continent, the empire’s and the beastman tribe alliance’s great war is close at hand.”

“The empire is preparing war supplies, causing the potions circulating in the market, regardless of quality or quantity, to sharply decline.”

“Especially in the vast ocean, gold level healing potions are always rare.”

“My conservative estimate says that this potion can sell for 1,800 gold!”

Just as he thought that Zi Di in room 21 bid, “1700 gold.”

Hun Tong couldn’t help but narrow his eyes, secretly applauding Zi Di’s skill at bidding.

Zi Di’s price increase was more immense than before, displaying to the other powers her determination to have it, which must have pressured the other competitors greatly.

Furthermore, the specific amount bid also had subtly. 1,700 gold, quite close to 1,800 gold, it was within one or two bids, that’s all.

If the other competitors lacked money, they might withdraw at their own accord.

“1,700 gold…”

“Right now, I only have 1,850 gold on me. Can I defeat room 21 in the next bid or two?”

“Forget about it, this price is too excessive for a consumable.”

For a moment, many hidden competitors were forced to retreat by this price.

Seeing that there weren’t any bids for a moment, Hun Tong shook his head and bid, “1750 gold.”

He took the initiative to raise the price himself.

As he expected, Zi Di immediately followed up, “1,800 gold.”

The price had reached Hun Tong’s expected price level, he hesitated, then chose to withdraw.

“Room 21, 1,800 gold, going once.” The auction began to count down.

“Room 21, 1,800 gold, going twice.”

“1,850 gold.” An unfamiliar voice suddenly interjected.

Everyone present couldn’t help but be disturbed.

“It’s room 2!”

“There is actually someone in that room?”

“This is the first time room 2 has ever made a sound.”

“It seems their main goal is that healing potion.”

The people whispered to each other, all of them were very curious at who the mysterious buyer in room 2 was.

In room 21, Zi Di’s eyes flashed brightly.

“Regardless of who the person in room 2 is, they must have a deep relationship with Hun Tong, otherwise it would be impossible for them to hide who they are up until now.”

“Are they bidding in response to me? I might be overthinking it; the other party might not understand the current market situation or has an immense demand for gold level healing potions…”

Zi Di also knew about the current market situation, 1,850 gold to her had already surpassed the ideal price level.

Zi Di felt a bit intractable.

So far, she had been very familiar with the other bidders, as this was the first time room 2 had bid, Zi Di knew little about them.

Zi Di didn’t know who was in room 2 or how they thought.

After thinking about it, Zi Di placed her bid, “1,900 gold.”

This was the first time she had raised the price by 50 gold, she raised it by the minimum required. Zi Di chose to be cautious.

Just as Zi Di had spoken, in room 14, Fei She immediately sensed something and said to the dragonman youngster, “Cap, captain, please bid immediately, do it now.”

The dragonman youngster didn’t hesitate and instantly shouted, “1,950 gold!”

In room 2, the cigar smoking gold fighter wanted to raise the price, but when he saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps bid, he felt somewhat surprised.

“Since it isn’t room 21, that’s fine by me.”

Room 2 didn’t respond.

The auction master was already counting down.

In room 1, Hun Tong’s eyes narrowed, and he had the urge to raise the price again.

But at the same time, he was hesitant too.

He was worried that if he bought the potion at a high price, it would be wasted in his hands.

“The crux is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ bid.”

“These arms smugglers are pressed to heal Shi Qi’s injury; I don’t need to damage our relationship.”

Hun Tong still wanted to draw support from this channel and make Two Eyes Island a covert point of sale for mutations.

“1,950 gold, room 14, going twice.” The auction master continued the countdown.

The audience was extremely quiet.

The price had surpassed auction records, and everyone silently awaited the outcome.

“If nothing unexpected happens, this gold level healing potion will fall into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hands.” Most of the people had come to the same consensus.

Only Zi Di was rapidly pondering: should I continue bidding?”

The dragonman youngster had shouted a bid of 1,950 gold, and Zi Di understood that must have been Fei She giving him timely advice.

Since both sides were acting in concert, Zi Di wanted to do more.

“Room 2’s intention is unclear.”

“Next up is a large amount of raw material transactions.”

“Even if I have to pay two or three hundred more coins, it’ll be worth it if I can probe some information on them.”

When she thought of that, Zi Di resolutely cried out, “2,000 gold!”

Everyone’s hearts trembled.

“It actually reached 2,000 gold?”

“It’s hard to imagine that its worth 2,000 gold, even if it’s a gold level healing potion!”

“I feel that room 21 was very hesitant to increase the price and did the minimal increase, are their financial resources reaching their limits?”

“We cannot infer that, several times before, room 21 had done that in other auctions that gave people the misconception and caused other bidders to buy items at a premium, depleting their money.”

The dragonman youngster was somewhat surprised too, “This was obviously going smoothly, why did Zi Di still want to increase the price? Could it be that she wants to continue the play to cover the true relationship between us?”

Fei She’s eyes gleamed; he knew from experience what Zi Di intended and hastily extended his hand to block the dragonman youngster, “Cap, captain, don’t, don’t follow up. Wait a moment, wait, wait just a moment.”

The dragonman youngster blankly stared, he saw Fei She was nervous but didn’t understand what he was nervous about. If Zi Di won, the gold level potion would still be theirs.

But the dragonman youngster readily followed good advice and instantly shut up and didn’t make a sound.

Chapter 355: Fragrant Gill Tea

Hun Tong pouted; the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ cowardice disappointed him, but he understood it.

After all, it would cost so many gold coins to buy a single potion, this price was too immense.

A few seconds later, room 2 transmitted, “I offer 2,050 gold.”

Zi Di’s and Fei She’s expressions shifted at the same time.

Zi Di’s brows furrowed, and she didn’t say anything.

Hun Tong couldn’t help but gaze at his neighbor in room 2 and think, “As it turns out, this was a probe…I’m afraid your intentions have already been felt out.”

Fei She at this time said, “Cap, captain, bid, this, that person won’t bid against us.”

Thus, the dragonman youngster yelled as if he was burning his own ships, “2,100 gold!”

Zi Di didn’t say a word, and room 2 was quiet too. Contrariwise, Qi You sneered and soliloquized, “It seems the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has money too.”

Like Fei She presumed, no other competitors appeared, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps became the ultimate victor and obtained the gold level potion.

Many people sighed, “It seems this was another one of room 21’s schemes.”

“Alas, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fell into the trap, they were forced to purchase the gold healing potion at such a high price.”

“I heard their second in command Shi Qi is injured and needs to use a gold level potion to cure it.”

“Oh? It seems the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ internal treatment is really good!”

The gold healing potion was the opening curtain to the medicine auction.

Next, all sorts of medicine wheeled out onto the stage.

There were oceanic magic beast transformation potions, blood weakness improvement medicine, sets of strength medicine, intelligence potions, and spirit stabilization inhalers.

Zi Di didn’t bid.

The survivors had the Magic Medicine Bathtub and could cheaply refine magic potions by themselves.

Contrary to this, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps acted and bought a batch of crystal glue.

Strictly speaking, crystal glue was classified as magic medicine.

But it wasn’t for human consumption.

They covered ship hulls and could help a ship resist water pressure.

Once it was very popular, but in recent years, the empire’s metal coating alchemy technology had surmounted it.

This alchemy product was being phased out.

But the metal coating market share wasn’t high and hadn’t gone into wide scale production yet.

Because metal coating belonged to alchemy processing technology, it needed a skilled alchemy master to make it.

Unlike crystal glue, it could be made through the magic medicine refinement process. Indeed, covering a ship hull did not need complex industrial arts.

Therefore, there was still lots of crystal glue circulating in the marketplace.

This commodity’s price had already tumbled quite a bit and was continuing to do so.

As a result, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps won the batch of crystal glue at a low price.

The auction master then said, “The next several auctions will be for a particular type of magic medicine.”

“Please let me solemnly introduce Two Eyes Island’s latest specialty – the Fragrant Gill Tea!”

The auction master’s tone became different from before, and the magic light beams on stage switched over to being multicolored, constantly changing to build the atmosphere.

Offstage, an alchemist unconsciously sat up straight.

He was Hun Tong’s subordinate and under Hun Tong’s directive, had developed the Fragrant Gill Tea.

He had made progress for years until finally, this year, he had improved the magic medicine’s prescription to the point where he could mass produce it.

“Perhaps some guests have heard of the Fragrant Gill Tea, the cup of Fragrant Gill Tea in my hands is a bronze level magic potion. After taking it, it can allow people to breathe underwater. How long it lasts depends mainly on the user’s life level. It will last 30 minutes for bronze cultivators, about 8 minutes for iron levels, and under a minute for silver levels.”

The magic medicine was all focused on cultivators, ordinary people would find it difficult to use them. That’s not to say the magic medicine wouldn’t affect them, rather ordinary bodies couldn’t stop the magic power from ravaging them.

Continuing on, the auction stage broadcasted a magic image.

A bronze level fighter first took out some Fragrant Gill Tea Leaves.

The tea leaves were as long as a finger, the dark green tea leaves appeared slightly spiraled and were very soft. The user held three tea leaves like he was holding three noodles.

He put the tea leaves into a cup, then soaked them with seawater.

The auction master aptly explained, “Esteemed guests, the Fragrant Gill Tea needs to use seawater. If seawater isn’t available, brine can be used too, but it will affect the medicinal effects a bit. As for the tea preparation temperature, that’s different for every individual, regardless of whether it is boiling or ice cold, it won’t affect the medicinal effects.”

After the bronze fighter drank the Fragrant Gill Tea, three horizontal lines appeared on both his cheeks.

There were six lines altogether, they were all dark green and emitted a faint fluorescence.

The bronze fighter sank into the water, still breathing normally.

The six lines on his cheeks moved in tandem with each breath.

When the auction guests saw this, they grew somewhat interested.

As the auction master declared the starting price, this interest grew stronger.

Under normal circumstances, people couldn’t breathe underwater.

With the help of magic potions, magic, and so on, a person could break the limits of their physiology and be able to breathe underwater.

The Fragrant Gill Tea’s emergence was not at all sensational.

But the auction price for this was rather cheap, and compared to the most ordinary underwater breathing scrolls, it had the advantage of a lower price.

Especially because this was an ocean island, and citizens that made their way on the seas never lacked situations where they needed to breathe underwater.

The auction price rose amongst constant bids.

Every increase wasn’t that big.

Everyone wasn’t that passionate about bidding for the Fragrant Gill Tea.

They were all reasonable, because they were comparing it internally.

There were many things that could allow one to breathe underwater, and from a practical point of view, the Fragrant Gill Tea wasn’t something in short supply.

The auction finished.

Altogether, 3 bronze level Fragrant Gill Tea Plants were auctioned off in succession. One went to the Lightning Pirates, another to the Applause Pirates, and the last to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The magic beams twinkled again, and the auction master yelled, “Next is another Fragrant Gill Tea Plant, an iron level one with a stronger potency!”

When the workmanship rose by a level, the Fragrant Gill Tea Plant not only gave underwater breathing but also dusky sight.

The so-called dusky sight could allow someone to distinguish objects in low light environments, it was a type of vision strengthening magic.

This improved its allure by a lot.

If drinking bronze level Fragrant Gill Tea allowed a cultivator to survive underwater, then the iron level one allowed a cultivator the fundamental ability to fight underwater.

Dusky sight suited underwater battles.

The deeper into the water one went, the dimmer the light, until it was pitch-black in the deep sea, where light was rarely seen.

Because of water pressure, even if human cultivators went into the water, they mostly limited themselves to fighting in the shallow layers, where the iron level Fragrant Gill Tea Plant was very practical to use.

Even Hong Zhu was interested. She was a treasure hunter and sometimes plundered seafloor shipwrecks.

Although it was in the deep sea, she had her own light source and could give herself a dusky field of view. If she drank the Fragrant Gill Tea, it would be better.

Although the Fragrant Gill Tea was iron level while Hong Zhu was a silver level fighter, the tea would still affect her for a few minutes.

What also made Hong Zhu quite interested was that making the tea was easy.

The Fragrant Gill Tea didn’t need boiling water, only seawater. Such a thing made things convenient when she went adventuring for treasure.

As long as she had a few tea leaves in her mouth, at a critical time, she could open her mouth and suck in some seawater, allowing her to drink the tea.

Of course, this tea making conduct was very boorish and treated her own mouth as a cup. During the tea making process, magic power would mix into it and harm the oral cavity.

But Hong Zhu understood that at many dangerous junctures, who cared about a minor throat injury? You might lose your life! Under these circumstances, abrasing one’s throat to give them some survival advantages was worthwhile.

There were 3 batches of iron level Fragrant Gill Tea too.

The auctions of these was much better than the bronze level ones.

Many silver levels, including Hong Zhu, joined the bidding.

The dragonman youngster joined in but wasn’t successful.

Zi Di didn’t say anything.

In a previous auction, she had received an iron level Fragrant Gill Tea plant. Magic potions didn’t appeal to the survivors since they had the Magic Medicine Bathtub.

“I’ve heard of the Fragrant Gill Tea before. Feudal Lord Hun Tong had always been trying to popularize it, but this is the first time he’s used something as powerful as the auction to do so.”

“To the market, this is a relatively unfamiliar medicine. But Hun Tong used his own reputation to endorse it and got the people to acknowledge it.”

This was one of his gains after years of painstaking enterprise construction. Then again, the iron level Fragrant Gill Tea already had two magic effects. If this was its market price, it’d be pretty good. It all depends on how the feedback from practical usage is.”

Everyone whispered to each other as the auction master took out another Fragrant Gill Tea plant and began to present it.

“Ladies and gentlemen, next is this Fragrant Gill Tea plant, it has attained the silver level!”

“IT can give the user three effects and is stronger than the iron level.”

“The fundamental effect of underwater breathing has been substantially improved, dusky sigh has been elevated into dark sight, and it also bestows the user with the ability to sense the flow of water.”

“Everyone, you didn’t mishear, its water flow perception!”

“This ability can allow you to sense every change in the water and augments your swimming ability greatly. Even if you are a non-swimmer, you can self-teach yourself and master the ability to swim.”

Next, a magic image appeared, showing a silver level fighter taking the Fragrant Gill Tea and diving into the water.

He swam like a fish, and in pitch darkness, he accurately found something concealed in the river. Finally, he freely tossed and turned in a rapidly changing whirlpool.

Everyone had all different expressions.

The bronze level Fragrant Gill Tea plant had similar competing products and only had the price advantage. The iron level Fragrant Gill Tea plant had two magic effects and in an ocean environment, had high practical qualities.

While the silver level Fragrant Gill Tea plant was actually a hundred percent quality good.

In particular, it had three magic effects, fundamentally changing the value of the entire magic medicine!

Chapter 356: Miracle Flower Pot

The silver level Fragrant Gill Tea plants caused another upsurge in bidding.

Regardless of whether it was lone figures, pirates, mercenary corps, and even the Wealth Church and Fertile Land Church priests, all of them joined in.

In the end, room 3’s Qi You, room 21’s Zi Di, and the Wealth Church’s priest each got one batch.

“Congratulations.”

“You deserve it.”

“This founding set of Fragrant Gill Tea will be certain to elevate your alchemy mastery level.”

In the auction hall, some people were whispering about the alchemy master.

The alchemy master was Hun Tong’s subordinate and had been the one responsible for the development of the Fragrant Gill Tea.

The Fragrant Gill Tea auction went great, it not only was revealed successfully, but it had also obtained a great beginning. All of this made the alchemy master the target of everyone’s eyes and praise.

The alchemy master was bright red; after so many years of hard work, he finally had gotten results.

But he had a regret in his heart, “A pity my talent has reached its limits, I can’t develop a gold level Fragrant Gill Tea. Otherwise, my title would have been higher.”

He looked at room 1.

Only he knew the inside story.

The Fragrant Gill Tea’s base materials was provided by Hun Tong. Its production needed fishmen, the higher the life level of a fishman, the better Fragrant Gill Tea could be made.

He only improved on this basis.

The Fragrant Gill Tea’s secret and benefits bound Hun Tong and the alchemy master tightly.

“It seems from now on, I can only live and study on his ship.” The alchemy master’s heart welled with comprehension.

In room 1, Song Shou congratulated Hun Tong, “With that unique product, Two Eyes Island will grow more prosperous.”

Hun Tong smiled so much that his eyes narrowed, it was his heart-felt joy.

Two Eyes Island was a commerce island, and what truly made it prosperous was pirate booty.

But soon, because of the imperial’s overall strategy, Two Eyes Island would turn Two Eyes Island into a transportation hub with strict defenses, and pirates would no longer be able to come.

Under those circumstances, Two Eyes Island having its own unique product could still attract many people to trade on the island.

Hun Tong’s investment in the development of the Fragrant Gill Tea and fully backing his alchemist had finally paid off.

Two Eyes Island’s development was central to who he was.

“There are bronze level, iron level, and even silver level Fragrant Gill Tea, shouldn’t there be a gold level one too?” The people who didn’t buy it hoped.

But afterwards, the auction master didn’t take out another Fragrant Gill Tea.

In fact, the magic medicine auction ended on this.

According to the auction list, the auction had entered the later stage.

A series of antiques and calligraphies were brought on stage.

Their biggest collective feature was that they all had high collectible worth.

The great merchant alliance dealers were the most active purchasers.

Even though these things were ordinary goods, they could be resold and snapped up on the Sheng Ming Continent by great nobles, scholars, and collectors brandishing large amounts of gold coins.

These last few years, Two Eyes Island gathered many such goods.

Part of it was from treasure hunters like Hong Zhu who plundered shipwrecks and discovered pirate treasures.

Another part of it was from pirates who had plundered it.

Although Cang Xu was a scholar, and these antique calligraphies could be studied, he wasn’t interested in them and didn’t act.

Zi Di didn’t either.

She was very interested and knew there was great profit in reselling them, however, she didn’t have time for that. She needed to contact the Bai Zhen Clan, redeem herself, make the medicine database, and build up the Bloodline Purification formation. In the coming days, she wouldn’t have the energy or time to return to the Sheng Ming Continent to resell these antique calligraphies.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were not interested at all in these things.

Then the extraordinary level goods appeared.

The auction master held an earthen yellow flower pot, and described it in detail, “Distinguished guests, I must properly introduce what this valuable thing in my hands is.”

“It seems ordinary and unremarkable; however, it is actually THE Miracle Flower Pot.”

“You didn’t mishear, it is THAT Miracle Flower Pot from the renowned history.”

“Before the Sheng Ming Continent was unified, there were 17 kingdoms seizing each other’s territory and embroiling themselves in war. The largest among them had a crafty and unruly princess. To save her meticulously bred magic plant, the Beast Rose Flower, she didn’t hesitate to give the order to attack Vitality Forest to capture the forest’s most central flowing spring.”

“The kingdom’s troops were powerful, and Vitality Forest’s tribal alliance was not their opponent; they were routed step by step.”

“In their moment of life and death, a great druid couldn’t bear to see the forest destroyed and personally came to mediate between the parties. He told the princess that even if she eradicated Vitality Forest and excavated the flowing spring, it wouldn’t revive her dying Beast Rose Flower, but he had something that would.”

“The princess and he made an agreement, as long as he could save her most beloved Beast Rose Flower, she would let Vitality Forest live.”

“The great druid prepared for three days and nights, then gave the princess a flower pot.

“This is that most famous Miracle Flower Pot in history!”

“When the princess’ withered Beast Rose Flower was planted into it, it miraculously came back to life and regained its vigor.”

A long section of past events spilled from the auction master’s mouth.

Many people listened to this with shining eyes, the Miracle Flower Pot became even more valuable to them.

The auction master then said, “Of course, the Miracle Flower Pot’s magical power is constantly consumed when it is used. It was once a legend level item, but after endless days of it being in continuous use, its workmanship fell from the legend level to just the silver level.”

“The starting price is 100 gold; every price increase must be at least 20 gold. Now let the auction begin!”

The dragonman youngster was the first bidder, “120 gold.”

Shi Fa acted too, “140 gold.”

The alchemy master that had just been pushed into the limelight bid, “180 gold.”

When the price rose to 260 gold, the Fertile Land Church priest on the second floor said, “300 gold!”

“Old friend?” The Wealth Church priest accompanying him threw a puzzled expression.

The Fertile Land priest smiled, “You know too that when people grow old, they hope to grow some flowers and plants.”

The Wealth Church priest, naturally, wasn’t deceived, he profoundly laughed, “With 300 gold, you can buy silver level armor. Old friend, you spend a lot on growing this, your hobby isn’t cheap.”

The Fertile Land priest chuckled and continued to elaborate, “I am also interested. I have to return with something from this auction, otherwise wouldn’t it be a waste to attend it? Of what I’ve seen that flower pot is the only one to catch my eye.”

The Wealth Church Priest’s eyes shined, and was outwardly silent, only guessing to himself, “That flower part was on the auction list. Although you bid several times in other auctions, you weren’t sincere in them, you just wanted to join in the fun. Nevertheless, this time you want to win in a single move. It seems that you value this flower pot greatly.”

The auction master began to count down, “300 gold, going once…going twice…”

“320 gold.” A competitor appeared.

The Fertile Land priest’s face slightly changed; a steep pressure formed in his heart.

Because the competitor was from room 21.

During this auction, the most in the limelight was room 21, and was accepted to have the most wealth here.

However, after the other people coordinated to block them, room 21’s momentum was greatly restrained and wasn’t as rampant as it was before.

The Fertile Land priest was silent for a moment, then calmly bid, “340 gold.”

Everyone in the auction hall began to whisper to each other.

340 gold to buy a flower pot, that was too extravagant.

Some of the people recognized that the Fertile Land priest was bidding based on his voice and seating.

Zi Di was the same.

She leaned over to mull to Cang Xu, “This priest hasn’t acted that much this auction. This time, to bid on a flower pot, he entered in the final phase and is still competing, that’s somewhat odd…”

Cang Xu had seen many auctions and had gradually grasped some knacks at it.

Zi Di smiled and nodded, “The other party seemed to have come here for it.”

“It is indeed odd. Even if it is the true Miracle Flower Pot, it’s only silver level and not worth 340 gold.”

In that case, this is only one possibility. That flower pot is actually concealing its worth, or to the Fertile Land Father, that treasure can provide him many things.”

When thinking of that, Zi Di bid, “360 gold.”

The Fertile Land priest’s heart immediately thumped.

Room 21 had placed another bid, this caused discouragement to well up inside him.

He had deliberately remained silent to express hesitation and feign weakness to his opponent: I might pull out at any time, if you want to maliciously raise the price to deplete my money, then be careful.

But Zi Di’s next bid didn’t give others a sense of hesitation.

The Fertile Land priest opened his mouth, naturally he wanted to bid again, but the price had risen to 380 gold.

This price was a little over his limits.

If he raised the price further, others would realize how valuable this Miracle Flower Pot really was!

Subconsciously, the Fertile Land priest didn’t want to expose this secret, nor did he want to provoke more covetous competitors as a result of the former.

“If I let room 21 purchase it, can I privately buy it from them?”

“That won’t do.”

“The people in room 21 are so mysterious that not even the Wealth Church priest knows who they are.”

“The other side has robust finances; what price will it take to buy it from them?”

“What if they have no intention of reselling it?”

“After a quick pondering, the Fertile Land priest still felt that now was the best chance to obtain the Miracle Flower Pot.

He was forced to clench his teeth and bid higher, “380 gold.”

“Huh, it increased to 380 gold.”

“It was the Fertile Land priest, that old man sure has money.”

“380 gold for a silver level flower pot? There must be a big secret inside it!”

The auction hall grew restless because of this bid.

The Wealth priest glanced at the Fertile Land priest, and saw the latter sitting upright and still, his face grave.

The former’s heart was trembling, but he didn’t ask any questions.

In room 21.

Zi Di’s eyes flashed, “Fascinating.”

She then bid, “400 gold.”

Room 21 following up was expected.

Since the auction had begun, room 21’s conduct and wealth had engraved themselves into the people’s hearts.

Seeing a flower pot priced at 400 gold gave others a strange feeling, however, it was easy to accept that room 21 was butting themselves in.

Who would blame them for being rich!

Chapter 357: Ruined Ambition

Upon hearing Zi Di bid 400 gold, the Fertile Land priest appeared unmoved, yet his heart was shaking.

“How terrible.” Sensing things were getting worse, the Fertile Land priest noticed that room 21 was like a predator smelling blood, picking up the first signs of prey and starting to pursue him closely.

“What to do?”

“Giving up is impossible.”

“Then I can only do my best to move forward.”

The Fertile Land priest grinded his teeth and yelled, “460 gold.”

He suddenly increased the price by 60 gold and was now offering 460 gold for a flower pot. Even though this flower pot was a silver level historical antique, that price was too abnormal.

The Wealth Church priest next to him was startled and puzzled, “What are you doing, old friend?”

The Fertile Land priest squeezed out a smile, “I can’t help myself; I fancy it.”

The Wealth Church priest’s heart questioned, “Still not telling the truth?”

Upon seeing the Fertile Land priest’s competitor was room 21, he was interested in seeing a good show.

“Interesting.” Zi Di smiled as she raised her voice, “480 gold.”

The Fertile Land priest’s face sank like water. There was no need for masks now as he resolutely followed up, “500 gold.”

Zi Di was fearlessly bid, each time only raising the price by 20 gold.

The Fertile Land priest unwillingly followed up each one.

The dumbfounded audience witnessed the flower pot’s price actually ascend to 680 gold!

“Insane, both of them are insane.”

“Room 21 is rich and overbearing, but the Fertile Land priest is acting like them too?”

“For a trifling silver level flower pot? No, this flower pot must conceal immense value!”

The buyers found it hard to hide their shock.

The three gold levels were also watching pensively.

The Fertile Land priest suddenly bid, “780 gold!”

All of a sudden, he added 100 gold to the price.

The auction master’s hand trembled, even though he had presided over many auctions, even this sum astonished him.

“He took the initiative to go on the offensive!” The Wealth priest understood clearly, he attentively looked at the Fertile Land priest next to him, noticing that the latter’s lips were tight, and his eyes were filled with determination.

The Wealth priest couldn’t help but feel extremely curious: what was the flower pot really worth that his old friend would spare no cost to get it?

“Hehe.” Zi Di laughed, since the auction had begun, this was the first time she had encountered this kind of situation.

She had expected it would come to pass, but it wasn’t from the Ten Touch Pirates or any of the gold levels, but rather the Fertile Land priest.

The Fertile Land priest had gone on the attack, it was clear he had ample money.

“Perhaps he joined this auction to acquire this flower pot. Unfortunately, I saw through it and interfered. “Comprehension formed in Zi Di’s heart.

“800 gold.” Zi Di continued to raise the price.

The Fertile Land priest bit his teeth, “900 gold!”

He raised the price again by an astonishing 100 gold.

Everyone was in a small uproar.

The priest’s strength in this amazed them.

Many people sighed to themselves: they really didn’t think room 21’s most ferocious competitor was a silver level priest.

Who would be so aggressive in bidding against room 21?

The Fertile Land priest was the first to do that.

Zi Di continued, “920 gold.”

The Fertile Land priest was receiving widespread praise and expectations, most of the buyers hoped he was victorious and would ruthlessly stomp down on room 21’s limelight.

But the aforementioned priest was now looking worse for wear, a trace of paleness spreading across him.

Zi Di’s bidding strategy made him feel jittery.

Every time she calmly raised it by 20 gold, leisurely even.

The Fertile Land priest’s price raising tactics didn’t intimidate the other party, on the contrary, Zi Di’s strategy of raising the price by the minimum of 20 gold caused the priest to feel how powerful his opponent was.

The priest’s third price raise was: “1,000 gold!”

With zero hesitation, Zi Di followed up with, “1,020 gold.”

Sweat soaked the priest’s head.

He couldn’t go on any longer.

He had brought along his life savings to this auction, 1,200 gold.

But he didn’t think he would have to use everything he brought on this flower pot. In his presumptions, other buyers wouldn’t notice this flower pot’s secrets, and even if they did, they couldn’t utilize it, only he could.

But reality was very cruel, and when the auction came, the flower pot’s price was now over a thousand, and he began to feel his hope of success becoming more and more remote.

“What should I do?”

“Substantial price increases don’t intimidate the other party.”

“Damn that room 21, they are so troublesome…”

As the auction master began to count down, the Fertile Land priest was forced to frantically bid, “1,080 gold.”

“A weakness!” Zi Di’s mouth twitched up.

If the Fertile Land priest continued to raise the price by 100 gold or the minimum of 20 gold, then there would be no issues.

But in his haste, he raised it by 60 gold, not high or low, which allowed people to see what was really going on.

“I am closing in on his bottom line. So, how about this?” Zi Di thought on it, then said, “1,180 gold.”

This was a mortal blow.

For a split second, the Fertile Land priest’s face turned white.

His brows wrinkled tightly, he turned deathly pale, and his forehead’s sweat grew more detailed.

“1,200 gold.” His voice was hoarse.

“I win.” When Zi Di heard his voice, she reveled in her victory.

“Old friend, it seems that this flower pot is quite important to you. I can help you!” The Wealth Church priest suddenly interjected.

“This…” The Fertile Land priest stared blankly.

To be forthcoming, he and the latter were just acquaintances, not close enough to lend money to each other. Even if he would loan money, it wouldn’t be much.

But this time, the Wealth Church priest volunteered at his own accord.

He then said to the Fertile Land priest, “I can lend you 500 gold.”

“You don’t need to worry about malicious intentions.”

“For one, I cannot bear to see room 21 so rampant. Second, I feel curious at what this flower pot’s secret is. Third, I believe in the Wealth Goddess, and know well how important investments are.”

The Wealth Priest’s words couldn’t help but move the Fertile Land priest.

The Fertile Land priest had been forced to the very edge of the precipice by Zi Di, and at the end of his rope, a bridge suddenly appeared in front of him.

He deeply sighed with a thankful heart. Then with a hapless mood, he disclosed his secret to the Wealth Church priest.

“I came to attend this auction for that Miracle Flower Pot.”

“I have no other goals.”

“It is indeed the true Miracle Flower Pot, and the materials it is made from is my religion’s Abundant Earth.”

The Wealth Church priest’s eyes widened, and he went hoarse, “Your church’s holy soil – Abundant Earth?”

The Fertile Land Church believed in the Fertile Land Goddess.

After every large celebration, the goddess might bestow the earth of her holy kingdom.

This earth was very fertile, could grow any crop, and provided an abundant harvest.

The Fertile Land priest continued, “By coincidence, I found out many secrets about the tale of the Miracle Flower Pot.”

“In those days, in order to protect Vitality Forest, the great druid contacted his two friends.”

“One was one of our bishops and the other was a great alchemy master.”

“With the three’s combined efforts, they refined the Miracle Flower Pot.”

“The flower pot’s main material was Abundant Earth, not only that, but also its seals were both my god’s divine spells and druidic nature divine spells.

“Because of that, the Beast Rose Flower survived.”

“I originally planned to purchase it and restore its Abundant Earth. It is my God’s holy soil, and even if it has gone through wear and tear, it is still my God’s holy soil!”

“With this holy soil, so long as I dedicate it, it might elevate my position.”

The Wealth priest suddenly saw the light, “So that’s how it is, old friend, that is why you keep on bidding. I shall lend you a hand!”

After receiving the Wealth priest’s help, the Fertile Land priest had regained his confidence. He tidied up his mood and calmly bid, “1,220 gold.”

His bid went a little beyond Zi Di’s expectations.

Especially the Fertile Land priest’s voice, it no longer had the franticness from before.

“Did he get someone to fund him? Or was he merely pretending?” Zi Di thought that the former was more likely.

She had no fear.

The Fertile Land priest’s dogmatic perseverance made her even more interested in this flower pot.

In any case, she had money!

Zi Di bid, “1,300 gold.”

This time she went on the offensive and raised the price by 100 gold.

The Fertile Land priest took a deep breath, “1,320 gold.”

“1,400 gold.” Zi Di continued to attack.

The Fertile Land priest glanced at the Wealth Church priest and noticed the other party had grown unsightly, yet he was forced to follow up, “1,420 gold.”

Zi Di was calm, “1,500 gold.”

The Fertile Land priest, “...”

The Wealth Church priest was somewhat baffled, he couldn’t help but urge, “Old friend, why aren’t you bidding?”

The Fertile Land priest looked agonized, at this moment he looked like an old farmer who had spent half a year of hard farming, only to see his crops fail. He shook his head and forced a smile, “I cannot, room 21 is determined to win this flower pot. Even if I offer the maximum price I can, it will be surpassed by them.”

“But this is a rare opportunity. If you restore the Abundant Earth, it will allow you to go a step further beyond.” The Wealth priest encouraged, “If it was me, I wouldn’t give up now. Even if it is only a sliver of hope.”

“You’re right, old friend.” The fertile Land priest then impassionedly kept on bidding.

But just as he expected, the final price he bid of 1,700 gold was surpassed by Zi Di’s price of 1,720 gold.

The Fertile Land priest powerlessly sighed.

The Wealth priest nevertheless still had some resolve and said, “I can subsidize you another 300 gold!”

The Fertile Land priest was flabbergasted.

The Wealth priest smiled, “I believe that investing in a bishop is worth the money!”

But this time, the Fertile Land priest shook his head, “Many thanks for your kindness old friend, but forget it this time. With that money, even if I offer 2,000 gold, the other side will still be the winner.”

“Even if I succeed, the price is too high.”

“I don’t have the ability to restore the materials, and as far as I know, the cost of asking a great alchemy master to do so doesn’t come cheap.
