Even if I restore it, how much Abundant Earth is actually left? The true Miracle Flower Pot was a legend level treasure, while in current times, it has degenerated to a silver level one.”

The Wealth Church priest’s gaze flashed.

He understood.

This price competition with room 21 had beaten to death the Fertile Land priest’s will and battle intent.

When faced with the high price needed to exchange for a possible future, the Fertile Land priest cowered away.

Chapter 358: Life Saving Pad

“Truly a pity.” The Wealth priest sighed, then consoled, “However we aren’t without our gains, us raising the price to this degree must have greatly depleted room 21’s finances. The entire auction hall should thank us for this!”

The Fertile Land bitterly smiled and nodded silently.

He had come to this auction to buy the Miracle Flower Pot, but he ended up suffering a crushing defeat and returning with nothing.

He was uninterested in the upcoming auctions, and if it wasn’t for how damaging it would be for the Wealth priest’s face, he would have left the venue already.

“Let’s congratulate room 21.”

“This was indeed a peerlessly marvelous bidding war.”

“Congratulations to our honored winner of the Miracle Flower Pot.”

“This antique is truly worth collecting!”

The auction master was very red with excitement.

This flower pot’s estimated auction price was 320 gold, but it ultimately reached a sales price of 1,720 gold, a full 1,400 gold above that.

This was a rare sight indeed!

That auction might have set a new record. It would surely be an experience the auction master could brag about for a good while.

In room 1, Hun Tong was smiling too.

The sales price was beyond his expectations too.

He thought about it, then called a subordinate over to instruct them to send room 21 some iron level Fragrant Gill Tea and to tactfully put forth a small request.

During the transaction, as the subordinate checked Cang Xu’s gold coins and handed over the Miracle Flower Pot, he said, “Two Lords, the Feudal Lord is very curious about this treasure. If you two Lords don’t mind, the Feudal Lord is willing to pay for this treasure’s secret.”

“What secrets could it have?” Zi Di raised her eyebrows, “I want to know too. Wouldn’t it be better if we cooperate and have the Feudal Lord step forth to ask the Fertile Land priest together? Don’t worry, I know information is valuable and am willing to assume the cost of it.”

The subordinate was gob-smacked, and he returned to Hun Tong to report the truth.

Hun Tong laughed.

If Zi Di rebuffed them, he might have guessed the other party was intentionally hiding something. But since the other party suggested this kind of demand, Hun Tong felt that they might not know anything about it.

“Then do that, go ask the Fertile Land priest about it.” Hun Tong was willing to cooperate.

“What, they actually don’t know the flower pot’s secret?” When the Fertile Land priest heard the subordinate’s question, he nearly coughed blood.

If they didn’t know, then why did they compete with me for so long?

They really were so rich and imposing!

They simply were intolerable bullies!!

“I won’t say, let them guess themselves.” The Fertile Land priest darkly spat out his refusal.

Naturally, the subordinate didn’t dare to offend a silver level cultivator, especially a member of the clergy.

“What a pity, if they agreed perhaps I could get in contact with room 21. Perhaps it would be possible to retrieve the flower pot from them.” The Wealth priest disapproved of the Fertile Land priest’s decision, but he didn’t say anything.

Right now, the Fertile Land priest was in a terrible mood, and the Wealth priest was unlikely to say anything that brought misfortune on his head.

The Miracle Flower Pot’s unexpected bidding surge and out of place sales price had left a deep impression on all of the spectators.

This also caused the following items to be unable to arouse any interest, they only felt a spell of boredom.

On the whole, antique items essentially had a higher collection value than practical value.

These things appealed to collectors and old school nobles, however, those attending this auction were looking for more useful things.

It wasn’t worth buying some antique pieces, even if they were extraordinary, they weren’t worth it.

Wouldn’t it be more appealing to buy some real equipment, medicine, and scrolls?

Thus, few people participated in these auctions compared to the previous parts.

The auction mood began to gradually grow muffled.

At this time, the auction master took out a long case and took out a fishing rod.

The fishing rod was blue, seemed to be made from bamboo, and was seeped in a gentle jade-like luster.

The fishing rod made many eyes shine.

The fishing rod could at least be used on the seas, unlike the calligraphy, paintings, and other antiques previously.

But the fishing rod was an antique too.

“Respected guests, this is the Ocean King’s Fishing Rod.”

The auction master presented, “Its owner was a prestigious legend level magic chef – Lan Bao Yi!”

“This auxiliary fishing rod was made by his close friend as a birthday gift.”

“Lan Bao Yi used this auxiliary fishing rod to personally hook many valuable fish for ingredients.”

“That’s right, the Ocean King Fishing Rod’s main purpose is to increase the chance of catching rare fish. It is not only an alchemy creation, but it has also been blessed by the gods.”

“There is clear evidence that indicates its prior owner used it to catch a Time Fish!”

“Of course, using it also has its risks.”

“The previous owner used it to catch a Savage Shark, and the hunter instead became the prey.”

Time Fish were a rare and highly difficult magic fish to catch.

It was said that they not only taste delicious, but that they also immensely benefited one’s cultivation.

As for the Savage Shark, that was a type of ferocious predator.

Every Savage Shark was at least a saint level.

It wasn’t a normal shark, instead through constant slaughter and possible bloodline change, a shark could become one.

Hun Tong was quite particular and didn’t deliberately hide its side effects.

After hearing that the fishing result could not be controlled, the dragonman youngster’s interest waned.

He was someone who acted in an orderly manner.

Although sea angling felt fascinating, if one drew in a Savage Shark, it would bring disaster not only to him, but also the other survivors too, something he didn’t wish to see.

As for Zong Ge, he was utterly disinterested in this leisure type item.

Finally, the Ocean God Fishing Rod was accepted by the Iron Club Mercenary Corps.

Their commander paid a considerable price for it.

The auction master introduced the next item.

This is the famous Flesh Changing Clothes.”

The auction master raised a flesh colored bra.

“The romantic tale between the female Marquis Rou Mei and the poor youngster Gang Bian is widely known.”

“When they first met each other, the Marquis Rou Mei had been observing how people lived, and went incognito, and to conceal her city-devastating beauty, she wore this alchemy undergarment.”

“After wearing it, the Marquis transformed from a graceful and refined lady into a fat woman. With additional disguises on top of it, she turned into a mediocre female cook.”

“The unaware Gang Bian never judged by appearances, and through a string of encounters where he repeatedly helped Rou Mei, he displayed his manly stature and kindheartedness, gradually gaining the Marquis’ affection.”

“This is the Marquis’ very Flesh Changing Clothes she wore in those days.”

“A silver level alchemy treasure.”

“When a hand touches it, it feels as warm as a beautiful woman. Smell it carefully, it seems to have some fragrance on it.”

The auction master looked infatuated, then declared the starting price and the beginning of the auction.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was even less interested in this item.

The next item appeared.

It was a completely rectangular white cloth; it looked like cotton. Cloth protruded from each side and looked like wings spreading out.

The white cloth emitted a silver level aura.

“Look carefully, everyone.” The auction master raised the white cloth and poured battle qi into it.

Concurrent with energy imbuing into it, the white cloth emitted a pure light that flowed in an exquisite pattern.

This item wasn’t on the list, and many people were looking at it inquisitively.

“The Life Saving Pad?” Someone in the audience identified it.

“That’s right, it’s the Life Saving Pad.” The auction master excitedly stated, “It is not a human thing, it comes from the elves.”

“In elven history, there was a demigoddess called Guang Hua Lan.”

“The Elven Kingdom was in a crisis, so Guang Hua Lan enlisted to spare her father. She always triumphed and victory was her constant companion.”

“When she had an audience with the king, she revealed herself willingly and made known she was a woman.”

“Early on in her enlistment, the Life Saving Pad helped her greatly.”

“It helped her disguise her gender and in crucial moments, helped staunch her bleeding.”

“When we imbue it with battle qi or magic power, the alchemy treasure’s main function will activate, which is healing.”

“The more of the user’s blood it absorbs, the stronger the healing effect.”

“The Life Saving Pad is worthy of the name!”

“It can staunch many injuries in a convenient fashion!”

“Let the next auction begin.”

The Life Saving Pad’s auction was far livelier than the last two.

Because it could heal, its functionality increased several fold.

A few pirate groups were among the fiercest fighters.

Pirates didn’t have a clergyman. They had a serious lack of instantaneous healing methods.

In the vanguard, whether there was a clergyman or priest that could heal in a timely fashion made a difference.

Only when the Pirate Throne had a new master were pirates able to have their own clergy.

The Life Saving Pad’s healing was instantaneous. Although it only affected one person, who would dislike having more things that preserved one’s life?

Although Zi Di was a ghost girl, she absolutely did not disparage it.

“I can’t use it, but I can give it to the commander.” With this notion in mind, Zi Di once again displayed her terrifying financial resources, and with a bullish attitude, intimidated the bidders into withdrawing and claiming the antique.

The Life Saving Pad’s auction was the climax of the antiques auction.

The auction order had been intentionally arranged by Hun Tong.

He had predicted that the Life Saving Pad would be welcomed.

However, the Life Saving Pad’s final sales price was still far lower than the Miracle Flower Pot’s.

After the Life Saving Pad was some extraordinary antiques.

There was the classical oil painting “The Dazzling Star Studded Heavens”. There was the famous statue “Sufferer”. And also a cookbook.

This cookbook aroused the interest of the dragonman youngster, Zi Di, and Cang Xu.

Because this cookbook’s author was a vampire duke. He was a historically renowned gourmet, warrior, and arsonist. He liked culinary delicacies and in his later years, he compiled this cookbook to be given to future generations.

Chapter 359: Vampire’s Cookbook

The food recorded in the vampire’s cookbook was naturally not conventional food, but rather blood.

The Vampire Duke recorded how every species’ blood tasted, when to drink them, what the optimal way to drink them was, and other such things.

To help his offspring and their descendants, he sealed the blood of many species in this alchemy book.

“Of course, the legend level dragon and nine headed serpent blood is no longer sealed in it.”

“This cookbook has changed hands too many times, and many collectors extracted its blood samples for alchemy materials and so on.”

After the auction master gave a detailed explanation, the auction began.

The highest level blood sample still in the Vampire Duke’s cookbook was merely silver level.

All of the gold level blood samples had been extracted already.

In fact, the existent blood samples were also incomplete, only parts of them were preserved.

Even so, this was something Zi Di and Cang Xu had to have!

Because with this cookbook, the survivors could save lots of time and energy.

In order to purify bloodlines, they were undertaking a major engineering project.

The blood samples inside the cookbook would reduce the construction period!

Not many people were bidding.

Besides magicians, there were a few merchants bidding too. The latter wanted to speculate while the former wanted to use the blood samples as alchemy or casting materials.

It had to be said that the guests at Two Eyes Island’s auction were pragmatic folk.

Zi Di didn’t act.

Her acting might trigger a malignant price competition.

The dragonman youngster didn’t disappoint expectations and beat the last competitor, Commander Tie Bang, to become the ultimate winner.

Commander Tie Bang spent too much money buying the Ocean King Fishing Rod earlier.

Overall, this was a relaxed auction.

What no one knew was that this was even more important to the survivors than the Miracle Flower Pot was to the Fertile Land priest!

Upon seeing the auction master’s hammer drop and room 14 becoming the victor, Cang Xu and Zi Di nearly cheered.

But it was only after the delivery was completed and the dragonman youngster clutched the Vampire Duke’s cookbook in his hands did Cang Xu and Zi Di completely relax.

The two people conversed; their hearts deeply moved.

“I didn’t expect to have such a thing at this auction.”

“Yes, I’d almost venture to affirm that this is our greatest gain from this auction.”

The Vampire Duke’s cookbook surpassed all previous items.

It was extremely significant to the survivors!

Especially since the dragonman youngster purchased it at a low price for such time saving thing.

It had to be said that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strategy of dividing into two teams was both proper and sagacious, and this result verified that it lived up to expectations.

The entire auction had been meticulously planned by Hun Tong, and the antiques section’s sales prices was inferior to the other sections. However, the various strange and bewildering goods was a feast for the eyes that increased everyone’s interest, opened their horizons, and left them wanting more.

When the first bulk sale appeared, signifying that the antique segment had finished, the people couldn’t help but sigh in relief.

The bulk sales was the auction’s second to last section; it was nearing its end.

Similarly, loners like Hong Zhu and Shi Fa lost interest.

But the Clam Cloth and Flint Merchant Alliance representatives and those like them were very spirited. They were the main supplier of this segment.

If the auction price was high, these branch managers would always benefit and allow them to improve their respective accomplishments and evaluations with their respective merchant alliance.

The first auction was food.

A large number of various types of magic beast meat appeared before everyone’s eyes.

This auction’s clientele was cultivators, and 80% of cultivators were fighters. Magicians and clergy made up the remaining 20%.

Fighters had a high demand for magic beast meat; it was their daily food.

Magic beast meat could help a fighter’s cultivation.

This was one of the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s main operations.

Because of the rigid demand of the six great human merchant alliances, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance was the strongest.

Upon seeing all kinds of magic beast meat on stage, everything from the bronze level to the silver level, the dragonman youngster couldn’t help but recall the mutton they had eaten on Mystifying Monster Island.

For this reason, they were poisoned.

After ascertaining the truth afterwards, they found out the true culprit was the Frost Poison Grass.

The grass’ life aura was very ordinary, however it actually contained abundant ice path magic power. After the artificial goats ate this grass, the magic power within the goats caused those who ate them to be poisoned.

“If we had a fixed base, we could farm this grass at scale.”

“Have some magic beasts eat them, then sell their magic beast meat.”

“If it worked, it would, it would be a big business.”

Despite the dragonman youngster not understanding business that well, he understood that this industry had maximum profits.

But all of this needed stability.

Currently, although the survivors had established the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, their external environment was not good, and from beginning to end, immense pressure was on them. They were seeking a noble contact that could help them towards imperial redemption, which would transform their circumstances and win them an opportunity to grow. After all, the empire was too powerful.

The auction began.

Pirates, mercenary corps, and even One Eye representing the patrol fleet along with great merchant alliances participated.

Essentially, every power required magic beast meat.

This kind of bulk sale was not commonly seen, and if one finely calculated the price, they would find that the cost per kilogram of meat was actually lower than market price, which immediately enticed many powers.

Of the 10 batches of magic beast meat, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps got one and Zi Di got another.

That was enough.

Magic beast meat had a shelf life and preserving it was quite troublesome.

The other 8 was partitioned up by the other powers.

This hearty feast was aptly named a bulk sale, nothing stood out, and nothing was scarce.

Many people were satisfied.

The auction hall grew more harmonious, with everyone enjoying themselves.

Of course, this ambience would not lead to high prices, therefore Hun Tong arranged to have this happen at the tail end of the auction.

Even if there was some bidding, all parties only fought a little, with one party conceding willingly and not deliberately hindering the other.

There were many things, and fighting was not necessary.

You have, I have, everyone has.

It was only when Zi Di bid differently. When room 21 entered, they would immediately suffer blockades and all parties would maliciously raise the price, for the unity of will was an impregnable stronghold. They wouldn’t give up even when the auction price rose above market price.

Zi Di tried several times before simply laying down the flag.

In any case, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had repeated success, and she didn’t need to waste money on this.

Seeing room 21 shrink back, many buyers were in a cheerful mood.

“We finally defeated them.”

“Everyone’s financial resources are limited, and at the end of the auction, they no longer have as much power.”

“This is the consequence of pushing into the limelight too much.”

The loners for the most part watched with folded arms, watching passively the mood of the auction’s participants.

They didn’t need such bulk sales.

After the foodstuff bulk sale was the alchemy material auctions.

Two fighters carried a large box onto the stage.

A single light beam shined on the wooden box, while the other lights went dark.

The wooden box slowly opened, exposing a small pile of metal ingots inside. Under the stage light, the metal ingots dazzled with a silver-white radiance.

Everyone’s eyes condensed onto these metal ingots.

Many hearts warmed and eyes lit up.

“As expected, it is Secret Silver ingots.” In room 21, Zi Di exposed a determined look.

Because the Bloodline Purification formation needed a large quantity of Secret Silver ingots.

Secret Silver was a type of rare metal, far more precious than gold. It was very light, it had a ductility comparable to bronze, yet it was harder than steel. In poetry, this metal was acclaimed to be “light as goose feathers, strong as dragon scales.” Although that was exaggerated rhetoric, it still illustrated a portion of truth.

Most crucial was that the Secret Silver possessed a formidable magical nature. It was very conductive of magic power.

Therefore, Secret Silver was almost always used to build magic items or was the main drawing material for formations.

There were so many places to use it.

That being said, even if the Bloodline Purification formation didn’t need it, Zi Di would still snap it up, for it was certain it would be used in the future.

The auction officially began.

The first box of silver ingots drew in panicked buyers.

The bids quickly rose, leading to a fierce contest between all parties.

As the price increases gradually stagnated towards the latter stages, Zi Di suddenly interjected with a high price.

“Hmph, it’s room 21 again?” Someone followed up fearlessly.

But Zi Di’s repeatedly large price rises revealed a determination to have it.

Superior financial resources defeated every competitor, even Hun Tong shook his head and gave up.

He held Two Eyes Island, if he lost this opportunity, he still had other stable channels to obtain Secret Silver ingots, he didn’t need to scramble for them.

“3, 2 ,1, sold.” The auction master finished counting down, “Congratulations to room 21 for purchasing this box of Secret Silver ingots.”

Afterwards.

“Congratulations to room 21 for winning the Mountain Copper ingots.”

“Congratulations to room 21 for obtaining three tons of Ocean Blue ore.”

“Let’s congratulate room 21 once again for becoming the new owner of this pile of obsidian!”

Zi Di acted again and again, buying large quantities of alchemy supplies.

During the food auction, Zi Di was subdued. But this time, she displayed her grand and graceful bearing, and her wantonly purchasing loftily forced its way through others.

Besides the things she disliked, the vast majority of alchemy materials were claimed by her.

The other buyers who got nothing hatefully gnashed their teeth.

Many people began to establish ties and privately joined hands, yet even so, they were still no match for Zi Di’s resolve.

Another item was shown off in the great hall.

It was a small wooden box.

When the wooden box was opened, everyone saw that there was a pile of silver-blue metal ingots inside that had a particular luster.

“Essence Gold!” Zi Di’s eyes seemed to burn slightly.

Essence Gold was seldom produced and was rarer than Secret Silver. It possessed the same formidable magic affinity and was used more to forge weapons and armor.

Essence gold was heavier, and its greatest strength was its hardness!

Currently, it was popularly known as the hardest metal to exist. It was far harder than steel.

Chapter 360: Bulk Sales

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was short on Essence Gold.

They had gotten some from Mystifying Monster Island. But they had used it up already, and to link together the Bloodline Purification formation, they needed a lot of Essence Gold!

“One small box of Essence Gold starts at a price of 1,500 gold. Every price raise must be at least 100 gold, now let the auction begin.” The auction master yelled with a heart filled with expectations.

Everyone was ready, this was bound to be a lively auction.

But before anyone could respond, Zi Di immediately bid, “2,000 gold!”

The large hall was silent for a moment; everyone was awed by Zi Di’s bid.

A 500 gold increase in a split second.

But room 1 followed up, “2,100 gold.”

Zi Di sneered, “3,000 gold!”

Instantly raising the price by 900 gold was simply deranged!

Every interested buyer’s eye shrank.

“Who is in room 21? How are they so vicious?!”

“Are we going to stake it all? After all, it is room 21.”

“To speak plainly, 3000 gold to buy this box of Essence Gold is not a losing transaction. When the market price is bad, it will be profitable.”

A few buyers weren’t intimidated and still wanted to compete.

In the prior section, Zi Di had snapped up many of their targets, thus they still had a lot of money on hand.

However, just as they wanted to bid, they heard Zi Di’s voice again, “3,500 gold!!”

They actually raised the price on themselves?

The buyers went rigid, and those with ample sense realized room 21 was determined to have this box, as well as their frightening strength.

Under such a high price, they were forced to give up.

The Essence Gold was efficiently won by Zi Di.

“Who can suppress room 21?” The buyers whispered to each other.

Zi Di’s ferocious performance shattered many hopes.

The hall was filled with pessimism.

Many people began to genuinely think no one could withstand room 21.

Soon after, the Essence Gold was delivered to room 21.

After paying the gold coins, Zi Di stuffed the small wooden box into the Balloon Knapsack.

The latter was already bouncing and floating in the air, and thanks to this treasure, the things they purchased could be conveniently transported.

The bulk sales’ climax didn’t stop at this.

Not long after, a small sensation once again broke out in the auction hall.

“Profound Gold!”

“Are my eyes deceiving me?”

“I didn’t expect to see Profound Gold in this auction!”

“Why wasn’t it on the item list?”

“It’s obvious that this was a special item inserted during the auction!”

Everyone conversed as their fervent eyes sized up the small pile of Profound Gold on stage.

Profound Gold was a type of metal alloy, while Essence Gold came from a natural vein of ore. This was the biggest difference between them.

In terms of price, Profound Gold was more expensive than Essence Gold, it was classified as a high level alchemy material.

This was mainly because the main way to refine Profound Gold was to combine Essence Gold, profound spell crystals, and other materials together instead of just smelting it.

Zi Di’s eyes were instantly attracted to it.

“I want that.” She vocalized.

Upon hearing her voice, every buyer grew discolored.

Many people were indignant, but even more were ashen with defeat.

“What terrible luck to come across such an opponent.” They sighed to themselves.

“Room 21 has their eyes on that Profound Gold, it’s theirs.” Many people chose to immediately give up.

Finally, the auction outcome came to pass.

Zi Di once again used her strong wealth to seize the box of Profound Gold.

Hun Tong looked a bit off.

He projected that this Profound Gold would have sold at a higher price. But in reality, since no one was confident they could contest room 21, many people opted out of the rat race to instead watch passively from the sidelines.

Hun Tong was somewhat remorseful that he didn’t suppress room 21 and allowed them to dictate the climate. The other’s existence had influenced the auction’s income.

However, he was also somewhat glad.

This was the last part of the auction, and the other party’s influence did not affect the big picture.

In addition, if the two people inside room 21 were room 2’s prey, their conclusion would be ruinous.

Hun Tong didn’t need to take action himself.

“Although this somewhat effects profits, they have bid many times, wantonly spilled coin, and gave me more revenue than losses.”

When thinking of that, Hun Tong’s heart felt relieved.

He could scarcely realize that the money Zi Di and Cang Xu were spending at this auction had been stolen from Two Eyes Island.

Zi Di continued to act, purchasing large amounts of alchemy materials. Such as Granite Extract, Obsidian Extract, and all kinds of coral.

Two Eyes Island was a Coral Ball Cage Tree, and its coral was also considered to be a unique local product.

The fly in the ointment was that this auction was mainly of metal and ore, while flesh type alchemy materials were sparse, and wood type alchemy materials were just as rare.

This shortfall needed to be patched up in the future.

Zi Di didn’t have extravagant demands.

In fact, she was very grateful that Hun Tong had held this auction. Although she lost some gold coins, the survivors had saved incalculable time and energy.

If they missed this opportunity, they might spend even longer and even more money searching for these materials in the vast ocean, and likely not even collect enough.

The ocean had abundant resources in fact, but humans couldn’t extract them easily.

After all, humans could not breathe underwater.

On top of that, the Sheng Ming Empire was invading the Beast Continent, and the main part of the empire’s forces was not on the ocean, but rather in the Wilderness Continent.

Therefore, something like Two Eyes Island’s auction was hard to come by.

The flaunting auction master took in a breath, “Honorable friends, time passed at lightning speed, and in but a flash, this auction has reached its final segment.”

“I believe that this segment is also one of the most awaited, it is the slave auction!”

“Please meticulously prepare for our first item.”

After saying this, a fighter pulled a rope tied to a female elf.

She was an adult woman and had the delicate and beautiful complexion distinctive to elves. She had few clothes on, it was obvious she had been intentionally dressed to expose all of the right parts of her that exhibited and protruded her graceful beauty as much as possible.

Despite being tied up and forced to stagger forwards, the elf held her head high and looked down at the audience, her eyes transmitting her intense hatred.

The buyers were all looking at her and whispering to each other.

“It really is an elf, and looking at those features, she’s truly beautiful.”

“It is said that when she was captured, many experts were lost.”

“Although she is iron level, her archery is very good. If she hadn’t been stupid enough to come back to rescue her clanmates, she wouldn’t have fallen into a trap.”

Hearsay on this elf was already known by everyone.

Hun Tong had deliberately disseminated it, another way he was preparing for the auction.

When some buyers saw the elf proudly looking hateful, they frowned and muttered, “It seems she hasn’t been trained well.”

“But if that is so, won’t that bring a greater sense of achievement when I train and conquer her with my own hands?” Some buyers grew more interested.

The auction master continued to present her.

The buyers weren’t worried the elf couldn’t be controlled, she had been bound with a slave contract and any buyer could control her life or death.

But if one wanted her to be obedient, that would depend on how a buyer trained her.

“I have to say, this training will be a pleasure, am I right?” The auction master aroused the cravings of many buyers.

Some people couldn’t help but giggle with dark intentions.

When the elf heard this laughter and felt innumerable hungry eyes looking at her, she panicked a little, however she covered it up with more hatred.

The auction began.

The bids rose and fell ceaselessly in the ears.

Hun Tong watched this with satisfaction.

This elf had been specially chosen by him and was the most enticing thing in this final part of the auction.

His diligent arrangements had received a fine result; the auction price quickly exceeded 1,000 gold and was still trending upwards.

The only thing that worried Hun Tong was that room 21 would intervene and suppress this wave of enthusiastic bids.

But all the way until the winner was decided, room 21 never acted.

The elf was only iron level, and to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they did not lack this kind of military strength.

She wasn’t easy to train, and elven arrogance caused her to look down on the dragonman youngster’s race, and becoming a captive caused her to be even more resentful to humans.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps did not need this kind of internal instability, while simultaneously not having the energy to harmonize the hatred and vengeful feelings in the elf’s heart.

As for Cang Xu and Zi Di, as believers in life and advocates of nature, elves were more so the mortal enemies of necromancers. It was utterly unnecessary to spend lots of money to recruit an enemy into their ranks.

In the end, the elf was sold for 1,450 gold.

This price was above the market price for iron level slaves, but the victor felt high and mighty, they felt wonderful and devoid of worry.

The losers sighed regretfully.

But soon after, their regret once again turned into hope.

Because the second item was an equal match to that elf.

She was a female beastman that had a petite stature, an adorable appearance, long cat ears, and a cat tail.

The auction hall thoughtfully dressed her with a customized maid outfit of coordinated monochrome, while also fastening a small copper bell to her nape.

The female beastman wasn’t shackled, and she was cutely standing motionlessly, it was obvious she had been trained well.

The cat girl maid led to a surge of panicked bids.

Once again, it triggered an upsurge of competition.

Zi Di still hadn’t acted.

Although this maid was iron level, she had no battle skill. She was a merchandise specially trained by some big slave merchant for great lords and imperial higherups.

As for why she was reduced to being here?

It was very simple.

An imperial higherup had the misfortune of being robbed, and this maid had become a pirate’s spoil of war.

The plunderers were none other than the Ten Touch Pirates.

The maid was finally sold for 1,300 gold, a bit lower than the elf, but its still surpassed the market price for iron level slaves.

It had to be said that there were a lot of rich people.

Sexual interest was one of the driving forces of the market.

Almost everyone on Two Eyes Island were pragmatic people, and regardless of whether it was a female elf or a cat girl maid, both were “practical”.

“The third slave is the only silver level at this auction!” The auction master introduced them, “He is a steady man, a heavy gunner, Chi Lai!”

Chapter 361: Heavy Gunner Chi Lai

The magic light beams swept in a circle until finally concentrating on Chi Lai.

He was a middle aged man with black hair and a square face. His face was expressionless, but his ramrod straight body and unswerving eyes of determination divulged many traits of a soldier.

“Finally, he’s here.” The dragonman youngster’s mind perked up.

He had been interested in Chi Lai ever since he saw him on the item list.

Zong Ge was more intrigued, and immediately stated his opinion, “We should recruit him, our mercenary corps lacks a true gunner.”

“He is from the imperial navy; we don’t need to train him, and his military strength is worth the money.”

“As for why he was expelled from the navy and reduced to a slave, I understand that this was due to a miscarriage of justice. This imperial soldier from a poor background was harmed by a higher-up framing him.”

Zong Ge had a good opinion of Chi Lai’s soldier background.

Zong Ge’s analysis made sense, and the dragonman youngster agreed, “He and I are the same, believers in the Justice God. Based on this, we can trust him even more.”

On the stage, the auction master continued the introduction, “Many people have heard his tale.”

“A young person from the slums was very gifted. His gunnery skills were noticed by the navy, and he was recruited by them.”

“He believes in the Justice God and held fast to the light in his heart, as a result, he violated navy disciple and offended an imperial bigwig.”

“Now he has become a slave and is under a slave contract.”

“However, it will only cause him pain, the contract won’t impair his cognition at all. Therefore, I ask all respected friends to take note of this bit.”

The buyers discussed this in whispers.

As the only silver slave, he had already been discussed a lot already.

“Chi Lai was once a navy elite, but even more so, he is a Justice God believer. Although he is a slave, the contract is quite relaxed, and he can disobey orders he thinks are unreasonable.”

“Therefore, he’ll be hard for pirates to make use of. Besides mercenary corps, the ones truly able to recruit him are the great merchant alliance fleets, oh I almost forgot, Hun Tong too.”

“Hehe, I think that it’s unlikely Hun Tong will recruit him. Two Eyes Island is the holy land for pirates disposing of their booty, and when Chi Lai finds out, he won’t comply with Hun Tong’s orders. If Hun Tong could recruit him, why would he have put Chi Lai up for auction?”

“Hm, that’s reasonable! In fact, Chi Lai has been resold many times, many slave owners that bought him couldn’t stand his disposition. Some owners were so distraught by his good deeds that they even wanted to kill him, but in the end, they decided against doing that.”

Chi Lai had offended an imperial bigwig with a powerful backer.

The reason why he had been placed under such a spacious slave contract was that he wanted to torment Chi Lai and humiliate him as much as he liked!

Therefore, Chi Lai was comparatively special.

If his master wanted to kill him, they had to consider the opinions of that imperial bigwig. If he was killed, that would violate the original intention that that imperial bigwig wanted him tormented. Although that lord perhaps already tossed Chi Lai to the back of his mind, what reason was there to risk their wrath? It wasn’t worth it.

Therefore, Chi Lai had been resold many times, and now, he was stranded at Two Eyes Island’s auction.

The magic images were constantly broadcasting many scenes of Chi Lai fighting.

Especially how svelte he controlled cannons, that left a deep impression on people.

He really was an outstanding gunner.

Only a small section of the presentation at the beginning showed him serving and training in the navy, the rest was when he had been sold as a slave where he had followed his owner’s commands and participated in battles big and small.

There were plenty of scenes of single combat, a few team battles, and many naval battles.

Upon seeing these images, everyone’s heartrate, including pirate bosses, increased.

Chi Lai’s battle strength was outstanding; he was neither weak at close range nor at long range. However, if he was allowed to operate cannons, his threat level would sharply rise!

“A true regret, why is he a Justice God believer?”

“If he could join us, I could make him my number two! What a pity that he will never become a pirate.”

Chi Lai had already been a slave for many years, and during those years, his belief in the Justice God never wavered, in fact it grew even deeper.

These magic images also illustrated a problem.

Some astute merchants made out that many images were purposely staged.

They easily could think why: they were obviously crafted by Chi Lai’s masters. Their purpose was to magnify Chi Lai’s strength so he could be sold at a good price and mend the blood they spent on him.

“Chi Lai, the earnest, man, the outstanding heavy gunner absolutely deserves more trust and expectations! The starting price is 5,000 gold, and every price raise must be no less than 200 gold.” The auction master declared the start of the auction.

But awkwardly, both the audience and the second floor were silent.

Silver level slaves were rarely seen on the market.

5,000 gold was not a high price.

Chi Lai was very strong, and should have been a widely regarded product, but information and stories on him had been widely circulated for many years, causing few people to be interested in purchasing him.

“Don’t tell me this will fail to sell?” The auction master’s heart sank, feeling things weren’t looking good.

Chi Lai had failed to be sold at many auctions.

Therefore, Hun Tong had arranged for him to be put in third. This sort of arrangement meant he didn’t have good prospects for his market price, but since the latter was still a silver level, he was placed in this position.

After a small stillness, Commander Beng Dai bid, “5,200 gold.”

It was the lowest price increase, but that alone allowed the auction master to let out a breath, “At least he won’t fail to sell.”

Failing to sell things at auctions would influence his performance.

Furthermore, this auction was estimated to be the pinnacle of his life, and if a failed auction blemished it, it would cause unavoidable regret.

5,200 gold to the Bandage Mercenary Corps was a huge sum, and it would be a huge loss for them.

Commander Beng Dai did not fight by himself.

Before Chi Lai ascended the auction stage, he had contacted a few merchant alliance branch managers and agreed to bid for Chi Lai together.

Each would share a part of the price and observe Chi Lai on his duties to watch how he really was.

If he was suitable, each of them would bid for him. The final winner would pay the missing gold coins to compensate the others and be the ultimate owner of Chi Lai.

This was inspired by when they had joined hands with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to face off against room 21.

Commander Beng Dai was merely the public face of it, that’s all.

In fact, the Iron Club Mercenary Corps was among them too.

The two mercenary corps were according to standard, and based on common sense, they could have Chi Lai serve them.

After the bid of 5,200 gold, there was no response from any other party.

This was estimated to be the most desolate auction ever here.

Then as the auction master began to count down, the dragonman youngster in room 14 transmitted, “5,400 gold.”

Commander Beng Dai stared slightly shocked, he didn’t expect competition.

But when he thought of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, Commander Beng Dai didn’t feel surprised, in fact it was quite reasonable.

Commander Beng Dai continued to raise the price, “5,600 gold.”

If he was competing with his mercenary corps alone, he would be greatly pressured. But now that he had other merchant alliances working with him, he had ample confidence.

In comparison, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps couldn’t collaborate with other merchant alliances.

Because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was going to leave Two Eyes Island, unlike the Bandage Mercenary Corps who was a permanent resident of Two Eyes Island and had become one of the native powers here.

It was because of this that the Bandage Mercenary Corps and other merchant alliances had a foundation for collaboration.

“5,800 gold.” Said the dragonman youngster.

“6,000 gold.” Commander Beng Dai continued to follow up.

There were only two sides to this content.

When the price rose to 6,600 gold, the dragonman youngster sank into silence.

After a moment, he followed Fei She’s suggestion and suddenly raised the price increase, “7,800 gold!”

In addition, the dragonman youngster specially added, “This is my final offer.”

Commander Beng Dai suddenly stared blankly, how could he say that in an auction?

But he quickly understood that this was the dragonman youngster’s auction stratagem.

“I didn’t expect you, an honest looking person, to play this trick?” When this thought appeared in Commander Beng Dai’s mind, he felt somewhat embarrassed.

If he wanted to press down on the dragonman youngster, he’d need to bid at least 8,000 gold. The allocated expenses of thousands of gold coins across his Bandage Mercenary Corps was already something he couldn’t bear.

But that’s not how the merchant alliance representatives felt.

While Commander Beng Dai was hesitating, the merchant alliance branch managers dispatched subordinates to urgently contact him and request that he continue to increase the price.

Commander Beng Dai sighed; he sensed the systemic problem with collaborative auctions. But it was too late to withdraw, and he was also worried about damaging his relationship with the other merchant alliances, thus he had no choice but to bid, “8,000 gold!”

As expected, the dragonman youngster didn’t bid again.

Commander Beig Dai suddenly felt somewhat remorseful, “It seems Commander Long Fu spoke sincerely, I misunderstood him. That really was his bottom line. Damn it, I’m actually spending so much money to purchase Chi Lai. Even if Chi Lai serves me in the future, will I have enough money to pay the other merchant alliances? Can he stay in a mercenary corps?”

The auction master began to count down, and as everyone thought that the Bandage Mercenary Corps was to be the final victor, Zi Di’s voice transmitted from room 21 once again, “8,200 gold.”

Everyone looked at each other in dismay.

They didn’t expect that a ferocious beast would be provoked at the end of the auction.

It was unquestionable that room 21 was the richest buyer at this auction!

Compete against them?

Commander Beng Dai’s fighting spirit suddenly slumped, and the other merchant alliance representatives were also clearly affected. Some expressed that they wanted to follow up, there was still a small bit before they hit their bottom line. Some stated that they didn’t need to waste energy, others still stated that this could deplete the other party’s money, but there were more that were worried about what they would do if they bought Chi Lai at this high price and fell into room 21’s stratagem?

After all, Zi Di only raised the price by 200 gold.

Her fuzzy attitude made these people hesitate.

Another systemic problem of collaborative auctions appeared, when ideas varied, it was difficult to adapt to the rapid tempo of an auction.

Just as the auction master’s countdown reached its end, Commander Beng Dai and the others were still arguing and weren’t able to agree on anything.

“8,400 gold!” Just as everyone thought the winner was decided, the dragonman youngster spoke again.

“7,800 gold really was my bottom line, but against room 21, this bottom line has no weight!” The dragonman youngster added on.

In a split second, everyone’s goodwill of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps shot up!

Chapter 362: Chi Lai Joins

In this auction, Zi Di and Cang Xu in room 21 had indeed been the center of attention.

Many buyers wanted them beaten.

They had helplessly watched the treasures they admired fall into their hands; this sensation was very unpleasant.

Take Yue Ban for example, she hoped she could buy the Mushroom Dancing Shoes. Those dancing shoes were very important to her life, but in the end, Zi Di walked away with them.

The Stench Pirates’ commander Yang Cong Tou had the Sun Halo on his mind constantly. To him, it was a great thing that could increase his meditation efficiency, yet Zi Di snatched that away too.

The Fertile Land priest had joined this auction to purchase the Miracle Flower Pot. After fixing the flower pot’s decomposed Abundant Earth, he could improve his position in the Fertile Land Church, which brought considerable benefits.

Apart from this, there was also the Star Iron Axe, the Shadow Walker Outfit, and the Grand Arena Medal.

With regards to the gold level healing potion, something Song Shou and Qi You were very interested in, Zi Di had used gold coins to defeat them.

Defensive tools like the Glass External Armor, the Sacred Plate Armor, the mushroom tights had greater attractiveness than weapons of the same rank, yet in the end, Zi Di had taken them all away!

Especially the Golden Silk Chainmail, that was the auction’s sole gold level armor, and Zi Di had won that too.

The hatred these buyers had for room 21 itched their teeth.

Zi Di’s existence caused the gains of those who joined this auction to sharply decline.

Pirates had tried every possible means to scout out who Zi Di and Cang Xu were; room 21 had been engraved deeply in their hearts. If Hun Tong and Song Shou weren’t suppressing the situation, perhaps they’d have robbed them on the spot.

Zi Di had snapped up too many treasures, causing the vast majority of buyers to resent her.

But there was no avoiding that.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ expansion strategy required them to increase their strength in this short window of time to prevent all possible mishaps.

Oneself was the most reliable.

They could not abandon a golden opportunity to grow themselves because they were afraid of offending others.

As for competing with room 21, the other buyers had struck back at room 21 many times. Such as when they joined hands for an auction and reaching a tacit understanding that everyone would maliciously raise prices on Zi Di.

But Zi Di had too much money, and even after purchasing so many things, she still had battle strength at the end of the auction!

It seems that Chi Lai’s auction had caused room 21’s might to reappear once again.

This time, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps stood up to them.

Even though many were uninterested in buying Chi lai, they grew to have a better impression of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Room 21 had unwittingly become the target of anger against a common enemy.

The dragonman youngster’s bid suppressed Zi Di’s.

Zi Di was silent.

The auction master yelled, “Room 14, going once.”

Commander Beng Dai was still urgently communicating over yonder.

They had joined hands for this auction.

Some people wanted to continue bidding, but some were like Commander Beng Dai, they didn’t want to.

“The price is already very high, why don’t we let the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and room 21 fight over it?”

The auction master cried out, “Room 14, going twice.”

Still, no one replied.

The dragonman youngster looked slightly nervous.

Fei She suddenly said, “Al, almost there.”

Sure enough, even as the auction master purposely slowed his voice when he reached the third count, still no one bid.

When the small hammer smacked down, the dragonman youngster unconsciously coughed. This victory had been unexpectedly uncomplicated.

The outcome had also corroborated Fei She’s words.

The young man glanced at Fei She and couldn’t help but admire his competence. Although he was an ordinary person, his auction skill was far greater than the young man and the half beastman.

That bid had been at Fei She’ suggestion.

He was worthy of being an elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance!

Zong Ge then reviewed this auction.

He had participated in only a few auctions, and this experience had given him a whole new understanding of the art of war.

“Dividing our forces at an auction has been exceedingly useful!”

“We need Chi Lai, but if we were bidding as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps against the Bandage Mercenary Corps, the price wouldn’t have ended here.”

“But Zi Di’s screen attracted all of their attention. Zi Di’s constant pressure bore an extraordinarily good result.”

After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps bid, Zi Di of course kept silent.

Beng Dai sighed, he was both surprised and not at this outcome.

“In the end, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps succeeded. The price isn’t low, it seems they are willing to take this risk.”

Thinking about their loss, Commander Beng Dai grew happier.

Some merchant alliance representatives expressed their remorse; saying they could have increased the price further.

Some merchant alliance representatives began to analyze, “Look, room 21 is very treacherous, they maliciously raised the price.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might have been the last to bid, therefore room 21 didn’t act again. If we acted, perhaps the other side would have maliciously raised the price again, putting Chi Lai in our hands was undoubtably room 21’s scheme.”

“Commander Long Fu is pretty good! Over and over again, he withstood room 21 with heroism. Chi Lai being in his care is a far better result than him ending up in room 21’s.”

“Actually, we should find the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to continue our discussion.”

Some merchant alliance representatives didn’t give up, they thought about continuing their cooperation.

The auction hall was constantly abuzz with the result of this auction.

“Finally, room 21 has been defeated!”

“8,400 gold… the price of this victory was rather high.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has money! Besides, a silver level slave is worth that price.”

“I hope that Chi Lai serves the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps well, otherwise they will suffer a great loss.”

“It isn’t easy to sell a silver level slave like Chi Lai; one can only do so at auctions of this scale.”

“It’s difficult, I heard that they are a group of arms smugglers. Will Chi Lai serve them? He is a Justice God believer. I don’t know what Long Fu was thinking, I feel that he was impulsive in doing this.”

“After all, he is a dragonman.”

Everyone exchanged their opinions.

Chi Lai could not be purchased by Cang Xu and Zi Di, because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed him out in the open exerting himself, especially during naval battles.

Although the Deceptive Disguise spell was a formidable trump card, it couldn’t be used on Chi Lai.

Chi Lai was not a survivor, not one of them, and it was inconvenient to let him learn the survivors’ core secrets.

8,400 gold for silver level strength like Chi Lai wasn’t that high actually.

But the prerequisite was his true effectiveness.

Pertaining to this, Zong Ge and the dragonman youngster were confident in that.

After all, arms smugglers were merely a fake identity of theirs.

Zi Di’s opinion was even simpler: even if he wouldn’t cooperate, at worst, they could resell Chi Lai in the future.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had overflowing coffers, they weren’t worried about their financial situation and could accept this risk.

Chi Lai was taken to the second floor and entered room 14.

“This is your new master.” The escorting fighter saluted the dragonman youngster then offered the contract scroll to him with both hands.

Accompanying them was also a bronze level magician, they were here to amend the contract scroll.

The dragonman youngster carefully read the scroll.

This was a blood contract.

It had Chi Lai’s name written in blood.

The dragonman youngster took note that the specific enslavement rules were comparatively roomy, allowing Chi Lai to retain some freedom. He couldn’t be forced to comply, and he couldn’t be killed.

Many slave contracts were not like this.

Chi Lai’s agreement was very spacious, and much of it was bound to the master’s life. If the master died, the slave would too.

There were also many preinstalled punishment measures.

With the power of a contract, a master could easily torment a slave so painfully that it’d be an existence worse than death.

There were also incantations that could kill the slave with the contract.

With the bronze magician’s aid, the dragonman youngster wrote his name in blood.

The bronze magician erased their own name.

Chi Lai was a silver level fighter, and only by doing this could his slavery to a master be maintained without possible slip ups.

The slave contract glinted with a light, and at the same time, the dragonman youngster’s and Chi Lai’s bodies glistened too.

The dragonman youngster sensed a spiritual connection.

This connection had been established between him and Chi Lai; it was very obvious he was in the dominant position.

The bronze magician inspected the contract again, verifying it had been reestablished, then withdrew and closed the door.

The dragonman youngster said to Chi Lai, “Pleased that you have joined us, we are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. I am Commander Long Fu; this is Auxiliary Commander Shi Qi.”

Chi Lai nodded to the dragonman youngster and didn’t reply.

He had been resold many times, and this scene was customary to him.

Chi Lai sized up the dragonman before him.

“Red Dragon bloodline?” Chi Lai guessed, based on the dragonman youngster’s red scales.

Chi Lai could also faintly feel the vigorous and abundant power inside the young man’s body.

Chi Lai then looked at Shi Qi.

Zong Ge’s strong and large physique was something he paid attention to.

“These physiques are not commonly seen in humanity. What bloodlines do they have?”

Both the commander and auxiliary commander were burly chaps, this was the first impression Chi Lai had gotten of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ style.

Chi Lai slowly spoke, “Commanders, you should understand my situation. I am Chi Lai, a believer in the Justice God. I will comply with rational orders, but so long as you violate righteousness, I won’t bow to your will.”

His tone was quite tranquil, but others could make out his determination and willpower from his voice.

“Understood.” The dragonman youngster smiled, “Luckily, I am just like you. I also believe in my God and keep to the path of righteousness.”

“?” Chi Lai stared slightly; his apathetic expression experienced a rare change.

The Justice God’s strength was not outstanding amongst the empire’s gods, consequently, the scope of their churches was very small and couldn’t compare to the Sheng Ming Church or the Wealth Church.

He wouldn’t have thought that in the boundless ocean, he would have met another Justice believer.

In a split second, Chi Lai’s opinion of the dragonman youngster shot up rapidly and his eyes grew softer.

Chapter 363: Magic Rune Swordsman Ye Zhou

Chi Lai didn’t doubt the dragonman youngster’s words, because it wasn’t worthwhile to deceive him. Beliefs were very difficult to disguise long term, and genuine believers wouldn’t lie about them, such conduct disrespected their own beliefs.

Furthermore, both sides have already signed the contract. The dragonman youngster was the master, deceit was unnecessary now.

“Please take a seat here.” The dragonman youngster pointed at the seat he had prepared.

Chi Lai was a silver level after all.

Chi La nodded and took his seat.

He had encountered many masters, some were courteous to him, some were not. After experiencing so many people, he didn’t value these surface level things.

Zong Ge observed Chi Lai for a moment, then shifted his gaze back to the auction stage.

The half beastman had a good opinion of Chi Lai, but when they met each other for the first time, he said nothing.

The dragonman youngster was very delighted.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only had two silver levels, and if Chi Lai could serve, he would be their third.

This was an immense addition in terms of high level military strength.

This was their first time meeting. If they wanted to gain each other’s trust, they needed experience and time with each other.

After Chi Lai left the auction stage, the auctions continued.

“Once again, congratulations to room 14, with Chi Lai joining them, their strength is bound to skyrocket. Chi Lai is indeed this auction’s sole silver level.”

The auction master changed the tone of the conversation, “Anyways, although the next slave is only iron level, they were once a silver level.”

“His life experience is very abundant, and from a certain angle, he isn’t outdone by Chi Lai.”

Soon after, a person was taken onto the stage.

It was an elf with a tall stature, two pointed ears, and nearly black skin.

Through careful observation, one could see that it was not his skin that was black, but rather the many magic runes engraved into his body.

These magic runes were brimming with dark energy.

His skin consequently was influenced by it and was nearly black.

“A magic rune swordsman?” Zong Ge raised his eyebrow.

Magic rune swordsmen possessed both battle qi and magic, however their magic power was external and not from their own cultivation.

Magic rune swordsmen have been around for a long time.

Magic rune swordsmen would carve magic runes and even magic formations into their bodies.

Magic rune swordsmen did not have or had poor aptitude for magic cultivation, but they had comparably strong spirits.

They used their spirits to connect to their body’s runes to maneuver the runes’ power, increasing their battle strength.

Magic rune swordsmen exist everywhere in every race.

Their advantage was obvious – cost effectiveness.

Magicians would engrave magic runes into them at a high success rate, and with a little training, magic rune soldiers could master these magic runes and utilize their magical power to multiply their battle strength.

The cultivation cost for this was far less than what it took to cultivate a magician.

Crucially, magic bloodlines were rarer than fighter bloodlines.

Very few people had the cultivation gift to become a magician, while there were many people that could cultivate battle qi.

Thus, magic rune soldiers were easier to select, cultivate, and replenish than magicians.

Of course, they also had shortcomings.

The runes engraved on their bodies were stuck there permanently and were very hard to modify.

Not only was the modification hard, but after the modification, the soldier’s body would be harmed and cause every aspect of themselves to decline.

The slave after Chi Lai was an elven magic rune swordsman.

Before the auction master could introduce him, he decided to open his mouth first.

“My name is Ye Zhuo.”

“I was once a Royal Court swordsman.”

“In search of more strength, I defected.”

“The Royal Court dispatched people to capture me, and although I am still alive, I paid that price with my left arm and silver level cultivation.”

“I can help you train soldiers; I was an assistant military instructor of the Royal Court’s swordsmanship!”

“As long as you give me a sword, I can still fight too.”

“I also know many things. This information cannot be gained through normal means, and much of it concerns the shipping routes of elven merchant ships.”

Ye Zhuo did his best to market himself.

The dragonman youngster looked at the item list.

Ye Zhuo’s name had been on the list.

Some information on him was also presented there too.

He was a traitor of some elven royal court, and once one of their royal swordsmen.

He had dark magic runes, the outcome of his pursuit for power.

He had absconded to the ocean but was still apprehended by elven troops.

When the elf royal court squad captured him, they didn’t execute him on the spot, instead they wanted to escort him back to the Elf Kingdom for a public trial.

But en route, their ship was attacked by pirates.

It was the Three Ring Pirates.

This powerful group of robbers had a gold level magician as their commander!

The elven royal court’s ship was attacked and sunk, many people died, and those left became the pirates’ captives.

Ye Zhuo was among them.

The Three Ring Pirates dealt with these captives in turn, some were killed, some were experimented on, and some were sold off for money.

Ye Zhuo was relatively lucky, he was dropped into the slave market and was being auctioned off at Two Eyes Island.

The auction began.

The first to bid was Dian Wan.

He had an irascible temperament.

Since he wasn’t able to buy the gold level healing potion, he still had money on him.

The first mate didn’t stop him.

If Ye Zhuo joined the Lightning Pirates, their entire group would be strengthened.

Although Ye Zhuo was just one person, he didn’t just bring his individual military force, he could also train soldiers and had precious intel.

After Dian Wan bid, other competitors immediately leaped in in quick succession.

The price quickly rose beyond the amount of money Dian Wan had.

“These wretched people…” Dian Wan grinded his teeth, unwilling but powerless to do anything about it.

Almost all of the bidders were pirates.

Apart from the Lighting Pirates, there was also the Stench and Applause Pirates, among others.

The Ten Touch Pirates also joined in during the later stages and was the most powerful competitor.

The mercenary corps didn’t join the bidding, and the merchant alliance representatives were silent.

The dragonman youngster was a cross-armed spectator.

They weren’t joining the auction for a simple reason, purchasing Ye Zhuo would offend the Elven Royal Court.

The Royal Court had dispatched special troops to arrest Ye Zhuo, and although they failed this time, it was clear the Royal Court wouldn’t take this lying down.

The survivors’ main goal was redemption, and new problems arising unexpectedly was not needed.

Of course, they could buy him and gift him to the Elven Royal Court.

But this was very risky.

Escorting Ye Zhuo back would use up a large amount of time, energy, and manpower.

What would be the Elf Kingdom reward?

It’s hard to say.

Because Ye Zhuo was just a minor iron level.

Furthermore, Ye Zhuo had already been exposed.

If they bought him, it would likely attract the attacks of every pirate on the seas.

Another key reason is Ye Zhuo himself.

Ye Zhuo was also very worried that his slave owner would treat him as a gift and turn him over to the Elf Kingdom.

Therefore, when he introduced himself, he displayed he was partial towards joining a pirate group.

What Ye Zhuo said was very appealing to pirates.

Pirates wouldn’t be worried about making trouble with the Royal Court and were looking for elf merchant ships to inconvenience.

The pirates were especially interested in information on the elf merchant ship routes.

Finally, after a fierce bidding war, Qi You was the final winner.

He purchased Ye Zhuo at a high price.

Ye Zhuo had also adapted to circumstances well.

Upon coming to room 3 and amending the slave contract, he knelt down and vowed loyalty to Qi You.

Qi You was satisfied by his display.

After Ye Zhuo, a few fighters escorted a dozen people onto the auction stage.

These people were short and stout, had flesh as rugged as stone, and regardless of gender, all had a long and thick beard.

They were dwarfs.

The auction master said, “The next option is this group of dwarfs, 80 in total. Those on stage are only some of them.”

The magic image appeared, revealing the remaining dwarfs behind the scenes.

These dwarfs were locked up in a gigantic iron cage, all of them looked apathetic.

Regardless of whether it was the dwarfs in the iron cage or the ones on stage, they were all shackled and chained. They were forced to shuffle their feet to move.

When the dwarf Men Shi saw these dwarfs, his expression immediately stirred.

These dwarfs were his clansmen, his relatives.

He had come to this auction for the sole purpose of rescuing these dwarfs.

When Men Shi’s clan was migrating, they suffered the perils of the sea.

Their ship ran aground, and they were stranded on an uninhabited island.

After a few months of being stuck on that uninhabited island, their distress fire caused a passing human merchant ship to rescue them.

The dwarfs expressed their gratitude to the merchant ship’s captain; they would pay gold coins and serve as sailors to help the merchant ship fend off ocean beasts.

But in the end, when the merchant ship reached port, they suddenly had a falling out with the captain.

Men Shi’s people ate food tampered with by humans, and they all sank into a coma.

By the time they woke up, the humans had stolen their belongings and sold them into slavery.

“These are dwarfs, as everyone knows.” The auction master brightly said.

“Whether it is mining, metallurgy, or forging equipment, and even brewing beer, they are experts at all of those.”

“Their skin is coarse and their flesh thick, nature fighters.”

“If you purchase them, you’ll find a use for them, regardless of whether it is as a soldier or a miner.”

“As for their cultivation, the highest is iron level, of which there are 3, along with 12 bronze levels…”

When she heard this introduction, Zi Di grew excited, “In the future, if we build an alchemy factory, we can find a place for these dwarfs as workers there.”

In room 1, Hun Tong smiled at Song Shou and said, “These dwarfs, are you interested in them?”

“If the island you choose has mineral resources, these dwarfs will be a money making machine.”

“Especially during the beginning period of development, through selling the ore these dwarfs excavate, you can obtain profits that can bring you great progress.”

Hun Tong didn’t need these dwarfs.

The reason was obvious at a glance.

Two Eyes Island was a coral ball cage tree, it had no minerals at all.

As for buying these dwarfs for soldiers, Hun Tong didn’t need them in that capacity either.

He would rather build some light cannon turrets and didn’t want to train troops, because the latter had a higher military expenditure while the light cannon turrets had a lower maintenance cost.

Song Shou’s heart rate inevitably quickened.

He thought of the choices the empire had given him.

Among them were two islands where ore veins had been discovered.

These dwarfs really did suit him.

Chapter 364: Water Pigmen

The dwarf auction began.

Room 1 and room 21 bid one after the other.

Both were wake up calls.

“One is the gold level fighter Song Shou, and the other is the richest and most mysterious buyer.” Men Shi’s heart seemed to sink into the ocean; pressure was piling onto him.

He bit his jaw and was the third to bid, making a firm resolution to himself: Even if he has to sell himself off, he will redeem his people!

The price competition continued, with some new competitors entering the fray as the price rapidly soared.

When the dwarfs on stage saw Men Shi, they were emotionally moved. Some did their utmost to struggle until the watching fighters drew whips to get them to behave. Others wanted to shout, but they had been silenced with magic and looked like mimes. Many more dwarfs were weak and without strength, for before they went on stage, they had been drugged.

The pirates were uninterested in these dwarfs.

Because 80 dwarfs packaged in a single auction, thus the powers that could absorb this amount had to be quite large.

Of the pirate groups at the auction, Qi You’s Ten Touch Pirates was the biggest. Second to him was Dian Wan’s Lightning Pirates, Yang Cong Tou’s Stench Pirates, and Hu Li’s Applause Pirates.

These four pirate groups were mainly composed of humans, with the outlier, the Applause Pirates, being composed of mostly frogmen.

Regardless of which group it was, recruiting so many dwarfs at once would have a strong impact on the original members.

Because their races, habits, and properties were different.

Especially since there were many dwarfs, no matter which power they join, they will band together to keep themselves warm, a challenge to the leader and the organization overall.

In this respect, entities like the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was rare. Because this mercenary corps not only had humans, but also a dragonman, dogmen, and a small giant.

To outsiders, it seemed this was perhaps because Commander Long Fu was a dragonman himself.

However, the real reason was that the survivors were a complicated bunch. They not only had a half beastman, but also a necromancer, a ghost girl, and a dragonman youngster that could change his race at will.

Because of the survivors’ foundation when they established the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they involuntarily reflected their common origin leading to the peculiar phenomenon of having many kinds of races.

As the price rose, so too did the bids slow.

“This price is out of bounds.” Song Shou shook his head and helplessly sighed.

He withdrew from the competition first.

Although he was very interested in these dwarfs, he hadn’t selected an island yet and he didn’t have enough money.

Surprisingly, the second to withdraw was actually room 21.

Zi Di indeed wanted these dwarfs.

The reason she withdrew was because the price was too unreasonable. This price could purchase better and more dwarf slaves.

“Not many gold coins left.” Zi Di carefully calculated the money on hand.

The chance fortune of 50,000 gold coins was almost gone.

Buying Chi Lai cost 8,000 gold.

The gold level healing potion, gold level Golden Silk Chainmail, the Miracle Flower Pot, and so on were all gained by the survivors through fierce price wars. That depleted much of their financial resources.

“I have to save some money for daily expenditures.”

“Next, I also need to buy the water pigmen.”

In the Wilderness Continent, there was the Three Color River, it was extremely long and ran through two thirds of the continent.

The Three Color River was white upstream, and had ice floating in it, yellow midstream due to mixing silt, and red downstream for scarlet red vegetation grew all over it down there.

The water pigmen always lived in the upstream part of the Three Color River.

The auction list stated that these water pigmen all came from a single village.

The village located upstream of the Three Color River had been raided by human slavers.

Aside from the casualties incurred from resistance and during transit, all of the remaining water pigmen captives were being auctioned here.

Zong Ge valued this group of beastmen.

Zong Ge said to the dragonman youngster and others, “We need these water pigmen, their bloodline is very compatible with the Backwater Battle Qi technique. Not only do I know the cultivation method of the Backwater Battle Qi technique, but I also understand how to establish a Backwater Battle Squad.”

“With these water pigmen, we will have top quality sailors.”

“Water pigmen are easy to provide for and aren’t picky eaters. They can fish for themselves and normally, every adult water pigman is an expert fisher. During famine, they can even use river sand to allay their hunger, their powerful stomachs can let them digest it barely.”

“During naval battles, we will have an all new route of attack – we can attack enemy ships with the water pigmen.”

“Backwater Battle Qi can let someone operate in water for a long time, and after it is given to the water pigmen, they will redouble its power.”

Zong Ge successfully convinced the dragonman youngster.

Cang Xu was somewhat worried.

He privately found the dragonman youngster and generally said this. If these water pigmen are purchased, they will be trained by Zong Ge. He already has the Quick Knife Battle Squad, if he increases his strength again, won’t that increase the hidden dangers?

The dragonman youngster didn’t mind and instead advised Cang Xu, “We are an integrated whole, with the same goal. We need to try any way that’ll increase our strength.”

“I trust Zong Ge, he is very ambitious and wants to be the leader. But he has never been a vile character.”

“Without Zong Ge, we won’t be able to form a Backwater Battle Squad, correct?”

The Backwater Battle Squad had a high performance to cost ratio.

Zong Ge was a crucial person.

The water pigmen would be controlled by the slave contracts they signed.

With this goal in mind, the dwarfs on stage could not compare to it.

Zi Di wisely gave up.

In the end, through his own power, Men Shi bought back all of his clansmen.

“I actually won!” Men Shi found it somewhat hard to believe he was victorious.

He wiped his forehead with his hand and noticed it was utterly sweaty.

Before this auction, he had never spoken. All of his money and energy was for the purpose of redeeming his people.

To him, this auction was heart stopping.

At a completely unreasonable price, these dwarfs were sold to Men Shi.

The market price couldn’t be used as a reference for this.

These were Men Shi’s people.

“We’re saved.” A dwarf gleefully sobbed.

Men Shi then left.

He strode towards backstage, prepared to depart with all of his people. He wasn’t interested in any upcoming auctions.

The originally full auction audience suddenly had a gap in it, in a somewhat abrupt manner.

The rest quietly waited for the next auction.

According to the auction list, the auction had reached its finale.

“Do we know what treasures will be on stage?”

Many people curiously guessed.

Hun Tong’s measures to maintain secrecy had succeeded.

Generally speaking, the final item was bound to be exceedingly precious. Even if this auction ended here, such an auction was bound to leave a deep impression on people and increase the influence of it further.

“There should only be two or three things before the final item.” Zi Di determined in her mind.

The next slaves weren’t the water pigmen Zong Ge was looking forward to, but rather a mother and child.

The mother had wavy blond hair and looked delicately beautiful. The son only looked like he was six or seven and closely gripped his mother’s leg.

As the two were escorted up, they looked panicked and hopeless.

“Baron Zai Xiang’s territory was attacked by pirates. He was the feudal lord, however, he fearfully slinked off and fled before the battle, causing his troops to be routed and his entire territory to be looted by the pirates and many thousands were massacred.”

“The empire dispatched troops to quickly subdue the lost territory, expel the pirates, and to imprison the baron’s entire family for his crime.”

“As of now, Baron Zai Xiang and his heir have been beheaded by the empire. The rest of the clan was sold into slavery.”

“What you see before you now is not the baron’s wife, but rather the baron’s lover as well as his bastard.”

“In addition to them, there are also some retainers and subordinates, please see the magic images.”

Everyone gazed up and carefully observed them.

The woman had a bronze level life aura, don’t expect that she’d be able to fight or kill.

It was obvious that she was the baron’s canary, her cultivation was just a part of her status as a canary, that’s all.

As for their bastard, although he had a noble’s extraordinary bloodline, it was hard to tell how much talent he really had.

Besides, few people here had the patience to train this child.

The reciprocation cycle was rather long.

Unless the bastard showed extraordinary talent.

Therefore, the quality of the retainers and subordinates in the magic images greatly influenced how the buyers judged the worth of these slaves.

Zong Ge sized them up too.

He rapidly lost interest.

These retainers and subordinates had mediocre strength, the strongest of them was a mere bronze level.

Baron Zai Xiang’s clan didn’t lack talents but it was clear that those had been divided up already.

It was like when a prairie bison toppled over. First the lions would eat most of the flesh, followed by the hyenas, and then the vultures.

The baron’s lover and bastard had ended up here due to their slave merchant having a flash of sudden inspiration: if they were sold in an uncivilized place, perhaps their noble identity could fetch a higher price here?

Chi Lai was also watching.

He was not focused on the mother and child on stage, but rather on Long Fu and Shi Qi.

When he noticed the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were not interested, his understanding of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps deepened a layer.

Suddenly, Fei She broke the room’s quietness.

He couldn’t help but wipe his eyes and cry out in alarm, “That’s, that’s Qing Xin?! Are, are my eyes deceiving me?”

For a moment, the three silvers all looked at him funny.

Fei She’s look changed, and his heart shouted, “Oh no”.

In a moment of desperation, he forgot there was an outsider inside the room with them – Chi Lai.

In room 21, Zi Di leaped from her seat, “Elder Qing Xin! She isn’t dead?”

In the magic images, she noticed there was a curled up figure in the corner of the iron cage.

Although she looked middle aged, and her skin, hands, feet, and other features showed he had been engaged in constant physical labor, Zi Di saw many things she was familiar with.

As the bewilderment faded, certainty grew ever more.

That should be Elder Qing Xin!

She was disguised.

Chapter 365: Qing Xin

Qing Xin and Fei She were both elders of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, but she was many years younger and had been highly regarded by Zi Di’s father.

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance was made up of smaller merchant alliances as well as individual stores and trading coalitions.

Qing Xin was once a shop owner, who engaged in low end alchemy materials.

The shop was crowded out and went nearly bankrupt.

After joining the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, she saved her store and displayed her unusual talents.

She was not only talented in business but also magic cultivation.

After obtaining Zi Di’s father’s appreciation, Qing Xin had enough resources to quickly become a bronze level magician.

Zi Di’s father wanted to keep cultivating her.

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s background was too superficial, it needed cultivators, especially cultivators on their side.

“I remember my father said that Qing Xin’s bloodline was neither high nor low, and according to his assessment, the silver level was her limit.”

“She should be an iron level magician, but from the outside, she looks like a normal person.”
