“Before my father died, she was declared dead. Her caravan had been looted and slaughtered by bandits.”

“Did she feign death?”

“Actually, did she escape and conceal her identity?”

“Perhaps I am just guessing at things. The person in the image just bears a resemblance to her.”

“No, even if there is a chance, I cannot give up!”

The auction began.

A strong impulse rose in Zi Di’s heart.

She felt just like Men Shi.

Qing Xin had been a tender and close older sister to her. There was an unforgettable experience from Zi Di’s childhood that influenced her positively.

Only a few people could enter Zi Di’s heart.

With her mother and father dead, Qing Xin and Fei She were perhaps the only two people she considered relatives.

“However.”

“I don’t have that many gold coins left.”

“If I acquire these slaves, buying the water pigmen will be very dangerous.”

“Don’t tell me we have to drain our liquid capital? No, that isn’t reliable.”

“Most crucially, I still can’t be certain she is Qing Xin!”

“Dang it…”

At that moment, the dragonman youngster’s voice came from room 14.

He placed a bid.

Zi Di blankly stared, then immediately understood that her beloved had passed on information.

Her eyes twinkled and her heart welled up with a warm feeling.

“Elder Fei She is by his side, he must have recognized Qing Xin too.”

“He wants to tell me that he knows the situation and won’t hesitate to purchase these slaves for the chance of saving a surviving relative!”

“That must be it.”

Zi Di’s entire being seemed to glow with light.

Her bid immediately provoked many more to take notice.

“I didn’t expect room 21 to be interested in these slaves.”

“Who are these people, they have so much money!”

“Maybe they are just pretending?”

Upon seeing Zi Di enter the contest, the dragonman youngster remained calm and collected.

He said to Fei She, “Are you sure she is Qing Xin?”

Fei She blinked, “No, no I cannot. I know the Wisteria Merchant; Merchant Alliance had a young elder that was responsible for many secret businesses. She was an iron level, but according to relayed rumors, she was dead.”

Zong Ge looked at the magic image and interjected, “Perhaps she is still alive, maybe hiding herself because the Wisteria Merchant Alliance fell to ruins. Due to unexpected events, she was reduced to a slave. She concealed her strength and waited for an opportunity to escape and recover her freedom.”

The dragonman youngster pretended to mutter to himself, “If we took a chance and bought her, we might be able to recruit an iron level magician. But how great that chance is cannot be ascertained.”

After saying that, the dragonman youngster glanced at Zong Ge.

As Zong Ge and he locked eyes, Zong Ge replied, “I believe this is a rare opportunity. We originally wanted to buy the pig watermen, but if it is a magician, an iron level magician, then her worth will utterly exceed that of the water pigmen.”

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but nod and continue bidding.

After three bids, the dragonman youngster withdrew from the competition.

He shook his head, “At this price, forget about it. The risks aren’t worth the money.”

Fei She also nodded, “The, the price indeed is high. Furthermore, I am not that familiar with Qing Xin.”

After the dragonman youngster withdrew, there was still three parties left.

Zi Di was one of them.

Not long after, she became the winner.

If the dragonman youngster purchased these slaves, it wouldn’t conform with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps standards.

Zi Di purchasing them was far less risky.

Zong Ge sighed to himself.

He also believed that by purchasing these slaves, he would let the water pigmen he admired slip away. Because the team had to save some gold coins as movable capital.

Room 21’s door quickly rang.

The auction’s fighter and magician brought the slave contracts and imperial documents.

The documents were law binding and stated clearly that this slave trade was legitimate and was protected under imperial law.

After the slave agreement was amended, Zi Di and Cang Xu left the room.

She decided to act like Men Shi, prioritizing the acceptance of the slaves and renouncing joining in the following auction.

Although the ultimate treasure hadn’t appeared yet and there was still hope the water pigmen could be auctioned.

Zi Di had given that duty to the dragonman youngster, she couldn’t hold herself back from verifying if it was Qing Xin or not.

“The next auction is a group of water pigman slaves.” The auction master presented.

A bronze level fighter dragged up a water pigman.

The auction master walked to the water pigman slave and shouted, “Everyone, as you can see, this is quite the unusual pigman – a water pigman.”

“Every adult water pigman is taller and fatter than a normal pigman.”

“Gaze at its stature and you will know they are an excellent source of troops.”

The water pigman slave on stage glowered at everyone.

His arms were tied behind his back, and he had been drugged. His entire body was limp and only his eyes could express his ire and hostility.

He was 2 meters tall, had very white skin, a pig head on a human body, his ears looked like two fans, and had a potbelly and was very rotund.

The auction master clapped the water pigman’s belly, causing a visible wave of flesh to break across it.

The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but associate it with Rou Zang.

Of course, the water pigman could never compare to Rou Zang.

After the auction master emphasized the water pigman’s outstanding swimming ability, the auction began.

Zi Di had left, so the dragonman youngster was the one to bid.

Fei She checked for him and informed him of the maximum amount he could spend.

Zong Ge paid close attention to this.

In room 1, Song Shou bid first.

A few merchant alliance representatives bid one after the other.

Zong Ge was somewhat nervous.

The bidders were all single powers, and one of them was even Song Shou, who was about to select his island to develop his territory.

The auction mood was not intense, and all who bid weren’t doing so fiercely.

The merchant alliance representatives were bidding especially cautiously.

After all the water pigmen were beastmen, and there was a barrier between them and humanity.

The Ten Touch Pirates, Lightning Pirates, and other pirate groups remained silent.

The dragonman youngster persevered until the end.

“There is still hope.” Zong Ge’s mood was somewhat stirred.

But a moment later, Hun Tong spoke.

He raised the price ferociously as he announced a high price.

The dragonman youngster sighed, the price had exceeded the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ limit.

He was forced to give up.

Zong Ge also hung his eyes down, his heart regretful.

Hun Tong bid only once, acting at the last phase.

He succeeded.

These water pigman slaves were now his.

“This price is affordable.” Hun Tong was mildly pleased, feeling it had been worth it.

The slave merchant who brought these water pigmen slaves to Two Eyes Island indicated that they would be joining this auction.

Hun Tong had been thinking about it and wanted to buy them.

Because the water pigmen could survive in water, and due to their unique body structure, they could breathe underwater.

Two Eyes Island was a special island, and the water pigmen were well suited to the bobbing island environment.

Just as he was envisioning his future on how he would employ these water pigmen, a rumble was heard.

Then, Hun Tong’s body began to shake.

Not only he, but everyone in the auction was also shaking about.

The auction master nearly fell off the stage.

“What’s going on?”

“What just happened?!”

“The entire ground shook, was it an earthquake?”

“Two Eyes Island can’t have earthquakes!”

Everyone was bewildered, and everyone sitting stood up and exhibited a cautious posture.

Hun Tong promptly ordered his subordinates to investigate.

At the same time, he smashed through the one way mirror and flew onto the auction stage, “Calm down!”

“An accident has occurred.”

“Don’t be impatient, don’t recklessly cause misunderstandings.”

“I am the feudal lord here; I will use my noble reputation to guarantee that this auction is safe and fair!”

Hun Tong handled it appropriately and exhibited his outstanding feudal lord knowledge.

But the situation couldn’t be controlled.

Because the auction’s floor tiling, walls, and even ceiling was being jabbed with thorns.

The entire auction hall became a trap that would kill everyone inside!

In room 3.

“Captain, what should we do?” A subordinate urgently asked.

Qi You coldly snorted, “Regardless of whether this is a scheme of Hun Tong or not, we need to break out first and discuss that later.”

Boom!

A loud sound caused Qi You to look to the side.

In another room, Dian Wan had already used a battle skill.

“How can this be?” Dian Wan’s battle skill left a hole in the wall.

Then that caused the entire wall to push towards him while simultaneously lining itself with spikes.

Dian Wan was forced to retreat, crashing through the one way mirror and falling into the first floor.

“Two Eyes Island is rebelling!”

“It is not an island; it’s a coral ball cage tree.”

“Lord Hun Tong, what’s going on?”

The buyers weren’t blind and quickly discerned the truth.

“What is happening…” Hun Tong’s eyes flashed, he unconsciously looked at room 2, a conjecture growing increasingly certain.

“It’s the fishmen!”

“They’ve made their move.”

“They decided to attack Two Eyes Island today.”

“It was actually today!”

Chapter 366: A Trap

Hun Tong and the imperial secret spy Shadow had conspired against the Blue Green Coral Fishmen Clan.

Two Eyes Island was once that fishmen clan’s home.

The fishmen had always wanted to take back their coral ball cage tree.

Previously, Xi Dai Zi had infiltrated Two Eyes Island to negotiate with Shadow, and Xu Ma had accidentally run into them. Afterward, there was the whole troublesome necromancer affair.

Shadow insincerely agreed to work with the fishmen and wanted to pry into when the other party would be precisely attacking the island but failed.

But privately, Hun Tong and Shadow discussed the hypothetical timeline of the fishmen clan’s attack.

They both believed that it was very probable they would during the auction.

Because during this period, Hun Tong and the others’ attention would be focused on the auction and the island’s defenses would be relatively weak.

Furthermore, with the pirates, merchant alliances, mercenary corps, and other visitors on the island, Two Eyes Island wasn’t under the feudal lord’s sole control.

The two people continued to analyze and felt that the likelihood of this was small.

Because during the auction, Two Eyes Island would have the maximum amount of cultivators gathered on it.

Although Hun Tong’s and the others’ attention was on the auction, Two Eyes Island had many cultivators and was mainly composed of humans.

If the fishmen attacked, they’d not only have to confront Hun Tong and the people here, but also many other human cultivators.

From the fishman’s point of view, the disadvantages outweighed the advantages if they attacked the island during the auction.

Besides, when an army moves, there will be noise.

Hun Tong didn’t receive any relevant information.

“I didn’t expect these fishmen would really attack the auction today.”

“Hmph, truly stupid!”

Hun Tong coldly snorted to himself.

He wasn’t caught unprepared.

It’s been three years since I’ve smashed the obstructions here, I really want to fight that old fishman.”

Hun Tong sized up the rebellious coral walls and floor around him as well as the constantly growing thorns, his eyes leaking a trace of greed and ferventness.

“I didn’t expect these fishmen to be able to control the coral ball cage tree to this degree!”

Hun Tong had always wanted this pertinent control method.

Upon seeing what was happening, his desire for this control spell grew even stronger.

“This isn’t the ocean.”

“Old fish head, when you attacked this island, you had already fallen into my trap.”

“No one can hinder me after I kill you.”

“The control method will be mine, and after this battle, Two Eyes Island will be under my complete control!”

Hun Tong had schemed for a long time, all for the purpose of luring the fishmen clan into attacking Two Eyes Island.

Now, his hopes had become reality.

The dragonman youngster also rushed out of his room but couldn’t find Zi Di and Cang Xu.

Beforehand, Zi Di and Cang Xu had left their room and gone backstage to accept the slaves.

The auction stage rebelled and formed a cage that was constantly growing and squeezing everything in, but it didn’t include the backstage.

“Two Eyes Island is a coral ball cage tree; I fear its rebellion has the fishmen’s hands all over it.” Zong Ge analyzed.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps knew about this secret.

But they made an agreement with Hun Tong to not publicize it.

Now that the auction stage was rebelling, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps knew more information than the others and was able to surmise the facts within the first moments.

“Since the fishmen want to take back their home, there is bound to be a large assault. If we remain trapped here, Two Eyes Island’s defensive line will lack a leader.” Zong Ge continued, “No matter what Hun Tong did, we must return to the port first to defend our ships and crew.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, “No matter what, we must converge with them first!”

With concerns of Zi Di and Cang Xu in mind, he activated his battle qi and raised his right fist.

In a split second, he emitted a fierce silver level life aura.

Bursting Explosion battle qi surged in his body, then concentrated in his right fist.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The first ruthlessly smashed into the coral wall, creating a gigantic explosion.

The wall was blown to bits and a huge hole split open, but it wasn’t connected to the outside. The coral wall had grown much more coral.

“Again.” The dragonman youngster’s eyes narrowed and he continued punching.

Bang, bang, bang…

With the sound of explosions, coral splattered everywhere and the one way mirror was pulverized, allowing people to see what was happening in room 14.

Everyone saw the dragonman youngster had acted and were watching closely to see the outcome.

But after breaking through the coral wall, there was still another coral wall.

“How thick is this?” The dragonman youngster stopped attacking, his brows creased.

But he didn’t gain nothing.

The coral had split open, creating not only countless fragments, but also flowing sap.

It was a blue-green liquid.

It was quite viscous and was something between water and oil.

As it slowly flowed out, the coral ball cage tree’s gold level life aura leaked from it.

When someone saw this, they suddenly yelled, “The coral ball cage tree is an extraordinary lifeform that has attained the gold level, we need a gold level cultivator to act!”

Hun Tong sank into hesitation.

He was somewhat worried that after he acted and broke out of here too early, it would cause the fishmen to promptly retreat.

But Qi You had already moved.

His five fingers stabbed the coral wall as effortlessly as poking tofu.

A burst of whirlwind-like battle qi dispersed, splitting a hole into the wall.

The hole looked like a funnel, growing narrower the further in it went, however it hadn’t gotten through.

Hun Tong’s brows pursed suddenly.

Qi You’s pirates by his side glared, they hadn’t foreseen that their boss was unable to get through.

The Fertile Land priest extended a wrinkled palm and softly stroked the coral wall.

Wherever it went, the thorns on the coral wall naturally fell off.

The Fertile Land priest’s eyes slightly opened, and he shouted his discovery, “This is a god’s power…it is the Suffering Endurance Barrier!”

“Suffering Endurance God…” For a moment, many people’s mind flashed with the same god’s name.

The Suffering Endurance God was a small god.

They were a sharkman but left the ocean to travel the lands.

He joined the Great Pain Temple, shaving his hair as a monk (even though he didn’t have hair), and constantly increased his life level.

Afterwards, he lived in seclusion for a long time in the Three Color River’s midstream.

It wasn’t until he became a god that he became widely known.

The Suffering Endurance God was deeply influenced by the Great Pain Temple.

After becoming a god, he founded the Suffering Endurance Church and advocated for endurance, suffering hardship, and to derive life’s vigor from within those things.

The Suffering Endurance Barrier amply displayed the Suffering Endurance Church’s creed. It could allow a spell caster to bear more attacks and pain.

This was why even when Qi You acted, he didn’t succeed either.

“You’re joking, even Qi You can’t break through?”

“The coral ball cage tree combined with the Endurance Barrier is actually so powerful!”

“Don’t tell me we are trapped here? The longer time goes on, the worse I fear the situation outside is!”

Everyone was somewhat frantic.

“Let me try.” Shi Fa came over.

Hope welled up in everyone’s heart again.

If battle qi doesn’t work, then let’s give magic a try!

Shi Fa instantly moved.

His characteristic playing cards revolved around him, floating in the air.

These were his custom-made magic scrolls.

A card flew out from the group and turned into a strong flame that pounced onto the coral wall.

The flame was very hot and concentrated.

The coral thorns were burned away, and the coral wall began to fuse.

But soon after, the blaze was weirdly extinguished.

“What’s going on?”

“It seems the magic has been suppressed.”

“It’s obvious that Shi Fa wasn’t attacked, why? Is magic ineffective?”

Everyone was clamorous.

Shi Fa shook his head, “I stopped my magic myself.”

He continued, “Not good, perhaps there is a nearby magic formation counteracting magic power. When I maintained my spell, I could feel my magic power dwindling.”

A Magic Power Counteraction formation!

This magic formation was often used by hunting groups against powerful magic beasts.

Magic beasts contained magic power and could use racial magics.

Racial magics were the greatest threat.

Hunting groups would use this formation to deplete a magic beast’s magic power, until it was reduced to only being able to use its physical body.

Many terrible magic beasts were captured alive that way.

Now that formation is being used here.

“No matter who it is, they are harboring unfathomable motivations!”

“Do they want to capture us alive like magic beasts?”

“Let’s attack together, I don’t believe this auction hall can hold us all!”

People were constantly shouting.

But although much was said, for a moment, no one did anything.

Regardless of whether it was the dragonman youngster, Qi You, or Shi Fa, all of them were waiting and watching.

Hun Tong was no longer calm; he was growing distinctly gloomy.

He noticed that it would be difficult to escape from the auction hall in a short period.

Whether it was the Suffering Endurance Barrier or the Magic Power Counteraction formation, both of them would deplete a large amount of battle qi, magic or divine power.

If everyone was united, trusted each other, and exerted themselves at the same time, breaking out of here was not difficult.

But the problem was that the cultivators here were an extremely mixed bunch.

There were imperial nobles, profit seeking merchants, mercenary corps, and pirates.

These people were wary of each other.

The situation outside was unknown, and no one dared to excessively expended their own strength.

If one was weak, then perhaps one by them would decide to take their life.

Some pirates viciously glared at Hun Tong.

Qi You spoke directly, “Feudal Lord Hun Tong, is this a scheme of yours? Do you want to catch all of us in a single net?”

Hun Tong coldly snorted, “By my clan’s honor, I swear that I had no hand in this.”

Qi You shook his head, “Don’t play tricks. This place is your territory, yet this happened. We trusted you and came here to participate in your auction.”

“Now then, you are in charge of obtaining the power that will let us leave!”

Many pirates echoed Qi You’s sentiment.

“That’s right, that’s how it should be!”

“Lord Hun Tong, we are now counting on you.”

“Please let your actions speak for yourself. That is the best way to prove your innocence.”

Hun Tong’s face was as dark as water, somewhat embarrassed he was being squeezed by the crowd.

“A group of morons.” Song Shou came to his aid.

He decided to jump out of the room and stood by Hun Tong’s side, “Who do you think loses the most if this place is attacked?”

“The spoiling of this auction will vilely impact Two Eyes Island.”

The two gold levels united and immediately suppressed the malicious hoots and crowd jeers.

Everyone was silent, and only the creaking from the growing coral thorns could be heard.

Chapter 367: Feudal Lord Hun Tong

Hun Tong threw a look of thanks to Song Shou.

Song Shou nodded to Hun Tong, and he stood shoulder to shoulder in firm support of him.

To Song Shou, there were many benefits for backing Hun Tong.

In the future, the island he would choose for his territory might be close to Two Eyes Island. Having a good relationship with Hun Tong ahead of time would be beneficial to his territory’s development.

If nothing else, just opening a trade route between the two islands would be an immense help to the newly established territory.

Operating an island had many trials.

Because an island’s resources were limited and might lack necessary construction materials.

For example, Two Eyes Island lacked metal and ore.

Unlike territory on the continent, where there were roads in all directions and vast tracts of land, even if one’s territory didn’t have something, one could look for it in the surrounding areas.

Although the auction hall had changed, Song Shou did not believe Hun Tong was defeated and routed.

“Hun Tong has run Two Eyes Island for many years and knows a great deal of inside information.”

“Two Eyes Island is imperial territory; I want to see what things are so brazen as to encroach upon this place!”

Song Shou and Hun Tong were both imperial feudal lords, the same type of people, and when confronted with a mutual external enemy, it was natural they’d help each other.

“One of the reasons I got close to Song Shou was to conspire against the Blue Green Coral Fishmen Clan.”

“I didn’t expect this day would come so suddenly.”

“Song Shou has stated his support of me, that saves me the cost of forming an alliance with him.”

Hun Tong regained his clarity of vision and glanced at room 2.

He had a deep relationship with the one in room 2.

The one in room 2 was the imperial secret spy – Shadow!

“Shadow’s troops have snuck into the Vinegar Jar Pirates and have become enemies hidden within their ranks.”

“Prior intelligence came through, yet we haven’t heard anything about the fishmen clan making such a big move.”

“It seems these undiscovered traitors are inauspicious.”

“Not just that.”

“We might have spies hiding among us.”

“Even now in the auction hall!”

Hun Tong’s mind flashed and he quickly analyzed the situation before him.

The auction hall’s transformation was the greatest proof in favor of Hun Tong’s hypothesis.

The Suffering Endurance barrier, Magic Power Counteraction formation, and coral ball cage tree through the fishmen clan’s secret control spell caused the auction hall to rebel and become a cage.

These were certainly deployed to waste time.

It was inexcusable that this could happen right under the noses of Hun Tong and the others without an inside man.

“Previously, the fishman Xi Dai Zi disguised himself when he went to Two Eyes Island.”

“Afterwards was the beast tide.”

“Preceding the auction, there were all kinds of ships landing with unusually complex crews.”

“They took advantage of this opportunity to mix in how many fishmen?”

“The most important thing now is to break out of here first!”

Hun Tong’s eyes flashed, and in an instant, his thoughts were restrained.

Although much was thought about, only a few brief seconds had passed.

Because the auction hall was closing in as countless thorns were frantically growing, the second floor was nearly crushed, and many people had jumped down to the first floor.

Hun Tong stood on the auction stage and ran his eyes over the first and second floor cultivators.

When he saw the Applause Pirates’ frogmen, his eyes couldn’t help but stop faintly.

If the undiscovered traitor that snuck into the auction hall wasn’t one of the frogmen’s humans, that would be very suspicious!

However, Hun Tong didn’t wan to question them directly.

Right now, they had to trust each other because of the imminent danger, and if he gave off misgivings, that was bound to cause everyone to take precautions against each other and lead to fights.

The fishmen had turned the auction hall into a net that caught nearly all of Two Eyes Island’s cultivators. Hun Tong now assumed that doing this was both bold and ingenious.

Time and tide waited for no man, so Hun Tong said, “Everyone, I am Hun Tong, the Feudal Lord here.”

“Like you, I am a victim.”

“Furthermore, I am the biggest victim.”

“For the empire, for the Duo Mu Clan’s reputation, and as Feudal Lord, I must act.”

“But I cannot do it alone!”

After pausing, Hun Tong continued, “I know you are apprehensive, I am the same.”

“To change the auction hall this much means we likely have an enemy in our midst.”

“If I go about it alone, I am bound to fall into our enemy’s schemes.”

“We must work together!”

“Whoever refuses will be my, Hun Tong’s, enemy, the enemy of everyone!”

“Whoever sneaks away is a traitor!”

At this critical juncture, Hun Tong’s usual warm tone had become sharp.

Gold aura broke out, exposing Hun Tong’s fierce determination as well as his increasingly strong killing intent.

Upon seeing Hun Tong like this, no one doubted he would follow through with his words!

“Hun Tong…is very skilled.” Some pirates’ eyes flashed with fear.

This was the first time many people had genuinely recognized what Two Eyes Island’s Feudal Lord was like.

Hun Tong’s candidness had its pros and cons.

He was a skilled linguist, first he put himself in the shoes of everyone here, then molded the undiscovered as the external pressure. Using threats or other means wouldn’t unite the cultivators here.

In this crucial moment, Hun Tong had displayed his leadership talent.

A voice soon responded in agreement.

It was One Eye.

One Eye was Hun Tong’s subordinate.

Soon after, the other subordinates reached the same, echoing the former’s agreement.

Hun Tong spoke again, “The enemy has trapped us here, they want to capture us all!”

“We will break out of here, and whoever extends Two Eyes Island a helping hand will receive my friendship and ample rewards after we win.”

“You know my reputation.”

“You also know my wealth.”

Benefits for motivation, sentiment for understanding.

Hun Tong had run Two Eyes Island for a long time and his past qualifications increased his persuasiveness.

Thus, the mercenary corps, merchant alliance representatives, lone wolves, and others agreed, each of them vocalizing their answer to Hun Tong’s appeal.

Hun Tong temporarily organized the auction hall’s cultivators together.

He also understood proper decorum and allowed every power to band together separately, then with this foundation deployed them according to his plan.

The auction hall’s coral thorns were constantly spreading and were being driven back by the cultivators.

The ceiling was pressing down, the floor was slowly bulging, and walls all around were shrinking, continuously reducing the space everyone could move in.

Luckily this shrinkage was slow, and it gave everyone plenty of time to marshal themselves.

The second floor had been compressed into nonexistence.

Everyone in the private rooms came out.

The representatives of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and Chi Lai converged with Xu Ma.

The dragonman youngster didn’t find Zi Di, which proved that the auction hall’s backstage was not part of the trap.

This discovery brought both worry and delight in equal measure.

“Where were the people in room 21?” Many people had taken note of this.

Zi Di and Cang Xu were the most imposing figures in the auction and had provoked many people. They were the two most memorable people here.

“Dang it, they aren’t here.”

“They escaped earlier, that’s very suspicious!”

“If there is an insider, then they are the biggest suspect.”

Many people exchanged, they had a deep resentment of Zi Di and Cang Xu.

The wise dragonman youngster didn’t say anything.

Fei She actually echoed their condemnation, drawing many friendly eyes.

After all, during the auction, the Drago Lion Mercenary Corps repeatedly “confronted” room 21.

Under Hun Tong’s organization, despite the chaotic situation, order was in the process of being reinforced.

Hun Tong divided everyone into three groups, one group to attack, one group to rest, and one group to supervise.

The dragonman youngster mixed into them, his heart worried about Zi Di and Cang Xu, for he didn’t know how they were doing right now.

A group of dwarfs were walking through Two Eyes Island’s streets.

Men Shi walked in front, walking while reminding his clansmen, “There is a hotel ahead, I reserved ten rooms.”

“We’ll stay there first for the time being.”

“In the room, I have prepared good food and clothing.”

“We don’t have…much money. You must be frugal and cut down on food and drink.”

Men Shi had nearly exhausted all of the money on him to purchase his people.

His people were sobbing emotionally, thanking Men Shi.

Some were embittered and indignant, “After crossing this pit, we still need to take revenge!”

“That’s right, we’ll find that wretched captain that bit the hand that fed him!”

“It’s like I said, all humans are wretched.”

Men Shi’s people had lived in the mountains and had mined for a living. After suffering this catastrophe, many of them had a negative opinion of humans and were seriously wary towards them.

“I want to leave this place now and return home.”

“Even though our home is already gone, at least I can fall asleep in those ruins without worry!”

Men Shi’s clan’s former home had been destroyed by a fire.

The dwarfs had dug into the mountains and multiplied there.

But an earthquake triggered the mountain’s collapse, and they were forced to migrate.

Men Shi sighed, constantly consoling his people, “Everything is in the past, we will find a new home. The ore there will surely be more beautiful and evocative!”

As they were talking, a rumble was heard.

At the same time, the ground violently shook.

Behind the dwarfs, a dazzling yellow light erupted like a volcano into the air.

“What’s going on?”

“What just happened?!”

The dwarfs cried out in alarm, they all turned around to look up to see that the auction hall was wrapped in that yellow light.

Large amounts of branches and vines extended from underneath and layered themselves around the auction hall.

The guards outside the auction hall noticed things were going south and immediately attacked, however the yellow light blocked them.

The dwarfs foolishly watched this.

They had walked far from the auction hall, however, from the dozen meter high yellow light, they could sense a strong divine spell, magic, and the life aura of a gold level magic plant.

“The auction hall had an accident!”

“Is…that the auction’s special item?” A dwarf foolishly asked.

Bang, bang, bang.

A series of fireworks flew in the skies about Two Eyes Island.

“Ggglmra!”

“Mmar!”

“Glmmwza”

Fishmen appeared from manholes that led underground, others rushed and attacked from shadowy alleys, and some kicked down the doors of buildings they were hiding in, their murderous spirit abound.

The dwarfs realized what was happening.

“It’s the fishmen!”

“Two Eyes Island is being attacked by fishmen.”

“Where did these fishmen come from?”

Chapter 368: Fishmen Ambush

The dwarfs were stupefied.

It took a few seconds for them to react.

“Hehe, how interesting, those damned humans suffered damage.”

“Be careful, we are in danger too!”

A group of fishmen rushing past noticed the dwarfs.

Men Shi bravely stepped forward and took out his stem war hammer, gripping it tightly with both hands, centering it in his chest.

“Fishmen, we are not your enemy.” Men Shi growled, his thick eyebrows and big eyes tenaciously watching the fishmen’s leader.

The fishmen’s leader was steeped in a silver level aura, riding a four legged ocean lizard, and had many bones covering his body.

The ash gray skeleton covering him looked like armor.

It was the Steel Bone Knight bloodline.

The fishman had an extraordinary bloodline!

The blue skinned lizard growled threateningly at Men Shi and was becoming agitated.

The fishman leader looked deeply at Men Shi, then across Men Shi’s dwarven people. Then with shivering killing intent, he clapped his lizard’s head.

“gklllrma”

He said something, and the subordinates he commanded charged from behind him and brushed past the dwarfs.

The dwarfs walked past the precipice of death.

Upon seeing the fishmen leave, the dwarfs whose hearts were in their throats groaned, all of them wanted to collapse from that.

“We were nearly finished.”

“What can we do? We can’t die on Two Eyes Island after getting free.”

“We don’t need to help those humans!”

Men Shi said, “We should still return to the hotel, I have food and clothing there, and I have some simple weapons for you too.”

The allocation of weapons was not odd.

This world had cultivators and numerous magic beasts. Even something as powerful as the Sheng Ming Empire had bandits in its hinterlands and was never able to achieve a completely safe society.

Adding on the rampant pirates, Men Shi would have prepared more weapons if he hadn’t been saving money to purchase his clansmen.

The dwarfs didn’t dare to stay in place, and they sped up their pace towards the hotel.

Boom, bang, rumble.

Three fireballs continuously bombarded the auction hall’s wall.

It was Zi Di and Cang Xu using fire path rings.

The auction hall’s external wall was thicker and was constantly wrapped up with the coral ball cage tree’s branches and vines.

“Even if we use all of our magic power to bomb indiscriminately, we won’t gain anything.” Cang Xu shook his head.

Zi Di looked grave and carefully surveyed the yellow light in front of her, “The auction hall is part of the coral ball cage tree. Its growth has gone wild. Furthermore, it seems there is also a divine spell’s power here.”

At the same time, detection alchemy puppets were constantly feeding information on the surroundings.

Zi Di and Cang Xu knew the fishmen had surged up and attacked every strategic location on the island.

Cang Xu replied, “The culprit behind this is the fishmen! Xi Dai Zi is a fishman, the Vinegar Jar Pirates are all fishmen, and although we haven’t seen them yet, this should be related to them!”

The necromancer’s brows creased tightly, and he gazed at the well-fortified auction hall as he calmly analyzed, “The Commander’s defense is outstanding, and he has Zong Ge at his side, he won’t be in danger for now.”

It wasn’t easy for the Vinger Jar Pirates to pull off such a big move.”

“However, this should be their limit.”

“If they were stronger, wouldn’t it be better to eliminate Hun Tong and the rest right away? The auction hall is just a cage that has trapped them temporarily, so they can’t take charge of the situation.”

“We are not the fishmen’s main target!”

When Zi Di heard Cang Xu’s words, she reluctantly restrained her fretfulness.

Cang Xu’s words indeed made sense.

Even if the auction hall was a fishman’s killing move, the most important target was not the dragonman youngster, but rather Hun Tong.

Of course, more crucially was that it was very hard for them to break into the auction hall.

Cang Xu sighed, “The Vinegar Jar Pirates’ move was so big that it caught nearly all of Two Eyes Islands’ cultivators in it.

“But the fact they aren’t defending this place indicates what?”

“It indicates they aren’t worried about people breaking into the auction hall. Our attempts just now also prove that we don’t have the internal energy to smash this obstruction in a short amount of time.”

After hearing what was said, Zi Di turned around to look at something close by.

It was a group of people.

They had been the slaves they had just purchased.

They were headed by a mother and child. The mother was blond and beautiful and was holding her son tight.

Just as they left the auction hall, the fishmen attacked Two Eyes Island, causing both of them to panic and at a loss.

Zi Di’s eyes stayed on the mother and son for only an instant before sliding past to look at a middle aged woman in the crowd.

“Is she older sister Qing Xin? If we have her, we’ll have three magicians, will that be enough to break into the auction hall?” Zi Di wondered.

Although she believed it was a high possibility that they were Qing Xin, before confirming it, everything was only just a possibility.

Zi Di couldn’t expose her identity right now.

Therefore, the situation was actually even more complicated.

Suppose the other person was Qing Xin…

Zi Di couldn’t say who she really was, and if that was the case, she wouldn’t be able to swiftly gain Qing Xin’s trust and count on her help.

There was another crucial thing, how strong was Qing Xin?

Prior rumors said she died in a bandit raid, and that she got away through playing dead, was that really the case?

She concealed her identity, does that mean she had a hidden trouble that was hard to talk about?

When Cang Xu saw Zi Di looking, he immediately understood what Zi Di was thinking.

His voice became somewhat cold, “I advise you not to do that.”

Zi Di sighed and nodded.

It was too risky to expose her true identity!

Furthermore, Qing Xin was a silver level magician, and even with her power combined with Cang Xu’s and Zi Di’s, the likelihood of breaking into the auction hall was very small.

Didn’t you see that Hun Tong and the other gold levels are trapped inside and unable to escape momentarily?

After thoroughly dispelling thoughts of attacking the auction hall, Zi Di recovered her former shrewdness, “We have more important work now, protecting the ships at port.”

“The fishmen are attacking Two Eyes Island; the port is one of their main targets.”

“We have to ensure our ships are still there.”

“By the time the commander and the rest break out, there won’t be any ships left to sail, they’ll be trapped on Two Eyes Island and in desperate struggle with the fishmen.”

Cang Xu’s expression eased somewhat, “Then we should do that! However, what do you intend to do with these slaves?”

Zi Di whispered back, “We will put these people near the port, but we cannot put them on the ships. I need a chance to talk privately, and when that time comes, we’ll play it by ear.”

This was a sensible plan, and it immediately won Cang Xu’s endorsement.

Zi Di looked deeply back at the auction hall, then bit her teeth as she turned to leave.

She and Cang Xu helped the slaves run towards the port.

“Nmlgmlalgsmlgn”

“Gnmnnla”

“Gmlamgngnmal”

The fishmen roared from under the light cannon turrets.

Their leader was the Steel Bone Knight who left Men Shi, and the dwarfs go – Fishman Gang Kui.

The reason why they let the dwarfs go was because they had more important military targets.

The light cannon turrets!

They had been recently built by Hun Tong and had been a very prominent defensive weapon during the beast tide.

But as Fishman Gang Kui and his troops charged towards the light cannon turrets, they remained still.

Ding.

Gang Kui controlled his mount, raised his 2 meter long lance, and crashed into a light cannon turret’s door, the first to charge up.

The light cannon turret’s guards were all on the ground.

None had any breath, their eyes were rolled back to their whites, and purple poisonous blood was flowing from their mouths.

In the shadows, the silhouette of a purple scaled fishman shaman appeared.

Gagn Kui promptly saluted her.

The fishman shaman nodded back to him, and like bubbles, the silhouette dissipated.

Guang Kui quickly ascended to the top, stuck his tribe’s totem pillar at the top, and lit the firework signal.

The light cannon turret had been captured!

Two Eyes Island’s barracks.

Large quantities of fishmen crazily attacked from all sides.

The barrack’s soldiers were fighting for their lives.

The situation for the humans was very unfavorable.

The biggest factor was the storm fishman leader, a silver level red-eye fishman – Hong Bo.

The Red Eye bloodline came with its own Blood Fury spell.

The red eyed fishman leader was a silver level, and after activating his Blood Fury spell, his military power would increase sharply, each attack shaking the barrack’s gates.

“We must persevere!”

“The auction hall also had a mishap, I didn’t see the Feudal Lord.”

“Then where is the Lord Commander? He didn’t participate in the auction.”

With their leader absent, the human soldiers’ morale was beginning to waver.

In a street.

Shops along the border had become ruins.

A fierce struggle had already unfolded.

There were only two people fighting, and both of them were gasping for air.

The silver level human city guard commander spat out some watery blood and a broken tooth.

Opposing him a dozen paces away was a silver scaled fishman.

This fishman was slender and was two heads taller than a normal fishman.

The outside of his forearms, back of his forelegs, as well as his entire fish head and back were covered in vertical fins. The fins were dark silver, and their points were very sharp.

His weapons were his pair of forearm knife fins.

The silver level blade fin fishman – Dao Hen!

As Hun Tong presided over the auction, the heavy burden of Two Eyes Island’s defenses was given to his city guard commander.

As the commander hurried towards the barracks, Dao Hen had ambushed him and caught him off guard, suffering minor injuries from it.

Fishmen had reduced military strength on dry land, and these minor injuries also decreased the city guard commander’s strength.

As time went on, the commander’s mind grew more irascible.

He wanted to break through this place and return to the barracks, but Dao Hen tenaciously blocked him.

“Damn fishman, after I kill you, I will chop you into pieces and turn you into salted fish!” The commander cursed, and with a vengeful glare, charged at Dao Hen.

Ding!

The commander’s blade crashed into Dao Hen’s forearm fin, creating a crisp noise and sparks.

The fierce battle began once more.

The port.

It was a sight of chaotic warfare.

A silver level spiky stinger fishman lead their subordinates into the toppled hastily created defensive line.

But the humans held fast.

Because the port had their ships berthed here, all of which were natural forts.

Especially the many magic ships which were easy to defend and hard to attack. Even though this place had the largest number of fishmen attacking this place, it was difficult to defeat their enemy quickly.

Chapter 369: Sisters Recognize Each Other

“Boss, open the door now!” The dwarf Men Shi banged on the hotel door, behind him a raggedly dressed group of dwarfs looked flurried and dispirited.

A window on the second floor opened and the hotel manager showed his face, “Stop knocking, this is an emergency, the door has already been barred, we’re closed. Get out of here!”

The fishmen attack had worried the island inhabitants very much, and one after the other, they took defensive measures.

Men Shi exploded in anger, “I already reserved the rooms, I paid the fee!”

The hotel manager answered back, “I’ll refund you, how much?”

The dwarfs’ expressions changed.

Men Shi coldly snorted, he needed to give his people time and space to breathe and rest.

“My stuff is still in the rooms, this is an emergency, now is not the time to abide by the rules!”

With this in mind, Men Shi decided to threaten the hotel manager.

“If you won’t open the door, we’ll break it down instead!”

“Or we’ll just lure some fishmen over to demolish your inn!”

The hotel manager stiffened.

The moment Men Shi raised his war hammer to smash down the door, he held out his hand, “Stay your hand, I’ll let you in. Just be quick!”

The moment the door cracked opened, the dwarfs flooded into the hotel.

“If the fishmen attack this place, I ask you to help us protect this place dwarven friends.” The hotel manager squeezed out a smile, “The price can be discussed.”

“We’ll discuss it later.” Some dwarfs replied.

After being trafficked by a human captain, they questioned the honesty of humans.

After assigning rooms, Men Shi prioritized arming them.

“What are these things?” The dwarfs weren’t pleased with these weapons.

“My grandmother’s braids are sturdier that these!” The dwarfs’ blunt nature caused them to grumble at once.

Men Shi shook his head and sighed, “Just make do, it’s better to have them.”

These weapons were very crude.

The hammers were obviously tools, not weapons.

Apart from them, there were bent one hand swords, chipped cutlasses, shabby daggers, and even a prosthetic iron arm hook. These were obviously equipment pirates had thrown out.

The dwarfs stopped talking, they firmly remembered their appreciation to Men Shi.

Although these weapons were rubbish, in the current situation, they still gave them some sense of security.

“Lord Men Shi, what are we going to do?” A dwarf asked.

Men Shi muttered to himself, “We will hide in this hotel. We won’t go outside unless it is absolutely essential. Let the humans and the fishmen duke it out, this does not concern us dwarfs.”

The dwarfs nodded.

Although they had a bit of weaponry, they didn’t have any armor.

Armor was far more expensive than weapons, therefore Men Shi couldn’t afford them.

As the dwarfs gained a stable environment for the time being, Zi Di, Cang Xu, and a group of people entered a big house.

Although the house was spacious and luxuriously decorated, no one was inside.

The house had the Flint Merchant Alliance’s symbol inside it.

Zi Di guessed that this might be the Flint Merchant Alliance branch manager’s house.

The branch manager was trapped inside the auction hall, and the house’s servants and attendants had fled without a trace.

After all, this place was quite close to the port.

The port was one of the fishman’s main targets.

Even inside, Zi Di could still hear the sounds of fighting from the port.

Ordinary servants and attendants naturally wouldn’t dare to stay in such a dangerous place.

Through scouting puppets, Cang Xu discovered this place and through a broken door, came in and made this their temporary base.

Cang Xu was responsible for standing guard.

A few scouting puppets under his control was quietly approaching the port.

Cang Xu quickly obtained first hand intel.

The port was in a bad state; however, it wasn’t that terrible. Especially compared to the already captured light cannon turrets.

The fishmen already forced their way into the port.

It seemed this was no surprise to Cang Xu.

Even though he wasn’t a military officer, he had reckoned this would happen.

Because the port was accessible from all sides and had no terrain to defend it.

If the port had numerous obstacles, it greatly inconvenienced the daily shipping of goods and movement of people.

The humans left on the port had retreated into their ships or took the high ground on the deck in defensive positions.

Like ants, the fishmen climbed the ships like city walls in a surging and unending offensive.

They were headed by a silver level spiky fishman.

He had a plump physique, white gray fish scales where many black spikes naturally grew from.

This gave him an innate secondary armor that could painfully stab any enemies fighting near him.

The fishman was extremely angry and was shouting constantly. As a number of fishmen were climbing, they were struck down and lost their lives. But as one fell, another replaced them, stepping over the corpses of their dead comrades to battle.

As their leader, the spiky fishman fought at the head of his troops and was the first to charge ahead.

“Defend, we must defend!”

“We must persevere until the captain and the others return!!”

“If you don’t want to die, then fight like you don’t want to!!”

The sailors inside the ships were also red with killing intent.

The ones most heavily under attack were the magic ships.

There were many magic ships anchored at Two Eyes Island’s port.

Most of them belonged to the five great merchant alliances, each merchant alliance representative had been provided with a bronze level magic ship. However, these magic ships were mainly used for commercial trade and were focused on loading capacity and speed.

The truly strong bronze level magic ships were the White Bandage and the Iron Lump. These two ships belonged to mercenary corps who depended on their military power to make a living, thus, their combat display far exceeded that of the merchant alliance ships.

The fishmen obviously knew a lot of information.

They focused their offensive the most on the White Bandage and the Iron Lump, with the spiky fishman launching several attacks on the White Bandage.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were also under attack; however, they weren’t the fishmen’s main target.

Perhaps because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only had ordinary ships.

The mercenaries defended the ships, holding a big advantage due to their commanding position.

Because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ armory had large amounts of alchemy crossbows and firearms, they had set up a sharpshooter team a long time ago. Zong Ge’s desire for a sharpshooter team was so strong that he even took out the Arrow Storm Battle Qi technique and Bullet Hail Battle Qi technique for that purpose.

But unfortunately, talents were difficult to find.

Due to a lack of suitable people, the two shooter battle squads were just plans and imagination. There were so few people that daily training requirements couldn’t be fulfilled.

Under these circumstances, the dragonman youngster was forced to make up the numbers with “inferior people”. They weren’t able to form a sharpshooter battle squad, but they were able to have ordinary archery teams.

Of course, Zong Ge didn’t forget to help these people during training.

Now, these people were displaying their formidable battle strength.

Even though they used ordinary arrows and ammunition, many slain fishmen laid sprawled out on their backs.

Often mid-charge, fishmen were shot dead.

While the magic ships had sides filled with climbing fishmen, and occasional fishmen charging onto the deck, the top half of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ hulls were all clean. At most, the fishmen were able to climb halfway before being shot dead.

Upon surveying all of the ships at port, one would find the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in the best shape.

This information from the scouting puppets let Cang Xu relax for the time being.

“The situation is still alright.”

“But I must preside over the situation as soon as possible.”

“The Commander, Zong Ge, Tripleblade, Lan Zao, Zi Di, and I are not with them.”

“The only ones there, such as Mu Ban, Bai Ya, and the others only have meager strength.”

“Without a true leader to preside over the war situation, if the situation goes south, morale will easily collapse, leading to flight and surrender.”

“I wonder how Zi Di is doing over there?”

“If it really is Qing Xin, it would be better if our side had a silver level magician.”

“No, even an iron level is a rare strength that can help.”

Cang Xu’s eyes flashed with expectation.

Inside the master bedroom, there was only Zi Di, and the middle aged women suspected to be Qing Xin.

“Master.” The middle aged woman dutifully came to the room, “You summoned me, where do you want tidied up?”

This was only an excuse.

Zi Di had been looking for a chance to privately speak with “Qing Xin”.

As she gazed at the woman, Zi Di couldn’t help but be somewhat nervous.

For one, she was worried that her hopes would be for naught, that the other person was just a stranger that looked like sister Qing Xin, nothing more. Second, she was also worried about the state of Two Eyes Island.

“The present situation is unknown, and time is short. I must act decisively, the more time I fight for, the more opportunities I will have.”

Zi Di decided to cut through the tangled hemp.

She gazed at the woman before her and directly asked, “Are you Elder Qing Xin?

The woman was flabbergasted and puzzled, but there was no shock to it, it all looked natural.

She cautiously replied, “Mas, master, what are you saying?”

“You must be mistaken.”

“I am called…”

Zi Di interrupted her, “I am not interested in hearing your lies.”

She took out a wisteria secret order.

The wisteria secret order represented her current identity – a level seven elder of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance, a remarkable identity.

When the woman saw this secret order, her expression fluctuated slightly.

Zi Di also knew what she looked like right now.

In their eyes, she was hooded and masked, a mysterious figure that was hard to trust.

She put the secret order into the woman’s hand.

“Elder Qing Xin, you are a magician. I know that even if you don’t have the relevant alchemy device, you can discern the authenticity of this secret order. I purchased them all because of you.”

“If you are Qing Xin, I am your friend, not your enemy.”

The woman forced to take the wisteria secret order was at her wits’ end and frantically shook her head, “Master, I’m really not…”

Zi Di interrupted her again, “You don’t need to say so much.”

“I have more important matters to attend to.”

“You can privately inspect if this secret order is real.”

“I long for your trust, but I cannot tell you my true name right now.”

Zi Di couldn’t expose herself, despite her understanding that identifying herself as Zi Di would surely gain Qing Xin’s trust.

But that was too risky to do.

Things concerning Mystifying Monster Island could not be revealed.

Chapter 370: Don’t Pretend, I am the Strongest Firepower

Zi Di took out some equipment from the Balloon Knapsack.

Among them were the star iron hatchets.

She had acquired them from the auction.

She had a full box of them.

Giving these iron level weapons to these ordinary slaves was extravagant.

But due to time constraints, Zi Di had to do it.

“Next, you need to protect yourself.”

“This place is still safe for the time being.”

“Stay here as long as possible, our slave agreement has already gone into effect. You know how chaotic it is outside, if you don’t want to be killed by fishmen, hide here.”

“Although it is close to the port, if anything happens, I’ll do my best to save you.”

“Upon seeing “Qing Xin” wasn’t admitting who she was, Zi Di could only sigh to herself.

The other person might be her sole “relative”, therefore, Zi Di didn’t take any drastic measures.

Although they didn’t recognize each other, this outcome was within Zi Di’s expectations.

Soon after, Zi Di and Cang Xu left the small villa together.

Zi Di and “Qing Xin” didn’t acknowledge each other, but Cang Xu approved of Zi Di’s caution.

Under the current circumstances, the survivors didn’t need to risk identifying themselves to obtain new cultivators.

Seeing Zi Di and Cang Xu leave, “Qing Xin” couldn’t help but reveal a complex look.

Soon after, the other slaves walked in and asked what her master had summoned her for.

“Qing Xin’s” peculiar look suddenly vanished and she became ordinary again, “The master called me to tidy up the room, he has an urgent matter to attend to.”

The bronze level mother, the mistress of Baron Zai Xiang, asked other questions out of concern.

“Qing Xin” respectfully answered, “Although master and I only exchanged a few words, I can feel that he…is a good natured person.”

Upon obtaining this answer, the blond mistress was soothed and relaxed her tone.

She was outwardly the strongest slave of them all.

But her bronze level strength only affected her sex appeal, she had no battle strength at all.

She moreso lacked any battle courage at all.

As one accustomed to being a canary, she never thought about running away or starting her own family, she only hoped that her new master could be someone she could live under.

Before Zi Di and Cang Xu left, they stealthily crushed a Pearl Bubble that affected the small villa.

For their auction attendance plan, they had prepared Pearl Bubbles in advance.

This facilitated their retreat.

Crushing the Pearl Bubbles was the shallowest was to use them.

But the divine artifact’s power was worth relying on!

With the Pearl Bubbles masking it, outsiders wouldn’t see anything wrong with the small villa.

For her own safety, Zi Di also crushed a second Pearl Bubble for herself.

She was carrying the Balloon Knapsack, and because it was loaded with the survivors’ auction spoils, it had inflated tremendously and was very conspicuous.

With the Pearl Bubble screening it, the Balloon Knapsack was thoroughly concealed from living beings and rendered “invisible”.

Driven by caution, Zi Di and Cang Xu didn’t rush to the port.

They first went to the edge of the seacoast far from the port.

There, Zi Di and Cang Xu separated.

Cang Xu circled back and snuck into the port.

Zi Di with the Balloon Knapsack entered the water and dove towards the seafloor.

The underwater was as tranquil as always.

“It seemed the raiding fishmen were hiding the depths of Two Eyes Island.”

“Could it be that they snuck in during the beast tide?”

“How did they do it? Neither the Deep Sea Monster Fish or Hun Tong detected them.”

Zi Di harbored suspicions as she continued to dive alone, her silhouette gradually covered up by the dark seawater.

The port battlefield.

The spiky fishman leader was beaten back again.

He withdrew from the frontline as he tenaciously glared at the White Bandage, unwilling to give up.

That nut was too hard to crack!

The spiky fishman’s anxious heart reflected outwardly, causing him to become more irritable.

The White Bandage had never fallen despite the chaotic battlefield.

The ship’s bandages danced in the breeze like big white pythons.

Some bandages attacked, twisting around fishmen and strangling them. Other bandages softly wrapped around mercenaries and glowed, healing them.

The spiky fishman suffered defeat after defeat on the front line, and soon after, there was not a single living fishman on board the White Bandage.

Upon seeing this, the spiky fishman growled and charged again.

At this moment, two iron level fishmen held him up.

“Ngamlgmnmalmlanm”

“Lnagnlagnlganlanlg”

The two iron level fishmen pointed at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships, quickly speaking with anxious looks.

The spiky fishman was red with killing intent, yet he sobered up now.

When he heard their proposal, he stared at them, then held his head high and ran his eyes across the battlefield.

Then he noticed that the greatest number of fishmen casualties was not the White Bandage, where the battle was the fiercest, rather it was where the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was.

There, the dead fishmen had piled up into a small hill.

The spiky fishman immediately roared in anger.

Compared to the defensive Iron Lump and White Bandage, all of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were ordinary.

But they had the most crossbows and firearms.

They didn’t have many ordinary cannons.

Previously, the dragonman youngster made a deal with the Flint and Secret Medicine branch managers, along with the two mercenary commanders to obtain many cannons, but that just made up deficiencies, that’s all.

In the port, the ships were crowded together, and if these cannons were fired, they could easily injure allies and damage the port.

Therefore, firearms and crossbows were the long range weapons of choice.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as a result have killed the most fishmen.

If they were left alone to keep shooting, the fishmen would suffer more disastrous casualties.

The spiky fishman was an accomplished military man and instantly realized the situation.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a great threat, in addition their ships were ordinary, and their defenses were far weaker than magic ships.

This also caused the spiky fishman leader to change their target.

At once, he flicked his arm and shouted loudly, leading his subordinates towards the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Be careful, they are coming towards us!”

“Fire arrows, fire arrows now.”

“Use the alchemy arrows and ammunition, target that spiky fishman!”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ members immediately grew tense.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were missing. Without a silver level fighter, they couldn’t stop the spiky fishman leader.

They could still rely on their long ranged weaponry though.

For a moment, all kinds of alchemy arrows and bullets appeared.

Watter arrows, wind needle arrows, smoke arrows, flash arrows, explosion bullets, blood poisoning bullets, and freeze bullets…

A multicolored wave of dazzling firepower interweaved in the air.

The firepower ferociously covered the charging fishmen.

The spiky fishman leader felt fear and trepidation in the face of disaster!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ sudden eruption of firepower really was too fierce.

In just a few short breaths, the fishmen beside him knelt to the ground.

Fishmen corpses were in all kinds of states, some were frozen, some were charred, some had blood necrosis, some were blown to bits, and some were turned to pools of mincemeat.

Even though the spiky fishman leader was silver level, he couldn’t endure it and was forced to cease his charge and pull back to a nearby bunker to take a breather.

He originally believed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a big threat, he didn’t expect that when he actually fought them that’d he greatly underestimate the other side’s battle strength!

His troops only made it halfway there before all of them were slaughtered, their corpses strewing the ground.

He barely resisted the firepower, his battle qi greatly expended, and his body dotted with many new wounds.

Rou Zang had once suffered losses against such firepower.

Let alone a silver level fishman leader.

In the prior battle, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had actually been restraining themselves, worried that if they showed off too much, they’d provoke the main fishman force.

They didn’t expect that despite doing that, they’d still be noticed.

Their outstanding firepower was to blame, and no matter how much they restrained themselves, their military strength would still be conspicuous.

Since the main fishman force was targeting them, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps might as well lay their cards onto the table.

Fine, I won’t pretend.

I am the one with the strongest firepower on this port.

The spiky fishman didn’t think the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would hide themselves so deeply, and for a moment, he had to bunker down and not dare show his head.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ members cheered, their morale surging.

For a moment, many people thought the silver level fishman wasn’t that dreadful.

Of course, their firepower didn’t come without its costs.

Both alchemy arrows and ammunition were consumable goods.

Money was shot out.

When Zi Di saw this, she felt a heartache.

Was it worth using so many munitions here?

The spiky fishman leader wasn’t dead.

Most crucially, they didn’t need to fight the fishmen to the death, that should be Hun Tong’s people’s job.

Even if they annihilate these fishmen, how much would Two Eyes Island reward them?

Taking advantage of this labor, Cang Xu quietly boarded.

When he saw it, he also sighed.

These arms were from War Merchant’s munitions stock.

After the Snake Rat naval battle and Two Eyes Island’s defensive battle, the survivors’ inventory was getting smaller.

This can’t continue, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps shouldn’t resist a calamity in place of another.

The fishmen were attacking this place to attack Two Eyes Island and Hun Tong.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps being attacked from all sides was not in the survivors’ interest.

The relationship between the survivors and Hun Tong was far from being that close.

But it had to be said that mass usage of munitions got instant results on the battlefield.

For the first time, the fishmen’s offensive was seriously bogged down.

Many fishmen were at a loss.

The spiky fishman leader was trapped in an impossible situation.

If he ignored the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the fishmen would suffer terrible losses, but if he went to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, how could he charge them? Even if he could kill off the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the fishmen would also be wiped out too!

At this moment, fishmen reinforcements arrived.

They were led by the Steel Bone Knight fishman, and with lizard steed mounted, he galloped forth boldly.

“Hgmaamnanaggmang”

The Steel Bone Knight fishman howled at the spiky fishman leader.

He was the one who let Men Shi and his dwarfs go, and with the mysterious purple scaled fishman’s aid had smoothly attacked the light cannon turret.

Afterwards, he noticed from his vantage point that the port battlefield situation was becoming deeply worrying.

After destroying the light cannon turret, he let his team over to reinforce.

Chapter 371: The Quick Knife Battle Squad Joins In!

“nmlagmlgalmnal”

The spiky fishman leader hiding in the bunker replied back, he looked resentful and unreconciled.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman laughed uncannily, as if he were happy to see the spiky fishman leader being humiliated.

He first ordered his men to assist other regions, then with a growl, drove his four legged lizard directly at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Because the others feared the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ firepower, he decided to charge in alone!

“Oh no.” The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps felt things were about to go horrible.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman was very fast, and the arrows and bullets peppering him at most rocked his body.

His defenses far exceeded those of the spiky fishman leader!

When the spiky fishman leader saw the Steel Bone Knight fishman was attracting firepower, he too jumped out of his bunker.

The two silver level fishmen converged and charged the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships together.

“Sho, shoot!”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ firepower increased further.

Large amounts of arrows and bullets interweaved in a multicolored curtain that covered a large area.

The spiky fishman was shot all over, his injuries mounting rapidly.

He charged further in, but couldn’t endure for long, and was forced to hunker down to the side again.

But this time, the bunker he chose was unreliable, and the indiscriminate bombardment was quickly whittling it down.

The spiky fishman still had a chance to breathe.

He took out a potion and poured it into his big mouth.

The potion took effect instantly, and his wounds swiftly recovered.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman had superior defense and was speeding up.

He withstood the firepower and simply rushed to just beneath the ships.

Even though the four legged lizard had tough skin and thick flesh, it was covered in blood, for there were many bullets and arrows lodged in it.

Its ferocious nature was stimulated, and with talons on each foot, it launched itself onto the ship and grabbed hold of the hull. As it rapidly ascended, each talon would pierce the hull, allowing it to firmly affix itself to the hull and wasn’t repelled by the casks and other heavy objects the mercenaries were hurling at it.

After a few short seconds, the lizard carrying the Steel Bone Knight fishman busted onto the deck.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman gave an odd laugh, then rampaged on the deck.

The mercenaries on deck were not his match, and they were impaled by his lance or swept away, the sounds of horrifying fractures and falling bodies echoed around.

The mercenaries on deck quickly suffered serious casualties and their formation was thrown into disorder.

When the fishmen saw this, their vigor grew, and their offensive grew stronger.

“Are we not going to support the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?” A mercenary on the White Bandage asked.

The fist mate hesitated, then shook his head fiercely.

“We have to wait for the commander, we must believe in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they are very strong!” The first mate superficially replied.

Helping recklessly was very risky.

It meant that support troops had to leave the White Bandage, fight through the fishmen on the port, climb onto the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ship, and then once they were there, they had to face a silver level fishman knight.

If they helped, they would definitely incur big casualties.

If they caused a desperate situation because they divided their forces, White Bandage could be captured. The first mate didn’t want to undertake that responsibility.

More crucially, they weren’t sure how profitable helping was.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t have a deep relationship.

It was like how Cang Xu didn’t want the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps too much in the limelight, and for them to assume the pressure and losses Hun Tong was supposed to take.

The fishmen were a united people, and despite everyone being in danger, the humans naturally divided themselves into groups by battle strength.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps was thinking the same way as the Bandage Mercenary Corps.

Since the two strongest groups decided not to, the other merchant alliance groups didn’t plan to provide support either.

For a moment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ situation was becoming increasingly treacherous.

On another ship.

“Young clan elder, what should we do?” A dogman asked as he tugged Kuai Tui’s arm.

“They are silver level, and our two leaders were at the auction!”

“They haven’t appeared yet; they just have had an accident.”

The dogmen gathered around Kuai Tui, without a leader, they couldn’t help but think of retreating.

They could only rely on Kuai Tui to come up with a strategy.

Kuai Tui was their young clan elder, their original leader.

The iron level dogman youth’s brows were tightly creased as he gazed at the fishman knight wreaking havoc on the other ship deck.

His expression flashed with hesitation, but it quickly faded out of existence.

“Without Commander Long Fu taking us in, we would still be slaves.”

“Our pestilence was also cured by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“Here, Lord Shi Qi did his best to cultivate us. We established a battle squad; we were treated better than the humans!”

“If we cower now, I will despise myself.”

“We dogmen will not treat our benefactors this way, right?”

As he said that, he turned to look at his clansmen.

Some dogmen lowered their heads in shame, others raised their heads to look at Kuai Tui with admiration.

The dogmen’s morale was boosted by Kuai Tui, and one after the other, they expressed their support.

“Young elder Kuai Tui, you’re right.”

“We can still fight!”

“Commander Long Fu and Auxiliary Commander Shi Qi are strong, they killed Rou Zang, they must be rushing back.”

Kuai Tui laughed, “Just understand that there is only us right now.”

“The other side is a silver level, leave the ordinary people behind.”

“Bronze levels with me!”

He took the lead, and with rope in tow, dashed across the side of the ship.

He leapt between the ships, his hands on the long rope.

The rope swung, carrying Kuai Tui into the air and over to the other ship.

Behind Kuai Tui, dogmen imitated him and used the rope to swing through the air.

This was a boarding tactic of naval combat.

This was one of the things Zong Ge had trained them on.

With the help of the rope, Kuai Tui and a group of his clansmen swung across a few ships and quickly arrived at the one where the fishman knight was wreaking havoc.

While in the air, Kuai Tui decisively relinquished his grip, and from the air, he dove at the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

Behind him, the dogmen also followed closely behind.

At the moment, there weren’t many mercenaries left.

Kuai Tui and the others weren’t moving quietly and the Steel Bone Knight fishman had noticed them.

He tilted his head up and coldly laughed.

Battle skill – Forest of Lances.

A moment later, the fishman knight brandished his 2 meter long lance.

Silver battle qi quickly worked inside him and a large amount of it simultaneously imbued the lance.

The lance thrusted so quickly and violently that it formed afterimages.

Countless afterimages filled everyone’s eyes, forming a forest of lances.

Every lance afterimage accurately targeted every dogman in the air.

This was the fishman knight’s trump card, his strongest battle skill.

He wanted to stab each of these daring dogmen to death and make these weak beastmen have a taste of the bitter result of overestimating one’s capabilities against the strong.

Kuai Tui growled and took out his knife midair to face the lance images.

The other dogmen fearlessly drew their knives too.

A moment later, they used the same battle skill.

It had been verified through training diligently in past battlefields.

Every battle knife and lance image collided head on with the crisp tinkle of blades.

The dogmen fighters held back the lance images and tumbled to the deck, quickly getting back to their feet.

Kuai Tui was in good condition, but the other dogmen were spitting out a little blood.

After all they were just bronze levels, barely able to withstand a silver level.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman and his mount were forced back a step by the force of the clash.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman was greatly affected, he tenaciously glared at the dogmen fighters with astonishment.

He didn’t kill a single one of them!

He was a grand silver level, him using his strongest battle skill and not killing the iron level was already a defeat. Yet now, not a single bronze level was dead either.

“A battle squad!” The thought suddenly appeared in his mind.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman found it hard to believe, “They are actually a battle squad!”

Battle squads were established through ingenious methods that combined many low level cultivators so they could deal with cultivators of a higher level.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ long range firepower was fierce, there were many factors that influenced the power and expenditure of arrows and bullets.

If it was an Arrow Storm or a Bullet Hail battle squad, only 30% of the arrows, bullets, and battle qi would be used to achieve an even better battle result.

A moment before, the Quick Knife battle squad had withstood a silver level battle skill, none of them died because they shared the brunt together as an integrated whole.

Now on the deck, the Quick Knife battle squad immediately surrounded the fishman knight to do battle.

It was like a pack of wolves fighting a fierce tiger.

The fishman knight charged to the left, still bold and powerful.

Kuai Tui led his battle squad like a formless net that entangled the tiger.

Kuai Tui was the main attacker, he was the only iron level, the others were assisting him.

The fishman knight attacked Kuai Tui but couldn’t catch him for the time being.

If he attacked another dogman fighter, the latter would avoid his tip, while the rest attacked in every direction.

They attacked intelligently and were very tough.

The fishman knight felt he was being ambushed from all sides and attacked from all directions. At first, he couldn’t adapt, losing sight of one thing when he had to attend to another, and was struck by Kuai Tui several times.

Luckily, he had the Steel Bone Knight bloodline and had a solid natural exoskeleton.

Kuai Tui seized several good opportunities, but his bronze level battle knife only left scratches on his opponent, no wounds.

Kuai Tui noticed that it was hard to break through the enemy’s defenses, his heart shouting, “Cut down his mount!”

The Quick Knife battle squad shifted their target of attack.

Henceforth, the fishman knight’s mount immediately suffered a calamity.

It had been wounded heavily when it charged onto the deck, and its ferocity had been stimulated already, thus its condition had been constantly tumbling.

After several attacks and blocks, the lizard had many new cuts on it, and its blood covered a wide swath of the deck.

The lizard was not an ordinary beast, it was an iron level magic beast, however, it instantly collapsed under the Quick Knife battle squad’s onslaught.

If this continued, the lizard would die in battle!

The knight wasn’t injured much, but he loved his mount dearly and was thinking of retreating.

I’m not incompetent, it is just that the other side played dirty and attacked the one I’m riding!

Chapter 372: The Quick Knife Squad vs a Silver

The Steel Bone Knight fishman thought of retreating, but that wouldn’t be easy.

He was already surrounded by the Quick Knife battle squad.

Whenever he tried to charge out, the battle squad leader Kuai Tui would bar his path.

Although Kuai Tui was an iron level, with the aid of his comrades in arms, he could stop any breakout attempt of the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman had a strong exoskeleton, natural heavy armor.

It was always advantageous in ocean combat.

Because the ocean didn’t have fire and an environment capable of forging metal.

Of course, the fishmen clan had their own forging techniques. They used them to create obsidian weapons.

Obsidian was a type of ore that was easy to break and process, often used to make sharp blades or spear tips.

Of course, it was easy to break.

In the ocean, the Steel Bone Knight fishman mainly dealt with enemies armed with obsidian weapons and could maintain an invincible position.

At the same time, seawater was buoyant while alleviating his weight.

But on dry land, the Steel Bone Knight fishman felt different.

He felt how heavy he was.

When one had abundant stamina, such a feeling was indistinct. But now, as his condition was declining, every time he brandished his lance, he felt how sore and swollen his arm was.

The mount beneath him felt tighter.

The lizard, who had spent its life in the sea, not only had to adapt to the gravity of dry land on itself, but also the gravity on its master too.

The lizard’s injuries were more severe than the fishman knight.

The Quick Knife battle squad was targeting it!

The iron level magic beast really was bearing too much.

Its condition fell to an all time low, and it was only hanging in by a breath.

When it charged, it had no strength and naturally had no speed either.

Another crucial thing.

The state of the battlefield.

They were battling on a ship deck; how much space was there to move on a ship deck?

There was the masts and the ship cabins, the fishman knight didn’t have enough room to accelerate.

As a heavy armor knight, if the Steel Bone Knight fishman didn’t have speed, he would be in an awkward situation.

The Quick Knife battle squad grew braver!

They had never battled a silver level enemy before.

Ordinarily, even if Zong Ge did battle with them personally, such training wasn’t as good as actual combat.

Real combat concerned victory and defeat, life and death, its stimulation was absolutely real.

“We can resist a silver level cultivator!”

“The other party isn’t just a silver level; they also have an iron level mount.”

“We actually have the strength; we can fight a silver!”

The more the dogmen fighters attacked, the higher their morale rose.
