It was just so; they could really impede the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

The fishman wanted to escape, but he couldn’t.

Upon seeing this, the onlookers were all stupefied.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps actually has a battle squad!”

“So strong, wit just an iron level and some bronze levels, they are able to sequester a silver level heavy armor knight.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ commanders are powerful, they once faced Rou Zang directly! Calculating in this battle squad, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps can be considered a quasi-gold group.”

The onlookers felt astonishment, admiration, and even jealousy.

Ordinary powers didn’t have a battle squad.

The White Bandage and Iron Club Mercenary Corps were veteran mercenary corps, and they didn’t have a battle squad between them.

Rou Zang had plundered the seas for ten years and had assembled the Sword and Shield Thrower Soldiers through arduous effort. When he fought the survivors, this battle squad was slaughtered, naturally making Rou Zang extremely angry.

It wasn’t easy for him to see ten years of accumulation go up in smoke!

Battle qi techniques were easy to gather, but battle squad organization methods were all kept confidential.

Even when they were circulated in the marketplace, they were almost all badly damaged and incomplete.

The main problem with battle squad formation was that it was hard to find talents for it.

Bloodline tightly restrained those that could cultivate the same battle qi technique.

“Did the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps just establish it? How do they have a battle squad?”

“Those are dogmen, beastmen. I heard the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps recruited them on Snake Rat Island.”

“What is this luck?!”

Many mercenaries lamented from the bottom of their heart.

It had to be said that Zong Ge running into and recruiting these dogmen was lucky.

From a different point of view, the survivors having Zong Ge was also lucky.

Neither could do without the other.

“Zong Ge is absolutely an army training master!” Cang Xu also lamented.

Upon seeing the Quick Knife battle squad’s excellent display, he had no choice but to exclaim in admiration.

Despite Cang Xu complaining about and being wary of Zong Ge, those facts didn’t influence his praise and judgement of the latter’s talents.

Even if the Dragon Lion mercenary Corps had battle squad organization methods and the dogmen, without Zong Ge’s training, the Quick Knife battle squad wouldn’t have been like this in so short a time.

Thinking about this carefully, how long had this battle squad existed?

With such battle strength!

It really was surprising.

Cang Xu was once a noble steward and had some scope of vision. Thus, despite not being an army officer, he knew what Zong Ge had achieved was of astounding quality.

More praiseworthy to him was how loyal the Quick Knife battle squad was!

At this crucial time, they welcomed difficulty and didn’t fear their powerful enemy to defend the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, this embodied the dogmen’s loyalty to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This made the necromancer Cang Xu feel warm inside.

“amngalmglmagnmllngm”

The spiky fishman furiously roared as he charged at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps once more.

He felt anxious again.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman’s reinforcements made him think that he’d be able to capture the port.

But good things didn’t last forever, and now the Steel Bone Knight fishman was surrounded.

Perspicacious people could see him failing to break out of the encirclement!

The Quick Knife battle squad’s appearance and battlefield performance caused the spiky fishman’s heart to shake greatly.

The high spirited Steel Bone Knight fishman who had ridiculed him was now in a worse state that he!

One could see that every leg of the lizard had been crippled.

This Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps simply was poisonous!

Kuai Tui’s tactics were wise.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman could no longer go on the offensive and was wearily defending himself.

He didn’t want his beloved mount to die in battle.

But he was unable to break out and his mount was growing increasingly worse, the anxiety of it caused his entire body to sweat.

He was an expert at charging through the battlefield, hit and run attacks. But now, he was fighting a dozen enemies alone, and because he wanted to save his mount’s life, he was using his weaknesses to attack the enemy’s strengths.

He felt strength within him, but the fact that it couldn’t be used against the enemy made him very sullen.

The deck had been cleared already; this was their battlefield alone.

Not only had the mercenaries retreated, but the deck’s masts and forecastle had been reduced to ruins.

The Quick Knife battle squad hadn’t lost a single man!

The more they fought, the smoother they became.

It had to be said that actual combat was the best thing to temper people with.

Past arduous training had established a strong foundation and cultivated a tacit understanding.

Now this tacit understanding had become deeper.

Under the spark of real combat, this strong foundation was faintly trending towards a qualitative change.

This change increased the confidence of many of the battle squad!

Through the baptism of combat, they had stimulated the precious temperament of being elite troops.

“Be careful, the spiky fishman is charging over again.”

“Block them, don’t let him get close!”

Upon seeing the spiky fishman attempting a rescue, the other members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps immediately acted to bar his path.

The Quick Knife battle squad’s fantastic performance boosted the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ morale greatly.

But the discerning people knew that surrounding one silver level was their limit.

If another one got in, they were sure to collapse.

Alchemy arrows and bullets interweaved through the air in a tempestuous show of firepower.

The spiky fishman didn’t hide in a bunker this time.

He had to save the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

Without this powerful battle companion, he would definitely be defeated!

It could be said that the fight between the Quick Knife battle squad and the Steel Bone Knight fishman would decide the result of the port battle.

The spiky fishman chose to fight on with gritted teeth!

The layers of firepower caused onlookers to watch on fearfully in the face of disaster.

The spiky fishman was soaked in blood head to foot yet stubbornly rushed out of the net of firepower.

The potion he took before was still in effect, causing his injuries to quickly heal.

This time, he had to charge!

Retreat was not an option.

The spiky fishman thoroughly displayed his dauntlessness.

In fact, his heart was filled with remorse.

If he had known earlier, he wouldn’t have turned his spearhead towards the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This mercenary corps was poisonous, too poisonous.

He seemed to have weak defense, but as a matter of fact, he was a hedgehog. If one recklessly bit him, heedless of the consequences, those that attacked would surely be pierced by his spikes and choke on their blood.

It was too late for remorse, and with his vicious nature stimulated, the spiky fishman’s charge speed increased somewhat.

“galma!”

He shouted, finally approaching the battlefield where the fishman knight was.

The spiky fishmen suddenly felt the pressure on him suddenly lighten.

At last, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was unable to fire at will.

They were liable to affect allies.

The Quick Knife battle squad were many, compared to the two fishmen, and regardless of whether it was the Steel Bone Knight fishman or the spiky fishman, both had tough skin and thick flesh.

If firepower covered them, the Quick Knife battle squad would be worse off by far.

Only those rare sharpshooters confident in their accuracy could continue to give the Quick Knife battle squad some sporadic assistance.

This couldn’t compare to the flood of firepower earlier.

The spiky fishman laughed out blood as he readied to climb onto the ship.

Just as the Quick Knife battle squad was about to sink into peril, a wall of fire suddenly rose from the ground, blocking the spiky fishman resolutely.

Cang Xu’s figure now appeared on another ship.

“It’s Lao Zhong.”

“Lao Zhong finally acted!”

“If you’re here, Lao Zhong, where is Hong Teng (Zi Di)?”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had two magicians; this information had long been known to everyone.

Under the pseudonym Lao Zhong, Cang Xu used a voice amplification spell to shout, “Persevere, the auction hall has had an unforeseen incident, Feudal Lord Hun Tong and the commanders are trapped inside.”

“But only temporarily.”

“These insignificant fishmen can’t hold some gold levels and won’t hold Lord Long Fu and Shi Qi.”

“Without a gold level cultivator, these fishmen will all die!”

Cang Xu’s words were like a cardiac stimulant, and all of the human fighters’ morale on the port rose.

Chapter 373: Cigar

The spiky fishman was forced to retreat.

The fire wall was surging violently with tempestuous heat.

He tried to go around so he could reinforce the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

But a moment later, a fireball shot at him, forcing him back again.

Cang Xu used his spirit power to lock onto his enemy, the fireball would almost certainly hit.

Unless the spiky fishman took out some counter equipment or special ability, based on his speed and agility, it would be difficult for him to dodge the fireball.

Although the spiky fishman was silver level, he wasn’t at peak condition.

After battling for so long, his condition had fallen gravely.

This was thanks to the White Bandage and the Iron Lump. Both had greatly depleted the spiky fishman’s battle strength.

Actually, even if he was in peak condition, the spiky fishman would have to strain himself to deal with just Cang Xu.

Based on life level, there was only one level between them, nothing more. Especially at the lower levels, the disparity between them wasn’t that obvious.

Besides life level, equipment also influenced battle strength,

Fire path rings were on five of Cang Xu’s fingers.

He also had a magic recovery mask, and a human leather chest storage bag filled with many magic scrolls and readily available magic potions.

With the Magic Medicine Bathtub as its core, the survivors controlled an entire set of magic potion refinement equipment.

On Two Eyes Island, the survivors had gained many alchemy materials and first satisfied the magic potion needs of Cang Xu and Zi Di.

By contrast, the spiky fishman had simpler and cruder equipment.

Besides his passable weapon, he had no decent armor, relying on his hard scales to endure as he charged the ships.

If he were as big as the transformed Rou Zang, and had been covered with more firepower, he might have collapsed in minutes.

“mnfaglamngn”

The fishman knight noticed the spiky fishman and roared at him in between blows.

He wanted him to not run over and instead kill the magician who had appeared!

When the spiky fishman heard this, he resolutely grinded his teeth and shifted his target, instead charging at the ship where Cang Xu was.

Magicians had too many abilities.

Eliminating the magicians on the battlefield first was common sense tactics.

Since the fishman knight said that it proved he didn’t have problems defending himself for the time being.

The spiky fishman locked his eyes onto Cang Xu.

Sensing a silver level’s confrontation from afar naturally put pressure on Cang Xu.

But on the contrary, it relieved him much more.

If the spiky fishman stubbornly assisted the fishman knight, Cang Xu would have a thorny problem on his hands.

Because he couldn’t protect the Quick Knife battle squad.

He was only pretending to be a fire path magician.

His real strength lay with necromancy.

He couldn’t use necromancy, so Cang Xu had to use other types of magic, ones he didn’t have a perfect command of.

If the spiky fishman and the fishman knight converged, he wouldn’t be able to help the Quick Knife battle squad and would be forced to attack another target and wipe out the other fishmen.

By doing this, he would force the silver level fishman fighter to come to him.

If that happened, the Quick Knife battle squad would suffer an unknown number of casualties.

Perceptive people can make out that the Quick Knife battle squad pinning down the fishman knight was already at their limits.

One couldn’t demand too much of them; they were already doing good enough!

He couldn’t be exposed as a necromancer.

If he used necromancy, even if he repelled the silver level fishman fighter and protected the Quick Knife battle squad, the gains wouldn’t make up for the losses.

Because it would provoke far more trouble.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would discover that Lao Zhong was a necromancer, which would cause morale to slump, wisdom to dissolve, and battle intent to wither away.

The fishmen would more firmly believe they were the righteous side.

As the feudal lord, Hun Tong would absolutely attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps when he broke out of the auction hall.

Even if these were resolved, the empire would dispatch a continuous stream of cultivators to kill this exposed necromancer.

The spiky fishman quickly approached Cang Xu’s ship.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were anchored together and weren’t far from each other, it was hard for firepower to provide cover.

Cang Xu used magic scrolls to fly into the air, then landed on an adjacent ship’s mast.

During this, he didn’t forget to attack the spiky fishman with fireballs.

The spiky fishman pursued closely behind.

Cang Xu constantly moved, maintaining distance to stall for time as much as possible.

He was an iron level magician; it was quite hard for him to kill a silver level. Now that the spiky fishman was watching him as the primary target, he didn’t have enough time to prepare more powerful magic spells.

And so, the spiky fishman pursued Cang Xu while the Quick Knife battle squad encircled the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

The port battlefield was deadlocked in these two critical war situations.

“Next, we need to wait for the commanders to return to help in time.” Cang Xu sighed to himself.

He also had some trump cards.

Such as the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

But such a trump card could not easily be revealed.

In the best case scenario, the auction hall trap would be destroyed, allowing the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge to aid in time.

Luckily, the port, the light cannon turrets, and other such places were strategic locations on Two Eyes Island that had to be defended. If Hun Tong and the others regained their freedom and joined the battlefield, they would prioritize helping this place.

Meanwhile.

Two Eyes Island’s auction hall.

Hun Tong didn’t know what was going on outside, and as time dragged on, his mood got more irritable.

But outwardly, he still remained calm.

“Group three rest.”

“Group one, prepare to act, group two supervise.”

Hun Tong had divided everyone into three groups, one would attack, one would rest, and one would inspect.

Although this measure was very crude, it was effective.

The cultivators trapped in the auction hall weren’t disorderly as a result and instead were trying to break out in an orderly fashion.

Such a spectacle was rarely seen.

Pirates, mercenaries, merchants, feudal lord forces, along with other powers had gathered to fight together.

Of course, there might be a traitor among them!

As a frogman and the Applause Pirates’ commander, Hu li suffered the most suspicions.

Most people were glancing at him and his underlings.

“If I had known about this earlier, I wouldn’t have participated in this auction!” Hu Li felt very regretful.

He wasn’t able to buy anything he wanted; it had all been taken by room 21.

Now, he was trapped in this auction, the myriad suspicious made him feel like he was sitting on pins and needles, he was very worried that he would be executed for being the traitor.

The auction hall was small, and most present were humans, so he couldn’t even run away even if wanted to.

“Those abominable people in room 21!” Hu Li clicked his teeth.

Those in room 21 were not present in the auction hall!

They had left for the backstage ahead of time to collect their slaves, and as a result, had avoided the coming trap.

That was too much of a coincidence!

“They are the traitors. If they aren’t, then who else could it be?” Hu Li felt very remorseful, “If they were here, things would be so much better!”

His opinion was quite universal.

Most people here suspected Zi Di and Cang Xu, the people in room 21.

Of course, the dwarf Men Shi had also left early.

But he was far less suspect than Zi Di and Cang Xu.

Room 21 had been too much in the public eye and had incited much jealousy.

Hun Tong’ was in the first group.

As feudal lord, he had to set an example.

With a hand holding a gold level dagger, he used a battle skill in passing.

With gold light shooting out, he instantly dug a hole into the depths of the wall.

The gold light had extraordinary penetration, but it didn’t make it through the auction hall wall.

From the moment the trap was sprung, the auction hall was constantly getting thicker.

The coral ball cage tree was a gold level magic plant, its vines and branches, along with the Suffering Endurance Barrier made it hard for even Hun Tong to pierce.

Soon after, the first team withdrew, and the second team acted.

This wave of people had two gold level fighters.

One was Song Shou, who had supported Hun Tong, while the other was the Ten Touch Pirates’ commander Qi You.

Both used their respective battle skills.

Song Shou smacked the wall with both palms.

With a fierce rumble, cracks formed across the wall, as if it had been covered in spider wells in an instant.

Soon after, a chunk of wall fell off, exposing a brand new layer.

Qi You then extended his fingers and pointed at the wall from a distance.

The gold level battle skill condensed a helix slipstream.

The slipstream looked like a drill bit that dug into the wall, small at the front, and large at the back.

A large hole was dug out of the wall, but after a few seconds, the hole was grown over and filled in by about half.

Qi You stopped there.

He obviously retained energy to maintain his guard against the people around him.

Hun Tong was expressionless.

Despite saying whoever held back would be a suspected traitor, since it was Qi You, he would handle it prudently.

Qi You was a gold level.

He was a pirate commander, and an opposing faction.

If both sides had a fallout at this crucial time, Hun Tong wouldn’t gain any benefits.

Hun Tong chose to restrain himself.

Thinking about it positively, Qi You acting was already good enough.

The second group withdrew, and the third group went on stage.

This group also had a gold level fighter.

He had been the middle aged smoker in room 2.

Even in the hall, he still had a cigar in his mouth.

This cigar was unusually thick and the point of the cigar still maintained a small flame.

The man was at the forefront and took a deep breath.

Following his breath, the cigar’s small flame expanded several folds and burned intensely.

The cigar’s length was rapidly shortened by the flame.

The man subsequently straightened his chest.

Battle skill – Tobacco Ash!

A moment later, two puffs of smoke jetted from the man’s nostrils.

The smoke appeared ashen black and contained some gold within.

Like two pythons, the smoke puffs pounded at the walls, one to the left and the other on the right.

The smoke permeated the walls in a quiet manner.

The man quickly finished smoking.

The walls looked like burnt charcoal and had turned completely black.

Soon after, the black walls looked like ash and dust, then a large area of it crumbled to the ground in a cloud of black ash.

Those that saw this were no longer surprised.

The mad had done this attack many times previously.

His identity had also been revealed during communications and discussions with everyone.

The man was called Yan Ding, a well known mercenary.

He had the Black Tobacco bloodline, and in conjunction with the Fireworks battle qi technique and matching battle skills, he was a very strong combatant.

His battle style was quite remarkable, and his battle skills were often large scale and imposingly huge.

Chapter 374: Vinegar Jar Pirates

“That Yan Ding was in room 2, he should have a decent relationship with Hun Tong.”

“The auction hall has four golds, is one of them the traitor?”

The dragonman youngster pondered while working.

He used Demolition Fist to smash shallow holes into the wall.

His battle result naturally couldn’t compare to the four golds but was one of the very best among the silvers.

Zong Ge’s wall breaking was also within the top ten.

Most of all, his wisp of gold tint within his silver battle qi attracted much attention.

This was indeed a sign of his near promotion to the gold level.

Although it was faint, it indicated that it was possible!

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will be a gold level power in the future.”

“It’s no wonder they were able to defeat Rou Zang!”

“If Shi Qi becomes a gold level. While Commander Long Fu is still stuck at the silver level, hehe, that will be interesting.”

The cultivators had their own thoughts.

Team three rested, and it was team one’s turn.

Hun Tong didn’t forget to inspire morale. “Continue!”

“Attack like this.”

“We are about to break through and regain our freedom!”

He was absolutely right.

Previously, the auction hall’s walls, floor, and ceiling had been growing wild, and constantly compressing space.

But when the cultivators struck back, the space was no longer decreasing and was now expanding instead.

The coral ball cage tree’s growth rate couldn’t compare to how fast it was being destroyed.

Not only that, but everyone could also distinctly sense that the Suffering Endurance Barrier was weakening.

It was because of this that the cultivators didn’t object to Hun Tong’s leadership.

All of them wanted to regain their freedom.

In the deepest depths of the auction hall, there was an immense cavity.

A dozen fishman casters were sitting cross legged, casting in unison.

They had blue scales.

The Blue Scale Fishman bloodline.

The blued scaled fishmen had a chance of becoming casters.

They were commonly referred to as blue scaled fishman sages.

Their limits of their gifts were quite low, the strongest of them would only be at the bronze level.

However, these fishmen were always treasured.

The aptitude of fishmen could not compare to that of humans, dwarfs, elves, or other races.

The Blue Green Coral Clan had two big bloodlines. One was the Blue Scale Fishman bloodline and the other was the Green Gill bloodline.

It could even be said that for the sake of retaking their homeland, the Blue Scale Coral Fishman clan had hollowed out their foundation to attack Two Eyes Island.

The group of blue scaled fishmen in this underground cavity consisted of nearly all the clan possessed.

The blue scaled fishmen sitting down were using all of their strength to support the auction hall trap.

Their chests or backs were all inserted into a coral ball cage tree branch.

Their magic power imbued the branches and with every breath, the branches glistened with a faint radiance.

It was precisely through doing this that their auras could be linked together and mixed into the coral ball cage tree, which concealed them somewhat.

A purple scaled fishman came in through a secret passage.

She had been at the light cannon turret earlier, and had slain the guards there, allowing the Steel Bone Knight fishman to easily conquer it.

Now, she was here.

When the blue scaled fishmen saw the purple scaled fishman, they stirred.

The purple scaled fishman’s identity and bloodline was far higher than theirs. She was not from the Blue Green Coral clan, rather she had come from the fishmen’s Holy City.

The purple scaled fishman asked about the auction hall.

The blue scaled fishman leader answered her regretfully, “Hun Tong stepped in, and with Song Shou working with him, they controlled the situation without delay. The auction hall didn’t turn to chaos, and our people inside have no way to act.”

As expected, the fishmen hid spies in the auction hall.

The purple scaled fishman nodded and asked the most crucial question, “How long will it last?”

The blue scaled fishman blurted out, “Looking at it currently, it can last 15 minutes.”

“15 minutes..” The purple scaled fishman whispered to herself.

This wasn’t long enough for her to attain her goal.

However, computing in the time they had already stalled, the blue scaled fishmen sages had done their best.

The purple scaled fishman couldn’t ask them to do more.

Because that would require the blue scaled fishmen sages to forfeit their lives.

They were using the Endurance spell.

The coral branches were inserted into their hearts, drawing not only their magic power, but also their vitality.

It was precisely because of this great price paid and with the help of the Suffering Endurance Barrier that the coral ball cage tree could trap Hun Tong and the others.

“My Lord, how are things outside?” The blue scaled fishman leader hesitated a bit before asking the deepest question in his heart.

The purple scaled fishman replied, “The light cannon turret and the grain depot have been captured, the city defense barracks is on the verge of collapse, and the port is in a deadlock.”

“The situation…isn’t good, isn’t bad.” The blue scaled fishman sage sighed.

“Our home has been occupied by human bandits for too long.”

“To be able to accomplish this, we had to take advantage of the new island’s ascent, the period of rebuilding where the city defenses were the weakest.”

“Before, it was thanks to your Lordship’s subordinate’s guidance that the beast tide attacked the island.”

“Otherwise, things would be more challenging.”

The purple scaled fishman looked at the destined brave sacrifices and inevitably revealed a merciful glint, “No need to thank me. We are all fishmen, we are all believers in our God.”

Sensing this pity, the blue scaled fishman sage laughed, “My Lord, please don’t be sad.”

“We are just a dozen bronzes.”

“To trap Hun Tong and his gang was worth more than the cost.”

“Maybe that is the charm of magic, it is like a crowbar, and as long as conditions are met, it can leverage even stronger power.”

“This is our home, since the day were driven out, our entire clan has awaited the time we could win it back!”

The blue scaled leader growled, “Everything is for the clan!”

“Everyone is for my God!”

The blue scaled fishman sage resonated with the other fishmen.

They shouted together, “Everything is for the clan!”

“Everyone is for my God!”

The port.

The spiky fishman bellowed, causing his spikes to suddenly jet out.

Mercenaries all around him suddenly toppled over.

Hulls and ship cabins were peppered with holes.

When the spiky fishman finished shooting his spikes, his aura clearly became dispirited.

He complacently looked into the air.

Cang Xu was tottering.

That attack had injured him.

His body had at least three wounds, causing blood to flow incessantly.

“mlgnalgnlag”

The spiky fishman laughed nastily.

His tactics had prevailed.

His pursuit of Cang Xu had seemed he was winning, but in actuality, Cang Xu had been leading him by the nose.

The spiky fishman knew this couldn’t go on, thus undaunted by the dangers, he took the spell attack to pull himself closer to Cang Xu, then sprayed his body’s spikes at him.

Silver level battle qi couldn’t leave a fighter’s body.

But with the Spiky Fishman bloodline, he had a racial magic which was Spike Spray.

Cang Xu became distinctly pale.

Although he couldn’t comprehend what the fishman was saying, he could roughly guess what their intention was.

“I’ll let you be proud of yourself for a bit.” Cang Xu said to himself.

He had wounded himself on purpose.

He had given the spiky fishman some hope. During the previous fight, the latter had shown he was intent on assisting the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

Thus, Cang Xu had to make himself a more “attractive target” to the spiky fishman.

As a matter of fact, his body had been transformed into a living dead person.

This sort of injury was harmless to him.

As for the flowing blood, that was of course from Deceptive Disguise.

The spiky fishman strode forward, slammed onto a deck, and charged at Cang Xu once again.

Cang Xu “strained” to raise a finger to use his magic.

Ring of Fire defense spell.

A ring of flames instantly condensed around him, then spread outwards.

The flames not only produced heat, but also a tremendous incorporeal force.

The force smashed into the spiky fishman, throwing them back from whence they came.

The spiky fishman wailed in anger.

Cang Xu seized the opportunity to fly to another ship.

Watching from a tall mast, he immediately took out a magic potion from his chest storage pouch and drank it down.

This was not fake.

Since he was fighting a silver level fighter and because he was casting frequently, his magic power consumption was massive and his spirit was also constantly decreasing.

The magic power potion could speed up his magic power recovery.

This was not the first time he had taken a potion during this fight.

Because he had consumed many consecutively in a short period, the medicinal effect had weakened.

There were many ways of quickly replenishing magic power.

At present, the best way was using magic power crystals.

Magic power crystals were made from a caster’s own magic power and refined through some alchemy technology.

Thus, it was the same as extracting one’s own internal magic power and sealing it in the outside world.

If one wanted to use it, they would crush it.

Because it was their own magic power, and so long as during the storage period they hadn’t changed their magic power that much, it wouldn’t be dangerous to mix this magic power into them.

Both were from the same source, and both could harmonize in a flash.

It was both quick and good for the casters to use.

Cang Xu naturally knew how to do this, and his storage pouch also had magic power crystals stocked up.

But he couldn’t take them out.

Necromantic magic power had distinct qualities, and if he took one out in public, it would provoke suspicion and expose that he was secretly a necromancer.

If Pearl Bubbles were used on the magic power crystals, then of course they could fool people.

But doing this was too extravagant.

The Pearl Bubbles were very precious and had to be used wisely!

Cang Xu drank the potion, causing his magic power to slowly rise.

The spiky fishman charged over again.

The fierce fight between them continued.

“Look there, there are ships on the sea!”

A group of people suddenly appeared, instantly attracting the attention of both sides fighting on the port.

The fleet was obviously heading straight towards the port, and in the wake of them getting closer, the fleet’s pirate flag and flagship reflected in the people’s eyes.

The fishmen immediately cheered.

While the humans sank into depression due to the increased pressure.

The pirate ships cut through the waves.

The pirate flag fluttering in the wind portrayed a single black jar, the jar was filled with black water, and inside the water was a rippling white inverted skull.

It was the Vinegar Jar Pirates!

Chapter 375: Whirlpool Trap

The Vinegar Jar Pirates.

A gold level power and formed almost entirely of fishmen pirates.

The imperial spy’s investigation had determined countless links between it and the Blue Green Coral Fishman clan.

The imperial secret spy Shadow and Hun Tong had worked together to plant secret agents into this pirate group to acquire a lot of precious intelligence.

Earlier, Xi Dai Zi had snuck into Two Eyes Island to plant an insider for when they attacked Two Eyes Island. This resulted in Xu Ma accidentally exposing him and triggering a series of events.

When the fishmen attacked Two Eyes Island, the Vinegar Jar Pirate would undoubtably be one of the main forces present.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates had 24 ships in total.

The other ships looked normal, only the flagship looked quite peculiar.

It didn’t have a bow or an aft, the entire ship was shaped like a large water jar.

The hull looked black, and it didn’t have any sails.

Its motive force came from a small hole in the bottom of the water jar, it was constantly sucking in seawater and spraying it out.

The force of this stream of water drove the Vinegar Jar forward.

The Vinegar Jar had three decks in total.

The top deck was steeped in water, and if an adult human stood on it, the water would reach to their knees.

The middle and bottom deck were completely submerged, and no normal human, dwarf, or even elf would be able to freely move there.

The Vinegar Jar was a magic ship specially built for aquatic races.

Fishmen could freely breathe underwater and could soak themselves in the water, maintaining their skin’s moisture.

Its hold was always suitable for fishmen.

Not only that, but each deck had a different liquid, quite a mystery how.

Although it didn’t have sails, it still had a mast.

Or rather, it would be more accurate to say it was a long pillar.

The pillar was not wood, but coral.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates’ commander Er Gua Zi was standing on top of the pillar, holding a telescope as he gazed at the port battlefield in the distance.

There was a fishman shaman standing next to him, it was the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ first mate – the fishman shaman Xi Dai Zi.

The Quick Knife battle squad appearing surprised Er Gua Zi.

But soon after, he coldly snorted and used the fishman language to evaluate, “Truly two pieces of rubbish. Their opponents are an iron level magician and a small battle squad.”

“They are taking so long to win.”

“Especially that idiot Peng Ding Zi, he can’t discern through the magician’s pretense that he’s playing with him.”

“How can that idiot still believe he can join my pirate group?”

The one he called Peng Ding Zi was the spiky fishman leader that was fighting Cang Xu.

First mate Xi Dai Zi laughed, “Peng Ding Zi has always admired you captain.”

“This blockhead really needs instruction.”

“But the most important thing now is capturing the port.”

Er Gua Zi nodded.

The port was very important.

It had to be captured!

As the attacker, if they seized the port, the fishmen would be able to hold the initiative entirely. No matter how many humans there were on the island, they would become turtles in a jar that could be slowly processed.

Even if Hun Tong and the others broke out of the auction hall, as long as the fishman had ships, they could easily retreat. They would leave any human ships behind. When that happened, even if the humans wanted to pursue, they could only gaze at the ocean and lament their inadequacies.

“Charge!” Er Gua Zi ordered.

All of the pirates went full speed ahead.

The Vinegar Jar took the lead

Upon seeing this, countless humans on the port battlefield grew uneasy and fretful.

“The enemy’s reinforcements have arrived, where are our commanders?”

“The Vinegar Jar Pirates! Their commander is a gold level fishman warrior.”

“We’re done for, we need to retreat inland.”

“Out of the question, it’ll be worse if we lose our ships, we won’t be able to leave Two Eyes Island!”

Cang Xu’s heart sank, he had to deal with Peng Ding Zi while quickly thinking of a countermeasure.

He didn’t think that the first gold level reinforcements would not be from their side, but from the enemy.

The situation had become untenable.

It would take less than ten minutes for the Vinegar Jar Pirates to reach the port.

What should he do?

“If there is nothing we can do, then we must retreat.”

“Safeguard the lives of Bai Ya, Mu Ban, and the rest.”

“The others can be abandoned.”

Cang Xu swiftly made his choice, his eyes filled with cold indifference.

He was not Zong Ge, he didn’t feel anything for those trained troops. He didn’t have the young commander’s righteous duty either.

He was a necromancer; he treated lifeforms as research materials and the other mercenaries as mere numbers.

“We established the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps because we needed a public identity.”

“If we lose our current outer members, we can still raise a new army later.”

“This outer identity won’t be lost!”

The seafloor.

Zi Di was in the cockpit, controlling the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The latter was indeed a gold level magic ship, and if it needed to fight, it could deploy gold level cannons, the Electric Lock alchemy formation, or the Hedgehog Firepower Countermeasures, all of these could fix the tone with a single blow, they could decimate all of the fishmen on the port or sink the Vinegar Jar Pirates together.

“But that isn’t required.”

“This is Two Eyes Island; it is not our base.”

“Using it to help Hun Tong will only gain his appreciation for a time, but in the long run, it will lead to fear and spying.”

“The risk of exposure will greatly increase.”

The auction gains all being stored in the hold put Zi Di much more at ease.

“All of the ships are ordinary vessels, and even if they all sink, we can bear this loss.”

“But those ships have many materials, and if they are lost, it would be quite a pity.”

“The most important thing now is helping the commander and Zong Ge break out, then safely bringing back Mu Ban and the others.”

“Perhaps I can use one of the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s forms.”

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had a mimicry hull and could transform into three forms.

Its usual shape looked like a giant magic beast fish.

The sail fish form: where the hull lengthened and the fishtail turned into a propellor. The propellor would rotate rapidly and form a spiral stream behind it. That form increased its underwater speed to 35 knots.

The third form was a fishman form.

The hull would lengthen into feet, and a section of the keel would be dismantled and turn into a harpoon, its main weapon.

After the survivors took control of the Deep Sea Monster Fish, they had tested each of the three forms.

The sailfish form was specialized in pursuit speed, the normal form was balanced in all functions, and the fishman form was the combat oriented form.

After Zi Di pondered for a bit, and if things didn’t work out, she planned on coming ashore in the third form to fight.

During this allowed the Deep Sea Monster Fish to disguise itself better than just using it as it was now.

Just as Zi Di was staying vigilant, the Vinegar Jar’s speed abruptly dropped.

“What’s going on?”

“A whirlpool!”

“A whirlpool suddenly appeared under the ship; it is sucking us in.”

The fishmen used their native language to loudly communicate with each other.

The Vinegar Jar was sinking into a whirlpool.

The fishmen quickly responded.

The Vinegar Jar sprayed a large stream of water, causing its motive force to rise dramatically, barely pushing the Vinegar Jar out of the whirlpool.

“This is no normal water flow; I sense the aura of magic!” Xi Dai Zi wrinkled his brows.

“Have the ships behind stay back, we’ll blow it up.” Er Gua Zi ordered.

The Vinegar Jar’s cannon muzzle was where the water had been spraying out of, it was a hole at the lowest part of the ship’s hull.

The artillery shells used also had some distinguishing features.

They were not normal alchemy artillery shells, rather they were compressed liquid, temporarily made liquid artillery shells.

Bang, thump, bang.

Three dim yellow balls of liquid hit the whirlpool with an explosion.

Space violently oscillated.

The whirlpool was completely destroyed.

The fishmen pirates dove down the check it and gained nothing.

“No alchemy equipment detected. A bit troublesome, this should be called the Tugboat Whirlpool magic trap.” Xi Dai Zi analyzed.

He looked at the ocean surface ahead of him, “Hun Tong unexpectedly deployed these magic traps near Two Eyes Island.”

“We weren’t able to detect any of them.”

“These magic traps can be artificially controlled, able to both be activated and shut down.”

“During the auction, these traps were shut down, therefore allowing ships to sail in normally.”

Xi Dai Zi’s complexion became unsightly.

The ocean surface before him looked like a wide expanse, but in reality, it concealed many traps.

Besides the Tugboat Whirlpool trap, what other magic traps were there?

If there were explosion traps or naval mines, what would they do?

Er Gua Zi coldly snorted and resolutely ordered, “Continue charging!”

“The Vinegar Jar will clear the way.”

“All other ships line up behind and follow in our wake!”

Er Gua Zi’s boldness steadied Xi Dai Zi’s mind.

Battlefield opportunities were transient.

At this time, they had to charge Two Eyes Island and capture the port, regardless of the consequences.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates changed formation and charged again.

Their journey was not serene.

Not only did they encounter whirlpool traps, but they also ran into water columns, freezing traps, and more.

At this time, the Vinegar Jar embodied its function.

It challenged the law, and was the first to trigger the traps, then rigidly charged through.

Thanks to the Vinegar Jar’s protection, the other ships weren’t harmed in the slightest.

But because of the traps, the Vinegar Jar Pirates had to slow their advance.

Seeing that the gold level pirate group was getting closer, the humans on the port had no choice but to respond.

“Withdraw now!”

“We cannot let the Vinegar Jar Pirates lock down the port.”

“As long as we leave the port, we can still run and fight on the wide sea!”

The first to leave were the magic ships belonging to the five merchant alliances.

Next was the Iron Lump and the White Bandage.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to set sail and leave port, however, due to the fishmen obstructing them with all of their strength, they weren’t able to.

They had become the fishmen’s chief target.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman and the spiky fishman wouldn’t let them evacuate easily.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were all trapped on the port.

The ships that left port also suffered heavy losses.

The first unlucky spirit was the Flint Merchant Alliance’s magic ship; it had triggered a water column trap.

A column of water slammed into the ship from beneath it with great force.

The Flint Merchant Alliance magic ship was nearly capsized by the water column.

Following it, other ships in turn fell into traps.

Magic ships could withstand them, but ordinary ships met a miserable end.

Several ships lost speed, some were mired by whirlpools, spinning in place. Others had holes blown into them and slowly sank.

Chapter 376: Er Gua Zi

“What happened?”

“Those must be magic traps Hun Tong had deployed.”

“I know. But why is he blowing us up?”

“Damn you Hun Tong!”

Some on the ships encountering these beatings reprimanded severely, while far more were hard pressed.

On the seafloor, Zi Di was watching and thought, “These are just magic traps, only able to activate and shut down, there is no foundation to them.”

“If they could be freely turned on and off, there would be a central hub. The construction cost of one is quite high and the large scale of it would cause it to be more easily exposed.”

“After all this is the ocean, not an underground city.”

Zi Di was a magician and a merchant. She had cultivated both paths at the same time and could determine some things.

Therefore, she understood Hun Tong’s arrangements.

The ships charging out were trapped and in an awkward situation.

Ahead of them were numerous hidden magic traps.

Behind them was the bitter port battlefield.

Continuing to forge ahead would trigger the magic traps.

If they returned, they would need to keep fighting. The crux was that if the port was blocked, the consequences would be disastrous.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates didn’t need to storm the port, they only needed to open fire to cause terrible losses to the humans.

Because these ships were anchored, they were all sitting targets and due to their close proximity to each other, none of them could move out of the way.

As the humans were hesitating, the Vinegar Jar Pirates attacked.

Both sides entered shelling range.

Compressed water shells shot out in succession.

They shot straight through the seawater.

The water around them swelled into knolls as they quickly approached the human ships.

The human ships were unable to intercept such artillery shells.

One after the other, the knolls struck the ships, meaning the shells underwater had hit their target dead center.

For a moment, explosions rang out continuously.

The humans sank into chaos.

Large holes split open the bottoms of their ships, and as seawater bubbled in, there was nothing they could do to stem the flow.

Not only were shells hitting from underwater, but there were also things hitting from the air.

Xi Dai Zi controlled an alchemy device, and the water that soaked the decks converged into two streams that flew into the air like large pythons.

The water pythons exploded in the air, changing into a shower of acid rain.

The acid rain covered the human ships completely.

In a split second, a strong vinegar smell assailed the nostrils and formed a strong acidic mist.

Some of the human sailors inside the acidic mist were stricken with malaria, and others spat out teeth, the teeth had been corroded by the acid.

The weaker sailors collapsed to the deck, and after violently cramping for a spell, they went limp.

Death by acid.

If it was the port, the Vinegar Jar Pirates would have some misgivings, after all there were many fishmen compatriots still on it.

But on the sea, there was only human ships, and so they could release all of their tricks to bombard a large area.

The Vinegar Jar displayed its ferocious firepower!

The human ships suffered heavy losses and were stunned for a short while.

Although they had many ships and people, they had no leader, they were a rabble.

Under this bombardment, some ships chose to fire back, some chose to flee in another direction, and others simply returned to where they’d come from, rejoining the port battle.

These many choices made the situation chaotic.

Some ships turned around, some ships put away their sails and prepared to shell, this led to interference with each other and even collisions.

Er Gua Zi’s eyes flashed with a biting cold.

He was commanding the flagship and took the lead to charge into the human ships.

The long serpentine line of Vinegar Jar Pirates, with the Vinegar Jar at the forefront, destroyed all in their way.

Its hull was like a sharp knife that easily cut through the human ships like “cake”.

“Open fire!”

After cutting through the enemy fleet, the Vinegar Jar Pirates did broadsides, none lacking the booming of artillery.

The human ships struck burst into flames, wood shavings and blocks flew, and humans screamed, with some of them being blown into the air, arms or thighs snapped off and fell into the deck or sea.

Some had even worse luck.

Ammunition stores were detonated, creating an even bigger explosion that instantly destroyed a ship’s movement and battle strength leaving only scant survivors to abandon ship as the ship swiftly and violently sunk, their bow or aft still raging with flames.

The Iron Lump and the White Bandage chose to keep evacuating.

The various hidden magic traps were a huge threat to ordinary ships, but these magic ships were sturdier.

They imitated their enemy.

The Vinegar Jar could wade into the traps, so the Iron Lump and White Bandage decided to try too.

They wanted to escape, but Er Gua Zi would never let them go.

Whether it was the Iron Lump or the White Bandage, both were key targets in the fishmen’s intelligence.

To the fishmen, leaving these two magic ships to their own devices would make them a considerable menace to them.

“If we let them go now, it’ll be ten times more troublesome to deal with them in the future!” Er Gua Zi understood things clearly.

Plop.

He jumped into the sea.

He didn’t have any flying battle skills, but he was a fishman and could swim. Not only that, but he was a stronger combatant in the water.

Er Gua Zi swam at lightning speed and slammed into the bottom of the White Bandage.

The White Bandage shook tremendously, and everyone on board turned pale.

A wave of water rolled up like a countercurrent waterfall and washed down onto the deck, clearing a large area of it.

The wave waned, leaving the gold level fishman fighter Er Gua Zi standing proudly on the deck.

The human fighters trembled in fear as they surrounded him, but no one dared to attack him.

Er Gua Zi coldly snorted, killing intent boiled up in his heart, and he launched his massacre.

None was his match!

White bandages wrapped around him like pythons.

Er Gua Zi slapped many bandages back with his palm, his gold level battle qi oscillating through the air.

A moment later, many more bandages attacked from all directions.

Er Gua Zi didn’t dodge or duck, he let the bandages wrap around his legs, arms, waist, and back.

In an instant, he was trussed up.

Battle skill – Scale Severing Blades!

All of his fish scales glistened with gold luster.

Soon after, battle qi formed into fish scales large and small that fluttered around him in untold numbers.

The white bandages were cut clean by the small blades.

Without the binds, Er Gua Zi continued his slaughter.

The strongest mercenary left on the White Bandage was at best iron level, they were never Er Gua Zi’s match.

Very quickly, all of the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ experts were slain by Er Gua Zi’s hand.

More fishmen swam over and helped Er Gua Zi take over the White Bandage.

Er Gua Zi, covered in watery blood, jumped back into the sea to pursue the Iron Lump.

When the Iron Club mercenaries saw this, all of them opened fire with numb scalps to stop Er Gua Zi’s approach.

Artillery shells smashed into the ocean, creating layers of waves and jets of water.

This caused Er Gua Zi to dive a bit deeper.

He quickly climbed onto the Iron Lump and was just about to carry out another massacre when he heard a gigantic rumble from the island.

Er Gua Zi changed color.

He knew the auction hall trap had been broken, Hun Tong and the others had regained their freedom!

And the port battle hadn’t subsided yet.

Er Gua Zi’s goal of controlling the port hadn’t been attained yet.

It had to be said that the magic traps delaying them granted this opportunity. The Iron Lump, White Bandage, and the other ships had used their lives to slow down the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ assault.

Now, Er Gua Zi’s best choice was to accelerate his slaughter efficiency and after dealing with the Iron Lump, continue charging the port, rushing to establish the local war situation before Hun Tong and the others could.

What made him grateful was that although he was on the Iron Lump, his first mate Xi Dai Zi was still leading the fleet towards the port.

Based on distance, they would be the first to arrive.

“Finally escaped.”

“Something has happened, it’s the fishmen!

“Those wretched people, they dare to attack imperial territory.”

The liberated cultivators were swearing in high spirits.

Hun Tong immediately flew high into the air.

The others soon gazed into the distance.

As Hun Tong looked down on the island, his complexion sank.

Numerous cultivators all looked towards the port as if by prior agreement.

“The light cannon turret and many other places have fallen into enemy hands.”

“The port is a strategic location; it cannot be lost too.”

“My fleet!”

Some cultivators were shouting, others were howling in grief, and one by one, all of them murderously gazed towards the port.

The Vinegar Jar looked like a rude and unreasonable magic beast, not decreasing its speed as it crashed into the port.

The anchored ships near it were smashed.

After crashing through five ships, the Vinegar Jar hit dry land and grinded many coastal buildings to slag.

Magic had finished brewing already.

Standing on the coral pillar, Xi Dai Zi aimed a finger at Cang Xu in the distance.

An icy ray shot out at razor sharp speed.

Cang Xu spun in the air and attempted to evade.

But the icy ray was under Xi Dai Zi’s control; he quickly changed its trajectory and easily hit Cang Xu.

Cang Xu was slowed greatly by this magic.

The spiky fishman howled with laughter, approached Cang Xu, and smashed Cang Xu’s hand with his wolf teeth club.

At this critical moment, Cang Xu still kept his calm.

He was just about to grit his teeth and use necromancy when he suddenly stopped.

Boom!

A fiery figure fell from the sky.

An iron fist smashed down, pounding the spiky fishman back.

Layer upon layer of deck was shattered, the exploding fist punching the spiky fishman into the water.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

The spiky fishman coughed out blood, and his two perfectly round fish eyes revealed his unconcealed shock.

“What did you want to do to my magician?” The dragonman youngster shook his dragon trail, restrained his fist, and looked down at the spiky fishman from the hole in the decks.

“Commandeer!” Cang Xu finally had strong reinforcements, and despite being a necromancer, he couldn’t help but be moved.

Chapter 377: The Powerful Dragonman

The port battlefield.

Sonorous fishmen war cries, the curses of human defenders, and the sounds of battle mixed together.

Blades glistened, the shadows of swords flew, and many arrows and bullets spat through the air.

Some fought selflessly, others struggled for their lives, and some upon seeing their comrades in arms fall, took revenge to wipe out their grudges with wild scarlet eyes!

It was a chaotic battlefield.

There were no battle lines.

Because when the Vinegar Jar Pirates approached, many ships sailed out and the originally crowded port became much more spacious.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships couldn’t leave the harbor, and they were the fishmen’s main target.

Therefore, they now had the most fishmen surrounding them.

It was the center of the battlefield.

“gram”

A fishman exclaimed as it climbed up the side of a ship and onto the deck, then threw themselves at the nearest Dragon Lion mercenary.

The mercenary was currently holding back another offensive.

He was in imminent danger, and didn’t notice the fishman charging in from his left.

Ding.

A gunshot echoed.

The fishmen toppled over, completely dead.

The mercenary was still gritting his teeth, and didn’t notice how close death had brushed past him.

The one who saved his life was a gunman.

He didn’t have any notion of taking credit, because this had happened many times already.

The line of battle across the ship sides had already been shattered, and increasing amounts of fishmen were rushing onto the deck. The mercenaries retreated around the ship’s bow and aft houses, as well as the mast in the middle and the doorway to the lower deck, barely able to defend themselves.

The gunman’s only hope was that they could repel these fishmen as soon as possible, then reorganize the defensive line around the ship, otherwise they’d all be in danger.

Ammunition ran out.

“I need to reload, cover me!” The gunman shouted, he took a big step back and lowered his head to reload his gun.

His battle companion behind him straightened his back and stepped forward to protect him.

But a moment later, a hurled harpoon skewered him.

His corpse fell onto the gunman.

The ammo the gunman had taken out toppled out of his palm and fell onto the stairs that linked the aft house and the deck.

“Damm it!” The gunman cursed, he was about to pick it up when suddenly, a shadow covered him.

The gunman’s hair suddenly rose, and without thinking, he raised his firearm.

A moment later.

A fishman cutlass ferociously hacked into the gun’s barrel.

The alchemy firearm came from War Merchant’s warehouse, and its quality was guaranteed. Thus, it wasn’t cut through and had saved the gunman’s life.

But only for the time being.

Seeing he missed, the fishmen pulled out his blade and swung again.

“Duck!”

The gunman heard someone yell behind him during this critical moment.

He promptly ducked.

A second later, a broadsword whooshed above the gunman’s head.

The fishman raised his blade to protect himself, but the broadsword sliced through his body, causing his blood to splash everywhere.

The gunman seized that opportunity to roll behind his battle companion to give the latter space.

As he got up, another gunman handed him some ammo.

This time, he smoothly reloaded.

The gunmen fired in quick succession, each bullet taking away a fishman’s life.

But the situation hadn’t improved.

There were so many fishmen, and at this moment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps needed stronger firepower.

The situation was deeply worrying.

On some ships, the fishmen held the advantage, while on others the humans firmly held their ground.

Both sides lost people during these shootouts and close quarters fights.

The dragonman youngster had such things as he rushed to the port.

When he saw his subordinates fall one after the other, despite them slaying more fishmen in return, the dragonman youngster’s heart still filled with fury.

The dragonman youngster had always held himself responsible as leader.

But now, it wasn’t the time to resolve these issues.

Anger did not tamper the dragonman youngster’s reason, he quickly differentiated and analyzed the battlefield.

Although the battlefield was disorderly, there were only two key fights. One was the Quick Knife battle squad and the Steel Bone Knight fishman, and the other was the spiky fishman pursuing Cang Xu.

Without hesitation, the dragonman youngster made a beeline for Cang Xu.

Cang Xu was in a more urgent situation, he was already wounded (the dragonman youngster didn’t know Cang Xu’s injuries were an element of his plan to paralysis his opponent).

Zong Ge, behind him, rushed over to assist the Quick Knife battle squad.

At the same time, the Vinegar Jar was approaching the port after sustaining innumerable magic traps.

Finally, within casting range, Xi Dai Zi immediately let out prepared magic.

Cang Xu couldn’t evade, and his speed slumped when he was struck by the ice magic.

The spiky fishman howled with laughter and seized the opportunity to get near Cang Xu and smash Cang Xu’s head in with his wolf teeth club.

He had finally found a chance since they started fighting, and for a moment was too excited to notice a blazing figure towering above him.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Bang!

A moment later, the dragonman youngster’s fist ruggedly pounded into the spiky fishman’s back and smashed him downwards.

Crack, thump, bump!

Three crunches in a row.

The spiky fishman crashed through the first deck, then the second deck, until finally crashing through the bottom of the ship.

Plop.

He fell into the sea, reaching a depth of four meters within seconds, causing seawater to surge upwards all around him.

The sharp pain from his back caused the spiky fishman to gasp and belch out lots of blood.

His perfectly round fishmen eyes glared angrily through the turbulent waters and holes in the ship and saw the murderer who attacked him.

A tall silhouette looked down at him through those three holes.

The sunlight shone on the murderer’s head, and a strong sense of oppression suddenly hit his face.

It was obviously silver level, but he felt like he was facing a gold level.

Vertical red eyes and sharp fangs and teeth.

A body of dragon scales just like battle armor.

Dragon claws balled into a fist had steam rising from it.

Information given prebattle streaked across the fishman’s brains.

In an instant, he figured out who had attacked him, “Long Fu!”

According to what the information said:

He was a silver level dragonman.

He once faced Rou Zang openly.

This alone caused people to look at him in high regard.

But the strike and his serious injury from it allowed the spiky fishman to completely understand how strong Long Fu was!

This was a monstrous silver level!

He had only felt this kind of oppressive power from Dao Hen.

But Dao Hen bore the strong Knife Fin bloodline and was an old fishman that had been renowned for a long time.

How old was Long Fu?

The spiky fishman spat out a bit more blood.

But this time, he finally recovered his strength.

The fist had left crushed scales and shattered bones in his back.

Every time he moved, he would feel intense pain.

However, this pain instead aroused the spiky fishman’s ferocity even more!

He howled and with all of his might, climbed back up.

“gram”

The spiky fishman rushed out of the seawater, and rushed up the holes in the ship, both fists raised as he advanced towards the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster remained calm as he suddenly extended his left palm to the left of his body.

Ding.

The left palm detonated.

The battle skill Surge activated, pushing him out of the way.

He timed it just right, and the spiky fishman breaking through the decks attacked with all of his might yet hit nothing.

Using the explosion’s force, the dragonman youngster rushed to the side of the ship.

He had been practicing this battle skill diligently.

Now, he had a skillful mastery of the battle skill Surge.

It was an easy drive on a familiar path for him now, to the extent that he actually expressed a hint of gracefulness when his strong body experienced sudden shifts in location.

The dragonman youngster softly jumped, his two feet landing on the inner side of the ship.

His knees bent in passing, and like a compressing spring saving kinetic energy, his entire body was nearly parallel with the deck.

Battle skill – Surge.

The center of both his soles exploded at the same time.

The explosions blew holes in the ship side.

This strong driving force bubbling out boosted his charge at the spiky fishman.

The spiky fishman cried out as he lifted his arm to block it.

This time it was different, he finally had time to withstand the dragonman youngster directly.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Bang!

A similar explosion boomed.

Similar firepower.

A similar scene played out again.

The spiky fishman was blown away, his arm bones completely disintegrated, and his chest cratered by the powerful strike.

At least four of his ribs were broken.

The spiky fishman was valiant, and before the explosion launched him, he used racial magic with all of his strength.

His frontal spikes aimed and fired at the dragonman youngster!

Ding, ding, ting…

A succession of metal clangs accompanied splattering sparks.

The dragonman youngster stood firm, for his unyielding dragon scales perfectly shielded him from harm.

Some spikes bounced back and hit some unlucky people.

A hole appeared in an iron level fishman’s head, they stiffened, then fell to the deck. Their brains and blood were pooling from the hole in their forehead.

The spiky fishman flew through the air for a moment before smashing into the middle of another ship hull.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were all ordinary vessels, so he broke through the hull.

The spiky fishman crashed into the ship hold, then broke a ship ladder and three ship pillars before stopping.

Lying in the wooden ruins, the spiky fishman madly coughed out blood once again.

The acute pain caused him to involuntarily howl out miserably.

Taking the dragonman youngster’s fist was simply like being hit by a battering ram!

He couldn’t raise his arms.

His breathing was also hindered, and his chest was congested.

The spiky fishman wanted to crawl up and continue the fight when suddenly, his vision went dark and he fell back down, nearly fainting on the spot.

Upon seeing this, the human fighters all cheered, and their morale greatly rose.

“That’s our commander!”

“Commander Long Fu has returned!”

“Woohoo, its time to kill these wretched fishmen bastards!!”

At that moment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was greatly influenced.

The fishmen swallowed their saliva, and they no longer charged wildly with no thought for personal safety, now many fishmen looked worried and their movements slowed somewhat.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps felt a great weight lift from them.

Both sides had limited training and weren’t standing armies. Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had Zong Ge, a drilling master, their training hadn’t been that long.

Thus, morale greatly affected them.

Chapter 378: Human Reinforcements

“lamgrr”

The Steel Bone Knight fishman shouted, he was very concerned about the spiky fishman.

After the spiky fishman took Long Fu’s bombardment and crashed into a ship hold, he had gone quiet.

The dragonman youngster’s battle strength far exceeded the fishman knight’s estimates!

Of course, if the spiky fishman was in peak condition, the dragonman youngster wouldn’t have beaten him in two blows.

The spiky fishman had madly rushed the White Bandage and the Iron Lump, greatly consuming his battle qi. Soon after, he attacked the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships and endured an unknown amount of arrows and bullets before finally being struck from behind by the dragonman youngster.

His condition had declined too severely.

This was also one of the main factors that let the dragonman youngster exhibit his supernatural might.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman couldn’t bear watching on helplessly as the spiky fishman was killed.

He regretfully looked down below him and finally made a decision.

A moment later, he clapped his lizard and used the fishman tongue to order his mount to flee.

Then he dismounted willingly and turned tail, charging towards the ship where the spiky fishmen had fallen.

“Don’t let him escape!”

Kuai Tui growled as he swung his curved blade at the fishman knight.

The others immediately followed Kuai Tui’s lead and surrounded the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

The lizard had managed to break out, and for a moment it slowed, as if it couldn’t believe what was happening.

The Quick Knife battle squad’s actual target had been the Steel Bone Knight fishman, so they let it get away.

Although it was an iron level magic beast, it was near death and had no battle prowess left.

Due to this unexpected turn of events, the lizard sized that opportunity to save itself.

Kuai Tui’s decision was very reasonable.

Beheading the lizard would waste time.

They had to stall the Steel Bone Knight fishman to give Commander Long Fu crucial time. Killing the spiky fishman as soon as possible would tremendously affect this battlefield.

However, this time when the Quick Knife battle squad did battle with the fishman knight, they did far worse.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman had strong defense, and bronze level knives would at beast leave a scratch.

Without a mount encumbering him, he could use all of his energy to go on the offensive.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman raised his lance and swept it in all directions.

Most of the Quick Knife battle squad were forced to get out of the way.

They were only bronze levels and were far beneath a silver level.

Kuai Tui was the only iron level, and he braced himself to block the fishman knight.

Lance and blade collided.

Metal shrieked and sparks flew.

The bronze knife flew away, along with Kuai Tui. His forearm was bent back, and his hand was stained with blood.

He was the core, and now that he had been thrown out of position and wounded, the entire battle squad was affected and became disorderly.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman saw this weakness, and his massive eyes glinted ominously.

He immediately changed his plans, now deciding to annihilate the battle squad before him.

The Quick Knife battle squad had entangled him for a long time, causing him to hate them greatly.

Now that he had a good chance to do so, of course he wanted to take revenge.

If he let this golden opportunity slip away, the Quick Knife battle squad would regroup and become a problem again.

Then, they would interfere with his fighting once again.

The lance ferociously thrusted towards Kuai Tui.

Kuai Tui had fallen onto the deck and was unable to evade.

Pop.

Blood splashed.

A dogman placed his own body in front of Kuai Tui.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman was very strong.

Compared to the lance, the dogman looked very frail, and he wasn’t able to stop the lance completely.

The lance impaled through the brave dogman warrior and into Kuai Tui’s lower back.

Kuai Tui roared in pain.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman was about to continue piercing Kuai Tui when suddenly, his ear felt a biting cold wind.

At that moment, he felt panic spread through his body, bursting through his fish scales.

The fishman unconsciously relinquished his grip and jumped to the right to avoid a blinding sword.

He quickly turned around to see a burly chap standing behind him.

The burly human turned his wrist, swinging the broadsword in a horizontal cut.

The fishman knight was confident in his defense, but still didn’t want to endure this strike.

The other person was a silver level like him, so of course he dodged.

The fishman knight rolled, dodging the broad sword’s cut and pulled distance from the burly human.

The burly human didn’t pursue, his swing before was intended to force the fishman knight back.

Holding his sword in one hand, he grabbed the shaft of the lance and yanked it out, saving Kuai Tui.

“Lord Shi Qi!” Kuai Tui and the others shouted.

Zong Ge nodded to them, “You have done good enough, it is my turn now. Kuai Tui, lead the others away.”

“Understood, Lord Shi Qi!” Kuai Tui followed the order.

He mournfully carried his dead comrade away.

The Quick Knife battle squad retreated in full.

This ship had become the battlefield for Zong Ge and the fishman knight.

The knight had lost his lance, so with a grave look, he took out an ornamental blade from his waist.

He couldn’t assist the spiky fishman now.

The powerful Zong Ge required his wholehearted efforts to deal with!

He also didn’t need to worry about the spiky fishman for the time being.

Because Xi Dai Zi had already acted, temporarily intercepting the dragonman youngster.

Xi Dai Zi was a fishman shaman and was adept at unleashing magic. He could make time to throw out a totem in front of the spiky fishman while being busy hindering the dragonman youngster.

The totem released emerald-green rays of light in a circle.

Within this green light, the spiky fishman’s injuries quickly recovered.

It was a healing totem!

“Follow me!” A silver level fighter led a group of bodyguards into the port battlefield.

“My flagship, where is my flagship?” Soon after, more cultivators came.

A wave of people anxiously stormed the port.

They were the merchant alliance representatives.

The ships were very important to the merchants.

If lost or damaged, transportation would become a big problem, greatly influencing their business.

More vital was the imminent danger before them – without ships, if Two Eyes Island was seized by the fishmen, they would be unable to escape!

Magic ships especially couldn’t be lost.

Their construction cost was too high!

Therefore, after breaking out of the auction hall trap, the merchant alliance cultivators urgently rushed towards the port.

They were nowhere as strong as the dragonman youngster or Zong Ge, and despite having movement enhancement battle skills, they were a step slower than those two.

The awkward thing was that the merchant ships they wanted to save first had basically all left port already.

But the merchant alliance cultivators didn’t despair.

Because the merchant ships weren’t that far from shore.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates assaulting the port scared many ships into leaving port.

This resulted in them triggering the many magic traps Hun Tong had secretly deployed.

As the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ flagship had come in, it had done close range bombardments and thrown the departing ships into confusion.

These ships clashed with each other, and many could only spin in place as they were slaughtered like leaderless beasts ambushed by wolves for a moment.

Ding.

A loud sound rang out.

The Vinegar Jar hadn’t slowed at all and smashed directly into the port.

An unlucky ship in its way was reduced to splinters within three seconds.

The silver level Vinegar Jar crashed into the shoreline!

The merchant alliance cultivators all got out of the way, their hearts trembling in uniform.

The Vinegar Jar’s attack stance simply was crazy, and no normal person dared to stop it.

Charging ashore with a ship was tantamount t o running it aground.

“The fishmen are too reckless!”

“If they can’t defeat Hun Tong, how will they retreat?”

“Surely they still have strength to reveal.”

The merchant alliance cultivators looked at each other in dismay, every single one utterly bewildered.

The Vinegar Jar displayed that it would succeed or die trying, something that indeed captured the people’s souls.

Such a strong showing caused the fishmen’s morale to rise, which had just been struck down by the dragonman youngster.

The merchant alliance representatives were somewhat hesitant.

There were still only two crucial fights on the port battlefield.

One was Shi Qi against the fishman knight, the other was Long Fu fighting Xi Dai Zi and the spiky fishman.

Shi Qi held a broadsword steadily, pressuring the fishman knight with strikes.

Long Fu was in a more awkward situation, he clearly held the advantage but wasn’t able to translate it into a victory.

Casters had too many tricks, they could slow, heal, and more, all of which prevented Long Fu from employing his power. The spiky fishman was constantly healing, and after eating a big loss, he had learned to not content with the dragonman youngster directly but just contend with him.

The spiky fishman had changed his combat style.

It was indeed somewhat troublesome to contend with his plump body.

However, compared to when he was coughing up blood injured, for the first time in his life, the spiky fishman sensed the benefits of this kind of tactic.

“We must first save our people!” The Flint Merchant Alliance representative took the initiative to decide and jumped out.

“The magic ships cannot be stolen.” The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance representative followed close behind.

The group of merchant alliance cultivators acted one after the other.

They jumped across ships and swiftly approached the maritime battlefield, each of them assisting their own fleet.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative was left behind.

He didn’t know a jumping battle skill, and could only impatiently take aim at the Treasure Merchant Alliance representative.

Although the Treasure Merchant Alliance representative was a silver level fighter, he had an alchemy item that allowed him to glide.

Just as he flew up two meters, his thigh was grabbed by the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative.

“Help me, take me along!” The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance shouted.

“Get off.” The Treasure Merchant Alliance representative exploded in anger, “This item can only bear one person!”

“Money, I’ll give you money!”

“Everyone is a victim, everyone is a businessman, help me.” The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative yelled repeatedly.

The Treasure Merchant Alliance Representative wouldn’t stand for it and was about to raise his other foot to kick the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative off.

“Don’t be so pedantic!” The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative shouted, “100 gold coins, I’ll give you 100 gold!”

The Treasure Merchant Alliance representative immediately changed, “200 gold.”

“Deal, now fly with me!” The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative agreed immediately.

But a moment later, a few spears and flying arrows shot towards the two.

It was quite dangerous to fly over the battlefield so recklessly.

It was easy to become a target.

Luckily both merchant alliance representatives were silver level and had impressive defensive power.

They fell down, but in spite of their dirty trajectory, they continued to rise into the sky and towards their ships to bring aid.

Chapter 379: Hun Tong’s Maneuver

Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai stormed the port side by side.

They were a step slower than the merchant alliance representatives.

That was because Hun Tong had them stay behind to give them a mission.

The mercenary corps also had their respective encampments on the new island. They had also spent time converging with their people.

One knew that mercenary corps were not regular armies.

Take the soldiers in the city guard barracks, they were rarely able to freely leave the barracks.

But mercenaries were different.

Under normal circumstances, these mercenaries had their respective lives and could freely travel between the various regions of Two Eyes Island. Some trained, some indulged in wine, women, or maybe gambling.

Only when the mercenary commander convened them would they assemble together and operate together to complete a mission.

The city wall guard barracks always had soldiers garrisoning and training in them, while mercenary corps encampments only had a few people left behind in them. Besides the mercenary corps members, there were also some old or disabled mercenaries or family members there too.

The two commanders did their best to marshal their people, then hurried to the port after the merchant alliance representatives arrived there.

“Damm it!” Commander Tie Bang grimaced.

“My White Bandage…” Commander Beng Dai gnashed his teeth.

The two were in a more awkward situation than the merchant alliance representatives.

The merchant alliance cultivators were able to barely jump over and fly the short distance to their fleets to provide aid.

While the two mercenary corps’ ships were farther from the port, so the two commanders were powerless to help.

Because the Iron Lump and the White Bandage were both magic ships, they were able to forge ahead and resist many magic traps, thus they were able to free themselves from the harbor.

But the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ commander didn’t let them slip away.

He personally acted and slaughtered the crew of the White Bandage first, then annihilated all of the humans on the Iron Lump next.

If the two mercenary corps’ ships didn’t leave port, the two commanders would have been able to help their people.

But their decision to head towards the ocean actually caused them to cut the distance between them and their enemies, thus saving their enemies’ time.

“What should we do now?” The two commanders stood together and were at a loss for a moment.

Er Gua Zi had displayed his gold level strength, and with just his power, he had effortlessly taken both of their flagships.

Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai were only silver levels, and didn’t want to fight Er Gua Zi.

“Those wretched fishmen pirates. Since they stole our ships, we will steal theirs!” Commander Tie Bang was steaming with anger as he gazed at the Vinegar Jar stranded aground in the port.

“Possible! Er Gua Zi will be Lord Hun Tong’s to handle.” Commander Beng Dai nodded, he shook his arm and shouted as he and Commander Tie Bang charged the Vinegar Jar together.

Hun Tong had come to the port; however, he didn’t join the battlefield.

He looked towards the ocean, sharply gazing at Er Gua Zi.

Hun Tong was a gold level cultivator, and based on his strength, if he wanted to, he could rush the port and sweep away the Steel Bone Knight fishman, the spiky fishman, and the others there.

But he didn’t do that.

“If I save those mercenary and merchant alliance ships, they will want to leave port.”

When that happens, the enemy’s pressure will focus on me.”

“Even if I hire them to do battle, that will waste resources.”

“Instead of that, it would be better to slow the pace and allow the port battle to persist.”

Hun Tong naturally schemed for his own benefits.
