He wasn’t worried about the port.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were sufficient enough for the fishmen to chew on.

There were many merchant alliance cultivators that were also aiding.

As feudal lord, Hun Tong naturally had a higher vision than others.

He had to keep the overall situation in mind.

This was his responsibility as leader!

The Vinegar Jar Pirates have appeared.”

“What about the Blue Green Coral fishman clan?”

“Their old clan elder is also a gold level!”

Hun Tong didn’t plan to act immediately.

The enemy hadn’t gone all out yet, and so he kept watch from the rear.

The current situation was somewhat subtle.

The auction hall trap had been shattered, and the humans who had broken out were not just merchant alliance people and mercenaries, but also pirates.

Ordinarily, these pirates would abide by Two Eyes Island’s laws.

But now that the auction was finished, and Two Eyes Island was in turmoil, would these pirates behave themselves?

If these pirates attacked Two Eyes Island, then they would be an enormous problem!

Therefore, after breaking out of the auction hall, Hun Tong negotiated with the others.

The focus of these negotiations was the pirates.

Hun Tong’s words of profound meaning used both soft and hard tactics.

Dian Wan, Hu Li, and Yang Cong Tou sank into silence.

But when met with Hun Tong’s proposal, Qi You burst into laughter, pointed at Hun Tong, and shook his head, “No, no, no, Feudal Lord Hun Tong, you can’t control us.”

“Who are we?” Qi You looked behind him, at the pirate commanders next to him.

His voice roused them, “We are pirates!”

“We are not some merchants bent solely on profit.”

“Nor are we those willingly mercenary dogs!”

“Hehehe.”

“We are pirates.”

“We are free.”

“Did you really think you could restrain a pirate’s freedom? Have them obey your orders?”

“Even a king wouldn’t daydream of such a thing!”

“Wrong, Qi You.” Hun Tong deeply sighed, “This is merely an invitation from a friend.”

“I am inviting you to be my guests.”

“Just like the innumerable times you have come ashore here.”

“As everyone can see, I have come across some small inconveniences…”

“Small inconveniences?!” Qi You interrupted Hun Tong.

The gold level pirate boss stared at Hun Tong with deep blue eyes, his gaze exposing his ridicule and sarcasm.

“I don’t believe this is a small inconvenience.”

“If I were you, I’d act personally, instead of talking big here.”

“Speaking with us will do nothing.”

“Your enemy is before you, my adorable feudal lord.”

Hun Tong and Qi You looked at each other, their gazes crashing with imperviable sparks.

Hun Tong could see Qi You’s greed and restlessness.

He could restrain Dian Wan and the other silver level pirates, but his power was not enough to deter Qi You.

These pirates were like a time bomb.

Hun Tong didn’t want these pirates to be a knife in his back while he dealt with the fishmen.

Thus, Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord raised his head and laughed.

He appeared to look down at Qi You, and replied with deep intent, “It seems we have differing opinions of our situation.”

“Although I am disappointed, I am not surprised.”

“Do you know why?”

“Because you are not me.”

“Now, as Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord, I shall let you see what the situation truly is!”

After saying that, he softly floated into the air.

He had a gold level flying skill, so doing this was quite easy.

As he floated in the air, he took out a portable flare gun.

It was a silver level weapon.

Inside it was a signal flare.

Hun Tong raised the gun and shot it into the sky.

Whoosh.

After sharply screaming through the air, the signal flare exploded in the sky into a bright yellow firework that was exceptionally striking.

“The signal!”

“Open the magic power reservoir.”

“Raise the turrets!”

After receiving this signal, some surfaces of the new island opened wide and pure white towers slowly rose from underground.

Light cannon turrets appeared in the highlands, the port, and in the middle of the barracks!

The pirates changed color.

As it turns out, Hun Tong hadn’t just built a single light cannon turret.

It was just a pretense.

It was a trap that befuddled the enemy.

The fishmen had attacked it, but they didn’t know that there were other light cannon turrets hidden underground elsewhere.

The towers were slender, and at their peak was 3 meter long cubes.

There was an eye on every single one of its sides.

On top of the cubes was a prismatic lens the height of an adult man.

When the light cannon turrets finished rising, the prismatic lens began to absorb light, and when the light elements concentrated, the prismatic lenses began to glisten with light.

“Lgmanan”

“Mglgml”

“Galmnnnalmgamllnga”

The fishmen detected the turrets, causing them to shout one after the other, some were angry, but many more were panicking.

After a dozen seconds, the prismatic lenses stopped absorption.

The light elements imbued the cubes.

The cubes’ closed eye patterns suddenly glistened with light.

The closed eyes then suddenly opened, and from the depths of their pupils, light rays shot out!

The incandescent light rays were blinding.

A light ray targeted the ground in front of the barrack’s gate, cutting a scorching black line through it that extended a dozen meters.

All fishmen in the line were vaporized, not even their skeletons remained.

Light rays weaved through the port, causing devastating casualties to the fishmen.

Light rays also hit the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ ships, blowing up parts of three ships and sinking three in an astonishing military success.

The many chaotic battlefields sank into stillness at the same time.

Both friend and foe were somewhat stupefied.

Hun Tong’s display of his defenses amazed most people.

The feudal lord had hidden himself too deeply.

“Two Eyes Island will prevail!” One Eye gazed at the light cannon turrets’ great might from a distance, his eyes were bright with the firm belief of victory.

He had just been on the new island, but through a short distance teleportation formation, now found himself on the old island.

Two Eyes Island had a new and an old island.

The new island had just finished rising, and the old island had sunken by two thirds.

Hun Tong’s patrol fleet wasn’t on the new island, rather it was on the old island.

Hun Tong hadn’t done this intentionally.

Instead, it was convention.

This happened because the new island’s port was used to welcome everyone that joined the auction.

The patrol fleet was stationed at the old island to also guard against upheaval that occurred in the new island.

Deploying troops like this was undoubtably wise.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates had to choose between the new and the old island.

When they chose to assault the new island, the patrol fleet at the old island was safe.

When upheaval occurred on the new island, the patrol fleet immediately detected it.

By the time One Eye arrived at his flagship, the entire fleet was ready and waiting!

“The enemy ships are all caught up in the fighting.”

“Attack!”

“We shall utterly defeat them!!”

One eye bellowed.

With vigorous morale, the patrol fleet gallantly sailed from port and charged towards the new island.

When this power joined the battlefield, it was bound to tip the scales of victory fiercely towards them.

Chapter 380: The Fishman’s Main Force

Dao Hen arrived at the army barracks.

He was wounded and holding a man’s head in his hand.

It was the city guard commander’s head.

The ultimate winner of the two silvers’ duel was Dao Hen.

Among the invading silver level fishmen, Dao Hen was publicly known to be the strongest!

Hong Bo was leading his subordinates in the bitter siege of the guard barracks, and when he saw Dao Hen rushing over to provide support, he was naturally ecstatic.

After the two used the fishmen tongue to communicate, the city guard commander’s head was thrown into the barracks.

The soldiers were immediately disturbed, and their morale took a hit.

“Don’t lose your heads!”

“Even though the Lord Commander is dead, we still have the Feudal Lord!”

“The Feudal Lord has already escaped, these fishmen will surely be defeated.”

“We can defend this place; we have the light cannon tower behind us!!”

Very quickly, the soldiers were no longer wavering and their morale stabilized.

Hong Bo sighed.

Dao Hen clapped his shoulder. After some observation, he decided to camouflage himself, stealthily approach the barracks from the southeast corner, then launch a surprise attack.

His plan succeeded.

He plunged into the people through a gap.

As silver level battle skills were used, he quickly looked like a silvery light that chopped human soldiers to bits wherever it went.

When it came to assaulting a fortified position, powerhouses always had a powerful impact.

The barracks were barely able to hold off the silver level Hong Bo.

Dao Hen increased the pressure on them, and in the end, their defenses broke.

A rout in one area would spur a rout in the entire front line.

The fishmen took advantage of this to wildly attack, bursting through a wall in the barracks.

The light cannon turret in the barracks shot white light, each one able to kill a hundred fishmen.

But the dauntless fishmen were unafraid of death, and they advanced wave up wave.

“Be careful, he’s charging in!”

“Block them.”

“Ah…”

Dao Hen had killed his way into the center of the barracks, withstanding every human soldier, for none was his match.

Hong Bo led the wild attack from the backline, admiring Dao Hen’s lone figure as he dove into the enemy troops.

“His target is the turret.”

“Protect the turret!”

The city guard’s few iron level leaders braced themselves and advanced to stop Dao Hen.

Dao Hen sneered, and in spite of his body, he exchanged injuries for lives.

With one hand, he killed two iron levels.

The other leaders wavered, and for a moment their morale shook, almost ready to run away.

At that critical moment, a divine spell soared through the air and fell onto the iron level leaders.

The iron level leaders’ injuries quickly healed.

Then, many more divine spells fell.

When the pleasantly surprised human soldiers looked to who it was, they saw the Wealth church priest leading a dozen clergymen along with thirty holy bodyguards rushing over to provide support.

The divine spells didn’t just apply their effects, they also greatly increased morale.

Although the human soldiers were wounded, with divine spells taking care of them, their injuries were quickly recovering. That made them fight more courageously.

By contrast, when the fishmen saw the wounds their comrades had inflicted at the cost of their lives melt away, their hearts inevitably felt setback and despondence.

Dao Hen sighed to himself again.

He had definitely sensed that the humans were about to fall, but the clergy had saved them all.

Hong Bo had already yelled himself hoarse during this fierce battle.

When the Wealth priest and the others appeared, he had immediately responded by ordering his people to kill them.

But the other side had holy bodyguards, while at the same time, other human soldiers quickly came to the rescue, reducing Hong Bo’s attempt on their lives to nothing.

Thus, things for the fishmen were getting worse.

Although they currently held the advantage, it was only because they had numbers.

With the clergy healing and assisting, the human soldiers would lose minimal battle strength.

The two races had different strengths and weaknesses, and at this moment, the cruel battlefield showed them in great detail.

The fishmen had far inferior talent to the humans.

They had a far lower percentage of casters in their race compared to the humans.

In this part of the barracks battlefield, the humans had clergy helping, while they didn’t.

The Blue Green Coral clan had many casters.

But they were precious, and had been placed underground, used to construct the auction hall trap.

The auction hall had been broken, and in the subterranean depths, the fishmen there had been turned to shriveled corpses.

There was a coral ball cage tree branch in each of the corpses’ hearts.

Their magic power and vitality had been dedicated to the cause.

They had been sacrificed.

While the mysterious purple scaled fishman disappeared with a trace.

The port nearby.

Qi You and the other pirates showed mixed feelings.

Hun Tong was still floating in the air.

The light cannon towers had been raised, and One Eye was leading the patrol fleet over, the scales were tilting towards the humans.

The pirates were intimidated and didn’t dare to act blindly without thinking.

Hun Tong wasn’t the slightest complacent.

He knew the enemy’s full strength hadn’t appeared yet.

He was waiting.

But Er Gua Zi wasn’t.

He broke a human sailor’s neck while surveying in the direction of the old island.

Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet looked like a fierce tiger pouncing into the chaotic maritime battlefield.

Er Gua Zi was a veteran island commander, and had abundant experience in naval battles, he knew that although the patrol fleet wasn’t that big, in the chaotic battlefield, these fresh troops would have an astonishing effect on the battle.

He wasn’t able to reorganize his ships any more.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates’ ships were all entangled with other ships, you and I, I and you.

The flagship Vinegar Jar had even charged onto shore!

There were benefits to this too.

The light cannon turret that rose in the port was only able to shoot Vinegar Jar Pirate ships on the periphery during its first wave of firing.

If the light cannon turret opened fire at will, it was liable to injure allies on accident.

Er Gua Zi pondered and decided not to swim towards the fresh patrol fleet to stop it, but rather towards Two Eyes Island.

As a gold level, his every move garnered much attention.

“He’s charging in!” Hun Tong’s gaze focused.

Hun Tong soon called to mind, “It seems the patrol fleet won’t arrive for a bit.”

As expected, a moment later, an alchemy device transmitted, “Feudal Lord, we have detected that there is a fishman army and magic beasts in the ocean, a gold level aura has also been detected!”

“The fishmen’s main force.” When Hun Tong heard this bad news, his worry instead decreased by a lot.

The time before arrows were fired was when they were the most threatening.

Hidden cards had been revealed, and so Hun Tong immediately replied.

He sincerely stated, “Distinguished Song Shou, please deal with the Vinegar Jar Pirate commander.”

Song Shou didn’t hesitate and instantly nodded his agreement, “We human feudal lords naturally must keep watch and defend each other, let me judge how fine this fishman fighter is.”

Since the auction hall, Song Shou supported Hun Tong.

If he wavered now, it would reduce the friendship between them.

Song Shou naturally wasn’t stupid.

He didn’t have a flying battle skill, so he jumped his way over and entered the port in a dozen seconds.

When the humans and fishmen nearby saw the gold level fighter arriving, they all withdrew independently.

This battlefield was set aside for the showdown between gold level cultivators.

Song Shou stood on the shore, no longer advancing.

He didn’t want to fight the gold level fishman in the water. On land, he had the advantage.

When Hun Tong saw Song Shou sternly standing, he turned to say to Yan Ding, “Distinguished Yan Ding, I need your strength now to deal with the Blue Green Fishman clan elder. I shall reward you handsomely afterwards!”

Yan Ding was outwardly a mercenary, but in reality he was an imperial secret spy.

He and Hun Tong were working together.

Yan Ding nodded and jumped away.

He spouted smoke from his nostrils.

Black ashen smoke supported his tall and sturdy body, carrying him forward.

From the skies, Yan Ding quickly discovered that a gigantic oceanic beast was floating up.

In the water, a black shadow was constantly getting bigger.

After a few seconds, there was a rumble.

A huge animal broke through the ocean surface, causing heavy ocean spray with ample might.

It was a gigantic whale.

Its body appeared flat, it emitted strong chilliness, and it had a silver level life aura.

The ocean magic beast – Freeze Army Whale!

On the Freeze Army Whale’s flat back was the fishman’s main force.

There was densely packed fishmen as far as the eye could see, at least two thousand of them.

At the forefront of the fishman army was a two meter tall old fishman.

He was covered in green scales, leaning on a walking stick, and stooped over. From his fishman gills there were many dark green kelps that dragged down to his feet, they looked like a beard and long hair.

His gold level aura was unmistakable.

It was the one Hun Tong always had on his mind, the Blue Green Coral fishman clan’s old elder!

The old clan elder gazed towards the new island, towards Hun Tong, then moved his walking stick.

The Frozen Land Whale beneath him gave a long wail, and with a tremendous wave, charged directly towards the old island.

How strange, instead of helping on the new island, he instead chose to charge towards the nearly sunk old island.

Hun Tong’s brows greatly furled at this moment.

This action didn’t make sense.

For a moment, Hun Tong couldn’t make out what the old fishman elder was intending.

However, even though he couldn’t make out the enemy’s intentions, devising every way and means to destroy it would be fine.

Hun Tong instantly ordered One Eye to not come to the new island for the time being, but instead to stop the main fishman army as much as possible.

One Eye received the order and altered the patrol fleet’s course at once.

Ships turned sideways, exposing rows of firing slits.

Boom, bang boom…

A moment later the cannons exploded and rumbled.

Artillery shells soared through the air in a parabola, heading straight towards the fishman’s main army.

The fishmen had been prepared for this.

The Frozen Land Whale cried, and from its air hole sprayed a large stream of sea water.

The sweater was like a gigantic umbrella that covered the skies above the Freeze Army Whale.

Some of the artillery shells that struck the gigantic umbrella exploded on impact, while others managed to get through, however, their momentum was greatly decreased and were easily hit out of the sky by the fishmen.

The gigantic umbrella gradually expanded and stretched downwards.

A half spherical water bubble protected the fishmen completely.

This was the Freeze Army Whale’s racial magic – Large Bowl Water Cover.

As a silver level magic beast, humans of the same level couldn’t compare to their magic power.

It was a difference between races.

Chapter 381: Mysterious Intent

The Frozen Land Whale could withstand the entire patrol fleet’s bombardment by itself, and its charge didn’t slow at all.

While One Eye was powerless to do anything, Yan Ding rushed over.

He suddenly inhaled, causing the cigar in his mouth to violently burn down a small chunk.

Yan Ding puffed up his chest, and from his nostrils, a large cloud of smoke sprayed out.

The smoke looked like a black cloud, its darkness covered an entire area and was descending towards the water.

The water bubble held back the black cloud, but it quickly eroded into black ash.

The Frozen Land Whale wailed mournfully, finally feeling pain.

It slowed down too.

Despite it having coarse skin, thick flesh, and massive magic power, it couldn’t compare to a gold battle skill.

Yan Ding used another gold level battle skill.

The Frozen Land Whale quickly fell into a disadvantageous position.

The old fishman elder naturally couldn’t let this continue.

He immediately threw himself into the fountain of water.

He jetted through the water all the way to the top of the water bubble.

The old clan elder used a battle skill to attack the distant Yan Ding.

Black smoke floated around Yan Ding.

This smoke condensed into a wall that blocked the fishman clan elder’s attack.

The old clan elder didn’t have a flying battle skill, and Yan Ding didn’t want to fight in close quarters.

He was an expert at long range warfare, with a massive area of effect, almost all of his battle skills were large scale.

The two became deadlocked.

With the fishman clan elder’s protection, the Frozen Land Whale quickly bumped into the old island.

The entire old island faintly rocked.

“Ogngrnmorn”

“gonmgoanga”

“gmnogognl”

The fishmen army had landed successfully, and one by one, they surged off the whale’s back like a beast tide and swarmed onto the island.

Yan Ding coldly snorted and used a battle skill with all of their strength.

The black smoke under him quickly swelled to an area of a thousand square meters.

Several holes appeared in the thick smoke.

The holes glistened bright red, like there were flames burning in them.

“Not good!” The fishman elder promptly strengthened his attacks, but he wasn’t good at long ranged attacks.

Yan Ding activated his battle skill without a hitch.

Battle skill – Cigar Butt Fire!

The holes in the thick smoke expanded, and like an anus defecating, large chunks of solid ash fired from them.

The solid ash was not heavy, and as they floated in the air, they suddenly exploded, distributing huge volumes of red cigar ash.

These cigar ashes looked like the flames that floated through the air after burning paper.

When they landed on fishmen, they immediately melted their fish scales and burned them, causing the fishmen to miserably shriek.

The cigar ash continued to fall.

Fishmen fell one after the other.

Yan Ding’s strength was quite outstanding, he really looked like a magician, instead of a battle qi cultivation expert.

When the fishman elder saw his people suffer sever casualties, he lamented.

But he couldn’t do anything.

He didn’t have a flying battle skill, nor could he trouble Yan Ding enough.

He used a communications battle skill, “Lord Priest, please act. Your divine spell can trouble Yan Ding immensely, we are all believers of our God.”

Inside the Frozen Land Whale, a priest was curled up, their eyes closed in meditation.

Her upper body was human, while from the waist down, her body was a snake.

She had a slim build, and most of her body was covered in a layer of green scales.

Unlike ordinary people, she had four arms, evenly distributed on both sides of her body. Her forehead also had green fin-like antennas.

She was not a fishman, but a naga.

She was also a caster, but didn’t have the aura of magic power, instead she was awash with the feeling of divine power.

Her aura was deeply restrained, and not a trace of it leaked out.

Upon hearing the old fishman elder’s plea, the worshiping naga opened her eyes and rejected it, “Old clan elder, I cannot act so rashly.”

“I bear a heavy mission on this trip. This mission comes before everything else.”

The old clan elder anxious insisted, “Lord Priest, to attack Two Eyes Island, our entire clan has gone to war. If this continues, even if we recapture our homeland, my clan will be wiped out.”

The naga priestess placated him, “Old clan elder, a turning point is coming.”

“Although I cannot act, there is one person who won’t be able to restrain themselves.”

The old clan elder faintly stared, “Who?”

“Qi You.” The naga priestess silently laughed.

The old clan elder hesitated, “He’s a human. Furthermore, when the Vinegar Jar Pirates contacted him and asked him to help attack Two Eyes Island, he refused to.”

“Don’t tell me the Holy City has secretly instigated him?”

The naga priestess denied it, “We didn’t. We have never contacted him.”

“But I can be certain that Qi You is bound to act.”

“You don’t understand this man.”

“At that time…how could the one who stole Xiao Shi away from me be content to live in obscurity?”

Whoosh!

As if responding to the naga priestess’s words, a sharp steam whistle shrieked from the new island.

Following the steam whistle, a silver level pirate ship appeared in the distance.

It had a pirate flag that flew high above it, a long and narrow hull, its sails looked like a black dragon’s wings, and it had a demonic charm that made it stand out from the masses.

Behind this pirate ship, there were many more ordinary ships.

“That is Qi You’s ship – the Black Whimper!”

“The Ten Touch Pirates are charging in !!”

“Qi You!!” Hun Tong suddenly glared at Qi You.

At that moment, he realized he’d been tricked by Qi You.

Qi You appeared to be doing nothing while secretly contacting his fleet in actuality.

Now, the Ten Touch Pirates were joining the fray.

They wouldn’t be dealing with the laughably poor fishmen, because they were pirates, and would be targeting the populous and affluent human lands.

“Qi You, do you want to be my enemy?” Hun Tong growled, “Think it over carefully. If you really attack, Two Eyes Island will forever be barred to the Ten Touch Pirates!”

“You, a human, will be helping the fishmen, you will be a traitor to humanity!”

Qi You burst into laughter, “Don’t try to bluff me, Hun Tong.”

“Your Two Eyes Island is about to become an imperial logistics base.”

“No more shall pirates dispose of their booty here. Even if there are auctions here, it’ll have nothing to do with us!”

Hun Tong’s heart shook, he narrowed his eyes, and his desire to commit murder boiled over in a flash.

“What?” Dian Wan and the others looked at each other in dismay, they were a level lower than Qi You and hadn’t received anything about that whatsoever.

“Don’t label me a traitor to humanity. I just want to grab a profit today.”

“Hehehe.”

“You’ve earned too much these last few years, Hun Tong.”

“You’ve actually been fleecing us pirates.”

“Did you really think you’d always be able to take advantage of us? Today, I will teach you a lesson.”

After saying that, Qi You leapt at Hun Tong.

Gold battle qi bubbled out from his back and condensed into a pair of demonic bat wings.

He also had a flying battle skill!

The naga priestess never spoke with Qi You at all.

Qi You had refused the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ invitation.

He was wholly acting as a pirate that saw a chance for plunder, and moved in less than a heartbeat.

Hun Tong had written off how prosperous the pirates had made Two Eyes Island, and was now eating a bitter fruit.

This bitter fruit had been grown by him,

He had no choice but to fight Qi You.

If he allowed this gold level to wreak havoc on the new island, they would reap a great toll!

“What should we do?”

“This guy Qi You, I have a whole new respect for him.”

“Hehehe, Qi You is right. This is a wonderful opportunity, we must grab a huge sum.”

“Looting a gold level’s foundation under their eyes is too stimulating, I like it!!”

Dian Wan, Hu Li, Yang Cong Tou, and the others excitedly stood up, each of them contacting their pirate groups.

Although they had participated in the auction, they were only able to bring some trusted men onto the island.

This was a rule drawn up by Hun Tong.

Of course, Hun Tong wasn’t that stupidly arrogant to allow a pirate group’s main force to tread on his island.

When they wanted to trade, they would comply with his rules.

Therefore, their respective pirate groups were roaming in nearby waters.

Generally speaking, after the auction finished, the main fleets would pick up their respective commanders and sail away.

But now, after receiving their commanders’ hails, they bulged their sails, shouted out, and rushed towards the new island.

Two Eyes Island’s war situation had once more sunken into chaotic unrest.

On the new island’s seafloor, the Deep Sea Monster Fish camouflaged as a reef was of no interest to anyone and had been left in peace.

This gold level magic ship’s camouflage ability was too powerful, and no one noticed it.

Zi Di was keeping watch, controlling the magic ship and examining the entire battlefield.

“I have discovered something significant!”

She transmitted the information.

“There are many life auras hidden inside the Frozen Land Whale.”

“A conservative estimate of at least a thousand fishmen.”

“Not only that but I have also sniffed out a gold level aura, initial analysis determines it’s a cleric!”

Hun Tong didn’t notice the naga priestess, however, the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection methods laid the naga priestess bare.

“The fishmen actually have another hidden gold level?” The dragonman youngster was astonished.

Ding.

An explosion rang out and his body soared to avoid Xi Dai Zi’s magic.

Taking advantage of this chance, he swept his eyes over the battlefield.

Hun Tong and Qi You were chasing each other in the sky, Yan Ding and the fishman elder were fighting, and Song Shou was following Er Gua Zi across the shoreline.

The humans’ gold levels were pinned down, while the fishmen had one other gold level power.

“Now it’s time to see if Hun Tong has any other tricks.” The dragonman youngster thought.

The chance of this was very high.

The light cannon turrets rising had increased the dragonman youngster’s faith in this conjecture.

“I’ll need to be patient; the current situation isn’t clear yet.” The dragonman youngster decided to restrain his strength as he tangled with Xi Dai Zi and Peng Ding Zi.

The situation was deadlocked, and he secretly preserved himself to guard against not having the energy to deal with the gold level.

Zong Ge did the same.

“The fishmen have more gold levels than the humans.”

“A rare situation.”

“I have always felt that the fishmen’s intentions for attacking the island aren’t so simple!”

At the same time.

In the depths of the auction hall.

Two silver level magicians cut open a passage and stepped into the space where the blue scaled fishmen were assembled.

They were Hun Tong’s subordinates.

One was an alchemist with weak alchemist abilities; he normally presided over appraisals mostly.

The other was a pharmacist, the one who had developed the Fragrant Gill Tea.

Although the auction hall trap was broken, all kinds of signs indicated that there should be a control center hidden nearby.

Even if the Suffering Endurance Barrier didn’t have one, the Magic Power Counteraction formation must have a control formation.

Naturally, Hun Tong wouldn’t allow the continued existence of this formation, so he ordered these two to investigate and destroy it.

“Found it, it’s here!” The alchemist raised his voice.

The pharmacist swept his eyes over the blue scaled fishmen corpses, “These fishmen are all dead. No, there is still the aura of magic power in the distance.”

“A silver level life aura…”

“That side!”

The pharmacist threw a medicinal powder that melted the wall, revealing a new tunnel.

The tunnel led to an even deeper abyss.

The alchemist hesitated, “Strange, the island is an abyss of suffering right now, yet the silver level caster is incessantly drilling deeper.”

“What are they up to?”

Chapter 382: The Enduring Dragonman

The port battlefield.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

A dragon fist smashed into the spiky fishman.

The spiky fishman used his arm like a shield, but when Demolition Fist hit it, it folded instantly and his defense crumbled.

An explosion banged, and the spiky fishman was thrown into the air by the dragonman youngster.

Winning this opportunity, the dragonman youngster didn’t relentlessly pursue the spiky fishman, instead he shifted directions, and charged at Xi Dai Zi.

His feet trampled across planks, the battle skill Surge exploding with formidable power!

The dragonman youngster traveled through the battlefield, jumping across ships and occasionally running across the sides of them and leaping from masts, making his trajectory hard to make sense of.

“Peng Ding Zi is useless!” Xi Dai Zi could only bite his teeth and use his magic to stop the dragonman youngster while retreating.

The two silver levels, Xi Dai Zi and the spiky fishmen, worked together to withstand the dragonman youngster, however, they were still suppressed and were unable to get the advantage.

Although Peng Din Zi was healed by Xi Dai Zi, his battle strength hadn’t recovered, and he couldn’t regain his peak within a timely window.

The dragonman youngster was too bold and powerful and was also an expert tactician.

He never let Xi Dai Zi cast with a peaceful mind.

The spiky fishman couldn’t stop him alone.

Xi Dai Zi interfered with the dragonman youngster, and after the dragonman youngster beat back the spiky fishman, he did the same, pursuing Xi Dai Zi to greatly disturb his actions.

The distance between the dragonman youngster and Xi Dai Zi swiftly shortened.

Seeing the tall and ferocious dragonman coming at him, Xi Dai Zi could sense a strong oppression!

He took out a totem from his storage belt and threw it behind him.

The dragonman youngster instantly sidestepped it.

The totem didn’t hit him, but after it landed onto the deck, it immediately erupted with magic power.

Magic covered the area.

Even though the dragonman youngster dodged it, he was still covered by the magic.

His speed suddenly dropped by more than two thirds.

The dragonman youngster was not amazed.

This was a speed reduction totem.

Xi Dai Zi used this item to fight for time earlier.

This time was no exception.

Xi Dai Zi once again put distance between him and the dragonman youngster.

“This can’t continue!”

Xi Dai Zi was coldly sweating, and his heart contained no joy.

He only had two speed reduction totems left.

If this situation continued, he would run out of options.

“Peng Ding Zi, if you want to join the Vinegar Jar Pirates, you need to go all out and stop him for me!” Xi Dai Zi shouted in the fishman language.

When the spiky fishman heard this, he suddenly quivered and intense battle determination burst out of him.

He madly roared and threw himself at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster got out of the speed reduction magic’s range and had regained his normal agility.

But the spiky fishman was barring his way again.

Xi Dai Zi nevertheless kept moving.

He clenched his teeth as he threw out his remaining two totems from afar.

The two speed reduction totems overlapped with each other, covering both the dragonman youngster and the spiky fishman.

Soon after, Xi Dai Zi stopped and began to loudly chant.

Unlike before, he now had distance and decided to take a chance to stop and strive for more time to cast.

The spiky fishman risked his life to intercept the dragonman youngster, and he too was slowed down.

More crucially, the latter didn’t want to use all of his strength to deal with Xi Dai Zi.

Zi Di’s survey of the fishmen revealed another gold level that was hiding and hadn’t acted yet.

Xi Dai Zi’s heart slammed, he was afraid that the dragonman youngster would surmount the obstructions and leap at him, he didn’t know the dragonman youngster had actually been throwing the fight.

If his cast was disturbed, it was likely to fail. If it failed, he would suffer a backlash while dealing with the dragonman youngster coming right at him!

After a dozen seconds, Xi Dai Zi’s cast succeeded.

Only then did he breathe and relax his mind.

Magic power surged violently, causing moisture to curl around Xi Dai Zi, condensing into a many headed water snake.

Water path magic – Three Headed Water Snake!

The water snake was mammoth sized, light blue, and translucent. Within the snake, one could faintly see Xi Dai Zi.

With the water snake protection magic, Xi Dai Zi’s defense received a considerable boost.

If the dragonman youngster wanted to attack him again, he’d have to destroy the three headed water snake.

It was not Xi Dai Zi’s most powerful spell, but it was his best for both attack and defense.

In this kind of battle, this magic most suited him.

Xi Dai Zi’s choice was quite sensible.

“I did it!” Hehehe…”

Complacency welled up as Xi Dai Zi sneered, his eyes pouring with killing intent as he gazed at the dragonman youngster.

“Next shall be your moment of defeat!” Xi Dai Zi bellowed to himself, using his spirit to move the three headed water snake into a charge.

The water snake shouted and quickly surged towards the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster was in the middle of fighting Peng Ding Zi, but his attention had always been focused on Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi using the three headed water snake had been expected by the dragonman youngster.

Because beforehand, he had obtained information on it.

When Xi Dai Zi snuck onto Two Eyes Island, a fight had taken place that had exposed this battle skill.

When he heard the same chant, the dragonman youngster determined that they were going to use this spell, he audaciously relaxed and decided to stay his hand.

If it was an unknown magic, the dragonman youngster wouldn’t have let it slide.

The three headed snake charged in, and due to Peng Ding Zi and the speed reduction totems, he couldn’t retreat.

Now, it was time to see the dragonman youngster’s battle abilities.

He stamped his feet onto the deck, detonating an explosion under his dragon feet, causing his entire person to drop down through the broken deck.

Peng Ding Zi cried out, and not wanting to let the dragonman youngster escape, followed after.

He was a head shorter than the dragonman youngster, but now that the dragonman youngster had lowered himself, Peng Din Zi now had the height advantage as he raised his weapon above his head, then ferociously swung down.

The dragonman youngster wasn’t alarmed but delighted instead as he raised his palms upwards.

When they were about to touch the enemy’s weapon, battle qi bubbled out from the hollows of his palms and suddenly exploded.

The force of the explosion greatly boosted the dragonman youngster’s downward trajectory.

Two of the three headed snake’s heads tore from the left and right,, but they hit nothing.

The two heads nearly collided with each other.

The dragonman youngster had dodged his strike, leaving Xi Dai Zi exceptionally out of sorts.

He laughed nastily and used the fishman tongue to hoot, “Scuttle away, I’ll find where you’re hiding!”

The three headed water snake under his control crashed into the level below the deck.

Xi Dai Zi was a deep sea fishman and had dark eyesight; the sudden change from light to darkness below deck did not disturb him at all.

But as soon as he charged down, he saw the dragonman youngster preparing an attack!

Battle skill – Surge!

Battle skill – Demolition Fist!

The dragonman youngster launched like a whirlwind!

Xi Dai Zi panicked, and due to their closeness, he couldn’t dodge.

However, he soon realized that he was within the three headed water snake had had layers of defense.

Xi Dai Zi was not a close ranged combatant, and his caster habits had caused him to slow a beat as he moved the three headed water snake’s nape to defend himself.

Boom!

A great clamor.

When both sides met with force, it generated a strong shockwave.

So strong that even the silver fighter Peng Ding Zi was forced back by the shockwave.

The tempestuous shockwave burst apart the ship.

For a split second, the second level deck became the first level deck, the bow house and the aft house both rocked, and the mast lifted up a bit, then fell into the sea.

The entire ship split in half, opening it wide open.

The shockwave dissipated the moisture.

The dragonman youngster jumped back, his gait relaxed.

Xi Dai Zi was pale, and his face was smeared with fear.

His three headed water snake only had one head left, the other two were severed necks and there was also a distinct crater in the snake’s belly. The crater’s bottom was less than a meter from Xi Dai Zi.

“He has the power to blow up the three headed water snake!” After realizing this, Xi Dai Zi suddenly felt that he had advanced too prematurely.

He immediately retreated and hoped that Peng Ding Zi would come to help him out of this trouble.

But when he turned around to look, he saw that Peng Ding Zi was in the process of stuffing seaweed into his mouth.

Xi Dai Zi: …..

He felt both indignant and powerless.

Because he could make out the seaweed Peng Ding Zi was eating grew on the seafloor and was a commonly used alchemy material that could be used to make most healing potions on the market.

The fishman clan had limited resources and didn’t have a pharmacist.

Therefore, fishmen often gathered these aquatic plants and ate them raw to recover their strength.

Peng Ding Zi was eating these aquatic plants because he was out of battle qi and needed to replenish it now.

He was not like the dragonman youngster.

He had been fighting for a long time, while the dragonman youngster had fought for half as long as him.

The Spike Fishman bloodline also couldn’t compare the dragonman youngster’s legendary bloodline, therefore him running out of battle qi first was normal.

Perhaps due to this warning, Xi Dai Zi’s eyes flashed as he looked at the dragonman youngster again.

He knew the dragonman youngster cultivated the Bursting Explosion battle qi technique.

This information had been known by many people for a long time.

The Bursting Explosion battle qi technique was widely known for its powerful might and severe expenditure.

“Wait a moment, why isn’t he showing signs of battle qi exhaustion?”

“Even if he joined the battle late, this last fight was extremely intense, how could he still have abundant battle qi?”

“Impossible!”

“Even if he isn’t a spent force, his battle qi reserves are very dangerous.”

When Xi Dai Zi thought of this, his battle intent was invigorated and most of his fear dissipated.

But the dragonman youngster was not as inundated with feeling as he, and after a bit of adjustments, he charged at the three headed water snake again.

Bang, boom, ding!

Explosions rang in succession.

Xi Dai Zi fought back with all of his strength, but the three headed water snake’s enormous body manifested a systemic problem.

Due to its slow movements, it had nearly become the dragonman youngster’s punching bag.

“How can this be?”

Xi Dai Zi was beaten up, and although his body wasn’t harmed, his magic power was being fiercely depleted and his spirit power was drying up too, bringing pain to his entire head.

“How much battle qi does he have?”

The dragonman youngster was as vigorous as a dragon and as fierce as a tiger!

The dragonman youngster’s legend level bloodline gave him a battle qi reserve and recovery rate far beyond that of any normal person.

If Xi Dai Zi knew that the dragonman youngster wasn’t going all out, who knows what he would think?

Chapter 383: Medicine Jar

In another place.

Zong Ge and the knight fishman were fighting.

Without a mount, the fishman was slow, but his exoskeleton was still unyieldingly thick, and his defense was unsurpassed.

This let him make bolder and more unrestrained moves.

Zong Ge couldn’t compare.

His armor was not silver level.

Therefore, many times, he had to dodge or block, and due to that, his attack frequency naturally decreased.

Although Zi Di had obtained many treasures from the auction, they couldn’t be used immediately.

If they were, the relationship between the two would be exposed and the risk of their true identities being exposed would greatly increase.

After all, “coincidentally having the same treasure” would foment suspicion.

Battle skill – Forest of Spears!

The 2 meter long lance thrusted ten times in mere seconds.

The speed of which left behind many illusory lance images in one’s retina.

The lance images were a wall that pressed towards Zong Ge.

Faced with this powerful offensive, Zong Ge didn’t bat an eyelid and harbored no ideas of retreat.

It couldn’t be intercepted with normal speeds.

Then, he would use Fast Fight!

Battle skill – Fast Fight

Silver level battle qi quickly circulated through his body, speeding up Zong Ge’s movement and attacks.

Ting, ding, ting…

A series of metal clangs pattered like rain.

Relying on his left handed shield and right handed sword, Zong Ge blocked all of the lance images.

The Steel Bone Knight fishman suddenly became discouraged.

Especially when he saw Zong Ge’s expressionless manner, causing his discouragement and powerlessness to aggravate further.

No matter how he attacked, Zong Ge could block them all.

He gave the impression of being as steady as a mountain peak.

Especially when Zong Ge changed his weapon, the feeling got stronger after he swapped to a sword and shield.

“Is he throwing?”

“Is he using all of his strength?”

The Steel Bone Knight fishman often thought that, impairing his fighting spirit by a level.

But soon after, his gaze paused.

He had noticed something entirely new.

This new discovery caused his eyes to suddenly flash and his battle intent ignited once again.

“He’s wounded!”

The fishman knight was pleasantly surprised to find that Zong Ge’s arm was seeping with blood.

“Did I do that?” The Steel Bone Knight fishman couldn’t help but wonder, “It seems my opponent isn’t invincible! I still have hope!!”

It was actually Zong Ge’s old injury.

Back from Mystifying Monster Island’s underground control center.

Since then, Zong Ge had tried to heal his arm injury.

During the auction, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had obtained a healing potion.

The gold level healing potion was enough to completely cure his broken arm!

This healing potion was on Zong Ge right now.

But he didn’t use it.

For one, his old injury relapsing could give the enemy hope and drag the fight on longer.

Zong Ge was an outstanding general, and knew the fishmen were still hiding a gold level cultivator.

He wanted to do his best to improve his control over the battlefield.

If he drew a gold level cultivator’s attack, then he would be in a passive situation and wouldn’t be able to do much at all.

On the other hand, the gold level potion was very precious.

If Zong Ge was wounded further, he could use the gold potion to heal his entire body.

Using it now was rather wasteful.

Leaving it unused could prevent some accidents.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge held the advantage, however, the port battlefield situation was changing.

There were no longer two crucial fights, now there were three.

The outcome of the third fight would decide who won this battlefield.

The third fight was not against a person, but the Vinegar Jar.

The gold level pirate group’s flagship was very dauntless, it had charged into all kinds of magic traps and slew many ships leaving port until finally crashing through ships and coming ashore.

The Vinegar Jar had run aground.

But its constant firepower made it a gigantic turret that reinforced the fishmen’s firepower.

Not only that, but there were still many murderous fishmen pirates disembarking from it.

With these fresh troops, the fishmen’s offensive did not dimmish, but expanded further.

If one were to say it in terms of battle progress, the humans had the advantage at the higher echelons due to the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, while on the lower echelons, the fishmen were suppressing the humans due to the spirited reinforcements from the Vinegar Jar.

“Follow me, kill these wretched fishmen!!” Commander Tie Bang roared.

“Seize the Vinegar Jar!” Shouted Commander Beng Dai.

The two people together were the spear tip that cleared the way for the troops behind them.

Ahead of them were layers of fishmen defensive lines, but against the two silver fighters’ all-out assault, they were parted like the water surging at ships, and even if there was a continuous stream of fishmen trying to stop them, they were torn to shreds and turned into mere ocean spray.

Countless hopeful human eyes gazed at the two commanders.

Their charge had encouraged and inspired the human fighters.

To protect the Vinegar Jar, more than half the fishmen returned to it to attack the two commanders and their troops.

The two commanders and their mercenaries were united as one.

Their comrades and relatives were slaughtered.

The two mercenary corps’ flagships had been attacked and their crew massacred by Er Gua Zi.

Vengeance was what motivated the charging mercenaries the most.

Many fishmen toppled over.

The two commanders slaughtered countless and grew somewhat lenient.

No new silver level fighter appeared on the fishmen’s side.

The two commanders had no opponents on their level and advanced.

Soon after, their blood drenched bodies successfully climbed aboard, and they began to attack the Vinegar Jar’s first deck.

“I want the Vinegar Jar!”

“By seizing this ship, we’ll settle the score!!”

The two commanders glanced at each other, quickly reaching an accord.

They were very excited.

One was the thrill of revenge; the other was that the Vinegar Jar was worth far more than the Iron Lump and the White Bandage combined.

The Vinegar Jar was a silver level magic ship, while the other two were mere bronze levels.

The mercenaries behind them also boarded and killed their way to their commanders’ side.

The two commanders went to the left and right, destroying and killing everything in front of them, annihilating all of the fishmen on the first floor.

They joined back together at the ship’s stairs, ready to storm into the Vinegar Jar.

One of the commanders took out a potion and drank it down.

The other took out an alchemy item.

The potion was a water breathing potion.

The alchemy item was an Air Mask. It could generate air within the mask, allowing the user to freely breathe.

The two mercenary corps had made a living on this island, such equipment were standard.

It wasn’t just the commanders; the other mercenaries had been allocated with similar things too.

The only difference was the level used; they were inferior to the two commanders’ superior quality goods.

The two commanders used their items as they followed the stairs down to the deck below.

Behind them, many mercenaries were following.

The Vinegar Jar was a magic ship built specifically for fishmen; its first deck was covered in water that went up to an adult human’s knees.

The lower decks were completely submerged.

This was undoubtably the fishmen’s turf, that gave the attackers many disadvantages.

However, the two commanders’ joint attack was still successful in every endeavor.

There were many fishmen fighting in the dark water, but without a silver, they couldn’t hold back the two commanders’ advance.

Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai were so engrossed in their slaughter that they unwittingly separated from their troops behind them.

As for the other mercenaries, fighting underwater greatly affected them.

The fishmen were routed by the commanders, but they were able to kill the other human mercenaries.

The two commanders did notice this, but they had no good answer for it.

How could they advance if they were defending their troops?

If that happened, they would be pinned down by the fishmen.

Compassion could not lead an army.

The two commanders had led their corps for many years and understood that if the enemy led them by the nose, their entire team would sink into desperate straits.

They chose to charge in an attempt to seize the Vinegar Jar’s control hub.

Every magic ship had a control hub.

Similar to the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s cockpit.

Finally, the two killed their way to the bottom hold of the Vinegar Jar.

The water here was dark, dense, and even viscous.

The two commanders had illumination skills and after identifying where the central cabin was, they immediately cut their way there.

A skinny fishman was in a dark corner.

The two commanders instantly slowed down.

Despite the fishman not directly intercepting them, his silver level aura gave them qualms.

“As expected, the Vinegar Jar has a silver level hidden in it.”

“It’s just one silver; can they stop the both of us?”

“He looks sickly, let’s take care of him first, then set sail!”

“Be careful. Er Gau Zi lets that guy keep watch here, he isn’t simple.”

“I don’t think so! If he was strong enough, he would have stepped in already. Why should he still be sitting as we came in?”

The two commanders had the methods needed to communicate unrestricted underwater.

After discussing it somewhat, they attacked the silver level fishmen together.

The silver level fishman was as thin as a match and too weak to stand up to the wind. When he saw the two truculent commanders attacking him, he needed to be oppressed by them and began to weakly cough.

But this cough caused the two commanders to change their expressions.

“Eh, how bizarre!”

“I’m poisoned!!”

Ten minutes later.

Pop.

A wave of water suddenly bubbled onto the first deck of the Vinegar Jar, from which Commander Beng Dai emerged.

Commander Beng Dai’s entire body twisted with bandages, his vigor taking a devastating decline and panic streaking across his face.

“Get away!” He yelled at the humans on deck.

“Commander!”

“Where is my commander?”

The human mercenaries turned pale with fright.

The contract between before and after was too great.

“Commander Tie Bang is dead already, this water is poisonous, don’t go in!” After leaving these words behind, Commander Beng Dai was the first to leap off and escape down the road.

Quick witted mercenaries followed closely behind.

The rest of the humans were killed fishmen who emerged soon after.

The silver level overseeing the Vinegar Jar was a skillful commander, he had adopted a defensive counterattack stratagem that first lured in a human assault, then exploited the favorable terrain to kill one silver and wound the other.

Afterwards, they smoothly launched their massive counterattack, and with the smallest price possible, completely routed the human troops.

Chapter 384: Pirate Chaos

“Yao Guan Zi didn’t disappoint me! Well done!” Despite controlling the three headed water snake, Xi Dai Zi never stopped paying attention to the Vinegar Jar.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates’ commander Er Gua Zi was a gold level, and he had three silver level commanders.

One was Xi Dai Zi, his first mate, a fishman shaman.

The second was Yao Guan Zi, his second mate, a silver level fighter, however he had many illnesses since childhood and relied on large amounts of medication to stay alive.

His third mate was Man Xing Zi, but because he was too slow, he wasn’t able to join the fishman’s attack.

“But if that happened, the Vinegar Jar’s water supply would be completely poisoned, and the other fishmen cannot remain inside.” Xi Dai Zi knew what Yao Guan Zi had done.

Although Yao Guan Zi was a silver level, his direct combat ability was very weak.

If it were in a straight up fight, he wouldn’t be the two commanders’ rival.

The only thing he had was his bloodline’s racial magic that made poisonous water.

This poisonous water was very devastating that even a gold level like Er Gua Zi found touching it unbearable.

Therefore, all of the Vinegar Jar’s fishmen came out, none dared to stay in the poisonous water.

“Oh dear, there is nothing to be done…”

Bang.

The dragonman youngster’s fist burst apart the water snake’s head, interrupting Xi Dai Zi’s train of thought.

“He seized upon the moment my mind wandered. This dragonman…” Xi Dai Zi grit his teeth and retreated.

Fortunately, the spiky fishman attacked in time to help him out of this predicament.

Xi Dai Zi promptly released his magic power and regrew the three headed water snake’s missing heads.

Xi Dai Zi turned pale.

He sorrowfully noticed that his magic power reserves were running out. More than half of his magic power had been used to maintain the three headed water snake magic.

The dragonman youngster was still moving like the wind, and his offensive didn’t slow in the slightest.

This enduring battle capability was beyond Xi Dai Zi’s, and he sensed how powerful his enemy was.

The Vinegar Jar’s bottom cabin.

In the dark cockpit.

“Cough, cough, cough.” Yao Guan Zi was constantly coughing, and with every cough, he coughed out blood.

Commander Tie Bang’s and Beng Dai’s attacks had seriously injured him.

But now was not the time to rest and reorganize.

Yao Guan Zi was not healthy, and healing was more troublesome for him, thus, he wouldn’t be able to be healed in a short time span.

Yao Guan Zi also knew his situation.

He had already realized that even though it might cost him his life, he would use everything he had to help his clan take back their homeland!

After seeing the other fishmen all advance out of the ship, he began to control the Vinegar Jar.

The Vinegar Jar’s bottom suddenly deformed, exposing countless jet holes.

From these jet holes sprayed several streams of water.

The powerful streams scoured the ground, while simultaneously the reaction force converged on the Vinegar Jar, causing it to slowly rotate.

The Vinegar Jar’s spin accelerated, and it began to move.

It was like a black steel hurricane, effortlessly cleaning its path through the crowded port battlefield.

The end of this path was the light cannon turret.

Ding.

The Vinegar Jar smashed into the light cannon turret.

The light cannon turret had been firing everything it had at the Vinegar Jar, but it couldn’t stop it.

The light cannon tower’s advantages and disadvantages were obvious.

The advantages was its relatively cheap cost and offensive abilities.

The corresponding weaknesses were that it was rather frail.

This was why when Hun Tong had secretly built more, he kept them hidden underground.

The light cannon tower could not withstand the careening silver level magic ship.

The collision instantly smashed the light cannon tower into smithereens.

Then the crumbling light cannon tower was crushed by the Vinegar Jar into a pile of slag.

The Vinegar Jar slowed as it pulverized the light cannon turret.

But it quickly sped back up and rampaged through the port battlefield in an arc, crushing everything in its path.

It cut a blood path through the battlefield towards the other light cannon turret.

The light cannon turret personnel were frightened and ashen.

But even if they wanted to flee, there was not enough time to do so.

They did their best to wildly fire the light cannon turret into the Vinegar Jar.

Ding.

They failed to save themselves.

A second light cannon turret was crushed by the Vinegar Jar.

The fishmen’s morale soared!

The human fighters were stunned.

As it turns out, the Vinegar Jar didn’t strand itself, for it had a special ability that allowed it to jet water from its bottom.

Thus, even though it was on dry land, the Vinegar Jar temporarily could create a thin wave of water that allowed it to move freely anywhere!

Because of Yao Guan Zi, the port battlefield swung back into the fishmen’s favor.

Seeing the Vinegar Jar wreaking havoc through the battlefield like a monster, even someone as staid as Zong Ge changed color.

Luckily the Vinegar Jar didn’t attack the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

It wasn’t because the enemy was merciful or stupid.

Rather the light cannon towers far surpassed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ordinary ships in perceived threat level.

At the same time, there were many humans and fishmen grappling with each other around the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

In these circumstances, the Vinegar Jar running through here would definitely crush its fellow fishmen, causing not only immense casualties, but also gravely strike at their morale.

“Hahaha, another one torn down, keep demolishing them!” Dian Wan shouted with great excitement.

He was the Lightning Pirates’ commander, and when he saw Qi You attack, along with Two Eyes Island’s other gold powers unable to stop him, his banditry tendencies flared up.

The light cannon turrets were an immense threat to the pirates’ ships.

After all, besides their flagships, most of the pirates’ ships were ordinary vessels.

Therefore, despite being a human, Dian Wan took pleasure in seeing the light cannon turrets topple.

“Qi You’s ships will attack the island.” Yang Cong Tou anxiously said.

He was the Stench Pirates’ leader, and although he had sent the signal, his fleet still needed time to arrive.

The Ten Touch Pirates were the first to act, therefore they arrived first.

The Ten Touch Pirates’ commander Qi You was in the middle of fighting Hun Tong, fighting for his fleet’s arrival.

To the side, Hu Li was gazing more at the Vinegar Jar.

“That silver level magic ship has such might!”

“Truly outstanding.”

“If I could have such a ship, robbing islands would be so much easier!”

“I say, the Vinegar Jar’s watery interior also suits frogmen.”

Hu Li was a frogman, and the Applaud Pirates he led was made entirely of frogmen.

Concomitant with the three pirate commanders’ expectations, on the horizon, three fleets sailed into view.

Pirate flags were fluttering high, and the pirates aboard were abnormally excited.

Soon after, many more pirate ships appeared.

These were smaller pirate groups.

Every year during the auction, there were always pirate fleets traveling nearby.

Pirates adventured to join the auction, and to develop his island, Hun Tong did not dispatch his fleet to chase away or destroy these fleets.

The fishmen assaulting Two Eyes Island took note of these fleets.

As soon as they obtained their captain’s signal, they immediately took action.

From above, Two Eyes Island’s new island looked like a gigantic whale, while the innumerable pirate ships were like sharks that were encircling it from all directions.

They actively avoided the port battlefield and tried to land on the island in other places.

The pirates would undoubtably make the war situation on Two Eyes Island more complicated.

“Those damned pirates!”

“You are humans, yet you don’t unite, instead you want to loot a burning house!!”

The merchant alliance representatives cursed one after the other.

They anxiously burned with rage.

Benefits deeply bound them and Hun Tong, and the new island not only had their shops, but also a lot of inventory, liquid capital, and much more.

But now, they couldn’t get back to save their stores.

Because their entire fleets were caught in the battlefield and doing battle with the Vinegar Jar Pirates, the battlefield situation was very gridlocked.

By helping the fleet, they would abandon their stores.

Returning to save the stores would mean they would abandon their fleets.

To every merchant alliance large and small, this was undoubtably a choice full of pain and challenges.

“None of those three mercenary corps are useful!”

“Where is the patrol fleet? Where did they run off to?”

The merchant alliance representatives knew they were powerless to do anything and were anxiously searching for external aid.

Hun Tong, Song Shou, and Yan Ding had their opponents.

Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet wanted to charge the new island, but when the fishmen’s main force appeared, One Eye chose to return to do his best to stop the fishman army.

Currently, the fishmen’s overall offensive situation was very explicit.

They were divided into two parts, one on the old island, the other on the new island.

But what was quite strange was that the fishmen’s main force was headed towards the old island, while the main force attacking the new island was the Vinegar Jar Pirates, a group with fewer people.

“The new island is floating up, while the old island will soon sink to the seabed. What are these fishmen doing?” Cang Xu communicated with Zi Di through an alchemy device.

He had already evacuated from the battlefield and hid in a secret place to rest and reorganize.

He had been wounded in his fight against the spiky fishman, such was obvious to all, and it undoubtably made for a good excuse for Cang Xu to pull back.

Zi Di replied, “Almost all of the old island’s facilities and personnel have been moved already. If the fishmen want to take back their homeland, they must first destroy the human installations and armies on the new island.”

“The fishmen came prepared, and there is another gold level hiding in the main army. Their true purpose is an open question.”

“Furthermore, I feel that Hun Tong’s side still has strength, he hasn’t used everything yet.”

Zi Di was constantly observing the fights between the gold levels through the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

The fights the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others battled concerned the outcome of the port battlefield.

While the outcome of the gold level battles would affect the entire conclusion of the battle for Two Eyes Island.

This was the world of the strong.

If one gold level fight was determined, it would cause a chain reaction and influence other gold level fights.

When all of these outcomes were decided, the remaining gold levels would sweep across the battlefield and overturn the war situation.

It was like when Er Gua Zi singlehandedly slaughtered everyone on the Iron Lump and White Bandage effortlessly.

Another communication device chimed next to Cang Xu.

It was a merchant alliance representative.

The other side offered a high price to hire the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to protect their new island store.

Cang Xu muttered to himself, first saying that at present, they couldn’t do anything. But if the war situation changed and circumstances allowed, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would take on this mission and protect their store.

The merchant alliance was unresigned and raised the price, attempting to entice the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps into abandoning their ships, breaking out of the siege, and going to protect their merchant alliance store.

The other party really was thinking too much!

Cang Xu sneered to himself, for even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was able to do so, he would still refuse.

Chapter 385: Plundering and Defending

Iron anchors entered the water.

Sails were put away.

Pirate fleets slowly stopped.

Landing skiffs were released, each one packed with pirates.

Because this wasn’t the port, there were few places to dock ships.

The pirates were familiar with such circumstances.

Because they often stopped by uninhabited islands to rest, and scrounged up as much potable water, food, and other supplies as possible.

The amimated pirates came ashore.

They met barely any obstructions along the way.

Two Eyes Island’s defenses were almost all entangled with the fishmen.

This was indeed a rare golden opportunity to plunder!

“You’re finally here. Follow me! Now!” Dian Wan was impatient.

On the other coast.

Yang Cong Tou also linked up with his underlings.

“Boss, which store will we loot?” An underling asked.

Yang Cong Tou shook his head, “We are not robbing a store. You have no ambition! We will rob the richest place!”

“The city government palace?” The underling inevitably hesitated, “If we besiege the government palace, what will we do if Hun Tong comes? Qi You won’t stop him for us.”

Yang Cong Tou shook his head, “The city government palace doesn’t have as much money as the Wealth Church.”

“The church priest has led his underlings to help the defense of the city guard barracks.”

“Now, that place is empty.”

“Robbing that place won’t provoke Hun Tong.”

The underlings’ eyes gleamed, and they roared, “You’re brilliant boss!”

The Stench Pirates aggressively rushed towards the Wealth Church.

“Defend, defend!”

“Defend the store, I’ll give everyone that does three month’s pay.”

The Secret Medicine store manager shouted as he attempted to organize a defensive line.

But pirates with swords held high were streaming in.

To engage in oceanic trade and transportation, the merchant alliance servants and salespeople of course had battle strength.

But they weren’t the main fighting force and were in charge of daily operations.

They were also far fewer in number compared to the pirates.

The Secret Medicine Store beat back three waves of pirates, but it came at a high price. Not only were their personnel gravely depleted, but their morale was quickly falling.

They were not an army and hadn’t trained that much. The bitter battle and casualties inflicted on their fellows brought anxiety and panic to the survivors.

Eventually, the fourth wave of pirates was the far more reputable Applaud Pirates.

Faced with the frogman Hu Li leading the charge, the Secret Medicine Store’s defensive line voluntarily crumbled.

The assistants inside the shop fled in the face of battle, actually giving the pirates a better opportunity. Before long, they were chased down and killed.

“Crew, grab it all for me!”

“Open your big eyes, search well.”

“Don’t let a single potion slip by.”

“This is all money.”

Hu Li burst into laughter.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance was one of the six great merchant alliances of the empire; they mainly engaged in magic medicine.

Every bottle of medicine was of guaranteed quality.

These were always fitting things to steal.

Because medicine was small and light, yet very valuable.

After the pirates stole them, they could either use them or sell them.

The marketplace always had a high demand for magic potions.

The Secret Medicine Store had fallen into enemy hands!

This information was transmitted to the other stores, causing the remaining four big stores, especially, to panic.

The Flint Store had comparatively fewer pirates attacking it.

For one, as one of the six great merchant alliances, they had top of the line defenses.

It was a difficult and big bone for the pirates to gnaw on.

On the other hand, they should be grateful for what they sell.

The Flint Merchant Alliance mainly engaged in the mining, transportation, and sale of ores.

These things were heavy and difficult to transport.

If the pirates stole them, these ores would be a burden that required people to keep watch over them.

Finally, their merchant alliance representative had gotten a cultivator’s aid.

The gunman Mei Ban!

A large group of pirates charged the entrance of the Flint Store.

The leading pirate was iron level.

“Boss, you have something on you.” An underling stated.

“What thing?” The pirate boss wiped his face, there was nothing on it.

“A bit of ash, it looks like a spot,” Replied the underling.

After the pirate boss heard this, he immediately changed color, “Oh no!”

Ding.

A moment later, his head exploded like a falling watermelon.

Red and white splashed everywhere, including onto the stupefied pirates around him.

The gunman Mei Ban had killed the pirate boss from the top of the building, where he did his utmost to conceal himself.

His weapon was a long rifle.

The rifle had an excellent targeting device.

When he killed the pirate boss, the other pirates reacted by immediately fleeing in disarray.

Mei Ban didn’t chase after them.

He was saving his bullets as much as possible.

“Under the circumstances, I shouldn’t show off.”

“As long as the Flint Store is well protected.”

Common rewards wouldn’t move the silver level loner.

But the Flint Shop representative was willing to pay with rare ore.

Mei Ban needed that ore to refine a special bullet.

Mei Ban reloaded on ammunition and surveyed the perimeter.

He quickly noticed something abnormal at the Wealth Church.

Dian Wan was charging into the Wealth Church with his pirates.

“Alas.” The Fertile Land priest sighed as he stood in the church gate, “Trouble still came, if I had known earlier, I would have gone with the Wealth priest to help the barracks.”

After the auction hall trap was broken, the Fertile Land priest didn’t join the battle.

He decided to stay at the Wealth Church to recuperate.

The Wealth Church priest needed to protect his parish and had to go to war, but the Fertile Land priest had no such responsibility.

The Wealth priest tried to persuade him to join the battle, but the Fertile Land priest refused.

He stated that he could help the Wealth priest take care of the church.

He didn’t anticipate what was happening now.

He had just fought alongside these pirates to break out of the auction hall trap, but now they were on opposite sides.

“Old Fertile Land chap, I don’t want you to die, move aside. This is not your church!” Dian Wan threatened him.

The Fertile Land priest took out his sacred hoe and held it in his hand, “Young man, don’t be so brash. You got quite the guts to ransack a church, to be an enemy of the Gods!”

Dian Wan cackled, “The Gods are indeed great, but the great Gods’ gaze won’t linger on this small church located on this remote island.”

“If you don’t scram, I’ll attack!”

The Fertile Land priest sighed and strictly replied, “Then come. No matter what you want to do, you’ll need to get past me first.”

Dian Wan chuckled, raised his fists, and rushed forth.

His two fists crackled with lightning as he engaged the Fertile Land priest in close quarters combat.

Although the Fertile Land priest was a cleric, he wasn’t skilled in close quarters combat.

He relied on divine spells to increase his bodily essence and used the sacred hoe to withstand Dian Wan’s fists.

For a moment, both sides were equally matched as they fought each other.

Dian Wan hurriedly cried out.

The first mate behind him led the pirates around the two fighting to storm the church.

The Fertile Land priest could only watch on helplessly.

Dian Wan stopped him, and his hands were tied.

The wandering ronin Ku Feng was walking an empty street.

In days past, this street weaved and streamed with people. But now, only Ku Feng and a store assistant were traveling through it.

The store worker was persistently beseeching Ku Feng for his help, “My Lord, please help, save our store.”

“Your strength can stave off most of the pirates.”

“The boss said to name your price!”

“We have no other options…”

But Ku Feng shook his head, “You saw the corpse.”

“That was the city guard commander.”

“The fishman who beheaded him is an expert, one who uses a rare and peculiar blade skill.”

“I must find him!”

To Ku Feng, mercenary mission rewards were just how he earned a living.

His true pursuit was improving his skill with the blade.

“If I fight this strong fishman, my blade skill can improve.” Said Ku Feng.

When the store employee saw his determination, he had no choice but to say, “I know where the fishman is.”

“Oh?”

“But Lord Ku Feng!” The store employee gazed at Ku Feng, “You have to promise that after you beat the fishman that you’ll accept our offer and protect our store.”

Ku Feng sneered and refused, “They are a silver level, a very conspicuous expert.”

“With a small inquiry, I can find out where he is.”

“I don’t need to agree to defend your store.”

The store employee turned white and was forced to say, “Lord Ku Feng, my mistake.”

“I will tell you that the fishman is at the city guard barracks.”

This response actually caused Ku Feng to respect the former somewhat more.

Ku Feng left this behind, “I will go to him at once. If I have time, I will hurry back to you.”

As he watched Ku Feng leave, the store employee shouted, “Lord Ku Feng, please return soon.”

“The barracks is over there, they have the Lord Priest and his people providing assistance, along with the light cannon tower, they are winning!”

When Ku Feng heard this, his footsteps accelerated.

He was a bit worried about the fishmen.

He didn’t want the fishman blade expert to already be dead by the time he got there.

The barracks battlefield.

Hong Bo gasped for air; his body cut all over.

He looked around, fishmen growing increasingly sparse.

Ever since the Wealth Church priest intervened, the fishmen were losing.

The battle damage between the two sides changed severely, and the fishmen were suffering disastrous damage.

This was the value of clerics!

On the battlefield, healing divine spells could fix many injuries on the spot.

In addition to this, there were also many buffing divine spells.

Furthermore, clerics didn’t just bring divine spells to the front lines.

There was also morale!

One healing while their enemies weren’t created an immense psychological advantage.

By contrast, the fishmen were watching human fighters they had maimed through untold hardships return, vigorous and ready to kill. Even though their will was steady, they were discouraged and wavering.

Chapter 386: The Barracks Battlefield

When the priest first led his team to war, there were fewer humans than fishmen.

But now, the fishmen numbers had been cut down to equal the humans, and were continuing to be substantially weakened.

As the fishmen leaders, Dao Hen and Hong Bo naturally knew they had to eliminate the priest and the others.

But they also knew that actually doing so was very difficult.

The humans knew the importance of these reinforcements and guarded them with their lives.

Dao Hen and Hong Bo attacked, and although they could cause the human defensive line to waver, this wasn’t an ideal result.

The priest and the other clergy were standing on top of the light cannon turret.

The turret was surrounded by city guards.

There were also Wealth Church temple guards inside the lower strata of the light cannon turret.

Bang.

A fireball exploded into the fishmen’s ranks.

Ten fishmen died in the explosion.

The attack came from the side of the barracks.

“It’s Shi Fa!” Soon after, a human soldier identified the help.

Shi Fa hurried to the barracks battlefield.

“I have received my Mercenary Guild mission and have come to help defend!” Said Shi Fa.

Merchants weren’t the only ones issuing missions to hire help at huge sums, Hun Tong had done so too.

Two Eyes Island had many loners, and there was no lack of silver level experts among them. If this power was not exploited, it would be rather wasteful.

Unlike Mei Ban, Shi Fa didn’t choose to be employed by a merchant alliance, instead he chose to help Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord.

“Let him come in!” The human fighters cheered.

Shi Fa was a silver level magician, undoubtedly strong aid.

At once, human soldiers gushed out to welcome and brought Shi Fa into their position.

Once that happened, Shi Fa would be defended by layers of human fighters.

He constantly cast all kinds of magic that pounded into the fishmen like rain.

Shi Fa’s signature feature was his magic playing cards.

These playing cards were small sized magic scrolls.

Shi Fa was fluent in this rarely seen alchemy skill.

Whenever he used his spirit to trigger a playing card, he could instantly use the magic inside the playing card.

Various magic spells fell like a torrential rainstorm.

Fishmen died at a terrible rate.

This was the straw that broke the fishmen’s back.

“Retreat!” Although he was extremely unwilling, Hong Bo still grinded his teeth and sounded the withdrawal.

If this continued, the fishmen would all die.

Even if all of the fishmen sacrificed themselves, they couldn’t capture the human barracks.

The fishmen could only retreat.

Hong Bo and Dao Hen brought up the rear.

Naturally, the human soldiers wouldn’t sit by and watch the enemy withdraw, and they immediately advanced towards them.

Hong Bo and Dao Hen did their utmost to stop them.

They were silver levels and stiffly relied on their personal strength to obstruct the pursuing human army.

Dao Hen was especially dangerous, and despite not using battle skills, his own battle techniques made him a constant threat.

As long as he hit a soldier, they would fall miserably and lose their life.

Essentially, no one could match three moves with Dao Hen.

Magic – Spider Web.

Magic – Electric Shock.

When Shi Fa saw the situation, his finger flicked two magic cards, casting two magic spells at nearly the same time.

A spider web fell from the sky and covered Dao Hen.

Lightning accurately struck Hong Bo.

Hong Bo trembled all over, he took a step back, and his weapon nearly fell from his hand.

Dao Hen was clearly stronger.

He used a battle skill, and his forearm blade fins glinted with silvery light.

Dao Hen brandished his forearm knife fins, cutting the spider web apart and easily escaped from it.

“grann”

Dao Hen grabbed Hong Bo’s arm and ran off, leaving the battlefield.

“After them! Don’t let them escape.” The human soldiers angrily pursued relentlessly.

The troops were motivated, but the city guard auxiliary leader was hesitant.
