He deeply understood that based on how strong he was, if he brazenly pursued, he might be the one who would be slain.

The auxiliary leader asked for Shi Fa’s and the priest’s opinion.

Shi Fa spoke candidly, “My mission is to help protect the barracks. However, I can pursue the fishman too. But I need another reward for that.”

The priest spoke from his knowledge and experience, “We must get in touch with the Feudal Lord and wait for his orders.”

Two Eyes Island was in chaos.

The barracks battlefield had just calmed down, while the port battlefield was still raging.

Large amounts of pirates had already come ashore and were looting everywhere.

Hun Tong and the other gold levels were busy fighting their opponents and weren’t able to attend to other things.

The city guard auxiliary commander promptly contacted Hun Tong.

Hun Tong fought Qi You while learning of this news from an alchemy device.

This was good news.

Hun Tong thought on it, glanced at the battlefield, and firmly ordered, “Defend the barracks!”

The barracks’ human troops were greater than Hong Bo’s and Dao Hen’s troops.

But their military power was heavily dependent on the priest, Shi Fa, and the light cannon tower in the middle of the barracks.

If they pursued, the city guard would lose their locational advantage, and even if they eradicated Hong Bo and Dao Hen, they would pay a heavy price for it.

In fact, pursuing had other risks.

Such as those plundering pirates everywhere.

The city guard was likely to clash with pirates, and would suffer losses from them, and could even be surrounded and annihilated by them.

The port battlefield situation was another essential factor.

From Hun Tong’s point of view, he saw the vast might of the Vinegar Jar. And that the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge “powerless” to beat their respective opponents.

Hun Tong had to consider the overall situation.

If they lost the port battlefield, he needed a new place to make a stand on the island.

That would be the barracks.

Hearing Hun Tong give the order to defend, the city guard auxiliary commander promptly complied and secretly relaxed.

He was only iron level.

After the city guard commander was slain, he assumed leadership during this crisis, summoning up the courage to shoulder the responsibility of command, the pressure was exceedingly massive on him.

He didn’t want to risk pursuing. Iron level strength on this battlefield did not give him a sense of security.

Shi Fa shrugged his shoulders high, “Since that’s the case, then my mission to protect the barracks is complete. I’ll take my leave first.”

The Mercenary Guild still had plenty of defense missions.

To a silver level loner, this was a golden opportunity to make money.

Shi Fa left the barracks at once.

As he saw Shi Fa depart, the Wealth Church priest also let out a breath.

The haze of an undiscovered traitor lingered in the priest’s heart.

Two Eyes Island was the Wealth Goddess’ parish, which meant this was the priest’s domain somewhat.

When Shi Fa rushed to provide assistance, the priest remained vigilant.

Therefore, the priest and the other clergy remained inside the light cannon turret, while the closest Shi Fa got was at the base of the turret, he never entered it.

“We’ll also be leaving.” The priest said his goodbye to the auxiliary leader.

The looting pirates had given him a new worry.

He had to return to protect his church.

However, just as the priest and other clergy were about to depart, fishmen sprung up again.

“Enemy attack, enemy attack!”

“Those fishmen came back. They dared to return?!”

“Lord Priest, don’t leave!”

The human soldiers were alarmed and prepared for battle at once.

The priest and other clergy rushed back upstairs.

“Were the fishmen reinforced?”

The priest looked down from the light cannon turret and noticed something odd.

The fishmen’s ranks hadn’t expanded on the contrary, there were fewer of them now.

Furthermore, they lacked fighting spirit and instead looked nervous and fearful.

What was going on?

The silhouettes of three silver level fighters appeared around a street corner.

Beside Hong Bo and Dao Hen, there was also a human fighter.

It was the wandering ronin Ku Feng!

As it turns out, the retreating fishmen had run into Ku Feng by accident, who had been searching for them.

Ku Feng wholeheartedly wanted to challenge Dao Hen and attacked.

The fishmen were not his opponent and some were killed by him.

Dao Hen and Hong Bo furiously fought back.

Ku Feng quickly secured the upper hand.

The main reason was because he was in peak condition while the two fishmen fighters were bruised and cut all over, moreover, their battle qi reserves had dropped to dangerous levels.

The fishmen fighters had to save battle qi and had their actions limited, while Ku Feng recklessly squandered his.

He used battle skills again and again, causing Dao Hen and Hong Bo to retreat again and again.

The two retreated, forcing the fishmen behind them to retreat too.

After constantly retreating, they had returned to the outskirts of the barracks.

After discovering the truth, the humans celebrated.

The auxiliary commander even hastily dispatched a soldier to inform the recently departed Shi Fa.

The light cannon turret took the lead to unleash its power.

The light bombardment burst into the fishmen crowd, melting the center of it and leaving only a big hole behind.

During the previous battle, the fishmen were fighting in close quarters and the light cannon turret was unable to bombard as it wished.

The circumstances are different now.

The fishmen failed to retreat and were being attacked from both sides, causing their morale to slump to record lows.

When Hong Bo saw this, he hastily used the fishman language to shout, “Scatter, flee!”

They would undoubtably die if they stayed here.

There was nothing else they could do but to disperse.

The fishmen dispersed in confusion.

Dao Hen and Hong Bo glanced at each other, and a moment later, they ran in different directions.

Ku Feng’s target was Dao Hen, and he chased after him.

Not long after Hong Bo fled, Shi Fa intercepted him.

The two silver level fights sank into a deadlock.

“Who should I help first?” The Wealth priest pondered upstairs.

A church aid scrambled over and panicky told the priest the bad news.

Pirates had broken into the church!

The priest’s eyes darkened, the church contained much of his property!

“The Fertile Land priest didn’t defend it?”

“He did, but the pirates were stronger. Lord Priest, return now so we can take back our church!” The aid wept.

But the priest shook his head.

It is too late to go back.”

“The best way to handle the big picture is to help the fights before us.”

“A destroyed church can be rebuilt.”

“Property can be stolen, but there is hope we can take it back. Pirates are greedy by nature, and the new island has many places they can loot. Their ships won’t be leaving Two Eyes Island soon.”

The priest calmly analyzed and commanded his subordinates to attack.

He decided to aid Shi Fa first!

Shi Fa had proven himself to him already.

Ku Feng appearing at an opportune moment was somewhat of a coincidence.

The priest was worried that Ku Feng was the undiscovered traitor, staging a bloodening of the fishmen to gain his confidence.

Chapter 387: Underground Magician Battle

Two Eyes Island’s new island.

The subterranean abyss.

A purple scaled fishman was ambling along.

The dark environment didn’t prevent her from seeing the world.

After departing the underground space for some time, she had been searching the new island’s underground.

Magic power was continuously being expended.

The purple scaled fishman maintained a mysterious magic.

This spell had been taught to her by the Blue Green clan elder, it could influence and control the coral ball cage tree to some degree.

Two Eyes Island was a coral ball cage tree.

But this coral ball cage tree was too big and had a gold life level.

The purple scaled fishman couldn’t control it completely by herself, only a part of it.

It was only a minute part of the coral ball cage tree.

Whenever the purple scaled fishman went, branches automatically separated and formed a passage.

Behind her, countless branches closed again and formed an inseparably tight wall.

The purple scaled fishman stopped walking.

Her chanting grew faintly louder, and although it was in the fishman language, her voice was different from other fishmen, melodious and beautiful.

Through her magic, branches withdrew and formed a cavity in front of her.

The cavity expanded, and after a dozen seconds, it formed an underground cave ten square meters big.

The purple scaled fishman entered the cave.

In the cave, among congested branches that looked like countless hands, there was a coffin being pushed up.

After the coffin was brought up, the branches bound together again and formed a strong and tenacious floor.

The purple scaled fishman opened the coffin expectantly.

Inside was a fishman corpse.

Because it had been there for a long time, the corpse was nothing but bones and some badly damaged fish scales.

The purple scaled fishman glanced at it, realizing this wasn’t what she was looking for.

She sighed, ready to continue forward when she suddenly heard voices behind her.

After a few seconds, the wall behind her violently corroded.

The purple scaled fishman instantly realized things were going south and threw up a sapphire.

It was a specialized magic gem.

After the gem was spat out, it suddenly erupted with blue radiance.

The blue light condensed into a round shield that protected the purple scaled fishman.

A moment later, an explosion, together with a sharp iron spear from what seemed to be a javelin shot at the blue round shield.

The blue light round shield was very strong and tough, and it blocked the javelin.

The javelin lost force and before it fell to the ground, two bottles of magic potion were thrown in.

The potion knocked into the wall, instantly erupting with large quantities of poisonous mist.

The purple scaled fishman promptly took out a bag.

The bag opened and exploded with a powerful suction force that absorbed all of the poisonous fog.

“A Swallowing Space Absorption Bag?” That alchemy treasure is mainly made of a gluttonous seafloor monster’s stomach pouch, it is rare!” The pharmacist sighed regretfully, “If she didn’t have that, my potions would have worked.”

The alchemist next to him controlled the iron spear while saying, “Your attack failed, I’m not finished yet.”

Magic spell – Turn Iron into Mud.

Magic spell – Temporary Transformation!

The iron spear that fell onto the ground suddenly turned into a pool of iron mud.

The transformation turned the iron mud into an iron snake.

The purple scaled fishman’s blue light shield was floating in front of her, unable to protect her feet.

The iron snake scuttled towards her ankles and like iron shackles, bound itself around them,

The purple scaled fishman was immediately constrained and her mobility suddenly dropped.

“Got her!” Hehe.” The alchemist laughed.

The purple scaled fishman’s expression changed, and in spite of the iron snake twisting around her feet, she used her magic at once.

But a moment later, her cast failed, the resulting backlash depleted her spirit power by a chunk.

“You think that was just poison? That potion had magic disturbance dust mixed into it.” The pharmacist proudly stated.

“I see.” The alchemy generously praised, “Well done.”

When magic disturbance dust stuck to one’s body, it would greatly perturb the use of magic, and the cast failure rate would rise by at least 30%.

To a magician, this was a vile effect.

“You’re not too bad either.” The pharmacist smiled, “Why don’t we consider this a tie then?”

Both were Hun Tong’s subordinates and had worked on many important things together on a daily basis, thus, they didn’t conflict much with each other.

Having a friendly competition was within the norms.

The two people had stealthily found the purple scaled fishman’s trail and had made a wager on who would contribute more to this fight, on who achieved victory.

The trapped purple scaled fishman coldly snorted and coughed out a ruby.

The ruby was imbued with a fireball spell.

As soon as it triggered, it changed into a fireball that shot at the alchemist and pharmacist.

Bang.

The fireball exploded.

The alchemist and pharmacist walked through the raging flames and towards the purple scaled fishman.

Their camouflage gradually dissipated, exposing their true features.

They were alchemy puppets!

The purple scaled fishman knew she had been hoodwinked and began to search for the enemy at once.

At this time, the concealed alchemist and pharmacist acted simultaneously.

Two magic spells pincered the purple scaled fishman.

The first spell diminished the blue light round shield while the second spell targeted the fishman directly, freezing them in place.

After confirming the purple scaled fishman stopped, the two silver magicians stepped out.

“Not bad.”

“I thought of this tactic.”

“Humph, without my alchemy puppets how would it have succeeded?”

“Tut, without my potions, our aura would have leaked already. It would have been impossible to ambush her!”

The two people argued for a few sentences, then inspected their surroundings.

When they saw the fishman corpse in the coffin, their curiosity couldn’t help but be piqued.

“That should be a buried fishman corpse. Two Eyes Island was once fishman territory, and they had buried many of their ancestors here. Most of the fishman coffins had been excavated, but because of the shifting branches and leaves of the coral ball cage tree, some of these coffins have been buried deeply within the island and moved about.”

The two were magicians, and after they saw the coffin, they called part of the truth to mind.

“But what is the fishman covertly searching for in these corpses?”

“They are a valuable silver level strength, yet they haven’t joined the battle.”

“No need to think about it too much. Interrogating our captive should yield many answers.”

The two stared at the purple scaled fishman, wearing smiles.

Torture was not their business.

They had actually captured a silver level magician alive, an impressive military success.

As long as the purple scaled fishman was delivered to Hun Tong, they were sure to receive ample rewards.

The two were thinking freely of their future when the purple scaled fishman suddenly said, “Are you so certain that you’ve captured me?”

The two magicians’ hearts quickened, and they thought the same thing.

“How can she still speak?”

“Why is she speaking the human language?”

Then a moment later, the purple scaled fishman’s body quickly darkened, turned into bubbles, and vanished from the two’s eyes.

“A divine spell!!”

“Strange, she was definitely struck by my Hold Body spell.”

“It is inadvisable to stay here for long, withdraw immediately!”

The two magicians glanced at each other, instantly communicating this sentiment.

They didn’t expect the purple scaled fishman to beat them at their own game!

This magician battle was brimming with ingenuity, far more complex than battles between fighters.

But there wasn’t enough time for the magicians to withdraw.

The surrounding branches seemed to come alive and extended towards the magicians from all sides.

At the same time, the purple scaled fishman’s magic pounded into them.

Gravity spell!

The two magicians suddenly felt something weighing 100kg suddenly attach to themselves, slowing their movement to a crawl.

Ding.

The pharmacist took out a bottle of potion and smashed it into the ground, raising a squall.

The alchemist formed a sphere of light that covered both he and the pharmacist.

The purple scaled fishman continued to cast, attacking like the tide.

The human magicians could only defend momentarily, unable to return fire.

“I fear the other party is a combat type magician!”

“How terrible, I am just a pharmacist.”

The two silver magicians were not expert combatants, the alchemist mostly attended to his duties while under Hun Tong’s orders, the pharmacist researched and improved medicinal recipes.

When they sank into this plight, one of them did his best to defend while the other utilized a communications device to seek outside help.

Hun Tong quickly received this distress signal.

“A silver level fishman magician?”

“Penetrating deep into the island to excavate fishman corpses?”

“Purple scales…unlikely to be a member of the Blue Green Coral clan.

Hun Tong pursed his brows and sank into deep thought.

Qi You seized that chance to attack.

His gold battle qi was constantly bubbling out.

The battle qi lingering on his body condensed into five gigantic squid tentacles.

A tentacle ferociously smashed towards Hun Tong.

Hun Tong was thrown back by it.

“To get distracted during our fight shows you don’t care that much about me, Feudal Lord Hun Tong.” Qi You brightly stated.

Hun Tong coldly snorted and took out a gold level dagger.

“Arrogant pirate boss, I should give you a lesson.”

·

Battle skill – Extreme Speed Stage Three.

Hun Tong’s speed sharply increased, and he rapidly closed the distance between him and Qi You.

Battle skill – Backstab.

His figure suddenly disappeared, then reappeared behind Qi You.

The gold dagger ruthlessly stabbed down.

Qi You’s eyes fiercely flashed.

Battle skill – Spiral Shield!

One of his battle qi tentacles turned into a constantly rotating shield.

The dagger penetrated the shield, but because the shield was spinning, it ended up hitting Qi You’s shoulder.

A bloody light shot out.

A three inch long wound suddenly appeared on Qi You’s back, but it wasn’t that deep.

The wound had Hun Tong’s residual battle qi in it that was constantly corroding Qi You’s muscle and bone.

It was the battle skill Hun Tong was maintaining – Decaying Blade.

Qi You increased his speed and pulled some distance from Hun Tong.

Facing Hun Tong once more, the pirate boss’ complexion went solemn.

“Impossible!”

“Hun Tong stayed his hand.”

“Even though we are plundering his territory, he isn’t in a great rush.”

“Don’t tell me he has bigger trump cards in his hand?”

Although Hun Tong had been fighting Qi You, he had just been putting on a show.

But when Qi You’s words had incited him, Hun Tong had displayed his true strength, which immediately wounded Qi You and increased the pressure on the latter greatly.

This made Qi You more suspicious.

According to common sense, when the new island was being looted by pirates and the old island being invaded by fishmen, Hun Tong should be the most anxious person.

But in fact, Hun Tong had been restraining himself in his fight with Qi You.

“A purple scaled fishman magician had just appeared…”

“Keep waiting.”

“I want to see what other evil monsters pop up.”

Hun Tong sneered to himself.

Chapter 388: Hong Zhu Robs a Tomb

Two Eyes Island.

The new island.

The barracks battlefield.

“A truly peculiar blade skill!” Ku Feng’s battle skill was blocked by Dao Hen again, but the former wasn’t discouraged in the slightest, on the contrary his battle intent flourished, and his eyes grew brighter.

After fighting Dao Hen, Ku Feng immediately realized that the origin of Dao Hen’s unusual bladework lay with Dao Hen’s pair of forearm blade fins.

This special body structure and Dao Hen’s handed down battle skills had united blades and being together from the beginning.

“You are a good opponent.”

“Unfortunately, you are a fishman.”

“It’s now my turn to use my full strength.”

Ku Feng had been holding his katana with two hands, but now he wielded it in one while taking out a dagger with the other.

The dagger emitted an iron level aura, it was an alchemy weapon.

Ku Feng imbued the dagger with battle qi.

He was silver level.

Silver battle qi could imbue things, a qualitative change that distinguished it from iron levels. But there was a restriction, the imbued thing had to be held by the fighter at all times. If it separated from them, silver battle qi couldn’t imbue it from a distance.

When the dagger was imbued with silver battle qi, it instantly grew bigger and longer.

The dagger quickly became a second katana.

This katana was a bit shorter than Ku Feng’s main weapon.

Ku Feng held this katana in one hand.

X

He held the katanas in front of him in the shape of an X.

X

Battle skill – Cross Chop!

The two katanas glistening with silver battle qi formed an integrated whole.

Dao Hen took a deep breath, restrained his vertigo, and used a battle skill too.

His blade fins shined with silver light too.

Ding!

The two launched at each other at the same time.

In the middle, they brushed past each other.

The moment the blades crossed formed a soundwave that nearly blew out eardrums.

The two figures swiftly landed a dozen paces from each other, neither moving.

A few seconds later, Ku Feng let out a breath and collapsed into a kneeling position.

He covered his chest, for near his heart there was a shallow blade cut.

“I lost!” Ku Feng was shocked.

During the blade clash, he had received outside help.

Shi Fa!

If he hadn’t, Dao Hen would have impaled Ku Feng’s heart.

Dao Hen remained motionless.

He had Shi Fa’s magic on him – Fishman Immobilization Spell.

X

Not only that, but his arms and chest fish scales were sliced up. Ku Feng had dealt this injury when his two blades intersected to form an X.

“rlngmmalrnganl”

The fishman Hong Bo charged over to attempt to save Dao Hen.

But the Wealth Church priest intercepted him.

Shi Fa sneered and took out an ace card.

The card whirling in the air suddenly activated and shot out a gloomy green ray of light.

The ray of light pierced Hong Bo.

A terrifying wound the size of a head was made in his chest.

One could see through Hong Bo’s body.

Magic spell – Sunder!

Hong Bo froze and stared like a statue.

A gust of wind blew, and he fell to the ground with a loud bang.

He was dead!

When Dao Hen saw this, he was furious; unfortunately, he couldn’t move.

“Shi Fa, well done.” The Wealth priest exclaimed in admiration.

He didn’t expect that it would be Shi Fa who would be the final contributor.

Dao Hen and Hong Bo were silver level fishmen fighters, and Shi Fa was the biggest figure that led t o one being injured and the other dying.

Shi Fa shook his head, “He didn’t have enough battle qi, he was already a spent force.”

“My Sunder spell wasn’t offset silver battle qi; thus, it was one shot, one kill!”

“Of course, the most important people are you two, without you both impeding them, how could I so calmly cast my magic?”

Shi Fa had good eyes, and although he was a loner, his communication skills weren’t lacking.

Upon seeing he was willing to divide the merit, regardless of whether it was with the priest or Ku Feng, people eased up on him.

“Put him down and have him wait for torture and interrogation!” After seeing the fighting finish, the auxiliary city guard commander promptly brought people over to clean up the battlefield.

Shi Fa nodded, “Look after him well.”

“What a pity.” When Ku Feng saw Dao Hen being captured, he felt regret.

If they weren’t an enemy, perhaps they might have been friends.

“Lord Hun Tong, we’ve won! Thanks to Shi Fa, Ku Feng, and the priest, we routed the fishmen attacking the barracks and killed one silver level and captured the other.” The auxiliary city guard commander reported to Hun Tong through an alchemy device.

Hun Tong was whirling in the air, dodging Qi You’s attacks.

This was the only good news he’d heard since the battle began.

“Nicely done.”

“When merits are evaluated after the battle, you all will be heavily rewarded!”

When the auxiliary commander heard this, his eyes brightened and he excitedly replied, “Feudal Lord, what will we do next?”

Hun Tong gazed at the port battlefield and the chaotic streets full of pirates, yet he still retained his prior order, “Defend and wait for aid!”

“Understood!” The auxiliary commander complied.

He secretly let out a breath and relaxed.

After crushing Hong Bo, Dao Hen, and their troops, he was worried that he would be dispatched to fight in other areas.

The new island was in a terrible situation.

There were pirates everywhere, the port battlefield was a desperate close quarters clash, the sea was filled with entangled ships, and the battle was spreading like wildfire.

One needed to be at least a silver level to feel somewhat safe.

The order to defend was what the auxiliary commander wanted to hear most.

“What do you two plan to do next?” The Wealth Church priest asked Shi Fa and Ku Feng.

“The barracks defense mission is complete. I want to go to the Mercenary Guild first to look at the missions there. No matter if it is attacking the fishmen or dealing with those pirates, I and ready to devote my strength to it. Of course, remuneration is necessary.” Shi Fa said candidly.

Ku Feng replied, “I need to help protect the Flint Store.”

After saying that, he passed like thunder and moved like the wind.

The priest looked at Shi Fa, “Then please move with me. A mission from the Wealth Church will have a greater reward.”

Shi Fa nodded, “Priest, do you want to take back your church?”

Shi Fa knew the Wealth Church had fallen into enemy hands.

But the priest shook his head, “We will go near the church first, link up with the Fertile Land Church priest, and amass more strength.”

“As for the church…”

“It is already too late, taking it back is insignificant, instead we need to divide our force to guard the area.

“At present, Lord Hun Tong, Song Shou, and Yan Ding are busy, and as silver levels, our presence on the battlefield can change the entire outcome.”

“I plan to help the port first.”

“After eliminating the silver level fishmen and taking back control of the port, we can then help the maritime battlefield.”

At this crucial moment, the Wealth priest displayed his responsibility and understanding of the situation.

Shi Fa nodded, “We’ll go then!”

The auxiliary commander bid them farewell and immediately gave the order to reorganize the barracks, secure the doors, and rely on the light cannon turret to defend this place to the death!

“Save me, save me!” Residents fled as they cried out for help.

Poof.

A pirate thrusted their blade into the back of a fleeing resident.

The white knife came out red.

After killing the resident, the pirate raised his foot and stepped on the corpse, using his foot to crush the corpse’s head, “Run away! Run away!”

He grinned maliciously, bent down, turned the corpse around, and searched them.

When he pulled his arm back out, he had wrested out a gold sculpture.

“If you gave it to me earlier, you might have lived.” The pirate complacently put the sculpture into his own pocket and ran his eyes around.

The chase had separated himself from the main force.

But the pirate was daring and impulsive, he wasn’t worried and instead excitedly stood back up.

With no accomplices, everything he found belonged to him alone.

“If I run across an expert that kills me, then that will be my own back luck!”

Ding.

The pirate ruthlessly kicked open a door and charged into a family courtyard.

The courtyard was decorated sparsely, but exquisitely.

The pirate plundered it, overturning trunks and boxes, but found nothing valuable.

He swore as he strode around, not knowing that a silver level fighter hiding in a corner had observed everything he had been doing.

It was Hong Zhu.

This was Hong Zhu’s small courtyard. When the new island floated up, she rented it and made it one of her bases.

After the auction, such radical changes occurred that pirates were even ransacking her residence.

Of course, it was no issue for Hong Zhu to kill the pirate.

“There is no need to do that.”

“Everything valuable is in my storage belt.”

Unlike other loners, Hong Zhu was not interested in taking on missions to deal with the fishmen or pirates for monetary gain.

“This is a chance that comes only once in a blue moon.”

“It’s time to move!”

Hong Zhu restrained her aura, masked her tracks, and quickly left the small courtyard.

She was a veteran treasure hunter and was good at invisibility and stealth.

During treasure hunts, she often needed to conceal herself and slink about, this was her trade and living.

Therefore, neither pirates, nor fishmen nor other loners noticed Hong Zhu.

She easily arrived at the cemetery.

It was her destination.

It was where the Wealth Parish buried their believers with as generous funerary objects as possible.

Two Eyes Island was a big commerce island, one run well by Hun Tong, and so it was filled with many rich inhabitants.

Therefore, the quantity and quality of the funerary objects were quite considerable.

Hong Zhu was a treasure hunter and had been planning to target these graves.

If the auction hadn’t gone to her satisfaction, she might have robbed these tombs to collect money for her next adventure.

The cemetery’s defenses couldn’t be looked down on.

Hong Zhu had probed into them several times before, but didn’t take genuine action, because it was too risky.

But right now, Hong Zhu instantly noticed that the defenses had vanished, now the main defenses here were magic traps and monitoring alchemy constructs.

Hong Zhu was certain she could deal with such things.

“As expected, the pirates didn’t think of coming to the cemetery, they have their hands full robbing living people and won’t be interested in this place.”

Hong Zhu grew increasingly excited.

She acted at once, bypassing the magic traps and avoiding the alchemy constructs to gradually get deeper into the cemetery.

Chapter 389: True Wealth

Hong Zhu took out a crystal ball.

The crystal ball was filled with an unknown transparent liquid.

In the middle of the crystal ball was a suspended rhombus pointer.

The pointer was small and delicate.

It was a treasure hunting pointer!

The crystal ball was only iron level, but the pointer was a silver level alchemy treasure.

It was Hong Zhu’s most reliable detection method when she went exploring.

Hong Zhu immediately transferred battle qi to the treasure hunting pointer.

The treasure hunting pointer began to sway, then quickly fixed itself towards the southeast.

Hong Zhu closed in to where it was pointing.

As she got closer, the pointer’s tip slowly moved downwards.

Finally, Hong Zhu was standing somewhere in the cemetery where the crystal ball pointer was completely vertical, pointing at the ground beneath her feet.

There was nothing left to say.

Time to dig!

Hong Zhu took out a shovel.

Her shovel was also an alchemy item too, it was of decent quality and was of iron level.

With the iron level magic shovel’s help, Hong Zhu quickly excavated a coffin.

She opened the coffin and saw a human skeleton inside.

Around the skeleton were large amounts of jewels and jewelry. They were mostly made of gold, adorned with silver, gems, agate, and other things that made them a feast for the eyes.

Hong Zhu’s eyes also glistened with golden light.

Without hesitation, Hong Zhu shoved the money and valuables into her storage belt.

After taking all of the funerary objects, she reburied the coffin and did her best to restore the ground above it.

Afterwards, she used the treasure hunting pointer to animatedly excavate the next coffin.

As a treasure hunter, Hong Zhu wouldn’t imitate the pirates in their plundering and killing. As for tomb robbing, she was fully on board with that.

When others were fighting and killing, she was relieving the dead of their money.

Yue Ban jumped on a roof.

She was an owlman with outstanding vision. Not only could she see far, but she could also see clearly in the dark.

She was very fat, but when she moved, her feet made no sound when she landed on the roof.

Her movements were nimble and had a distinctive sense of beauty to them.

She was a silver level dancer.

Yue Ban wasn’t like Mei Ban or Shi Fa who were busy defending places, nor was she like Hong Zhu who was making a profit, instead she was preoccupied with Zi Di and Cang Xu.

Or more accurately, she was obsessed with the Mushroom Dancing Shoes Zi Di had bought.

That pair of dancing shoes was very important to her.

Losing the auction hadn’t completely obliterated Yue Ban’s hopes.

“It’s a pity they left the auction early, otherwise I might have been able to negotiate with them.”

Zi Di and Cang Xu left early to receive slaves.

Yue Ban had missed them as a result.

“Where are you?”

“Where are you, come out now!”

Yue Ban constantly repeated that thought.

She traveled all over and scaled to high places to see father to find Zi Di and Cang Xu.

This search was quite troublesome for her.

Because she didn’t know what Zi Di and Cang Xu looked like.

She was mainly focusing on cultivators.

Especially the strange cultivators that appeared in the turmoil, they might be the ones she was looking for.

“Captain, the Wealth Church has already been looted!” A pirate fretfully returned to inform his captain.

“What?” When Yang Cong Tou heard this bad news, he instantly grimaced.

His amazing plan to rob the Wealth Church that had been full of joy and expectations had been overturned by this discovery that someone had beaten him to the punch.

“Move!” Yang Cong Tou was naturally unreconciled and led his underlings to the church gateway.

If these pirates weren’t strong enough, Yang Cong Tou planned to victimize the villainous!

They saw many pirates entering and exiting the church, each one moving a large box.

Some had gold and silver coins, many had gold and silver plates, and some had renowned paintings, sculptures, and other works of art.

Even the window curtains were torn down and taken by the pirates.

Upon careful examination, the window curtains had gold and silver thread, were made of the finest silk, and would definitely fetch a good price.

The Wealth Church was affluent, and its place of worship was bound to be adorned extravagantly and grandly.

Of course, any sculpture, portrait, or sacred icon depicting the Wealth Goddess were not touched by the looting pirates.

This was a world of faith and gods.

There were many problems when looting a church, but if one touched anything related to the gods and disrespected them, it might provoke divine retribution.

When that came, even a saint level pirate would fall dead in the streets.

The ones robbing the Wealth Church was the Lightning Pirates.

They were familiar with what to and what not to take.

“Dian Wan!” When Yang Cong Tou saw the Lightning Pirates’ commander, he couldn’t help but fume with rage with an indignant heart.

Previously, it was Dian Wan who had been a step faster and had robbed the fleet with the Sun Halo.

It had been for this Sun Halo that Yang Cong Tou was forced to participate in Two Eyes Island’s auction.

During the auction, he had lost to Zi Di and Cang Xu, causing the Sun Halo to slip through his fingers.

Now, Dian Wan had once again been a step ahead of him in robbing the Wealth Church.

When Dian Wan saw Yang Cong Tou and his people, he proudly said, “Ah, I thought it was you. Fellow Stench Pirates, you’ve come a bit late, everything here is ours!”

“Boss, please stop talking.” The Lightning Pirates’ first mate advised.

But Dian Wan didn’t listen, he never thought well of the Stench Pirates, and wouldn’t let this chance slip by.

Dian Wan put his hands on his hips and burst into laughter, “Oh? I remember, it was us who robbed that fleet. Yang Cong You, you’re always eating my butt’s dust. Does it taste good?”

“Young man, you are too arrogant!” Captain Yang Cong Tou became enraged that a sore spot had been exposed.

As soon as he said that he cast a spell.

Dian Wan felt the threat and jumped back at once.

A clump of brambles suddenly scuttled across the ground where he had been standing, it seemed to snap at the air like a monster and its densely packed sawtooth thorns frightened people.

Dian Wan avoided the spell and grew very angry.

“You’re looking for a fight!” Dian Wan launched at Yang Cong Tou through the air, his fists crackling with lightning.

“I ought to give you a lesson!” Yang Cong You suddenly transformed into a treeman.

Druids were experts at wood path magic, and some also knew how to use nature divine spells and spells that could turn them into a bear, wolf, eagle, and other animals.

Yang Cong Tou was a depraved druid, and the treeman transformation turned him into a depraved treeman.

The treeman was sinister and terrifying, and its long tree trunk had a person’s face that looked like Yang Cong Tou.

Vines on the sides of the tree trunk formed a pair of arms that were wildly scratching at Dian Wan.

“Are we attacking?” The Lightning Pirates asked their first mate.

“No, keep calm.” The first mate shook his aching head, “Let them fight, you guys get out of here now!”

Meanwhile, the Stench Pirates hadn’t been given the order to attack yet either.

Both groups did their own thing, only their captains were fighting each other.

Several rounds later.

The depraved treeman Yang Cong Tou had been scorched black by lightning, smoke curling from his tree trunk.

Dian Wan was also a sorry figure, his clothes had been shredded and the front of his body dripping with wounds.

Suddenly, both stopped at the same time.

They sensed life auras on the same level as theirs.

Pirates on both sides reported simultaneously that it was the Wealth Church priest and the playing card magician Shi Fa!

Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou glanced at each other and stopped fighting.

After the barracks were stabilized, the Wealth Priest led his subordinates and Shi Fa back to the church.

Near the church, they had converged with the Fertile Land priest.

When they met, the Fertile Land priest shamefully apologized.

He was responsible for looking after the Wealth Church, and when the Lightning Pirates attacked, he bravely stepped forward to fight Dian Wan.

He blocked Dian Wan but couldn’t protect anyone.

The Lighting Pirates’ first mate headed the violent attack, and because the Wealth priest had taken all of the church’s elites with him, the church’s defenses collapsed in a dozen seconds.

The pirates rushed into the church and when the Fertile Land priest saw that the situation was unsalvageable, he was forced to helplessly retreat.

He didn’t retreat far, just near the church.

Because when the battle began, he had sent someone to inform the Wealth priest.

Now that the church had been stormed, the Fertile Land priest had assembled the remaining people and waited for the Wealth priest to return.

When he finally saw the Wealth priest return, he was pleasantly surprised to see Shi Fa with them.

“What are we waiting for, let’s take back the church together!” The Fertile Land priest suggested.

Yang Cong Tou and Dian Wan had stopped fighting.

Yang Coing Tou gazed at the two priests’ forces in the distance and laughed, “Trouble has come for you.”

Dian Wan frowned, but his words were vigorous, “There is no such trouble. If you can’t take it away, then you might as well burn it!”

Yang Cong Tou couldn’t help but chuckle, “Perhaps we can work together.”

Dian Wan muttered wordlessly to himself for a moment.

When faced with the Fertile Land priest’s proposal, the Wealth priest shook his head and tactfully refused.

“It’s just some property.”

“Let them fight over it.”

“This is my God’s parish; the most precious wealth is my God’s believers!”

“Protecting Thy believers’ lives and beating back the fishmen and pirates as soon as possible will again bring stability to the new island.”

“We have come here to link up with you, that’s all.”

“We need your strength to assist the port battlefield over yonder.”

“Now, all of the gold levels are busy with each other. Our performance will shake up the war situation and thereby influence the result of the entire battle!”

The Wealth priest’s words moved the Fertile Land priest!

Shi Fa gazed towards the church, his eyes fixing on Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou, “It won’t be easy to attack the church.”

“Just now, the two pirate leaders had been fighting each other.”

“If we attack, I fear both their groups will unite to deal with us.”

In terms of silver level experts, the priest’s side had a slight advantage, but in terms of subordinate numbers and quality, the pirates undoubtably dominated in that aspect.

3 silvers against 2 wasn’t a big advantage, and if they were deadlocked, the pirates would slay the priest’s subordinates, and the outcome would be unpleasant to speculate on.

The priest and the others slowly retreated.

This surprised the two pirate bosses.

Dian Wan let out a breath.

“Humph, a group of cowards.” Dian Wan was the most frustrated.

If the priest and the others attacked, he could join with Dian Wan and have reasonable grounds to divide the spoils.

If the enemy was formidable, he could retreat from the attack.

In any case, he hadn’t snatched any property, and when the two sides began to fight, he could run away, while the Lighting Pirates who had the goods would be watched closely by the priest and others, not so easily able to escape.

If that happened, he could hoodwink the Lightning Pirates.

But that evil scheme didn’t go his way.

The priest and the others voluntarily retreated.

Chapter 390: The Dwarfs Run Away

“Open the door!” A group of pirates came to the hotel entrance.

They had pockets of silver and gold, some had fine ceramic vases, and others were carrying enormous bundles.

These were the things they had stolen.

“Open the door now, offer up your money and valuables!”

“Open the door now, and you’ll live. I’ll give you 10 seconds, and when its up, we’ll kill you!!”

The pirates’ knocks and constant threats echoed through the hotel.

These threats were actually very useful.

Ordinary residents were scared witless and would open their doors to offer their belongings.

Pirates usually followed this agreement, and after milking the people dry of their property, they usually wouldn’t kill them.

This was in fact the standard for pirates.

Normally, when they were plundering the seas, they usually wouldn’t commit wanton massacres as ransoming was an important revenue stream.

More crucially, this kind of standard could usually reduce the risks of looting and reduce their prey’s resistance.

After this standard and common understanding formed, pirates could pay a smaller price to obtain loot.

To pirates, there was more benefits than losses to doing this.

Of course, this was only during normal situations.

If a pirate was desperate or had a murderous nature, pirates could choose to slaughter everyone and leave no survivors. When that happened, they would usually raise the blood flag.

Currently, the pirates were robbing Two Eyes Island, their main goal was looking for riches and it wasn’t worthwhile to kill indiscriminately.

Murder also wastes time.

With that time, couldn’t more things be stolen?

Furthermore, this was Two Eyes Island, and the gold level cultivators were merely impeded. If one massacred too excessively, they might provoke Hun Tong or Song Shou into disregarding all negative considerations for the sake of revenge, people almost every pirate here couldn’t withstand.

“I beseech you.”

“Lord Men Shi, I took you all in.”

The hotel owner implored the dwarfs, kneeling towards them.

The hotel was surrounded by layers of pirates, and the doors were on the verge of being smashed open by their blows.

Men Shi was very vexed.

He had returned from buying his people from the auction and came to this hotel to rest.

He didn’t want to get involved in the chaos outside.

“Wait until things calm down, then I and my people will depart to continue our search for a new home.” This was what Men Shi, and the dwarfs had planned to do.

“Don’t listen to the humans!”

“Why should we help these humans? We can stay here because we reserved a room in advance.”

“That’s right. When we came here, the boss wanted to lock his doors and ignore us.”

“But what are we going to do about the pirates outside? They have surrounded this place already.”

“We will charge out!”

“We have nothing to lose; we are all destitute.”

“Those wretched humans are to blame. They swindled the clan’s savings!”

The dwarfs’ blunt disposition caused them to not mince words and to tell it as it was.

Men Shi looked around.

The dwarfs all looked determined.

Men Shi knew breaking out was risky. Although he had done his best to arm them, they only had some inferior weapons and no armor.

But Men Shi could feel his people’s resolve.

“We’re leaving!” Men Shi ordered.

“No, don’t go. Help me, please, I can pay you, I can pay you a huge sum!” The hotel boss anxiously wept.

The dwarfs displayed an unfeeling demeanor.

Under Men Shi’s leadership, they walked down the stairs and turned a deaf ear to the hotel owner’s pleas.

In desperation, the hotel owner suddenly changed his attitude and began to loudly curse the departing dwarfs.

The dwarfs loathed humans, but none of them attacked the hotel boss.

Just as they reached the first floor, they saw a window break, with three iron balls rolling onto the floor.

Men Shi blankly stared, then his face radically changed as he roared, “Get back!”

Boom!

The iron balls exploded a second later.

The force of the explosion and sharp iron shrapnel inflicted a terrible price on the dwarfs.

Three were killed and 8 were injured.

It was obvious the pirates’ countdown hadn’t finished yet.

As it turns out, the situation outside the door had changed.

A stronger group of pirates had rushed in, pushing aside the previous pirates.

“Hahaha, get lost you cowards.”

“Charge in, this hotel belongs to the Applaud Pirates!”

The newly arrived pirates spoke more arrogantly.

They belonged to the Applaud Pirates.

This pirate group’s commander was the frogman Hu Li, a silver cultivator almost as strong as Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou.

Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou had landed on different sides of the island, then both rushed towards the Wealth Church, leading to some friction between them there.

The Applaud Pirates landed closer to the port.

Unlike Dian Wan and Yang Cong Tou, Hu Li was deeply fascinated with the Vinegar Jar.

In the port battlefield, the mighty Vinegar Jar, driven by Yao Guan Zi, had smashed into many light cannon towers, something that had stimulated Hu Li’s greed.

Hu Li was waiting for a chance to seize the Vinegar Jar.

Therefore, he had his men move near the port.

The hotel the dwarfs checked into was near the port.

The reason why Men Shi had selected this place was because he wanted to be near the port after he redeemed his clansmen.

First, it was easy for them to board a ship bound for other islands.

Second, if there wasn’t an appropriate outbound ship, they could work at the port as porters, selling their toil to provide for themselves for a time.

Men Shi’s choice seemed to be the right one, but now it had led to a terrible outcome.

The house was inundated with dust.

The dwarfs’ ears were ringing.

From the dim ringing, wailing gradually rose.

Men Shi was a silver level and stood at the forefront, tall and unmoving.

But he was only one person, and silver battle qi couldn’t form outside the body, thus he couldn’t hold back the explosion.

He looked at his clansmen behind him.

The dwarfs had fallen to the ground, some were crawling back up, and others clutched their dead relatives, crying bitterly.

Men Shi’s heart welled with tides of fury and hate.

“Hehehe!”

“Charge in!!”

“Take all they have!!”

The pirates kicked the door down and flooded in.

“You will die!!” Men Shi angrily narrowed his bloodshot eyes.

He roared, raised his long war hammer, and like a mad bull, brazenly crashed into the pirates.

The pirates were caught off guard, how could they have foreseen this hotel had a silver level fighter hidden in it?

Men Shi’s hammer smashed down, and like a falling watermelon, a pirate’s head burst open.

Men Shi’s charge broke bones and bruised flesh as he launched them out of the hotel.

Five or six landed as corpses, while many more were badly bruised.

Men Shi rushed out of the inn and began to slay pirates.

By the time he came to his senses, almost all of the pirates around him were slain by his hand, with only one or two able to escape alive.

“Just wait! Men Shi, we recognize you.”

“You killed our Applaud Pirates’ crew, damn you dwarf!”

“Boss Hu Li will avenge us!!”

The pirates ran for their lives as they shouted threats.

Men Shi’s face changed.

His people came out of the hotel with anxious hearts: “Hu Li is a silver level frogman; he should be close by.”

“We need to go now.”

“We cannot stay here for long!”

The dwarfs knew the severity of the situation, and a number of them lent arms or carried their dead relatives on their backs before proceeding to leave the hotel.

“What? Our people were killed by Men Shi. He’s got guts!” Hu Li quickly obtained this information.

He gazed at the port battlefield.

The port battlefield was still deadlocked.

The Vinegar Jar was still mighty and had smashed into the final light cannon turret.

“It still isn’t time to snatch the ship.”

Let’s deal with Men Shi first!”

This guy, leading his burdens, still dares to provoke us!”

Hu Li sneered incessantly.

Although he never interacted with Men Shi, it was clear the dwarfs as a whole were far weaker than Hu Li’s pirates.

Hu Li was not just one solitary person; he had troops ready to fight.

Although the dwarfs could fight, they were not soldiers and their battle experience was inferior to a full time pirate group.

More importantly, the disparity of equipment was stark between both sides.

“Little ones, follow me!”

“Avenge our dead brothers, kill those wretched dwarfs!!”

Hu Li issued a call to action and led away a large group.

Of course, he left behind a few people to shadow the Vinegar Jar.

“Where is my first mate?” Hu Li ordered, “Call them back!”

The Applaud Pirates had been plundering near the port; Hu Li now gave the order to assemble back together.

Like a magnet, he gathered pirates around him as he moved.

The Applaud Pirates’ first mate was in the middle of attacking a villa.

The humans inside the villa were doing their best to fight back.

“Try harder!”

“Little ones, this is the Flint Merchant Alliance representatives’ residence, there must be good things inside!!”

The first mate shouted encouragement from the back.

The pirates tried to get through windows and force their ways through doors, however, the defenders stood firm.

The first mate narrowed his eyes, following the progress of the battle.

He had led his pirates all over in plundering; this was the only place that met unexpected resistance.

To him it seemed that although the defenders weren’t outstanding, they were luxuriously equipped. All of them had a star iron hatchet in hand.

They were iron level weapons!

Unfortunately, these people didn’t have much armor.

The first mate wasn’t sure of who these people were, therefore, he didn’t attack himself, instead he urged his underlings to attack in his stead.

Although the pirates ere fully equipped, their armor couldn’t withstand even bronze level weapons.

In addition, the other side had location on their side, and for the moment, the attackers could only search for gaps in their defense.

“First mate, the captain wants us to return.”

“We need to hunt down Men Shi and his dwarfs.”

“They killed our people!”

Hu Li had dispatched a pirate to inform the first mate.

The first mate’s complexion changed, and he hesitated.

He didn’t want to let the cash cow before him go.

One couldn’t be a good pirate without greed.

As the first mate hesitated, he saw an iron level pirate kill a defender at a window, finally able to charge into the villa.

The first mate suddenly burst into laughter, he took out his cutlass and said to the messenger pirate, “Wait, let me attack this villa.”

Chapter 391: Magician Qing Xin

“Little ones, follow me!” He roared as he guided the remaining pirates into the broken window.

“They are coming in!”

“My Lord, break the siege quickly.”

“We can’t wait for support.”

The villa was in chaos.

These were not some nobodies; they were the people Zi Di had purchased from the auction.

At the time, Zi Di and Cang Xu had led the blond mother and child’s people to a place near the port, found this villa, and used it to temporarily house them.

Their leader was Baron Zai Xiang’s mistress, a pretty blond woman.

She had bronze level cultivation, but no battle experience at all.

Everything was in a panic.

Upon hearing her subordinate’s proposal, she promptly nodded and grabbed her child, “We must escape now!”

But escape to where?

The pirates had surrounded the villa utterly, and at this very moment, a number of iron levels had stormed in, their defensive line had collapsed.

The blond mother and child wanted to escape, but it was too late.

They were entangled with the pirates.

“Wow, there is a belle here!”

“Too ugly, much too ugly!”

“Don’t hurt her, let her be, we’ll give her to the boss!”

“We don’t want the old, you can dishonor them.”

“Idiot, that human belle can be captured alive and be sold for a high price!!”

The Applaud Pirates were almost all frogmen.

Different races naturally had different tastes.

But pirates knew what things were.

The Applaud Pirates had plundered for years and knew what was valuable.

The blood mistress’ beauty in some sense had saved her life.

The pirates slowed their attack.

The first mate leading the charge had killed his way to the blond mother and child.

“Well done!” The first mate laughed heartily and complacently.

Seeing the first mate was attacking personally, the blood mother and child, along with the rest all sank into despair.

“Oh dear.” At this moment, someone helplessly sighed.

A moment later, several cyan wind blades flew out.

The frogmen who didn’t dodge them were cut apart by the wind blades, with several of them being cleaved into two in a shower of blood.

“A magician!” The frogmen took cover at once and withdrew.

The blond mother and child, along with the rest, looked behind them in shock.

In their memory, she had been a common cook.

The one who had cast the wind blades was Qing Xin.

Zi Di’s eyes didn’t deceive her.

Forced by circumstances, Qing Xin had no choice but to attack and kill the pirates to defend herself.

At best, the first mate and his people were iron level.

While Qing Xin was a silver level magician!

Wind blades appeared again, reaping frogmen lives away, none were strong enough to withstand them.

The first mate wasn’t brave, otherwise he wouldn’t have been in the rear previously.

“Run away!”

The first mate was the first to flee.

The frogmen’s morale was broken, and they lost their heads and fled in disarray.

Qing Xin coldly snorted, murderous intent flashing through her.

Wind blades flew out and a thunderbolt fell into the center of the first mate’s back.

The first mate ferociously vibrated and fell to the ground.

The wind blades severed his heart.

He struggled to get back up as his head raised and coughed out blood.

After several bloody coughs, his physical strength vanished, and he stopped crawling.

“First mate!” One of Hu Li’s people rushing over witnessed it all.

As it turns out, Hu Li’s people had come here looking for trouble with Men Shi and his people.

“Boss, avenge me!” The first mate’s eyes flashed open, leaving behind his dying words a moment before death.

Hu Li shouted furiously.

The first mate was not just his underling, he was also a close blood relative of his.

It was because of this relationship that the first mate was faithful and true to him. Although the first mat wasn’t strong, Hu Li trusted him and made him his first mate. While looting, he also kept an eye on him.

He didn’t expect to lose him on Two Eyes Island today!

Hu Li’s bloodshot eyes glared towards the villa.

Because Qing Xin had acted, her silver level magician aura was no longer masked and was exposed in its entirety.

“Kill!”

Hu Li growled as he pounded towards the villa.

“Leave now, his target is me!” Qing Xin had no choice but to fly out of the villa and head in another direction alone.

As expected, Hu Li pursued her.

His pirate troops marched towards the villa.

“Kill them all!”

“Avenge the first mate.”

“Leave no one alive!!”

Pirates were hooting.

“My Lord, what should we do?” The people inside the villa were panicking.

“Flee, flee now!” The blond mistress clutched her bosom with a pale look.

Fortunately, the villa wasn’t surrounded by pirates yet so they could easily escape.

“Me Shi, look there!” A dwarf shouted as they ran.

After they had beaten back the Applaud Pirates, they left the hotel right after.

Men Shi looked up and saw Xing Xin flying through the air.

As for Hu Li, he didn’t have any flying battle skills, and even if he did, he was only a silver level and couldn’t use them.

But Hu Li had a battle skill that allowed him to jump high and far.

Every time he jumped, he could charge into Qing Xin and threaten the latter.

Qing Xi didn’t have any armor, but instead of flying high, she decided to lure Hu Li over and did her best to curb the pirate’s strongest cultivator to give the blond mistress and the others a chance to flee for their lives.

Men Shi gazed at it with rapt attention, somewhat hesitantly.

They had provoked the Applaud Pirates, and now there was someone drawing fire away from them, this was a good deed to the dwarfs.

But Men Shi knew Hu Li’s reputation.

The frogman was vindictive and was driven by vengeance.

Men Shi had killed many Applaud Pirates and Hu Li wouldn’t take that lying down.

“From a reasonable point of view, I should attack immediately and with that unfamiliar magician, we can deal with Hu Li together!”

“But…”

When Men Shi thought of that, he looked at his people behind him, hesitating once again.

Protecting his clansmen was his most important duty.

Pirates were running amuck right now, and he was very worried that if he left to fight, his people would suffer immeasurably.

But a moment later, the dwarfs were in an uproar.

They saw the blond mother and child, and their retinue running away.

“It’s them!”

The dwarfs were friends with these people.

They had all met misfortune, been reduced to slaves, and been auctioned off.

Because misery loved company, although they had interacted with each other briefly, they had a decent relationship.

“Lord Men Shi, we must save those people.”

“Although they are humans, they were slaves just like us.”

“Without our help, they will die!”

“Everyone’s enemy is the Applaud Pirates; we must fight alongside them.”

“That’s right, by killing these pirates, we will avenge our relatives!!”

Men Shi was instantly moved somewhat.

Although humans had entrapped them and they hated humans, their nature hadn’t changed, and they were still frank and kindhearted.

When they came across their friends, their reaction was to save them, not delight in their disaster!

Of course, there was another reason to join in opposition to a shared adversary.

“Then let it be so, follow me!” Men Shi made a firm resolution.

The dwarfs turned around and with loud shouts, quickly attacked the pirate’s rear.

Men Shi took the lead, and was the first to charge into the heap of pirates and reduced them to a pitiful puddle.

Hu Li exploded in anger; he stopped chasing Qing Xin to attack Men Shi.

When Qing Xin saw this, she used magic, using all of her strength to impede Hu Li.

The dwarfs converged with the humans.

The blond beauty promptly decided, “Quick, give our axes to our dwarven friends!”

At this crucial moment, she actually made a very wise decision.

The dwarfs were poorly equipped, and now that they had iron level alchemy weapons, they were like a fierce tiger that regained its claws and teeth, a massive boost in attack!

Both sides grappled with each other, and for a moment, the situation was deadlocked.

This new fight attracted many resolute people.

“A strange silver level magician?” Yue Ban’s gaze was attracted.

She was looking for Zi Di and Cang Xu.

As a result, she noticed Qing Xin.

“Is she the person I am looking for?”

“Doesn’t look like them.”

Yue Ban harbored doubts.

Because although Qing Xin was a silver magician, she looked impoverished and lacked equipment.

Yue Ban didn’t know what Zi Di and Cang Xu looked like, but she knew those two were rich and had to have treasures on them.

Even if they didn’t have any prior to the auction, they had bought many during it.

“Wait a moment, weren’t those the slaves they purchased?”

After Yue Ban saw the blond mother and child, her mind shook with alarm and joy.

Since these were the slaves they had bought, Zi Di and Cang Xu might be close by.

Yue Ban harbored this conjecture and with an excited heart, quickly drew closer.

Her conjecture wasn’t wrong.

Cang Xu and Zi Di were nearby.

However, the former was hiding in his ship on the port battlefield, while the latter was overseeing the battle in the Deep Sea Monster Fish, at the bottom of the seafloor.

The cockpit.

The magic images on the wall were constantly fluctuating, displaying the fight between Qing Xin, Hu Li, and Men Shi.

“It really is Sister Qing Xin.” Zi Di’s eyes shined, and her heart stirred.

She hadn’t identified the former successfully, but the pirates and the chaotic situation had forced Qing Xin to act and expose herself.

As she looked at the magic images, Zi Di muttered to herself, “Think of a way to save them.”

Two Eyes Island was in chaos, but it wasn’t a good opportunity to come to the rescue.

After all, the battle between Qing Xin and Hu Li was a focus of many eyes.

“Furthermore…”

Zi Di pursed her brows as she gazed at the magic images with rapt attention.

Qing Xin’s display had all been seen by Zi Di.

“Sister Qing Xin is a wind path magician, she’s obviously able to fly beyond Hu Li’s reach.

“But she isn’t doing that.”

“Previously, she had exposed her identity to protect the blond mother and child, and her companions.”

“She wants to protect these people. Its likely that while living incognito, she formed friendships with them.”

Zi Di only wanted to help Qing Xin.

But she had to consider Qing Xin’s feelings too.

If Qing Xin was recruited, perhaps in the future she would be let into the core secret that was the Bloodcore.

In that case, the wisest course of action was to show consideration for both Qing Xin and her companions like the blond mother and child, doing their best to keep them alive.

“Should I come to the rescue or not?”

Identity was not a problem.

Zi Di and Cang Xu could use their prior features and appear as Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders.

Qing Xin had a background, and her identity couldn’t be concealed. Even if no one knew who she was now, it would be very easy to figure out who she was upon future examination.

Therefore, Zi Di using her identity from the auction to save Qing Xin was very reasonable!

“The current problem is insufficient strength.”

“Furthermore, even if I provide support, I still need a ship.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships couldn’t be used.

Don’t even think about using the Deep Sea Monster Fish!

Chapter 392: Puppet Clone

“Perhaps we should steal some ships.”

“If we do that, then our strength will be stretched thinner.”

Zi Di and Cang Xu could move as Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders.

But they were only bronze and iron level magicians.

Their true strength (necromancy) could not be used.

“Out of the question, the only thing we can use is alchemy puppets.”

Currently, most of the survivors’ puppets come from the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

There were basic maintenance types, swordsman guard types, deep sea salvaging types, and scouting types that were mass manufactured.

Most of these alchemy puppets had been exposed already.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps having some alchemy puppets was not valuable information.

Fortunately, alchemy puppets were not enough to become a distinctive feature, every organization large and small had some alchemy puppets.

Zi Di pondered carefully and patiently.

As the battle stood, Qing Xin and Men Shi naturally held the advantage against Hu Li.

After the dwarfs and the blond mistress’ group converged, they were dealing with the pirates in a sound manner.

“Preparations must be done in advance. You are in charge of taking care of things on the Deep Sea Monster Fish, let me go to them.” After Cang Xu and Zi Di spoke, he decided to slip away from the port battlefield.

In a ransacked and empty house, Cang Xu changed his identity, once again looking like a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder and waited for the right opportunity.

The port battlefield didn’t need Cang Xu.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were still flubbing their battles.

“Open fire! Open fire!” One Eye’s hoarse voice fumed with rage.

He commanded the patrol fleet to fire constantly.

Large amounts of shells poured down on the main fishman army, but it didn’t do much.

The racial magic of the Frozen Land Whale the fishman army was riding on was protecting them as they made a successful landing.

Large swaths of fishmen were wreaking havoc on the old island.

Shells from One Eye’s fleet that were able to smash into the fishmen caused considerable casualties.

But the greatest threat was not the patrol fleet, but Yan Ding.

As he rode on a cloud through the air, the gold level cultivator’s massive battle skill really looked like magic!

Roughly five to six hundred fishmen were already slain by his hands.

The Blue Green Coral Clan’s elder was incessantly distressed.

He was going all out, but without a flying battle skill, he could only perturb Yan Ding, he couldn’t stop him from his merciless slaughter.

Yan Ding was about to use a stronger battle skill when suddenly, a divine spell fell from the sky and hit him right on the head.

The divine spell had been used at a good opening, revealing that the caster had a deep battle mastery.

Yan Ding’s battle skill failed, and for a moment, the backlash caused a large amount of his internal battle qi to scatter within him.

“There is another gold level hiding!” With anger and alarm, Yan Ding promptly retreated.

But the naga priestess inside the Frozen Land Whale had been plotting for a long while, how could this be her only move?

At once, three divine spells shot out in a row, their tempo and speed making them impossible to dodge.

Yan Ding had defenses prepared, but the enemy was faster, he was only able to avoid the first one and was struck by the other two.

Yan Ding violently coughed out blood, most of the smoke under him dissipated, his eyes were black, and he was tottering.

“Lord Priestess, thank you for acting!” The old clan elder was endlessly grateful.

The Frozen Land Whale’s large mouth opened up and the naga priestess slowly swam out.

Behind her there were more hidden fishmen warriors that howled and charged past the priestess to attack the old island.

With these reserves joining in, the fishmen on the frontline received a morale boost, they regrouped after their setback and charged constantly, ready to sweep everything before them away.

Of course, the biggest reason for this is that Yan Ding was in a bad state and was retreating after being struck by the naga priestess’ prepared divine spells.

This alarming change naturally attracted many eyes.

Gold levels were the apex on this battlefield, and every gold level fight enormously influenced the entire war situation.

Qi You burst into laughter, “Hun Tong, there is no need to chase me around. I only want money, while those fishmen want your land.”

Hun Tong coldly snorted, his expression grave.

He wasn’t surprised the fishmen had a hidden trump card but that it was this big!

A gold level cleric!

To some degree, this naga priestess was a bigger menace than fighters and magicians.

Because clergy could heal.

“Too rarely seen!”

Fishmen in general had less talent than humans.

Fishmen who had the talent to become magicians and clergy was always rare.

But now, not only was there a purple scaled fishman magician, but there was also a gold level clergy.

Of course, she was not a fishman, but a naga.

This point only warned Hun Tong further.

“Not only is there the Blue Green Coral fishman clan, but there is another power too.”

“The old clan elder has asked for a powerhouse’s help!”

The fishmen’s spirits roused greatly, while Hun Tong’s party wavered.

The humans weren’t united and there were pirates looting everywhere.

The merchant alliance representatives were stuck in the ocean battlefield, like feet stuck in the mud, and they were unable to protect their stores.

Two Eyes Island’s port battlefield was still deadlocked.

This place had the most corpses on both sides.

Two Eyes Island’s old island had been captured by the main fishman army.

The defenses there had mainly been the patrol fleet, and it falling into enemy hands was inevitable.

The fishmen wailed as they rescued many fishmen captives from the old island’s government building.

These fishmen were all emaciated and had magic runes engraved into their bodies.

Their gill flaps had been cruelly torn off, exposing their internal gills, and within their gill lines, there were many lush tea plants growing from them.

These tea plants were the source of the Fragrant Gill Tea.

Hun Tong had been developing a unique product for Two Eyes Island built on the suffering of fishmen.

The fishmen were the soil the tea plants grew in.

The Blue Green fishmen clansmen had the Green Tail Bloodline and the Blue Scale Bloodline, the former was excellent “soil” for the Fragrant Gill Tea.

“Daughter!” The old clan elder went to one of the captives, half-knelt down, and hugged the fishman, sobbing inconsolably for a moment.

His daughter had been Hun Tong’s captive.

At the time, Hun Tong had dove to the seafloor in an attempt to annihilate the fishman clan, and although he had failed in doing so, he didn’t return empty handed.

He had captured many fishmen alive.

The clan elder’s daughter was one of them.

But Hun Tong didn’t know that.

The naga priestess came to the old elder’s side, her face frosty and her gaze ice cold, “I didn’t find what I was looking for. However, you have returned to save your clansmen, including your daughter.”

“This is inconsistent with what you’ve said before.”

“I need an explanation, Blue Green Clan Elder.”

The old elder released his doting hug and let go of his daughter.

His daughter was already delirious and wasn’t aware that she’d been saved.

The old elder looked at the naga priestess, he had been in a half-kneel, which turned into a full kneel before the naga priestess a moment later.

He confessed, “Everything is my mistake. Back then, I lost my home. Now, I have deceived you, Lord Priestess.”

“Your objective is not on this island; it is on the new island.”

The naga’s face grew colder, her killing intent leaked out profusely, “You really are audacious! Do you want to renounce your faith?”

“Of course not!” The old elder raised his voice, “At most, I was just deceiving you, that’s all.”

“After this, I shall stake my entire life to help you accomplish your mission!”

The naga priestess sensed that the elder was dying, and her expression softened. She coldly snorted, “Then I will watch your performance.”

They had attacked the old island for the sake of this mission.

In the end, the naga priestess noticed she had been swindled.

The old elder was selfish.

The real target was on the new island.

There was nothing she could do, despite the fact that she wanted to butcher the fishman elder on the spot, the naga needed his help still to accomplish her mission.

Time to attack the new island!

The fishmen climbed onto the Frozen Land Whale and vigorously charged towards the new island.

The patrol fleet shelled them with all they had, but it didn’t do much.

Upon seeing this, all parties battling on the new island grew tense.

The situation was already chaotic enough, it would be difficult to imagine how much trouble these fishmen would bring to the new island when they arrived.

“Abominable, don’t tell me Two Eyes Island is finished?” A merchant alliance representative was very anxious.

Their ships were stuck in battle, and when the fishmen’s main army charged in, the aftermath would be inconceivable.

Er Gua Zi maliciously cackled, and his offensive grew wilder and more ferocious.

By contrast, his opponent Song Shou gave ground repeatedly.

The fishman’s main army would have a huge impact on the gold level fights.

“Hehehe,” Qi You felt schadenfreude as he jeered Hun Tong, “Feudal Lord Hun Tong, you’d better go all out, or you won’t be able to defend your territory.”

Hun Tong coldly snorted with an iron complexion as he slowly flew back.

Qi You didn’t stop him.

He wasn’t a fishman ally, it wasn’t worth it to fight to the death.

“Since you have come to court death, then I shall help you do so.” Hun Tong took out a statue.

This statue was only the size of his palm, it was a vivid depiction of Hun Tong himself.

As soon as it was taken out, it moved Qi You emotionally.

Because he sensed the statue’s gold level aura!

A Puppet Clone.

Hun Tong imbued it with his battle qi, and in an instant, the Puppet Clone burst with gold light.

The gold light soared up, then like a meteor, it flew through the air in an arc as it shot towards the Frozen Land Whale.

The Frozen Land Whale’s water bubble was up, protecting itself.

The old elder was standing in the water bubble, ready to block the golden meteor.

Bang.

Both sides met with force, and as flowing water dissipated the gold light, it revealed a second Hun Tong.

This Hun Tong, regardless of stature or appearance, was a mirror copy of him. The only difference was that his skin emitted a faint golden light that looked like a layer of golden leaves.

The clan elder used a battle skill and fought Hun Tong’s clone, and for a moment they were evenly matched.

Hun Tong’s clone was very smart; not only could it use all of Hun Tong’s battle skills, but it knew how to recreate the true body’s battle awareness.

Smoke rolled in.

When Yan Ding received powerful aid, he immediately counterattacked.

Hun Tong’s clone was a gold level power.

Thus, both sides had two gold levels, sinking the situation back into a deadlock.

In terms of means, the fishmen had more available to them. The fishman elder was a fighter and the naga priestess was a clergyman, while Hun Tong’s clone and Yan Ding were both fighters.

But Hun Tong’s clone and Yan Ding were unrestrained, and many more of their attacks were aimed at the Frozen Land Whale and the main fishman army.

The old elder didn’t have a flying battle skill, and more times than not, had to protect his clansmen.

Thus, the fishmen sank into a passive situation.

“The longer time drags on, the worse our situation gets!”

The naga priestess’s chilliness grew denser.

But she was pinned down and unable to get onto the new island for the time being.

Now out in the open, her every movement was closely watched.

After pondering it, the naga priestess immediately used a divine spell to contact the purple scaled fishman magician on the new island, “Wei Er, I have confirmed the target is on the new island.”

“I cannot disengage for the time being, you must find him!”

“Use that scroll, we don’t have much time.”

The subterrane depths of the new island.

The purple scaled fishman magician was constantly coughing up blood.

The alchemist was lying before her, while the pharmacist had escaped with heavy injuries.

Although the purple scaled fishman magician was alone, she was the ultimate victor of the underground magician battle.

The purple scaled fishman used a divine spell to respond, “I am in bad shape, it’ll be difficult to continue the search.”

The naga priestess was silent for a moment.

She could sense the purple scaled fishman’s condition through the divine spell.

The purple scaled fishman was gravely wounded, her request really was forced upon the former.

The naga priestess gave a deep look, then communicated, “Heal first.”

“I will activate the secret spy.”

“Let him come help you!”

The imperial secret spy could place undiscovered traitors into the Vinegar Jar Pirates, the fishmen could do the same too.

The secret spy had been hidden deeply.

Using one means one less available.

The naga priestess was compelled by circumstances and had no choice but to use a divine spell to transmit her order.

Chapter 393: The Priest Provides Aid

Two Eyes Island.

The fires of war was everywhere.

Shouts of murder echoed through the heavens.

On a desolate beach, Song Shou was standing ready for battle.

The seawater suddenly surged, and Er Gua Zi’s silhouette flashed in the waves.

Fishman battle skill – Wave Blade!

From the three meter high wave shot out five or six gigantic water blades. The water blades were perpendicular to the sea, and with sharp edges, they plowed into the beach and towards Song Shou.

Song Shou suddenly reached out and stomped his feet onto the beach.

Tree-like cracks suddenly spread through the beach, the cracks emitted gold battle qi.

When the tree pattern and water blades collided with each other, both burst and destroyed the other.

Subsequent waves whistled as they came in, covering the aftermath of these two battle skills.

Er Gua Zi’s figure revealed itself in the waves, and with bubbling battle qi, he condensed the sea waves next to him into two enormous fists.

Fishman battle skill – Water Fist – Battering Ram!

Song Shou coldly snorted, he didn’t move to avoid it, instead he met it face to face.

His palms clapped his chest, and half his body armor suddenly emitted a dazzling light that instantly formed a tower that protected Song Shou within it.

The two water fists swung from the left and right, smashing into the tower at the same time.

The tower shook, and distinct cracks formed on its surface.

Er Gua Zi sneered, his battle intent increasing sharply.

His attack wasn’t that fast, and Song Shou was completely able to avoid it.

But the latter didn’t do that and instead chose to block it.

After blocking it, although his defensive battle skill didn’t collapse, it would only last temporarily.

As long as the attacks continued, it would be dispersed.

Song Shou had sunken into a passive state.

Er Gua Zi had abundant fighting experience, and he immediately mobilized his battle qi to condense two more water fists to continue his high tempo offensive.

Thus, Song Shou wasn’t able to catch his breath or adjust himself, he was forced to maintain his Tower Defense battle skill and wearily take the tide of attacks.

Song Shou was giving ground.

But although Er Gua Zi took the initiative, he wasn’t able to break his opponent’s defensive battle skill for some time.

In the other gold level battle, the naga priestess began to pray.

It was obvious she was trying to use some powerful divine spell.

“Can’t let her do that!”

Like a sharp arrow, Hun Tong’s clone shot straight towards the naga priestess with ice cold killing intent.

The old elder roared, and with a walking stick in hand, intercepted Hun Tong’s clone.

Yan Ding instigated his smoke and used a large-scale battle skill that wildly covered the area with pitch-black smoke.

The old elder and Hun Tong’s clone were within the battle skill’s area of effect.

Since they were both gold levels with outstanding defense, they easily withstood it.

This was Yan Ding’s weak point.

Since his battle skills sought to affect a large area, when dealing with cultivators of the same level, they didn’t do that much to them.

But Yan Ding’s target was not the old clan elder or Hun Tong’s clone, instead it was the fishman army on the Frozen Land Whale.
