The Frozen Land Whale’s water bubble defense had massive holes eroded into it by the black smoke through which clouds of it squeezed through like loathsome pythons that pounced at the fishman army.

The fishman main army didn’t have a silver level, a gold level battle skill was slaughtering them like sheep!

In that crucial moment, the old elder attacked back and used a gold battle skill that curtailed and dissipated every wisp of smoke.

Hun Tong’s clone didn’t stop him, as he was going through the smoke to kill the naga priestess.

The naga priestess was forced to deal with the alchemy puppet and had to stop her prayer halfway through.

On the fishman’s side, they had a fighter and a caster.

According to common sense, they had better and more rational troop arrangements.

But in actual combat, the fishmen were instead at a disadvantage.

The biggest reason was the alchemy puppet.

The clone puppet was an exact duplicate of Hun Tong’s battle power and possessed his fighting experience, yet it fought more fiercely than the actual Hun Tong.

Because it was an artificial clone puppet – it wasn’t scared of dying!

The clone puppet’s defenses were also astonishing.

Hun Tong’s specialty as an assassin meant his attack was strong while his defense was weak. But this clone puppet had his attack and displayed a comprehensive strength that was somewhat stronger than the actual Hun Tong.

Yan Ding’s attacks against cultivators of the same level was lacking, but nevertheless he still provided excellent help.

Because he was culling the main fishman army.

If the old elder didn’t pay attention, his people would succumb to Yan Dinng and suffer disastrous losses.

Because Yan Ding was always attacking the main fishman army, the old elder was quite sullen that he had to save them every single time.

Not saving them was beyond question.

The old elder and his entire clan had attacked to retake their home.

But if almost all of his clansmen were dead, even if Two Eyes Island was taken back, who would live on it?

Losing one’s home, yet the clan surviving meant there was still hope.

Losing one’s clansmen and retaining one’s home meant the enemy could come back again, no one was there to defend it.

The old elder knew this, thus, he had to protect the fishman army.

For these reasons, the fishman’s gold levels were on the downturn, and for a moment, they couldn’t break through Hun Tong’s clone and Yan Ding’s defensive line.

Qi You and Hun Tong were fighting and observing the battlefield.

He evaluated the two other gold fights.

These were the biggest elements that impacted the battle for Two Eyes Island.

“Song Shou is losing but isn’t in danger for now.”

“The alchemy clone is actually stronger than the main body, how outrageous!”

“But that’s fine.”

Of all the gold levels present, Qi You was probably the least worried.

The pirate boss’s biggest hope for the situation was that it continued to remain deadlocked.

The fishmen and feudal lord forces pinning each other down meant this was the best opportunity for pirates to plunder.

Although Qi You wasn’t doing the looting himself, his underlings were sure to be reaping an immense harvest.

Of all the pirates on the new island, Qi You’s were the most impressive.

No other pirate groups clashed with them.

It was because of Qi You.

As he dodged Hun Tong’s attack, Qi You ridiculed, “Hun Tong, this can’t continue.”

“One clone puppet can only hold back the offensive; it cannot reverse the situation for you.”

“Well then, let me give you an idea.”

“Hire me for ten thousand gold, oh and the secret recipe for Fragrant Gill Tea, and I will help you deal with these fishmen. How about it?”

At this moment, Qi You expressed his pirate shamelessness.

Hun Tong coldly snorted and responded by attacking Qi You more intensely.

Hun Tong couldn’t agree to Qi You’s demand.

Even if Qi You was acting in good faith, as a human feudal lord, if Hun Tong agreed to cooperate with pirates, what would be his noble honor? How could he continue to mingle among other imperial lords?

Qi You’s eyes slightly narrowed.

After Hun Tong had used the alchemy clone, he began to attack more fiercely.

All of this was rational.

But Qi You constantly felt something was fishy.

This feeling couldn’t be vocalized properly, but Qi You trusted it.

Because he was an old pirate that had sailed the sea for many years, previous fights proved the veracity of this feeling.

Many times, this feeling had come true.

“What are you waiting for? Feudal Lord Hun Tong!” Qi You taunted again.

But Hun Tong was silent, he just attacked.

“Huh?” Qi You blankly stared, upon noticing something new, he couldn’t help but think, “Don’t tell me he is waiting for the outcome of the battle between these silvers?”

New developments in the port battlefield were being keenly observed by Qi You.

Naturally other gold levels were paying attention too.

There was just a difference in timing between them.

What produced a radical change in the port battlefield was not bystanders, it was the Wealth priest, the Fertile Land priest, Shi Fa, and the temple guards and clergymen under their command.

Two silver level clerics as well as a silver level magician were a formidable force!

The moment they entered the battlefield they would instantly affect it.

Divine spells fell onto humans, healing many wounded to a battle ready state, greatly increasing their morale.

Magic smashed into fishmen, instantly changing the situation and causing disastrous fishmen casualties.

When the fishmen that were entangled with these humans suffered this backstab, they were suddenly thrown into disorder.

Because their leaders were fighting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ cultivators, there was no one to lead the troops.

The only one that could interfere was Yao Guan Zi.

Bang.

The port’s final light cannon tower also collapsed.

The Vinegar Jar under Yao Guan Zi’s control had destroyed all of the light cannon towers in a swift manner, an outstanding military merit!

But the Vinegar Jar had paid a heavy price in exchange.

The magic power reservoir had fallen to 32%.

The Vinegar Jar was bruised and dented, and its own mast had snapped off.

Not only that, but the Vinegar Jar’s bottom cannon mouth had been half destroyed.

It was this cannon mouth discharging water that allowed the Vinegar Jar to move across dry land.

The human soldiers manning the light cannon turrets weren’t idiots either; after seeing this, they focused fire on the cannon mouth.

The light cannon turrets had strong firepower, and even rarer, had high accuracy.

The latter bit wasn’t a strong point of alchemy cannons.

The swiftly rotating Vinegar Jar couldn’t avoid this bombardment.

Fortunately, the Vinegar Jar was close to the light cannon turrets, allowing it to smash into them in short order.

Without the light cannon turrets threatening it, Yao Guan Zi’s next target were the two priests.

The Wealth priest was forced to order, “Scatter!”

The Vinegar Jar’s charge didn’t achieve an ideal result.

The priests’ troops loudly dispersed, and the huge Vinegar Jar could only catch all of its enemies when they were concentrated in one area.

Yao Guan Zi had no choice but to target the Wealth priest.

But the priest had divine spells that allowed him to move quickly.

The Vinegar Jar was a silver level magic ship, and driving the ship to deal with a single enemy was not something it could do.

It was like using a cannon to hit a mosquito, the costs were high, and efficiency was extremely low.

In fact, the Vinegar Jar was actually restrained by a single silver level.

Because a person was far more flexible than a magic ship.

But the priest didn’t choose to bravely charge the Vinegar Jar.

Previously, Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai had been defeated by it, and Commander Tie Bang even lost his life.

Xi Dai Zi knew the Vinegar Jar only had Yao Guan Zi, a single silver level, in it, but the humans didn’t.

Since the enemy’s power was unknown, the Wealth priest decided not to take a risk.

This was his parish.

His mentality was the closest to Hun Tong’s, he hoped to stabilize the war situation and protect this territory as soon as possible.

Chapter 394: Chi Lai Fires!

The priest evaded the pursuing Vinegar Jar while doing his best to reinforce the other human fighters.

Divine spell – Weaken Armor.

Divine Spell – High Speed.

The Wealth Priest’s main target was the closest to him, Zong Ge.

The High Speed divine spell doubled Zong Ge’s speed.

While the Weaken Armor divine spell affected the Steel Bone Knight fishman, decreasing his defenses.

With that aid, Zong Ge didn’t need to fully use speed increasing battle skills to ward off the Steel Bone Knight fishman’s Lance Image battle skill.

While his sword blade immediately gouged incisions into the fishman’s body.

The fishman’s steel bones no longer gave him a strong defense.

The pressure on the fishman knight multiplied, and for a moment, he suffered defeat after defeat.

When Yao Guan Zi saw this, he was hit with inspiration.

A moment later, he changed his attack target and no longer chased after the nimble Wealth priest, instead he headed for the fight between Zong Ge and the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

His choice was very rational.

It was hard to have an impact chasing the Wealth priest around.

Zong Ge was a silver level fighter, and compared to the priest, he seemed to be far slower.

More crucially, the fishmen’s silver level knight was already surrounded by perils; if he wasn’t reinforced, he might be slain in battle.

In the port battlefield, the fishmen’s side was already in a vulnerable state, their silver levels couldn’t be lost.

If one died in battle, it would set off a chain reaction that would lead to the other silver level fishmen being beheaded sooner, then the port battlefield would be won by the humans.

If the humans won, they would immediately clear up the new island and reorganize their defensive line.

Following this, if the fishman main force tried to land on the island, they would need to pay a more disastrous price to do so.

That wasn’t the main point, however.

If the human silver levels got their hands free, they could interfere with the gold level duels.

As for the others, Yao Guan Zi wasn’t that worried.

He was most worried about the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

Not long ago, they had taken on the gold level Rou Zang.

Seeing the Vinegar Jar heading towards Zong Ge, the dragonman youngster decisively ordered, “Fire!”

Chi Lai had been waiting for this order for a long time!

Right now, he was hidden in one of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships, in control of their gold level cannon.

After escaping the auction hall trap, he had followed the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge to the port battlefield.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge went into battle while he snuck into a ship to gain control over the gold level cannon.

The survivors had one gold level cannon.

This cannon had played a significant role when they faced off against Rou Zang.

Of course, successes should be recreated.

Therefore, early on, the cannon had been smuggled onto a ship. Its specific position was kept strictly secret, and only the survivors knew where it was.

When a battle appeared in the future, the formation shielding the cannon would restrain its aura to near nothing, allowing it to catch an enemy unprepared when it opened fire!

When Chi Lai came face to face with the gold level cannon, he found it utterly irresistible.

He was a gunner, a heavy gunner at that. Instead of high fire rate cannons, he liked cannons with a lower fire rate and stronger shelling power.

Chi Lai imbued his silver battle qi into the c annon, gradually bringing it under his control.

He didn’t rashly open fire.

The dragonman youngster had emphasized that he wasn’t authorized to start shooting yet.

He had to restrain himself.

Chi Lai had good military attainment and had been observing the port battlefield.

Even if the dragonman youngster hadn’t ordered him to do so, he, himself, wouldn’t have decided to recklessly attack.

The reason was simple.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships were frail.

When he opened fire, he was sure to attacked on all sides.

Enemies would crazily attack, and they wouldn’t hesitate to pay silver level fishmen lives to destroy the gold level cannon.

This wasn’t the worst thing though.

The scary thing was that a gold level cultivator was bound to bring their ruthless hand down.

Because the gold level cannon was a powerful threat to them too!

When the Vinegar Jar had come ashore and displayed its terrible might, Chi Lai was more unwilling to open fire.

Compared to the light cannon turrets, ordinary ships were as weak as paper.

Two Eyes Island’s war situation was constantly changing.

The gold level battles were deadlocked, and when the priests came in to provide support, they had broken the port battlefield’s impasse, causing the Vinegar Jar to crush the ship Zong Ge was on.

“A chance!” Chi Lai keenly sensed his opportunity in battle.

If he attacked now, it would be hard for the gold level cultivators to respond to him.

The Vinegar Jar was getting closer at its own volition, and hitting it was a near certainty.

Furthermore, with the priests and other cultivators’ help, they could work together to wipe out the fishmen and secure victory on this battlefield!

But Chi Lai was constrained by the dragonman youngster.

Just as he was worried that the dragonman youngster was going to let this opportunity slip away, he received the order through an alchemy device.

“Fire!” The dragonman youngster’s order at that moment was the sweetest thing Chi Lai could hear!

After receiving his commander’s order, he immediately acted.

“Great Justice God, my Lord!”

“The sinister enemy is close at hand; I need thy prestige and strength to beat back evil and danger.”

“We must let the people know that justice will always defeat evil!”

Divine spell – Mark Evil.

Divine spell – Justice Cannon!

The glory of the first divine spell fell from the sky and onto the Vinegar Jar, denoting it as an evil enemy.

The second divine spell’s power was imbued into the gold level cannon, then condensed into the artillery shell loaded in it.

The divine spell violently fluctuating instantly attracted the attention of the fishmen.

“Not good!” Yao Guan Zi realized things were getting bad and immediately opened fire.

The Vinegar Jar’s bottom cannon mouth immediately jetted out a water shell towards the ship.

The dragonman youngster wanted to help, but it was beyond his reach.

If Chi Lai hadn’t used a divine spell, the ship’s concealment formation wouldn’t have leaked any aura. But after he had done so, he instantly revealed himself and the alchemy cannon, drawing the enemy’s timely attacks.

Several spherical water shells fired, each the size of an elephant.

They whistled through the air, and when they smashed into ships, they would tear their hulls into splinters in a flash.

Chi Lai faced this fatal crisis with a confident smile.

His square face illuminated with the light of the Justice God, giving him an increasingly resolute appearance.

Bang!

A moment later, the cannon fired.

A gold level cannon.

A gold level artillery shell!

The entire cannon mouth seemed to eject a gigantic pillar of light.

At the very front of the light pillar was the gold artillery shell.

The artillery shell pierced hulls without any hindrance.

The white pillar of light stung eyes; none could look straight at it.

The formidable artillery shell squeezed air into a spatial ripple.

Several water shells were in front of the gold artillery shell.

Bang, thump, slam…

The gold artillery shell smashed through the water shells, its speed only decreasing a mere 20%.

Yet, under everyone’s shocked eyes, the artillery shell drilled into the Vinegar Jar’s hull.

The hull had remained invincible against all other artillery shells.

Bang.

The sound was deafening.

The rapidly spinning and momentous silver level magic ship was suddenly damaged heavily.

It suddenly came to a standstill, its forward momentum completely offset.

After rotating half a circle, it began to fall.

The Vinegar Jar was shaped like a water jar, and now, the water jar’s circumference touched the ground, while the bottom was perpendicular with it, totally exposed to everyone before it.

“The Vinegar Jar!!!”

“Only one shot…incredible.”

“What was that?”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps…gold cannon! How could I forget about it!”

The entire port went silent for a second because of this cannon, then soon after, shouts of alarm continuously arose and resonated to the skies.

Huff, huff, huff…

Chi Lai gasped for air; his arms propped up his body on the fiery rear section of the cannon.

He had been building up his power for that one attack.

He had depleted much of his battle qi, carried out prayer, and had used a divine spell.

After attacking, too much of his strength had drained away; he felt like collapsing.

But at the same time, he also felt an intense invigoration.

“How long has it been since I fired such a cannon?”

“Perhaps joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the right choice.”

In that moment, Chi Lai had greater and more beautiful hopes for his future in this mercenary corps.

“Outstanding!” Zong Ge sincerely exclaimed in admiration to himself, “If it were me, it would have produced an immense explosion, bringing smoke, dust, and disaster to the surroundings.”

“But Chi Lai didn’t.”

“His artillery shell’s power was concentrated in one place, allowing him to penetrate the Vinegar Jar entirely.”

The gold level artillery shell had gone through the front of the Vinegar Jar, then out the back, creating a convex protrusion as it exited.

Large amounts of poisonous water leaked from this hole.

The humans and fishmen who weren’t able to get out of the way and were soaked by the water softly groaned, and fell to the ground, poisoned to death.

Chaos reigned around the Vinegar Jar.

The fishmen cried out in panic, their morale dipping.

The humans got out of the way, their battle intent growing more vigorous.

Cough, cough, cough.

Yao Guan Zi violently coughed in the control cabin.

He looked on the verge of vomiting out all of his blood.

Although the cannon merely brought misfortune to him, he had sensed the strong aura of death at that moment.

He had used all of his strength to beat Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai.

That his ill, barely clinging on body had driven the Vinegar Jar into many light cannon turrets could be said to be a small miracle.

His body was already at the edge of collapse.

That it hadn’t yet was entirely due to his own willpower.

When the Vinegar Jar was shot, Yao Guan Zi’s life aura also fell too, and in a flash, it had reached rock bottom.

Sensing his life aura change, the humans hesitated, worried that this was an enemy trap.

Xi Dai Zi’s heart grieved, he knew Yao Guan Zi would die where he lay.

He called out to Peng Ding Zi to disengage with the dragonman youngster and to go to the Vinegar Jar.

The dragonman youngster growled and pursued close behind.

The spiky fishman was forced to stay behind and top the dragonman youngster for the time being.

On the other side, the Steel Bone Knight fishman acted according to circumstances, attacking while retreating in an attempt to converge with Xi Dai Zi.

“Stop him!” The Fertile Land priest shouted.

“Stop right there.” The Wealth priest promptly interfered, barring Xi Dai Zi’s way.

“Shi Fa!” The Wealth priest called his helper.

Shi Fa, with playing cards whirling around him, flew into the air and waved them into the hole in the Vinegar Jar.

In an instant, a dozen playing cards traveled through the hole and flew into the Vinegar Jar.

Bang, thump, boom.

The playing cards’ magic triggered.

The Vinegar Jar was bombarded by multiple magics.

“rlnagrlanlrm”

Xi Dai Zi wailed, calling Yao Guan Zi’s full name.

Yao Guan Zi’s life aura completely faded.

“Well done!” The Wealth priest exclaimed.

Shi Fa had killed Hong Bo, eliminated Yao Guan Zi, and proved that the Vinegar Jar was empty.

“Seize the ship!” The first to respond was Zi Di.

She expressed her opinion to the dragonman youngster via an alchemy device.

Chapter 395: Seizing the Ship

The Vinegar Jar, a silver level magic ship!

Although it suited fishmen the most, humans could operate it too.

Besides, the survivors could remodel it in the future.

“Good idea!” The dragonman youngster was adept at accepting good suggestions and changed his offensive towards the Vinegar Jar.

Over yonder, Zong Ge also received this information and moved likewise.

Battle skill – Rapid Advance.

With the divine spell and his battle skill Rapid Advance combined, he was able to instantly shake off the Steel Bone Knight fishman.

The fishman knight had been attacking and retreating, intending to group back up with Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi was heading for the Vinegar Jar, and the fishman knight was the same, Zong Ge was right behind him.

As a result of Zong Ge’s sudden increase in speed, he quickly passed his opponent in their race towards the Vinegar Jar.

The key was that Zong Ge ran faster than him!

For a split second, the fishman knight was stupefied.

A moment later, he reacted as panic rushed into his mind.

“Yao Guan Zi is dead, and the Vinegar Jar cannot be stolen away!”

“Although it was struck by a cannon and capsized, because its main operator Yao Guan Zi is dead, it is possible another can operate it.”

Even though the Vinegar Jar was immobilized and capsized on its side, its numerous bottom cannons could still be used.

At minimum, it could be treated as a fort and cannon turret.

It would give the fishmen a large terrain advantage.

In the port battlefield, the fishmen were already at a disadvantage, and if they lost the Vinegar Jar, it was bound to cause a rout.

“Defend the Vinegar Jar!”

“First, it is still formidable, and second it is a symbol. If it falls into enemy hands, our morale will take a big hit.”

“We won’t be able to support the main force’s arrival.”

A sacrificial mentality emerged in the Steel Bone Knight fishman’s heart.

With red eyes, he ran as fast as he could and began to desperately chase Zong Ge down.

Bang.

The dragonman youngster’s fist smashed the spiky fishman into the air.

The dragonman youngster’s Bursting Explosion battle qi and the legendary Flame Dragon King bloodline meant that every time he used Demolition Fist or Surge, it would trigger explosions big and small that echoed with ferocious prestige.

The spiky fishman was beneath mention, and even if there was another of him, it would still be hard to bar the dragonman youngster’s way.

The young man easily punched the spiky fishman away and was about to swagger off and march towards the Vinegar Jar.

At this moment, the spiky fishman behind him coughed out blood, then suddenly roared wildly.

Racial magic – Swelling Balloon.

This was Peng Ding Zi’s trump card, it couldn’t be rashly used, and the consequences of using it was very severe.

He had no choice but to use it now.

This was an ability that came with his bloodline.

After the spiky fishman used it, his body began to rapidly inflate like a puffy balloon, his limbs, chest, and buttocks all swelled to twice their size.

His weight substantially decreased, to the extent that when he wasn’t moving, he would slowly float into the sky.

Silver battle qi suddenly flashed across Peng Ding Zi’s body.

When Peng Ding Zi used this battle skill, his speed dramatically increased, and within two seconds, he was able to attack the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster narrowed his eyes, looked at the strange, swelling enemy, and tried to attack them.

But when Demolition Fist hit Peng Ding Zi, it seemed to sink into the latter like he was hitting cotton.

The dragonman youngster’s expression changed.

Demolition Fist had obviously worked, but it didn’t do anything and not even the most trivial explosion could be heard!

“What’s going on?”

The dragonman youngster was forced to focus all of his energy on dealing with Peng Ding Zi.

The spiky fishman became very troublesome.

After his weight had lightened, his movement and attack speed had also increased several fold. Before this, he had to cope with the dragonman youngster’s many attacks, but now he could easily evade them.

After a few attacks and blocks, the dragonman youngster began to understand what was going on with his opponent.

Demolition Fist had been properly used, but when it hit the spiky fishman’s body, its might was instantly absorbed by them.

Every time it was absorbed, Peng Ding Zi’s body swelled some more.

At the beginning, Peng Ding Zi had only swollen two fold. But after several rounds with the dragonman youngster, he had swelled to double that!

“There should be a limit to this ability.”

“His absorption cannot be limitless.”

“Furthermore, he cannot absorb sword attacks and other direct attacks.”

Although the dragonman youngster wasn’t using a sword, his dragon claws were natural weapons.

Instead of using battle skills, he decided to attack with his dragon claws.

Peng Ding Zi’s wounds quickly piled up and his blood was shed, yet he was fighting to the death and didn’t retreat.

“This guy doesn’t want to live.” The dragonman youngster quickly realized this fact.

The spiky fishman would rather be wounded and risk dying in battle to ensnare him here.

As for Zong Ge, he was still further from the Vinegar Jar than the dragonman youngster.

In another battlefield.

Hu Li jumped high into the air.

“The Vinegar Jar is mine!” His heart cried out as he grew impatient.

The Vinegar Jar’s powerful display of strength had deeply engraved itself into Hu Li’s mind.

The Applaud Pirates’ boss had wanted to steal this ship for a long time.

But since then, his crew had been slain by Men Shi, Qing Xin, and the others. As the leader, he was forced to leave the port to take back his territory.

Hu Li was fighting Qing Xin and Men Shi alone!

From the beginning of the fight, he had been winning.

Despite the fact that his opponents were a silver level magician and a silver level fighter.

Hu Li was a renowned silver level, and his fighting style also helped him too.

Hu Li had a jumping battle skill, and he was very nimble. Men Shi was a dwarf, he had outstanding defense, but he was too slow, only able to eat the dust left behind by Hu Li.

Although Qing Xin was a silver level, she had nothing on her and no equipment on hand.

She was flying in the air, but that didn’t give her safety.

For one, she had to evade arrows and bullets, and second, every time Hu Li got near, he was a massive threat.

Hu Li suddenly screamed powerfully.

Although he was winning, he wasn’t able to beat his opponents.

Chi Lai’s shell had made a giant impact, causing everyone nearby to pay attention to the port battlefield.

Hu Li constantly had the Vinegar Jar in his mind, and now that it was about to fall into another’s hand, how could he not be anxious?

He suddenly shouted something in the frogman language.

His underlings were currently fighting the combined force of the blond mother’s group and the dwarfs.

After hearing this order, a group of frogmen separated from this.

There were sixteen of them in total, and although they had varied builds, with some old and some young, all of them were wearing the same gloves.

The gloves on their hands mad their already gigantic frogman palms even bigger.

The frogmen urged their battle qi and sed the same battle skill – Palm Sound Force!

Clap, clap, clap.

They clapped their palms together in an orderly fashion.

The applause quickly expanded to cover an area of several hundred meters.

Qing Xin and Men Shi were enveloped by this sound force and were immediately perturbed.

By contrast, Hu Li was like a fish back in water, drawing power from it that increased his military power slightly.

Battle skill – Musical Palm Scale.

He waved his palm, influencing the sound force around him, instantly condensing it into a translucent flight of stairs.

When Hu Li was jumping, he had to land back on the ground. But now on this flight of stairs. He had a new foothold to step on, greatly changing the situation.

Like a bouncing ball, he was constantly bouncing around on the musical scale, the ground, and roofs.

His patterns varied and weren’t as easy to predict as before.

“This is the Applaud battle squad, the namesake of the Applaud Pirates.”

“It isn’t just those frogmen, Hu Li is also a part of the battle squad, that is why he’s able to use the sound force too!”

The enemy was competently working together, while Qing Xin and Men Shi had no such coordination.

Qing Xin was constantly being forced into danger by Hu Li.

Men Shi wasn’t able to hit the enemy high in the air, he had no other option but to turn around and attack the Applaud frogmen.

There was a cheer on the White Bandage.

“We won!”

“We finally took back out flagship.”

The blood soaked Bandage mercenaries had fought bravely to finally retake the White Bandage from the fishmen, the magic ship was now back under their control.

The mercenaries crowded around their commander, their expressions inspiring and their eyes filled with adoration.

During this retaking of the ship, Commander Beng Dai was the most crucial person. It was he who had led the charge, he who fought with all he had, and he who had destroyed the defenses here.

The mercenaries followed their commander in and won the battle.

But at this moment, Commander Beng Dai wasn’t looking good.

He was standing on the side of the ship and looking at the Vinegar Jar sitting on the port.

Chi Lai’s shot also attracted his gaze.

Commander Beng Dai and Tie Bang had forced their way into the Vinegar Jar together, resulting in the former fleeing in defeat and the latter losing his life.

After Commander Beng Dai got out of danger, he spent a short time resting.

Afterwards, he adjusted his target and led a squad in an attempt to retake the White Bandage.

The Iron Lump and the White Bandage were magic ships, they had been the furthest from the port and had been swiped by the Vinegar Jar Pirates.

Er Gua Zi and Song Shou were doing battle.

His subordinates were steering the two magic ships to join the ocean battlefield.

Merchant, transport, and Vinegar Jar Pirate ships large and small were locked in battle with each other.

Commander Beng Dai jumped from ship to ship and killed their way across them until they were finally able to charge onto the White Bandage.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates’ experts weren’t present on the ship, those on board weren’t able to hold off Commander Beng Dai’s wild attack.

After all, Commander Beng Dai was a veteran silver level fighter.

“The Vinegar Jar…is going to be taken by someone else!” Commander Beng Dai sighed to himself.

The joy of taking back his flagship was lost amidst the bubbling disappointment, envy, and regret that filled his heart.

The White Bandage could not compare to the Vinegar Jar.

The former was a mere bronze level, a far cry from the silver level Vinegar Jar, a whole level above iron.

He thought about the danger he had faced and the blood he had paid yet ended up empty-handed.

“If it really falls into another’s hand, then I would have contributed greatly to it!”

Commander Beng Dai’s whole heart was agonized and he also felt wronged.

So much so that a dark thought remained in his mind, he hoped the humans would fail.

“Commander, we can’t take the Vinegar Jar, but we can attack the Iron Lump!” Someone next to him suggested.

When Commander Beng Dai heard that, his eyes suddenly flashed.

He looked at who suggested it, it was the person who understood him the most, his auxiliary commander.

Chapter 396: The Undiscovered Traitor Exposed

“You still understand me.” Commander Beng Dai secretly praised, but on the surface he hesitantly replied, “Is that a good idea? If we seize the Iron Lump, how could we face the Iron Club Mercenary Corps?”

The auxiliary commander resisted the urge to roll his eyes as he responded, “Commander, you worry too much about old friendships. Right now, taking the Iron Lump is how we help our friend.”

“Commander Tie Bang is dead.”

“We must help them.”

“Of course, when we return the Iron Lump to them in the future, they’ll need to reward us somewhat for that.”

Those words interpreted from another point of view implied that “if the reward wasn’t to their satisfaction, then the Iron Lump wouldn’t be returned”.

“Your words have merit.” Commander Beng Dai immediately smiled when he heard the auxiliary commander’s veiled intent and was eager to adopt this idea.

He raised his voice as he looked to his other subordinates, “Did you hear that?”

“Let’s help our friends take back the Iron Lump!”

The mercenaries cheered together, vigorously responding to Commander Beng Dai’s call to battle.

But a moment later, someone reported something.

“The Iron Lump is being attacked!”

“Not by people, but by many alchemy puppets.”

“What?” Commander Beng Dai and the others turned pale, and they all leaned forward to gaze into the distance.

Commander Beng Dai took out a telescope and saw the swordsmen guard puppets killing their way across the Iron Lump.

He was utterly bewildered, “What’s going on?”

“That can’t be the Iron Club Mercenary Groups’ trump card.”

“Whose troops are those?”

The Bandage Mercenary Corps was normally the Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ rival, and sometimes partners.

Two Eyes Island is only so big, and after interacting with each other for so long, they knew each other’s foundation.

Therefore, Commander Beng Dai knew for certain that this wasn’t how the Iron Club Mercenary Corps operated.

Was it the feudal lord’s forces? Some merchant alliance force? Or perhaps…the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

Commander Beng Dai was suspicious of everyone.

“Commander, what should we do?” An obtuse mercenary asked.

Commander Beng Dai ruthlessly glared at the asker, “The enemy is powerful and unknown, we are spectators for the time being and don’t need to take the risk!”

“The commander is wise!” The auxiliary commander immediately approved.

The person that mobilized the alchemy puppets to attack the Iron Lump was Zi Di of course.

When she suggested to the dragonman youngster that it was time to seize the ship, she had secretly dispatched alchemy puppets from the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

Although the Iron Lump had fallen into the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ hands, there was no experts on board it.

The Vinegar jar Pirates’ experts were involved in their respective fights and weren’t able to reinforce.

Their main force were still fighting merchant alliance ships.

After Er Gua Zi had personally slaughtered the humans of the White Bandage and Iron Lump, the people transferred onto them were universally weak.

This gave the Bandage Mercenary Corps a golden opportunity to retake their flagship.

Likewise, it was an opportunity for Zi Di too.

Through the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection methods, she could see the entire battlefield clearly and knew where the enemy’s weak links were.

The alchemy puppets quickly slew all of the fishmen around them.

But now there was a difficult problem before Zi Di.

Such as how to operate this bronze level magic ship.

She didn’t have authority over it and had to try to figure it out.

Another thing was that alchemy puppets weren’t flexible enough to control the Iron Lump.

“Cang Xu, please come over and take charge of the Iron Lump.” Zi Di contacted Cang Xu.

Cang Xu had quietly left the port battlefield and was prepared to help Qing Xin and the others. But something unexpected happened, Qing Xin and Men Shi had joined forces to fight a fierce battle against Hu Li.

Cang Xu was only an iron level, and recklessly interfering in a fight between three silver levels was obviously unwise.

He continued to hide, waiting for the right opportunity.

As a result, before his chance arrived, Zi Di had contacted him.

“Qing Xin’s situation is getting worse, but she should last for a time.” Cang Xu didn’t leave immediately; he made his own judgement.

Zi Di didn’t command him, only the dragonman youngster could do so.

Cang Xu swept his eyes across the port battlefield.

He saw the humans were gaining the upper hand, and after weighing things swiftly, he decided to heed Zi Di’s proposal.

Cang Xu moved again, leaving his hiding spot and diving into the ocean.

He wanted to swim through the ocean and around the maritime battlefield to get onto the Iron Lump.

Magic spell – Sudden Spring!

Just as Cang Xu was leaving, Xi Dai Zi was obstructed.

The magic he used split open the ground around him, the resulting shockwaves devastated everything around him.

Wherever they went, human fighters were flung into the air.

Sudden Spring was about as powerful as Battering Hammer, and when many people were struck by it and thrown into the air, their falling corpses left sights so horrible, no one could bear to look at them.

Sudden Spring was very powerful and well hidden; thus, the Wealth priest was forced to get out of its way.

But a moment later, the Fertile Land priest behind Xi Dai Zi took out his sacred hoe.

He dug the hoe into the ground and used a divine spell.

Divine Spell – Soil Crusting.

The ground was suddenly reinforced and became very hard.

Even though Sudden Spring could still break out of the ground, its might had fallen and was no longer a threat.

Xi Dai Zi became furious and took out his belt whip.

It was his magic weapon.

At the same time, he took out a totem from his storage bag.

A Rage Totem!

A bloody phantom suddenly rose from Xi Dai Zi.

The phantom looked like him, and after appearing, it quickly dissipated.

In a split second, Xi Dai Zi’s attack and speed dramatically increased.

The Wealth priest was no longer able to intercept him.

Xi Dai Zi charged towards the Vinegar Jar.

They couldn’t allow Xi Dai Zi into the Vinegar Jar.

He knew this magic ship well, and if he got in, he was sure to regain control of it quickly.

“Shi Fa!” The Wealth priest shouted.

The playing card magician was the final line of defense.

After he had killed Yao Guan Zi, he began to drain the poison water and used detection magic on the ship’s interior.

Before he could examine it properly, Xi Dai Zi had charged in.

Shi Fa waved his cuff, and three playing cards flew out, turning into three fire balls shaped like a to stop Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi snapped his magic whip, scattering the three fireballs.

But his speed had slowed by 30%.

As it turns out, Shi Fa had used another playing card with the Slow spell.

“Well done!” The Wealth priest saw his chance to catch up.

He cast a divine spell onto Xi Dai Zi, instantly causing the latter to shake with heavy injuries.

When Shi Fa saw this, he took out three blue playing cards.

Every playing card was a Lightning Spear.

The three playing cards discharged together, and the lightning spears combined into one three meter long spear.

The crackling spear emitted an extraordinary biting cold aura.

“Oh no!” When Xi Dai Zi sensed the danger of death from this spear, he promptly dodged.

But in response to that, the Wealth priest combined his efforts.

Another divine spell fell onto Xi Dai Zi, locking him in place, making him an immobilized target.

“I’m dead!” A moment later, Xi Dai Zi saw the lighting spear shooting towards him.

It brushed past his eyebrow and shot straight into the Wealth priest’s heart!

!!!

This startling development shocked everyone.

Not just the humans, but also Xi Dai Zi and the other fishmen.

“What just happened?”

“Did his shot stray off target?”

“No, the Lightning Spear spell is a directed magic, it is controlled by a magician’s spirit, then why did the shot deviate?”

A number of thoughts churned in people’s minds.

The fact was present before them.

In that case, there was only one thing Shi Fa could be.

“You are a fishman plant?!” The Fertile Land priest furiously asked.

His heart filled with ire and lingering fear.

Shi Fa had hidden well.

He had killed Yao Guan Zi and Hong Bo with his own hands, and had taken Dao Hen captive, yet the one who had eliminated all of these fishmen was the traitor!

The Wealth priest had fallen into his trap when he had done his best to pin down Xi Dai Zi and wasn’t moving.

Shi Fa had killed him in a single blow!

“I didn’t expect that after hiding for ten years, this would be the moment I revealed myself.” Shi Fa sighed, “In that case, everyone, I shall take my leave first.”

Shi Fa gracefully bowed humbly to everyone.

A playing card flew out and stopped above his head, then exploded in sparkling light.

The light moved across Shi Fa, then faded away.

Magic spell - Short Distance Teleportation!

He actually ran away.

According to common sense, after killing the Wealth priest, he should have helped Xi Dai Zi and the others in their counteroffensive against the humans.

But he didn’t do so and instead left.

The people left behind were utterly bewildered.

What was Shi Fa doing?

What was his purpose?

His conduct didn’t make any sense!

The light of a teleportation spell suddenly appeared in front of the purple scaled fishman.

The light withered away, revealing Shi Fa to the purple scaled fishman.

“You are?” When the purple scaled fishman saw Shi Fa, she exposed her amazement and wariness.

She had contacted the naga priestess, who told her that someone was coming to help her.

The one who came was Shi Fa.

Shi Fa nodded to the purple scaled fishman, he rolled his Adam’s apple and extended his tongue, in his tongue was a pearl.

When the pearl was taken out, Shi Fa’s form also changed; it began to overlap and grow fuzzy.

It was obvious that he had been disguised, he had another true appearance.

When she saw the pearl, the purple scaled fishman’s vigilance was dispelled instantly.

She knew that was a gold level alchemy treasure – Disguise Pearl.

This main refinement material of this pearl was god level, it came from the extraordinary Pearl Bubbles.

The Pearl Bubbles came from her Goddess’ divine artifact, the Mermaid Fairy Tale.

There were none left nowadays.

Shi Fa swallowed the Disguise Pearl again, “Twenty years ago, my God’s oracle created Disguise Pearls and arranged spies to infiltrate every continent. I am one of them.”

“But today has made the identity I painstakingly built utterly useless.”

“Alas, I hope exposing it will be worth it.”

As it turns out, Shi Fa had also received a secret order from the naga priestess, leading to his sudden attack.

Before that, he had killed fishmen to continue hiding, establish merits, and win the trust of the humans, succeeding greatly in all counts.

The purple fishman nodded to Shi Fa and used a respectful and affirmative reply, “Your sacrifice will be worth it!”

“My Goddess’ oracle told us to come here.”

“We are looking for a fishman corpse, in life he was called Hai Ping.”

“Hai Ping?” Shi Fa sensed this name was somewhat familiar.

He soon reacted, “I remember, Hai Pin was a fishman powerhouse who reached the saint level.”

The purple scaled fishman nodded, “He lived incognito in his later years and was buried in this coral ball cage tree.”

“Through dreams, my Goddess told our oracle of his whereabouts.”

Shi Fa frowned, “He has been dead for a long time, don’t tell me we can still resurrect him?”

The purple scaled fishman shook her head, “We don’t need to resurrect him, we just need to bring back his corpse because…he has the Surging Wave fishman bloodline!”

Chapter 397: God Level Bloodline

“What?!” Shi Fa’s body shook, despite hiding for many years, he couldn’t conceal his amazement.

The Surging Wave fishman bloodline was a god level bloodline!

Fishmen had less potential than humans and only had three great god level bloodlines.

The Surging Wave fishman bloodline was one of them.

In other words, fishmen that possessed that bloodline had the possibility of becoming a god!

God level!

This was above the saint and legend level; it was the strongest level.

Every god level bloodline that appeared greatly impacted the power balance of the entire world.

Shi Fa was grave, “But Hai Ping was only able to be a saint level his entire life, this proves that even if he has the Surging Wave fishman bloodline, his bloodline concentration is small and not complete.”

“He has been dead for many years too.”

“We have paid an immense price to just get his corpse, what use is it?”

The purple scaled fishman slyly smiled, “This is a secret that cannot be easily divulged. Shi Fa, you just need to know that this action concerned my Goddess’ recovery, the fishmen, and even the future world order!”

“We must succeed!”

Shi Fa’s mind shook, he soon took a breath, and heavily nodded.

“I will do my best to help you!” Shi Fa was solemn and resolute, “What can I do?”

The purple scaled fishman stroked her chest and coughed, “We have already confirmed that Hai Ping’s corpse is on this island, but where it is exactly is something that needs special magic to locate.”

“However, I was seriously injured in battle.”

“I can no longer use this magic.”

“This magic has to be used by you.”

Shi Fa suddenly looked distressed.

The purple scaled fishman consoled, “I know what you’re worried about, I will help you through it.”

“This will indeed be the first time you will use this magic. But that doesn’t matter, I have an item that can link our spirits together.”

“Therefore, when we use this magic, the success rate will greatly increase.”

“The real problem is…”

“The magic undulation is hard to conceal; it will inevitably expose our location. During the cast time, enemies might search for, attack, and obstruct us.”

“I understand!” Shi Fa nodded confidently with playing cards circling around him, “My cards can deploy magic traps that will offer us brief protection.”

The purple scaled fishman nodded, “I have a defensive skill too.”

The two fishmen casters began to prepare their casting in the depths.

Meanwhile.

The sky above some streets on the new island.

The frogman Hu Li was chasing the magician Qing Xin.

“Chirp.”

He closed his lips tightly, and his belly suddenly bulged like he was nine months pregnant, and from the belly came a muffled sound.

His bulging belly suddenly discharged, regaining its flatness as a force flowed from his belly, up his esophagus, and poured into his nape.

His nape instantly swelled, becoming thicker than his thighs.

“Urp.”

Hu Li barely rolled his Adam’s apple, letting out a sound.

His nape instantly reduced in size as his two cheeks swelled like two bass drums.

“Ha.”

Hu Li opened his mouth, spitting out the thing inside it.

His cheeks immediately turned back to normal.

After arduously brewing this for a long while, what he spat out was of course not normal.

It appeared to be a translucent insipid red and looked like a giant ball whose surface was constantly undulating.

Hu Li stretched out his palm, turning the translucent ball into a tuning fork.

·

Battle skill – Musical Instrument Three Tone Tuning Fork!

The tuning fork was two meters long, its shaft was slender, and it had three prongs at its front end.

Hu Li grabbed the three pronged tuning fork, aimed, then threw it ferociously at Qing Xin.

The tuning fork swiftly cut through the air without a sound, a biting cold killing intent forming in the stillness!

Qing Xin turned grave, ready for it.

She shot out wind blades in an attempt to intercept them.

The light cyan wind blades accurately hit the three pronged tuning fork and were smashed apart like jade.

Qing Xin flew away while launching a ball of wind.

The ball of wind collided with the three pronged tuning fork yet failed to sweep it away and was instead split apart by the tuning fork.

The tuning fork was headed right for Qing Xin.

Qing Xin hastily cast a spell, and didn’t fail to cast Wind Wall.

The tuning fork pierced through the wind wall and stabbed Qing Xin’s underbelly.

Qing Xin covered her underbelly and flew away in a sorry state.

Blood seeped from the gap under her fingers.

Luckily, Hu Li didn’t press the attack.

It wasn’t that he didn’t want to, however, his battle squad had landed in a crisis.

The dwarf Meen Shi couldn’t coordinate with Qing Xin, so he chose to help by attacking the other members of the Applaud battle squad.

The highest of these was only iron level and weren’t Men Shi’s match.

Hu Li threw a cutlass from a distance.

The cutlass contained the battle skill he had hastily used.

Battle skill – Sound Wall!

The cutlass landed on the ground just in front of Men Shi.

From the cutlass, a powerful force erupted that instantly created a surging soundwave that formed a translucent wall.

The wall stopped Men Shi’s charge.

But only for a split second.

Men Shi broke through a moment later and kicked the cutlass away in passing.

He continued to attack the Applaud battle squad, hammering a head open.

Hu Li flew into a rage; he had grown this battle squad with great difficulty, repairing it would be quite hard.

However, that thrown cutlass and one death had given Hu Li the time to rush over to confront Men Shi.

The two fought a fierce battle.

Xi Dai Zi was stuck in the Vinegar Jar.

The Wealth priest who had impeded him was slain by Shi Fa.

Shi Fa teleported away a moment later, vanishing from where he stood.

Everyone else was too far to interfere, and so Xi Dai Zi was easily able to retreat to his flagship.

He followed the ladders to the bottom floor.

“Yao Guan Zi!” At the bottom of the ship, Xi Dai Zi saw Yao Guan Zi’s corpse, his heart suddenly welling with powerful grief.

Yao Guan Zi was changed beyond recognition, his entire body had been torn apart by magic, as if he had been cruelly tortured by magic pages.

He didn’t move and had no aura of life.

“He wasn’t in the cockpit.”

“It should have been Shi Fa’s magic, he realized that death was certain, therefore he left the cockpit and drew the magic elsewhere to protect it.”

It had to be said that Shi Fa had been ruthless.

Xi Dai Zi didn’t have the time to take care of Yao Guan Zi’s corpse, he sat before the control station and filled in Yao Guan Zi’s seat.

“Vinegar Jar, rise for me!” Xi Dai Zi shouted.

When the dragonman youngster saw Xi Dai Zi entering the Vinegar Jar, he immediately transmitted via alchemy device, “Chi Lai, open fire there now!”

Chi Lai hesitated, but a trace of resolve and battle intent appeared, “I can still fight!”

This time, he was a bit slower.

Long term imprisonment and life as a slave had impacted his state.

Otherwise, he wouldn’t have been drained by just one shot.

Well… he could do at least two shots!

From the communication device, the dragonman youngster could also sense Chi Lai’s fighting spirit.

During a brief gap between his fight with Peng Din Zi, he asked, “Are you confident you can sink the Vinegar Jar in one shot?”

Chi Lai was sluggish as he told the truth, “No.”

One cannon shot wouldn’t sink the Vinegar Jar.

Even if it was a gold level artillery shell.

The Vinegar Jar was a silver level magic ship, only a level below. The Vinegar Jar had strong defenses, were that not the case, Yao Guan Zi wouldn’t have dared to charge the Vinegar Jar through the port.

The fact that all of the port’s light cannon turrets had been pulverized was testament to its defenses.

The Vinegar Jar wasn’t able to block Chi Lai’s shell, but it was massive, and unless it was a fatal blow, it could still fight.

This was like when a dagger stabbed someone’s arm or thigh, the person could still fight despite that. But if one was stabbed in the heart, head, or other vital points, then they would be dead in the streets.

Therefore, it was possible that Chi Lai could destroy the Vinegar Jar in a single shot.

The prerequisite was knowing where the Vinegar Jar’s mortal weak points were.

However, he was unfamiliar with the Vinegar Jar’s layout.

This magic ship was built by fishmen and was poles apart from normal ship architecture. For example, its cannon mouths were on the bottom and not high up on the ship.

“Then retreat.” The dragonman youngster ordered.

Chi Lai replied, “What about the gold level cannon?”

Without the slightest hesitation, the dragonman youngster said, “It just a cannon, how can it compare to your life?”

“Chi Lai, trust me.”

“Even if the gold cannon is destroyed, we can get another in the future.”

“Compared to that cannon, I need you more.”

Chi Lai’s heart jumped, he sensed the dragonman youngster’s sincerity.

Both were Justice God believers, and Chi Lai had rarely received such warmness from the secular world.

“I understand.” Chi Lai replied and immediately evacuated.

“Vinegar Jar, rise for me!” As Xi Dai Zi shouted that, the Vinegar Jar immediately responded.

Its cannon mouths once more burst with jets of water.

The water surged violently and smashed into the ground, its reaction force pushing the Vinegar Jar back up to an upright position.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge had just reached the ship.

Bang, thump, bang.

Xi Dai Zi opened a number of cannons.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge sidestepped and weren’t hit by the recklessly fired water shells.

The close range bombardment of water shells formed powerful waves.

The waves swept away the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, the Fertile Land priest, and even Peng Ding Zi, the fishman knight, and many others too.

In order to protect the Vinegar Jar, Xi Dai Zi was forced to fire, despite causing collateral damage to his comrades in arms.

However, a moment later.

When the turbulent waves swept into the spiky fishman and fishman knight, it actually washed their enemies away and helped their allies save them.

The two silver level fighters quickly swam into the Vinegar Jar.

Compared to Yao Guan Zi, Xi Dai Zi was a better controller of the water cannons.

Because he was a fishman shaman, a caster, and under spiritual control, these cannons could be operated more finely.

With two silver level fighters inside it, the Vinegar Jar became harder to capture.

Chapter 398: No Subordinates

Xi Dai Zi’s nostrils ran with two streams of blood.

A strong vertigo also assailed him.

Xi Dai Zi tenaciously pushed on.

Before the battle, he had been wounded.

It was an old injury inflicted when the ghost Lan Lu had ambushed him.

Soul injuries were difficult to recover from completely.

Then the fierce battles ashore had taxed Xi Dai Zi’s abilities to their limits.

“I must push onwards!”

“Everything for the clan, everything for the homeland!!”

Xi Dai Zi’s body was in a bad state, but his fighting spirit remained high.

He firmly took a few breaths, slowly regaining his strength.

After getting the two silver levels back, his position was finally stable, and his next action needed no consideration, the first target of attack was the gold cannon.

A survey of the entire battlefield determined the gold cannon was the biggest threat.

Bang, bang, bang.

The water cannons opened fire without the slightest hesitation.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ship had been berthed at port, and had been used as a fort.

The water shells hit the hull in quick succession, turning the ship into unrecognizable wreckage, sinking it.

Luckily because the dragonman youngster had given the order to retreat ahead of time, the mercenaries had evacuated and few were lost.

“The gold cannon…” Chi Lai lamented as he dove deep into the sea, dodging the bombardment.

Gold level equipment was very valuable, and weapons like this cannon was strictly controlled and very difficult to buy on the market.

To Chi Lai, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was not the imperial navy, it would be difficult for them to replace this cannon in the future.

“I can still repair it!” Zi Di judged from the Deep Sea Monster Fish from the ocean floor.

Before the bombardment, she had dispatched two deep sea salvage alchemy puppets to infiltrate the seafloor under the port, prepared to retrieve the cannon.

Through the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection methods, she saw the moment the gold cannon was about to sink down.

The cannon had been struck, bending its barrel distinctly.

As for repairing it to working condition and how much that would cost, a verdict could only be determined after recovering it.

Losing the gold cannon was the biggest damage the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had suffered yet during the Two Eyes Island battle.

Zi Di, Zong Ge, and the dragonman youngster were prepared for this loss.

Because the gold cannon was a one shot weapon.

If the gold cannon fired and the enemy still remained, they wouldn’t be so stupid as to allow the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to fire it a second time.

Rou Zang was like that, and so too was Xi Dai Zi.

Although the gold cannon was good, it needed a ship of the same level, or a minimum, a silver level magic ship.

That would protect the cannon, giving it a chance to fire two or even three times.

This deficiency was something the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had.

Xi Dai Zi’s cannon barrage solved the threat the gold cannon posed.

The Fertile Land priest appeared hesitant.

The Wealth priest was dead, sapping most of his battle intent.

This was not his parish, he still wanted to help, but didn’t want to take risks.

When the Fertile Land priest didn’t step up, the human fighters’ morale slumped.

A priest was dead, and the magician Shi Fa had changed sides, causing an enormous impact.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were determined to fight their way onto the deck.

They couldn’t allow the Vinegar Jar to show off its might again!

Otherwise, the port battlefield situation would go completely south.

Now, the main human force on the port was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t have the motivation to fight to the death, this wasn’t their territory.

But the dragonman youngster never gave the order to retreat.

Because if he retreated, morale might crumble, exposing their backs to a fishman slaughter.

Continuing to fight meant there was still a chance of victory.

Turning heel to retreat meant disastrous casualties!

The brutal close quarters fighting meant the dragonman youngster couldn’t stop halfway.

The Wealth priest’s people wanted to help pacify the fishmen, but the unexpected had happened, spurring on the ultimate climax of the battle, thus the dragonman youngster and the others had no choice but to start using their full strength.

It was an utter mess.

Fishmen, pirates, Hun Tong, mercenary corps, merchant alliances, loners, and more were all entangled with each other.

They had all sunk into an immensely chaotic maelstrom that gradually spun beyond anyone’s control.

Every group had their own determination, everyone had their own opinion. These determinations and opinions were transmitted through sturdy bodies, ice-cold weapons, resplendent magic, ferocious battle skills, and collided with each other.

What the final outcome was, who the final winner would be, no one could say at this moment.

The aura of magic began to come from the depths of the underground, then quickly permeated in all directions, forming a vast undulation.

As it turns out, in the underground depths, the purple scaled fishman and Shi Fa had finished preparing for the spell and were not beginning to cast it together.

Just as the purple scaled fishman said, when the cast began, it was quite loud.

This magic undulation was instantly detected by all parties.

“The magic fluctuation came from the island’s underground!” Song Shou thought as he was busy defending himself from Er Gua Zi’s attacks.

“What are the fishmen up to?” Yan Ding slowed his offensive as he looked towards Hun Tong.

Hun Tong couldn’t pull away.

He was still entangled with the gold level pirate Qi You.

Hun Tong was also reflecting over the fishmen’s true goal.

The fishmen’s main army had already been exposed and had been intercepted by Hun Tong’s Puppet Clone and Yan Ding.

But Hun Tong didn’t know what his enemy’s goal was.

He thought the fishmen wanted to take back their homeland.

But the fishmen army’s deployment and attacks all illustrated that they had another goal, taking back their homeland was at best a secondary objective.

“It isn’t just the Blue Green Coral clan, there is also a third party.”

“The third power is above the Blue Green Coral clan; they are the leader of this offensive.”

“What in the devil are they doing?”

Hun Tong immediately contacted the pharmacist to ask about the situation.

The pharmacist and alchemist were silver level casters that served Hun Tong.

Their battle with the purple scaled fishman had left one dead and one injured.

The pharmacist that survived immediately reported the situation to him.

He was in the middle of emergency healing and responded to Hun Tong with agony, “Their true objective?”

“Unclear.”

“But I can sense that it seems she is looking for something in the underground.

The pharmacist stated his guess but had no proof.

“Useless!” Hun Tong angrily scolded him internally.

He was never satisfied with the two casters under him.

But there was nothing he could do.

Casters were rare, and those that would live on his island, far from the bustling imperial mainland, were even rarer.

Hun Tong could only follow up on the pharmacist’s guess, “Don’t tell me that there is a treasure on Two Eyes Island?”

Although Hun Tong had captured Two Eyes Island, he needed the fishmen’s secret spell to truly control it.

“If there really is a treasure, then the fishman elder might be using it to pay the third power.”

“That gave the fishman clan powerful help, creating an alliance to launch this attack.”

“There is another possibility. They are unleashing a most powerful secret spell that would turn Two Eyes Island into a living weapon!”

The more Hun Tong reflected, the darker his expression grew.

He quickly reacted, shaking his head internally.

“There is a serious lack of information currently, it’s unnecessary to think this much.”

“It isn’t wrong to block whatever the enemy wants to do.”

“The magic undulation has leaked, indicating that they don’t have the energy to mask it and that they are very pressed for their goal.”

When Hun Tong thought of that, he immediately gave the order.

He first ordered the pharmacist that after he was done recuperating, that he would attack the magician with all of his might.

The pharmacist lacked battle intent, but when he candidly agreed, he demonstrated his determined battle intent.

Hun Tong understood him and gave more precise orders. The pharmacist would hurry to the barracks, converge with the city guard, and after that, not delay in healing themselves.

The pharmacist had no choice but to do that.

Hun Tong then firmly ordered the city guard’s auxiliary commander, “When you converge with the pharmacist, monitor him secretly, coerce them if need be, and immediately lead the army into the underground depths and destroy the enemy magician’s cast!”

The city guard auxiliary commander looked agonized, but he could only summon his courage and agree to do so.

After transmitting these orders down the command chain, Hun Tong still didn’t feel at ease.

“I have no subordinates.”

At that moment, he fully felt how scarce his talents were, and that he didn’t have reliable subordinates.

His troops had many silver levels, and normally this was fine. Word went out saying that these numerous silver levels made Two Eyes Island seem strong.

But this war had become a quality touchstone test.

In the end, these silver levels had an unbearable performance and were all repulsive with no exceptions.

Of the two casters, one was dead, and one was wounded. The city guard commander was ambushed and beheaded by Dao Hen, utterly unable to do anything leadership wise, and One Eye’s patrol fleet had minute battle results.

“If these guys could perform like Long Fu and Shi Qi, then Two Eyes Island wouldn’t have gotten to this state!”

Although Hun Tong had been fighting, he had been paying attention to the overall situation than the battle.

His information system had been working normally, giving him first hand news.

From the defender’s side, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were the absolute best.

Hun Tong’s subordinates had universally dragged their groins, and although the merchant alliance representatives had many silvers, they had been trapped on the ocean battlefield to protect their fleets. Commander Tie Bang was dead, and after Commander Beng Dai took back his flagship, he was unable to get involved with the port battlefield and couldn’t fulfill anyone’s expectations.

By contrast, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the port battlefield’s main force; they had slaughtered the most fishmen and entangled the fishmen’s silver levels. The gold level shell had even inflicted serious damage on the Vinegar Jar. If they had a little more luck, the blow would have been fatal to the Vinegar Jar, which would have determined the outcome of the battle then and there.

Hun Tong didn’t think the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were just treading water.

Prior intelligence stated that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could face Rou Zang openly, this proved the dragonman youngster’s impressive defense.

Thinking about it, it wasn’t strange.

He was a dragonman, it was likely that through his impressive bloodline, his dragon scales were comparable to gold level armor.

“This mercenary corps’ strength is perhaps worthy of expectations!”

Upon thinking of that, Hun Tong personally contacted Long Fu.

He offered a high price to hire the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to abandon the port battlefield and to instead rush into the depths of the new island to prevent the enemy magician from using their large-scale magic.

Chapter 399: Final Form

The dragonman youngster’s face flashed with a queer expression.

When Hun Tong personally contacted him, the price he offered truly moved the heart.

It was evident that this time, Hun Tong was hiring the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in good faith.

Not long after Hun Tong contacted him, Zi Di also used a communication device to get in touch with the dragonman youngster.

“My Lord, Qing Xin’s situation is getting worse.”

“We might need to come to the rescue!”

Cang Xu had already snuck onto the Iron Lump and was attempting to control the magic ship.

He was the only person on the ship, everything else was alchemy puppets.

But he completely understood that this ship was not like the Vinegar Jar, it couldn’t go on dry land.

Zi Di needed to keep watch on the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and if just Cang Xu and the alchemy puppets came ashore to help Qing Xin and the others, they wouldn’t be a considerable force.

Because Cang Xu couldn’t publicly use necromancy.

Without that, he and the alchemy puppets couldn’t change the situation there.

Zi Di considered past causes and future effects; only the dragonman youngster would have the most certainty of success.

“Just now, Hun Tong decided to contact me to hire us to destroy the fishman magician’s casting. Of Hun Tong’s two magicians, the alchemist is lost and the pharmacist returned alive but was seriously injured.” The dragonman youngster explained the situation to Zi Di.

Since Hun Tong wanted to hire the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he naturally informed the dragonman youngster of the pertinent information.

After Zi Di obtained this information, she muttered to herself, “Shi Fa should have been teleported to the underground depths. The caster is likely him!”

“No matter what the enemy wants to do, breaking the cast is absolutely the right decision.”

“But I fear the underground is very dangerous.”

“The magic fluctuation was very strong, the fishman caster’s location is easily determinable. This also means that they foresee our obstruction and should have deployed defensive measures.”

“We don’t have anyone good at detecting magic traps, a deficiency of ours.”

“If we really want to go, we can utilize scouting puppets to go ahead and sacrifice them to trigger the magic traps.”

The dragonman youngster knew he was faced with another choice.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been involved in the battle for Two Eyes Island since the beginning.

Although the dragonman youngster didn’t want to fight, as the situation developed, he and the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps became stuck in the battlefield, unable to free themselves from it.

Agreeing to Hun Tong’s offer would be profitable. When Zi Di heard his opening offer, Zi Di unconsciously took in a breath of excitement.

But the risks were also massive.

Qing Xin over there also needed help.

Zi Di was good at understanding others, and didn’t advise the dragonman youngster to help Qing Xin, instead she gave him options.

But how could the dragonman youngster not know how important Qing Xin was to Zi Di?

Therefore, the dragonman youngster was more inclined to help Qing Xin.

Furthermore, dealing with Hu Li was far easier than dealing with the caster underground.

“It would be strange to recklessly provide assistance.”

“As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, we do not have sufficient motivation to help Qing Xin.”

“Furthermore, regardless of either choice, breaking out of the port battlefield will be costly.”

The dragonman youngster sighed to himself.

The port battlefield had already reached its climax.

Regardless of human or fishman, the fierce fighting had sunken into a malevolent frenzy.

Retreating now would put a red eye on their backs for their enemies, undoubtably courting death.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had just been established, and its members were in a killing frenzy, not all of them would heed the dragonman youngster’s order.

If the dragonman youngster gave the order to retreat, some would comply, and some wouldn’t.

Differing ideas creates different actions. Some wanting to retreat while others wanting to fight would crumble the front line and give the enemy a golden opportunity to storm through.

“Therefore, there is really only one choice before me.”

“That is to kill them!”

The dragonman youngster’s amber vertical pupils glistened with sharp radiance.

Tempestuous battle intent churned constantly from his heart.

The spiky fishman on board the Vinegar Jar firmly gazed at the dragonman youngster in the distance.

He saw the dragonman youngster suddenly stop moving, then heard the dragonman youngster’s loud declaration.

“Enough.”

“I am already fed up!”

“Fishmen, open your big eyes and watch well.”

“Witness my final form!”

A moment later, the spiky fishman’s eyes widened.

He saw the dragonman youngster’s body transform.

His originally smooth and sleek arms, back, legs, and other body parts formed ring mounds on them.

It was the spurting smoke ring mounds.

Click, click.

The sound came from these ring mounds as saffron flames constantly flashed out.

The Spurting Smoke Dragon bloodline!

Silver battle qi looked like a flame that burned across the youngster’s red dragon scales.

His silver life aura was displayed ostentatiously all around.

No matter who saw it, everyone knew Long Fu was using a trump card!

Under the stares of many people, the dragonman youngster slowly crouched to a half kneel.

He adopted a running start position.

His thick and broad back and vigorous muscles were magnified, along with his fervent masculine aura.

Ding!

Two gigantic spurting smoke ring mounds on his back jetted out fire at the same time.

A strong driving force suddenly erupted!

In the same moment, the dragonman youngster sprinting at maximum speed.

Whoosh!

In an instant, he turned into a walking red artillery shell that plunged into the Vinegar Jar.

Wherever he went, regardless of the fishmen defensive positions or port buildings, all were knocked to the wayside and into the air.

Devastated and destroyed!

“He’s coming, he’s coming!” Peng Ding Zi’s heart jumped as he shouted in a flurry.

The dragonman youngster’s burst of unprecedented speed, his sturdy body and defense similar to gold armor combined to create a projectile that was a fatal threat to the enemy.

“Is this Long Fu’s true strength? No wonder he can restrain the quasi-gold level Shi Qi,”

“Don’t panic!”

“That kind of straight running makes him a target.”

Xi Dai Zi forced himself to calm down and fired the cannons at once.

The water cannons fired at the dragonman youngster.

Because both people were facing each other, barely a half second later, the shells were shooting towards the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster was prepared for this, and when he saw the cannons firing, he had already begun to sidestep them.

His sidestep was different from normal ones.

He simply stretched both hands to his left.

His right arm straightened and extended its palm, pointing the spurting smoke ring mound to the space on his left.

He bent his left arm’s elbow and also opened his palm, pointing both the left the ring mounds at his palm and elbow towards the left too.

Ding.

Three ring mounds sparked with yellow light.

The strong motive power instantly produced firmly pushed the dragonman youngster to the right.

In everyone’s field of view, this was what they saw:

As the water cannon shells came, the dragonman youngster suddenly changed the direction of his charge, turning his perfectly straight movement to the right.

The water cannon shells brushed past the dragonman youngster.

This was the result of the dragonman youngster’s arduous practice!

Without that hard training, he would have been hit by the water cannons, undoubtably wounding him and delaying his battle opportunity.

The dragonman youngster had dodged the water cannons, and in the blink of an eye, arrived at the bottom of the Vinegar Jar.

He hadn’t been far from the magic ship to start with.

With a bang, the dragonman youngster crashed into the Vinegar Jar, leaving a dragonman shaped crater in the steel hull.

The dragonman youngster’s speed vanished as a result.

He shook his head, slightly throwing off the dizziness he felt.

His strong defense here seemed very reliable.

The dragonman youngster bent his body, and like an ambushing tiger ready to pounce for the kill.

His four limbs acted together as he ran up the perpendicular Vinegar Jar’s side.

His sharp dragon claws left marks in his path.

His body was nearly level with the side of the ship, still quite fast as his dragon tail was held up high.

Peng Ding Zi, stationed on the side of the ship, locked onto the youngster, ready on the deck.

But then the dragonman youngster suddenly changed direction, instead running towards a hole in the hull.

It had been the hole Chi Lai had created with his shell.

Peng Ding Zi turned pale with fright; he immediately pulled back and rushed to the ship’s ladder.

The dragonman youngster drilled his way into the big hole and happened to run into the knight fishman.

Xi Dai Zi had deployed him there.

He had Peng Ding Zi outside on the deck and the knight fishman stationed at this hole.

Seeing the far stronger dragonman youngster charging in, the knight fishman yelled and braced himself for the approach.

Bang.

The dragonman youngster’s fist smashed into his belly.

The knight fishman’s exoskeleton shattered, a fist sized hole forming in it.

He flew into the air and smashed into a wall, leaving him stuck in it and hanging in an awkward position.

The dragonman youngster didn’t move to finish him off, instead he ran to the ship’s ladder, wanting to reach the bottom floor.

At that crucial moment, Peng Ding Zi met him, dancing with death to do so.

Bang, bang, boom.

The dragonman youngster beat up the knight fishman and Peng Ding Zi, putting them in a losing position.

The two silver level fishmen were unable to retaliate.

Yet their fighting spirit was strong, and they wouldn’t retreat at the risk of their lives.

Xi Dai Zi in the cockpit beneath used the ship’s monitoring installations to watch the fight play out, his heart couldn’t help but fear trepidation in the face of disaster.

“The Bursting Explosion battle qi technique, Demolition Fist, and the Surge battle skill, along with those round mounds.

“They are all a perfect match!”

“Not just two silvers, if there was a third, I fear his path still wouldn’t be blocked.”

Xi Dai Zi’s scalp went numb.

The dragonman youngster’s trump card made him feel incomparably thorny.

“I can’t stay in the cockpit anymore.”

“It’s too dangerous here.”

Xi Dai Zi opened a secret box and took out a crystal ball.

The crystal ball was a movement control device, it could control the Vinegar Jar to an extent.

Of course, its functions were basic and didn’t match the cockpit’s features.

Xi Dai Zi began to slip away.

Not long after, the dragonman youngster smashed into the bottom floor, but upon breaking down the door, he couldn’t find Xi Dai Zi.

Peng Ding Zi and the fishman knight were injured all over yet were fearlessly throwing themselves from behind.

The dragonman youngster was uninterested in them.

He attacked while moving, actively searching for Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi was hiding everywhere, playing a fatal game of hide and seek with the dragonman youngster.

“Long Fu really is a humanoid cannon.”

“Wherever he goes, things explode.”

“How is he this strong?!”

“This can’t continue. Not only will those two be blown to bits by Long Fu, but the Vinegar Jar will also be gravely wounded and unable to set sail again.”

Xi Dai Zi knew things were going south.

While hiding, he used the water crystal ball to command the Vinegar Jar to keep firing, slaughtering humans while sending a distress call to Er Gua Zi.

Er Gua Zi was busy fighting Song Shou on a beach.

He was on the offensive while Song Shou was forced to passively defend.

Er Gua Zi firmly held the advantage, but when he heard Xi Dai Zi’s call for help, he turned around to head to the port after a moment’s hesitation.

“Long Fu has spoiled my good fun.”

“I will kill you!”

Chapter 400: Fighting a Gold Level

“Take care, the Vinegar Jar Pirate Commander is coming towards you.” Zi Di transmitted to the dragonman youngster.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection methods were numerous and outstanding.

Zi Di’s flow of information was faster and more accurate than even Hun Tong’s.

Er Gua Zi’s move was an abnormal trend that Zi Di noticed.

The latter immediately sent the warning call to both the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge.

Chi Lai had just joined and didn’t receive such treatment.

“Understood.” The dragonman youngster was calm within the Vinegar Jar.

Er Gua Zi doing this was within his expectations.

The dragonman youngster had surmised that this would be the outcome of using the Spurting Smoke Dragon bloodline and using his full strength.

“At this point in the fight, the fishmen don’t have many hidden cards left.”

“Thus, when I kill them, they will begin to collapse.”

“The Vinegar Jar is their flagship, and if the fishmen crumble here, we humans will be the winner of the port battlefield.”

“The fishmen’s gold levels won’t stand idly by.”

“The one most likely to rush over will be Er Gua Zi!”

When the Sheng Ming imperial lords made war, there was a mutual understanding that battle would normally happen between those of the same level.

Gold levels would fight gold levels, and silver levels would fight silver levels.

This was an unspoken rule.

If the strong preyed on the weak, and bullied the lower levels, it would cause people to hold them in contempt.

But now, there were no countries here, and both sides were different races, thus, this battlefield understanding was unheard of.

In this tight battlefield, anyone would fight anyone.

In fact, during this battle, the humans had the most battle results when the strong bullied the weak.

The biggest contributor in this aspect was Yan Ding.
